    var cx = cap(); var connCard = '<div class="card"><h3>' + I('plug') + ' How we post <span class="sp"></span><a class="btn225 sm" data-w225go="#studio/connections">Connections</a></h3>' + PORDER.map(function (k) { var m = modeOf(k), mm = MODE[m] || MODE.reminder; return '<div class="row" style="margin:5px 0;font-size:12.5px">' + mark(k) + '<b style="width:80px">' + E(PLX[k][0]) + '</b><span style="color:' + mm[1] + ';font-weight:700">' + E(mm[0]) + '</span></div>'; }).join('') + (cx && cx.missing && cx.missing.length ? '<p class="muted" style="margin:8px 0 0;color:' + FLAG + '">The Meta token is missing ' + cx.missing.length + ' permission' + (cx.missing.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + ': comments and some formats wait for the new token.</p>' : '') + '</div>';
    var cmCard = '<div class="card"><h3>' + I('chat') + ' Latest comments <span class="sp"></span><a class="btn225 sm" data-w225go="#studio/inbox">Comments</a></h3>' + (open.length ? open.slice(0, 4).map(function (c) { return '<div style="font-size:12.5px;margin:6px 0">' + mark(c.platform) + '<b>' + E(c.author_handle || c.author_name || 'Someone') + '</b> <span class="muted">' + E(ago(c.commented_at)) + '</span><div>' + E(clip(c.text, 140)) + '</div></div>'; }).join('') : '<div class="empty">' + (S.inboxLast ? 'Every comment is answered.' : 'The inbox fills once the Meta token has the comment permissions (Connections).') + '</div>') + '</div>';
    var top = S.social.filter(function (sp) { return sp.published_at && now - new Date(sp.published_at).getTime() < 30 * 86400000; }).sort(function (a, b) { return Number(b.engagement || (Number(b.likes || 0) + Number(b.comments || 0) + Number(b.shares || 0) + Number(b.saves || 0))) - Number(a.engagement || (Number(a.likes || 0) + Number(a.comments || 0) + Number(a.shares || 0) + Number(a.saves || 0))); })[0];
    var topCard = top ? '<div class="card"><h3>' + I('star') + ' Best post, 30 days</h3><div class="pl" style="cursor:default">' + (top.thumb_cached || top.thumb_url ? '<img src="' + E(top.thumb_cached || top.thumb_url) + '" alt="" loading="lazy">' : '<div class="ph"></div>') + '<div style="min-width:0"><b>' + E(clip(top.caption || 'A post', 80)) + '</b><small>' + mark(top.platform) + ' ' + num(top.reach || top.views || 0) + ' reach \u00b7 ' + num(top.likes || 0) + ' likes \u00b7 ' + num(top.comments || 0) + ' comments</small></div>' + (top.url ? '<a class="btn225 sm" href="' + E(top.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + I('ext') + '</a>' : '') + '</div></div>' : '';
    return kpis + '<div class="grid2"><div>' + week + attnCard + apprCard + '</div><div>' + goalsCard + connCard + cmCard + topCard + '</div></div>';
  }

  /* ---------- the queue and the posting schedule ---------- */
  function slotGroups() {
    /* one row per weekday and time, with the platforms that post then */
    var m = {}; slots().forEach(function (s) { var k = s.weekday + '|' + String(s.at).slice(0, 5); (m[k] = m[k] || { wd: Number(s.weekday), at: String(s.at).slice(0, 5), pls: [], ids: [] }); m[k].pls.push(s.platform); m[k].ids.push(s.id); });
    return Object.keys(m).map(function (k) { m[k].pls.sort(function (a, b) { return PORDER.indexOf(a) - PORDER.indexOf(b); }); return m[k]; });
  }
  function queueHtml14() {
    var t = today(), nowHm = hm(new Date().toISOString()), groups = slotGroups(), all = posts();
    var ideas = all.filter(function (p) { return !p.scheduled_at && p.status === 'draft'; });
    var html = '';
    for (var i = 0; i < 14; i++) {
      var d = addDays(t, i), wd = dowOf(d);
      var dayPosts = all.filter(function (p) { return p.scheduled_at && dayOf(p.scheduled_at) === d && p.status !== 'cancelled'; });
      var taken = {}; dayPosts.forEach(function (p) { taken[hm(p.scheduled_at)] = 1; });
      var free = groups.filter(function (g) { return g.wd === wd && !taken[g.at] && !(i === 0 && g.at <= nowHm); });
      var rows = dayPosts.map(function (p) { return { at: hm(p.scheduled_at), html: postRow(p) }; }).concat(free.map(function (g) {
        return { at: g.at, html: '<div class="slot"><div class="ph">' + E(g.at) + '</div><div>Free slot \u00b7 ' + g.pls.map(function (k) { return mark(k); }).join('') + ' ' + E(g.pls.map(function (k) { return PLX[k][0]; }).join(', ')) + '</div><div class="row">' +
          (ideas.length ? '<select data-w225fill="' + d + '|' + g.at + '" aria-label="Fill with an idea"><option value="">Fill with an idea\u2026</option>' + ideas.slice(0, 40).map(function (p) { return '<option value="' + p.id + '">' + E(clip(titleOf(p), 50)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' : '') +
          '<a class="btn225 sm" data-w225slotnew="' + d + '|' + g.at + '|' + g.pls.join(',') + '">' + I('plus') + ' New post</a></div></div>' };
      })).sort(function (a, b) { return a.at.localeCompare(b.at); });
      if (!rows.length) continue;
      html += '<div class="day">' + E(i === 0 ? 'Today' : i === 1 ? 'Tomorrow' : niceDate(d, { weekday: 'long', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })) + '</div>' + rows.map(function (r) { return r.html; }).join('');
    }
    return html || '<div class="empty">' + (groups.length ? 'Nothing in the next two weeks.' : 'Set the posting schedule below: the queue then shows the free slots to fill.') + '</div>';
  }
  /* suggested times: our own results, per platform, weekday and hour (engagement over reach), at least two posts a cell */
  function suggestTimes() {
    var out = {}; var by = {};
    S.social.forEach(function (sp) {
      if (!sp.published_at || PORDER.indexOf(sp.platform) < 0) return;
      var d = new Date(sp.published_at), wd = (d.getDay() + 6) % 7, h = d.getHours();
      var base = Number(sp.reach || sp.views || 0), eng = Number(sp.engagement || (Number(sp.likes || 0) + Number(sp.comments || 0) * 2 + Number(sp.shares || 0) * 3 + Number(sp.saves || 0) * 2));
      if (!base) return;
      var k = sp.platform + '|' + wd + '|' + h; (by[k] = by[k] || { pl: sp.platform, wd: wd, h: h, n: 0, rate: 0 }); by[k].n++; by[k].rate += eng / base;
    });
    Object.keys(by).forEach(function (k) { var c = by[k]; if (c.n < 2) return; c.rate = c.rate / c.n; (out[c.pl] = out[c.pl] || []).push(c); });
    Object.keys(out).forEach(function (pl) { out[pl].sort(function (a, b) { return b.rate - a.rate; }); out[pl] = out[pl].slice(0, 3); });
    return out;
  }
  function schedHtml() {

    var head = '<div class="sched"><div></div>' + WD.map(function (d) { return '<div class="h">' + d + '</div>'; }).join('');

'<a data-w225slotx="' + s.id + '" title="Remove">\u2715</a>' : '') + '</span>'; }).join('') + sl.map(function (c) { return '<span class="t sug" title="Suggested from our results: ' + Math.round(c.rate * 1000) / 10 + '% engagement over ' + c.n + ' posts">' + pad(c.h) + ':00' + (mk ? '<a data-w225sugadd="' + k + '|' + c.wd + '|' + pad(c.h) + ':00" title="Add it">+</a>' : '') + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
      }).join('');
    }).join('');
    var add = mk ? '<div class="row" style="margin-top:12px"><select id="w225spl"><option value="all">Every platform</option>' + PORDER.map(function (k) { return '<option value="' + k + '">' + E(PLX[k][0]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<span class="seg" id="w225swd">' + WD.map(function (d, i) { return '<a data-w225swd="' + i + '" class="' + ((S.keys.swd || []).indexOf(i) >= 0 ? 'on' : '') + '">' + d + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</span><input type="time" id="w225sat" value="' + E(S.keys.sat || '18:00') + '"><a class="btn225 sm pri" data-w225slotadd="1">' + I('plus') + ' Add the time</a><span class="sp"></span><a class="btn225 sm" data-w225sug="1">' + I('bolt') + (sug ? ' Hide the suggestions' : ' Suggest times from our results') + '</a></div>' : '';

  }

  async function addSlots(pls, wds, at) {
    var rows = []; pls.forEach(function (k) { wds.forEach(function (wd) { if (!slots().some(function (s) { return s.platform === k && Number(s.weekday) === wd && String(s.at).slice(0, 5) === at; })) rows.push({ platform: k, weekday: wd, at: at, created_by: myName() || 'staff' }); }); });
    if (!rows.length) { toastS('Those times are already in the schedule'); return; }
    var r = await db.from('content_slots').insert(rows); if (r.error) throw r.error;
    var w = W(); if (w) await w.studio(); toastS(rows.length === 1 ? 'Added' : rows.length + ' times added');
  }

  function approvalsHtml() {
    var all = posts().filter(function (p) { return ['draft', 'scheduled', 'failed', 'published', 'posted'].indexOf(p.status) >= 0; });
    var pend = all.filter(function (p) { return p.approval_status === 'pending' && ['draft', 'scheduled', 'failed'].indexOf(p.status) >= 0; });
    var back = all.filter(function (p) { return p.approval_status === 'changes' && ['draft', 'scheduled', 'failed'].indexOf(p.status) >= 0; });
    var mine = all.filter(function (p) { return lower(p.submitted_by) === myEmail(); });
    var done = all.filter(function (p) { return p.approval_status === 'approved' && p.approved_at && Date.now() - new Date(p.approved_at).getTime() < 30 * 86400000; }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(b.approved_at).localeCompare(String(a.approved_at)); });
    var tabs = [['pending', 'Waiting', pend], ['changes', 'Sent back', back], ['mine', 'Mine', mine], ['approved', 'Approved, 30 days', done]];
    var cur = tabs.filter(function (t) { return t[0] === S.f.apr; })[0] || tabs[0];
    var intro = S.pub ? 'You approve posts: open one to check it, then Approve or Send it back with a note. The person who sent it hears about it in the Hub and on Slack.' : 'Scheduling a post sends it here: the approvers hear in the Hub and on Slack, and once approved it goes out at the time you picked. You hear back the same way.';
    /* wave241: who gets the approval pings (hub_settings content_studio.approvers); empty means the marketing leads and admins */
    var aps = ((S.settings || {}).approvers || []).join(', ');
    var mk277 = S.marketing || [], apSet = ((S.settings || {}).approvers || []).map(function (x) { return lower(x); });
    var apHelp = '<p class="muted" style="margin:6px 0 0">The people pinged when a post waits (with Approve and Send it back links on Slack). Admins and marketing leads can always approve; empty means all of them are pinged.</p>';
    var apBox = S.pub ? (mk277.length
      /* wave277: pick the approvers from the marketing team; the text input below is the fallback when the team list is empty */
      ? '<div class="card" style="margin:0 0 12px"><div class="row"><b>Approvers</b>' + mk277.map(function (m) { var em = lower(m.email), nk = (m.nicknames || [])[0]; return '<label class="row" style="gap:6px;cursor:pointer"><input type="checkbox" data-w277ap="' + E(em) + '"' + (apSet.indexOf(em) >= 0 ? ' checked' : '') + '><span>' + E(m.name || em) + (nk ? ' <span class="muted">(' + E(nk) + ')</span>' : '') + '</span></label>'; }).join('') + '<span class="sp"></span><a class="btn225 sm pri" data-w277apsave="1">Save approvers</a></div>' + apHelp + '</div>'
      : '<div class="card" style="margin:0 0 12px"><div class="row"><b>Approvers</b><input type="text" data-w241approvers placeholder="ian.odhiambo@welfareavenue.com, kahkashan.ansary@welfareavenue.com" value="' + E(aps) + '" style="flex:1;min-width:240px"><a class="btn225 sm pri" data-w241apsave="1">Save</a></div>' + apHelp + '</div>') : '';
    return apBox + '<div class="row" style="margin:0 0 12px"><span class="seg">' + tabs.map(function (t) { return '<a data-w225apr="' + t[0] + '" class="' + (cur[0] === t[0] ? 'on' : '') + '">' + E(t[1]) + '<span class="n">' + t[2].length + '</span></a>'; }).join('') + '</span><span class="sp"></span><span class="muted">' + E(intro) + '</span></div>' +
      (cur[2].length ? cur[2].map(apCard).join('') : '<div class="card"><div class="empty">' + (cur[0] === 'pending' ? 'Nothing waits for an approval.' : cur[0] === 'changes' ? 'Nothing was sent back.' : cur[0] === 'mine' ? 'You have not sent anything for approval.' : 'Nothing approved in the last 30 days.') + '</div></div>');
  }
  /* wave241: the Slack links: #studio/approvals?post=<id>&do=approve|back */
  async function applyApDo() {
    var d = S.apDo; if (!d) return; S.apDo = null;
    var p = posts().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(d.post); })[0];
    if (!p) { toastS('That post is not here any more'); return; }
    if (p.approval_status !== 'pending') { toastS(p.approval_status === 'approved' ? 'Already approved' + (p.approved_by ? ' by ' + E(String(p.approved_by).split('@')[0]) : '') : 'That post is not waiting for approval'); return; }
    if (!S.pub) { toastS('Only an approver can decide; open the post to read it'); return; }
    S.f.apr = 'pending'; paint();
    if (d.do === 'approve') { await decideFrom('approve', p.id); return; }
    var inp = document.querySelector('#w225host [data-w225apnote="' + p.id + '"]');
    if (inp) { inp.focus(); try { inp.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center' }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } toastS('Write the note, then Send it back'); }
  }
  async function saveApprovers() {
    var boxes = document.querySelectorAll('#w225host [data-w277ap]'), list;
    if (boxes.length) list = Array.prototype.filter.call(boxes, function (c) { return c.checked; }).map(function (c) { return lower(c.getAttribute('data-w277ap')); });
    else { var inp = document.querySelector('#w225host [data-w241approvers]'); if (!inp) return; list = inp.value.split(/[,\s;]+/).map(function (x) { return lower(x); }).filter(function (x) { return /@/.test(x); }); }
    try { var value = Object.assign({}, S.settings || {}, { approvers: list }); var r = await db.rpc('studio_settings_save', { p_key: 'content_studio', p_value: value }); if (r.error) throw r.error; S.settings = value; toastS(list.length ? 'Approvers saved: ' + list.length : 'Approvers cleared: the marketing leads and admins are pinged'); paint(); }
    catch (x) { toastS('Could not save: ' + (x.message || x)); }
  }
  async function decideFrom(dec, id) {
    var inp = document.querySelector('#w225host [data-w225apnote="' + id + '"]'); var note = inp ? inp.value.trim() : '';
    if (dec === 'changes' && !note) { toastS('Write a note so they know what to change'); if (inp) inp.focus(); return; }
    try { var r = await db.rpc('studio_decide', { p_id: Number(id), p_decision: dec, p_note: note || null }); if (r.error) throw r.error; toastS(dec === 'approve' ? 'Approved' : 'Sent back with your note'); await loadAll(true); paint(); }
    catch (x) { toastS('Could not save the decision: ' + (x.message || x)); }
  }

  /* ---------- the comments inbox ---------- */
  function scoreOf(list) {
    var since = Date.now() - 30 * 86400000;
    var theirs = list.filter(function (c) { return !c.from_us && c.commented_at && new Date(c.commented_at).getTime() > since && c.status !== 'deleted'; });
    /* answered: the inbox marked a reply (replied_at), or one of our replies sits under it (its time counts) */
    var repAt = function (c) { if (c.replied_at) return c.replied_at; var k = list.filter(function (x) { return x.from_us && x.platform === c.platform && x.parent_external_id === c.external_id && x.commented_at; }).sort(function (x, y) { return String(x.commented_at).localeCompare(String(y.commented_at)); })[0]; return k ? k.commented_at : null; };
    theirs.forEach(function (c) { c.__rep = repAt(c); });
    var answered = theirs.filter(function (c) { return c.__rep; });
    var times = answered.map(function (c) { return (new Date(c.__rep).getTime() - new Date(c.commented_at).getTime()) / 3600000; }).filter(function (h) { return h >= 0; }).sort(function (a, b) { return a - b; });
    var med = times.length ? times[Math.floor(times.length / 2)] : null;
    var within = theirs.length ? answered.filter(function (c) { return (new Date(c.__rep).getTime() - new Date(c.commented_at).getTime()) < 24 * 3600000; }).length / theirs.length : null;
    var score = theirs.length ? Math.round(((answered.length / theirs.length) * 0.6 + (within || 0) * 0.4) * 100) : null;
    return { total: theirs.length, answered: answered.length, med: med, within: within, score: score };
  }
  function postFor(c) {
    var sp = S.social.filter(function (x) { return (c.social_post_id && x.id === c.social_post_id) || (x.platform === c.platform && (x.external_id === c.media_external_id || String(x.external_id).split('_').pop() === String(c.media_external_id || '').split('_').pop())); })[0];
    if (sp) return { thumb: sp.thumb_cached || sp.thumb_url, text: sp.caption, url: sp.url };
    var cp = c.content_post_id ? posts().filter(function (p) { return p.id === c.content_post_id; })[0] : null;
    if (cp) return { thumb: thumbOf(cp), text: titleOf(cp), url: c.permalink };
    return { thumb: null, text: null, url: c.permalink };
  }
  function cmHtml(c, kids) {
    var mk = S.mk, pub = S.pub, r = S.reply[c.id];
    var nm = c.author_handle ? '@' + c.author_handle : (c.author_name || 'Someone');
    var ours = kids.filter(function (k) { return k.from_us; }), theirs = kids.filter(function (k) { return !k.from_us; });
    var replies = lib().filter(function (x) { return x.kind === 'reply'; });
    var acts = mk ? '<div class="acts">' + (c.status !== 'deleted' ? '<a data-w225rp="' + c.id + '">' + I('chat') + ' Reply</a>' : '') +
      (c.platform === 'facebook' && !c.liked_by_us ? '<a data-w225act="like|' + c.id + '">' + I('heart') + ' Like</a>' : '') +
      (c.hidden ? '<a data-w225act="unhide|' + c.id + '">Show again</a>' : '<a data-w225act="hide|' + c.id + '">' + I('eye') + ' Hide</a>') +
      (c.status === 'open' ? '<a data-w225act="resolve|' + c.id + '">' + I('check') + ' Resolve</a>' : c.status === 'resolved' ? '<a data-w225act="reopen|' + c.id + '">Reopen</a>' : '') +
      '<select data-w225assign="' + c.id + '" aria-label="Assign" style="font-size:11.5px;padding:3px 8px;border-radius:999px"><option value="">' + (c.assigned_to ? 'Assigned: ' + E(c.assigned_to) : 'Assign\u2026') + '</option>' + members().filter(function (m) { return m.department === 'marketing' || m.department === 'exec'; }).map(function (m) { return '<option value="' + E(m.name) + '">' + E(m.name) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      (pub && c.status !== 'deleted' ? '<a class="bad" data-w225act="delete|' + c.id + '">' + I('trash') + ' Delete</a>' : '') +
      (c.permalink ? '<a href="' + E(c.permalink) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + I('ext') + ' Open</a>' : '') + '</div>' : '';
    var box = r ? '<div class="rbox"><textarea data-w225rtext="' + c.id + '" placeholder="Reply as Welfare Avenue">' + E(r.text || '') + '</textarea><div class="row" style="margin-top:6px">' +
      (replies.length ? '<select data-w225saved="' + c.id + '"><option value="">Saved replies\u2026</option>' + replies.map(function (x) { return '<option value="' + x.id + '">' + E(x.name) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' : '') +
      '<a class="btn225 sm" data-w225ai="' + c.id + '"' + (r.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('star') + (r.busy ? ' Writing\u2026' : ' Suggest a reply') + '</a><span class="sp"></span><a class="btn225 sm" data-w225rpx="' + c.id + '">Cancel</a><a class="btn225 sm pri" data-w225send="' + c.id + '"' + (r.sending ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('send') + ' Send</a></div></div>' : '';
    return '<div class="cm"><div class="av">' + E(String(nm).replace(/^@/, '').charAt(0).toUpperCase() || '?') + '</div><div style="min-width:0"><div class="who">' + mark(c.platform) + E(nm) + '<small>' + E(ago(c.commented_at)) + (c.like_count ? ' \u00b7 ' + c.like_count + ' likes' : '') + (c.status !== 'open' ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(c.status) : '') + (c.assigned_to ? ' \u00b7 for ' + E(c.assigned_to) : '') + '</small></div>' +
      '<div class="tx' + (c.hidden ? ' hid' : '') + '">' + E(c.text || '') + '</div>' +
      ours.map(function (o) { return '<div class="ours">' + I('chat') + ' ' + E(o.text || '') + (o.replied_by ? ' <span class="muted">(' + E(o.replied_by) + ')</span>' : '') + '</div>'; }).join('') +
      theirs.map(function (o) { return '<div style="margin:4px 0 4px 10px;font-size:12.5px"><b>' + E(o.author_handle ? '@' + o.author_handle : o.author_name || '') + '</b> ' + E(o.text || '') + '</div>'; }).join('') +
      acts + box + '</div></div>';
  }
  function inboxHtml() {
    var f = S.f, all = S.comments.filter(function (c) { return c.status !== 'deleted' || f.inbox === 'all'; });
    var tops = all.filter(function (c) { return !c.parent_external_id || !all.some(function (p) { return p.platform === c.platform && p.external_id === c.parent_external_id; }); }).filter(function (c) { return !c.from_us; });
    var kidsOf = function (c) { return all.filter(function (k) { return k.platform === c.platform && k.parent_external_id === c.external_id; }); };
    var counts = { open: tops.filter(function (c) { return c.status === 'open'; }).length, resolved: tops.filter(function (c) { return c.status === 'resolved'; }).length, hidden: tops.filter(function (c) { return c.status === 'hidden' || c.hidden; }).length, all: tops.length };
    var list = tops.filter(function (c) {
      if (f.inbox === 'open' && c.status !== 'open') return false;
      if (f.inbox === 'resolved' && c.status !== 'resolved') return false;
      if (f.inbox === 'hidden' && !(c.status === 'hidden' || c.hidden)) return false;
      if (f.ipl && c.platform !== f.ipl) return false;
      if (f.imine && lower(c.assigned_to) !== lower(myName())) return false;
      if (f.iq && (lower(c.text) + ' ' + lower(c.author_handle) + ' ' + lower(c.author_name)).indexOf(lower(f.iq)) < 0) return false;
      return true;
    });
    var sc = scoreOf(S.comments);
    var last = S.inboxLast;
    var errs = last && last.result ? Object.keys(last.result).filter(function (k) { return last.result[k] && last.result[k].error; }) : [];
    var head = '<div class="kpis"><div class="kpi"><b class="' + (counts.open ? 'warn' : 'ok') + '">' + counts.open + '</b><span>open</span></div>' +
      '<div class="kpi"><b>' + (sc.score == null ? '\u2013' : sc.score) + '</b><span>comment score</span><em>' + (sc.total ? sc.answered + ' of ' + sc.total + ' answered, 30 days' : 'No comments in 30 days') + '</em></div>' +
      '<div class="kpi"><b>' + (sc.med == null ? '\u2013' : sc.med < 1 ? Math.round(sc.med * 60) + ' min' : Math.round(sc.med) + ' h') + '</b><span>typical reply time</span><em>' + (sc.within == null ? '' : pct(sc.within) + ' within a day') + '</em></div>' +
      '<div class="kpi"><b style="font-size:18px">' + E(last && last.at ? ago(last.at) : 'never') + '</b><span>last check</span><em>' + (S.mk ? '<a data-w225sync="1" style="cursor:pointer;color:' + ROSE + ';font-weight:700">' + (S.busy.sync ? 'Checking\u2026' : 'Check now') + '</a>' : 'Every 10 minutes') + '</em></div></div>' +
      (errs.length ? '<div class="card" style="background:rgba(220,165,39,.10)"><b>Some platforms could not be read:</b> ' + errs.map(function (k) { return E(PLX[k] ? PLX[k][0] : k) + ' (' + E(clip(last.result[k].error, 120)) + ')'; }).join('; ') + '. <a data-w225go="#studio/connections" style="cursor:pointer;font-weight:700">Connections</a></div>' : '');
    var filters = '<div class="row" style="margin:0 0 12px"><span class="seg">' + [['open', 'Open'], ['resolved', 'Resolved'], ['hidden', 'Hidden'], ['all', 'Everything']].map(function (x) { return '<a data-w225ib="' + x[0] + '" class="' + (f.inbox === x[0] ? 'on' : '') + '">' + x[1] + '<span class="n">' + counts[x[0]] + '</span></a>'; }).join('') + '</span>' +
      '<span class="seg"><a data-w225ipl="" class="' + (!f.ipl ? 'on' : '') + '">All</a>' + PORDER.filter(function (k) { return S.comments.some(function (c) { return c.platform === k; }); }).map(function (k) { return '<a data-w225ipl="' + k + '" class="' + (f.ipl === k ? 'on' : '') + '">' + E(PLX[k][0]) + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</span>' +
      '<label class="muted" style="display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center"><input type="checkbox" data-w225imine' + (f.imine ? ' checked' : '') + '> Assigned to me</label><span class="sp"></span><input type="search" data-w225iq placeholder="Search comments" value="' + E(f.iq) + '"></div>';
    /* grouped by post */
    var groups = {}, order = [];
    list.forEach(function (c) { var k = c.platform + '|' + (c.media_external_id || c.social_post_id || 'none'); if (!groups[k]) { groups[k] = []; order.push(k); } groups[k].push(c); });
    var body = order.map(function (k) {
      var g = groups[k], pp = postFor(g[0]);
      return '<div class="card"><div class="post">' + (pp.thumb ? '<img src="' + E(pp.thumb) + '" alt="" loading="lazy">' : '<div class="ph"></div>') + '<div style="min-width:0"><b>' + mark(g[0].platform) + E(clip(pp.text || 'A post', 110)) + '</b>' + (pp.url ? '<a class="muted" href="' + E(pp.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open the post</a>' : '') + '</div></div>' +
        g.map(function (c) { return cmHtml(c, kidsOf(c)); }).join('') + '</div>';
    }).join('');
    if (!S.mk && !S.pub) body = '<div class="card"><div class="empty">The comments are for the marketing team and admins.</div></div>';
    else if (!list.length) body = '<div class="card"><div class="empty">' + (S.comments.length ? (f.inbox === 'open' ? 'Every comment is answered.' : 'Nothing matches.') : 'No comments yet. They arrive here within 10 minutes once the Meta token has the comment permissions (Connections), and from Threads and YouTube once they are connected.') + '</div></div>';
    return head + filters + body;
  }
  var REPLY_SYSTEM = 'You reply to comments on Welfare Avenue\u2019s social media posts. Welfare Avenue is a youth-powered charity in Montreal and Toronto that serves meals and builds community. Write one reply as Welfare Avenue: warm, specific, short (one or two sentences), plain words. Thank people by name when there is a handle. Answer questions only with what the post or comment says; otherwise point to info@welfareavenue.com or the link in our bio. Never promise anything, never invent facts, dates or numbers. Reply in the language of the comment (Quebec French for French). No em dashes. At most one emoji, only when natural. Return only the reply text.';
  async function suggestReply(id) {
    var c = S.comments.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0]; if (!c) return;
    var r = S.reply[id] = S.reply[id] || { text: '' }; r.busy = true; paint();
    try {
      var pp = postFor(c);
      var res = await db.functions.invoke('llm', { body: { task: 'draft', caller: 'content-studio', max_tokens: 300, temperature: 0.5, system: REPLY_SYSTEM, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'PLATFORM: ' + c.platform + '\nPOST: ' + clip(pp.text || '', 600) + '\nCOMMENT by ' + (c.author_handle || c.author_name || 'someone') + ': ' + (c.text || '') } ] } });
      if (res.error) throw res.error;
      var txt = ((res.data && res.data.content) || []).filter(function (x) { return x.type === 'text'; }).map(function (x) { return x.text; }).join(' ').replace(/\u2014/g, ',').trim();
      if (!txt) throw new Error('nothing came back');
      r.text = txt;
    } catch (x) { toastS('The Hub could not help this time: ' + (x.message || x)); }
    r.busy = false; paint();
  }
  async function inboxAct(action, id, extra) {
    if (action === 'delete' && !confirm('Delete this comment on the platform? It cannot be brought back.')) return;
    try {
      var body = Object.assign({ action: action, id: Number(id) }, extra || {});
      await fn('social-inbox', body);
      toastS({ reply: 'Reply sent', like: 'Liked', hide: 'Hidden', unhide: 'Shown again', resolve: 'Resolved', reopen: 'Reopened', delete: 'Deleted', assign: 'Assigned' }[action] || 'Done');
      if (action === 'reply') delete S.reply[id];
      var r = await q(db.from('social_comments').select('*').gte('commented_at', new Date(Date.now() - 90 * 86400000).toISOString()).order('commented_at', { ascending: false }).limit(1500));
      if (r.data) S.comments = r.data;
      paint(); try { if (window.w153) window.w153.paint(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    } catch (x) { toastS('Could not ' + action + ': ' + (x.message || x)); if (action === 'reply' && S.reply[id]) { S.reply[id].sending = false; paint(); } }
  }

  /* ---------- the library: templates, hashtag groups, saved replies, tags ---------- */
  var LIBT = [['templates', 'Templates', 'template'], ['hashtags', 'Hashtag groups', 'hashtags'], ['replies', 'Saved replies', 'reply'], ['tags', 'Tags', null], ['partners', 'By partner', null]];
  /* wave241: past posts by partner organization (content_posts.partner_id, set in the composer) */
  async function loadPartners() { if (S.partners) return S.partners; try { var r = await db.from('wa_partners').select('id,name,status').order('name'); S.partners = ((r && r.data) || []).filter(function (x) { return x.status !== 'declined'; }); } catch (e) { S.partners = []; } return S.partners; }
  async function loadPartnerPosts() {
    await loadPartners();
    var id = S.f.partner; if (!id) { S.partnerPosts = null; S.partnerFor = null; return; }
    if (S.partnerFor === id && S.partnerPosts) return;
    try { var r = await db.from('content_posts').select('id,title,platforms,format,status,scheduled_at,published_at,link_url,canva_url,campaign,caption,partner_id').eq('partner_id', Number(id)).order('scheduled_at', { ascending: false, nullsFirst: false }).limit(120); S.partnerPosts = (r && r.data) || []; }
    catch (e) { S.partnerPosts = []; }
    S.partnerFor = id;
  }
  function partnersHtml() {
    var list = S.partners || [], sel = S.f.partner || '';
    var pick = '<div class="card" style="margin:0 0 12px"><div class="row"><b>Partner</b><select data-w241partner style="flex:1;min-width:220px"><option value="">Choose an organization\u2026</option>' + list.map(function (x) { return '<option value="' + x.id + '"' + (String(sel) === String(x.id) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(x.name) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></div><p class="muted" style="margin:6px 0 0">Every post that names the organization in the composer, newest first: a throwback, a recap, last year\u2019s LinkedIn article. Set the partner on a post to find it here later.</p></div>';
    if (!sel) return pick;
    var ps = S.partnerPosts;
    if (!ps) return pick + '<div class="card"><div class="empty">Loading\u2026</div></div>';
    if (!ps.length) return pick + '<div class="card"><div class="empty">No post names this organization yet. Open a post, pick the partner under Partner organization, save.</div></div>';
    return pick + '<div class="lib">' + ps.map(function (p) {
      var when = p.published_at || p.scheduled_at; var st = stateOf(p);
      return '<div class="it"><div class="row"><b style="flex:1;min-width:0">' + E(p.title || (p.caption || '').split('\n')[0] || 'Untitled post') + '</b><span class="pill">' + E(st.label || p.status) + '</span></div>' +

' <span class="muted">' + E(niceWhen(when)) + '</span>' : '') + (p.campaign ? ' <span class="muted">\u00b7 ' + E(p.campaign) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>' +
        '<p>' + E(clip(p.caption || '', 220)) + '</p>' +
        '<div class="row" style="margin-top:auto"><a class="btn225 sm" data-w225open="' + p.id + '">' + I('eye') + ' Open</a>' + (p.canva_url ? '<a class="btn225 sm" href="' + E(p.canva_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Canva</a>' : '') + (p.link_url ? '<a class="btn225 sm" href="' + E(p.link_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Link</a>' : '') + '<span class="sp"></span></div></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  var TAGCOLORS = ['#6F64AB', '#2A8C7A', '#B4467A', '#DCA527', '#1877F2', '#B3402F', '#103F39', '#C13584'];
  function libTab() { var t = LIBT.filter(function (x) { return x[0] === S.sub; })[0]; return t || LIBT.filter(function (x) { return x[0] === S.f.lib; })[0] || LIBT[0]; }
  function hashCount(s) { return (String(s || '').match(/#[\p{L}\p{N}_]+/gu) || []).length; }
  function libEditor(t) {
    var e = S.libEdit; if (!e || e.tab !== t[0]) return '';
    var nm = '<label class="muted" style="display:block;margin:0 0 4px">Name</label><input type="text" data-w225lf="name" value="' + E(e.name || '') + '" style="width:100%" maxlength="80">';
    var body = '';
    if (t[0] === 'tags') {
      body = '<label class="muted" style="display:block;margin:10px 0 4px">Colour</label><div class="row" style="gap:6px">' + TAGCOLORS.map(function (c) { return '<a data-w225lcol="' + c + '" title="' + c + '" style="width:26px;height:26px;border-radius:50%;background:' + c + ';cursor:pointer;outline:' + (e.color === c ? '3px solid ' + GOLD : 'none') + ';outline-offset:2px"></a>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
    } else {
      var lab = t[0] === 'hashtags' ? 'The hashtags (Instagram takes 5 at most)' : t[0] === 'replies' ? 'The reply' : 'The caption';
      body = '<label class="muted" style="display:block;margin:10px 0 4px">' + lab + '</label><textarea data-w225lf="body" rows="' + (t[0] === 'hashtags' ? 3 : 6) + '" style="width:100%">' + E(e.body || '') + '</textarea>';
      if (t[0] === 'hashtags') { var n = hashCount(e.body); body += '<p class="muted" style="margin:4px 0 0;' + (n > 5 ? 'color:' + FLAG : '') + '">' + n + ' hashtag' + (n === 1 ? '' : 's') + (n > 5 ? ': Instagram keeps the first 5' : '') + '</p>'; }
      if (t[0] === 'templates') {
        var pls = e.platforms || [];
        body += '<label class="muted" style="display:block;margin:10px 0 4px">Platforms</label><div class="row" style="gap:6px">' + PORDER.map(function (k) { return '<label style="display:flex;gap:4px;align-items:center;font-size:12.5px"><input type="checkbox" data-w225lpl="' + k + '"' + (pls.indexOf(k) >= 0 ? ' checked' : '') + '>' + mark(k) + E(PLX[k][0]) + '</label>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
          '<label class="muted" style="display:block;margin:10px 0 4px">First comment (optional)</label><input type="text" data-w225lf="first_comment" value="' + E(e.first_comment || '') + '" style="width:100%">';
      }
      if (t[0] === 'replies') body += '<p class="muted" style="margin:4px 0 0">Use {name} for the person\u2019s name or handle.</p>';
    }
    return '<div class="card" style="border-color:' + ROSE + '"><h3>' + I(e.id ? 'star' : 'plus') + ' ' + (e.id ? 'Change' : 'New') + ' ' + E(t[1].replace(/s$/, '').toLowerCase()) + '</h3>' + nm + body +
      '<div class="row" style="margin-top:12px"><span class="sp"></span><a class="btn225 sm" data-w225lcancel="1">Cancel</a><a class="btn225 sm pri" data-w225lsave="1"' + (S.busy.lib ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('check') + ' Save</a></div></div>';
  }
  function libraryHtml() {
    var t = libTab(), mk = S.mk, qq = lower(S.f.lq);
    var tabs = '<div class="row" style="margin:0 0 12px"><span class="seg">' + LIBT.map(function (x) {

    }).join('') + '</span><span class="sp"></span><input type="search" data-w225lq placeholder="Search the library" value="' + E(S.f.lq || '') + '">' + (mk ? '<a class="btn225 sm pri" data-w225lnew="' + t[0] + '">' + I('plus') + ' New</a>' : '') + '</div>';
    var intro = { templates: 'Start a post from a template: the platforms, caption, first comment and tags come with it. Instagram counts 5 at most since December 2025, so keep the groups short.', replies: 'Answers the team sends often, ready in the Comments inbox.', tags: 'Tags sort posts by theme or campaign. Filter the calendar and the report by them.' }[t[0]];
    var items;
    if (t[0] === 'partners') return tabs + partnersHtml();   /* wave241 */
    if (t[0] === 'tags') {
      var used = {}; posts().forEach(function (p) { (p.tags || []).forEach(function (g) { used[g] = (used[g] || 0) + 1; }); });

'<div class="row"><a class="btn225 sm" data-w225lcal="' + E(g.name) + '">' + I('cal') + ' See the posts</a><span class="sp"></span><a class="btn225 sm" data-w225ledit="tag|' + g.id + '">Change</a><a class="btn225 sm bad" data-w225ldel="tag|' + g.id + '">' + I('trash') + '</a></div>' : '<div class="row"><a class="btn225 sm" data-w225lcal="' + E(g.name) + '">' + I('cal') + ' See the posts</a></div>') + '</div>';
      });
    } else {
      items = lib().filter(function (s) { return s.kind === t[2] && (!qq || (lower(s.name) + ' ' + lower(s.body)).indexOf(qq) >= 0); }).sort(function (a, b) { return Number(b.uses || 0) - Number(a.uses || 0) || String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); }).map(function (s) {
        var d = s.data || {}, txt = t[0] === 'templates' ? (d.caption || s.body || '') : s.body;
        var meta = t[0] === 'templates' ? (d.platforms || []).map(function (k) { return mark(k); }).join('') + ((d.tags || []).length ? ' <span class="muted">' + E(d.tags.join(', ')) + '</span>' : '') : t[0] === 'hashtags' ? '<span class="muted">' + hashCount(s.body) + ' hashtags</span>' : '';
        var use = t[0] === 'templates' ? '<a class="btn225 sm pri" data-w225luse="' + s.id + '">' + I('plus') + ' Use</a>' : '<a class="btn225 sm" data-w225lcopy="' + s.id + '">' + I('copy') + ' Copy</a>';
        return '<div class="it"><div class="row"><b style="flex:1;min-width:0">' + E(s.name) + '</b>' + (s.uses ? '<span class="muted">used ' + s.uses + '\u00d7</span>' : '') + '</div>' + (meta ? '<div>' + meta + '</div>' : '') + '<p>' + E(txt) + '</p>' +
          '<div class="row" style="margin-top:auto">' + use + '<span class="sp"></span>' + (mk ? '<a class="btn225 sm" data-w225ledit="' + t[2] + '|' + s.id + '">Change</a><a class="btn225 sm bad" data-w225ldel="' + t[2] + '|' + s.id + '" title="Remove">' + I('trash') + '</a>' : '') + '</div>' +
          '<small class="muted">' + E(s.updated_by || s.created_by || '') + (s.updated_at ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(ago(s.updated_at)) : '') + '</small></div>';
      });
    }
    return tabs + '<p class="muted" style="margin:-4px 0 12px">' + E(intro) + '</p>' + libEditor(t) +
      (items.length ? '<div class="lib">' + items.join('') + '</div>' : '<div class="card"><div class="empty">' + (qq ? 'Nothing matches.' : 'Nothing here yet.' + (mk ? ' Press New to add the first one.' : '')) + '</div></div>');
  }
  function libNew(tab) {
    var t = LIBT.filter(function (x) { return x[0] === tab; })[0] || LIBT[0];
    S.libEdit = { tab: t[0], id: null, name: '', body: t[0] === 'hashtags' ? '#WelfareAvenue ' : '', color: TAGCOLORS[tagsAll().length % TAGCOLORS.length], platforms: t[0] === 'templates' ? ['instagram', 'facebook'] : [], first_comment: '' };
  }
  function libEdit(kind, id) {
    if (kind === 'tag') { var g = tagsAll().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0]; if (!g) return; S.libEdit = { tab: 'tags', id: g.id, name: g.name, color: g.color || PURPLE, oldName: g.name }; return; }
    var s = lib().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0]; if (!s) return;
    var t = LIBT.filter(function (x) { return x[2] === s.kind; })[0] || LIBT[0], d = s.data || {};
    S.libEdit = { tab: t[0], id: s.id, name: s.name, body: t[0] === 'templates' ? (d.caption || s.body || '') : s.body, platforms: (d.platforms || []).slice(), first_comment: d.first_comment || '', data: d };
  }
  async function libSave() {
    var e = S.libEdit; if (!e) return;
    var name = String(e.name || '').trim(); if (!name) { toastS('Give it a name'); return; }
    S.busy.lib = true; paint();
    try {
      var r;
      if (e.tab === 'tags') {
        if (tagsAll().some(function (g) { return lower(g.name) === lower(name) && String(g.id) !== String(e.id); })) throw new Error('That tag already exists');
        r = e.id ? await db.from('content_tags').update({ name: name, color: e.color }).eq('id', e.id) : await db.from('content_tags').insert({ name: name, color: e.color, created_by: myName() || myEmail() });
        if (r.error) throw r.error;
        /* a renamed tag follows onto the posts that carry it (the ones this person may change) */
        if (e.id && e.oldName && e.oldName !== name) {
          var hit = posts().filter(function (p) { return (p.tags || []).indexOf(e.oldName) >= 0; });
          for (var i = 0; i < hit.length; i++) { await q(db.from('content_posts').update({ tags: hit[i].tags.map(function (x) { return x === e.oldName ? name : x; }) }).eq('id', hit[i].id)); }
        }
      } else {
        var kind = { templates: 'template', hashtags: 'hashtags', replies: 'reply' }[e.tab];
        var row = { name: name, body: String(e.body || ''), updated_by: myName() || myEmail(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() };
        if (kind === 'template') row.data = Object.assign({}, e.data || {}, { platforms: (e.platforms || []).slice(), caption: String(e.body || ''), first_comment: String(e.first_comment || '') });
        r = e.id ? await db.from('content_snippets').update(row).eq('id', e.id) : await db.from('content_snippets').insert(Object.assign({ kind: kind, created_by: myName() || myEmail() }, row));
        if (r.error) throw r.error;
      }
      S.libEdit = null; var w = W(); if (w) { await w.studio(); if (e.tab === 'tags') await w.load(true); }
      toastS('Saved');
    } catch (x) { toastS('Could not save: ' + (x.message || x)); }
    S.busy.lib = false; paint();
  }
  async function libRemove(kind, id) {
    var isTag = kind === 'tag', item = isTag ? tagsAll().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0] : lib().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0];
    if (!item) return;
    if (!confirm('Remove "' + item.name + '" from the library?' + (isTag ? ' Posts keep the tag name, without its colour.' : ''))) return;
    try { var r = await db.from(isTag ? 'content_tags' : 'content_snippets').delete().eq('id', id); if (r.error) throw r.error; var w = W(); if (w) await w.studio(); toastS('Removed'); }
    catch (x) { toastS('Could not remove it: ' + (x.message || x)); }
    paint();
  }
  function libUse(id) {
    var s = lib().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0]; if (!s) return;
    var d = s.data || {}, w = W();
    var pre = { platforms: (d.platforms && d.platforms.length ? d.platforms : ['instagram', 'facebook']).slice(), caption: d.caption != null ? d.caption : s.body, first_comment: d.first_comment || '', tags: (d.tags || []).slice(), title: d.title || '' };
    if (d.variants) pre.variants = d.variants;
    if (d.campaign) pre.campaign = d.campaign;
    q(db.from('content_snippets').update({ uses: Number(s.uses || 0) + 1 }).eq('id', s.id));
    if (w && w.compose) w.compose(pre); else if (window.w121Compose) window.w121Compose(pre);
  }
  function libCopy(id) {
    var s = lib().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0]; if (!s) return;
    copyText(s.body || ''); q(db.from('content_snippets').update({ uses: Number(s.uses || 0) + 1 }).eq('id', s.id));
  }

  /* ---------- short links that count clicks (the link pages live in l_pages.js since wave230) ---------- */
  function shortUrl(code) { return HUB + '/l/' + code; }
  function randCode(n) { var a = 'abcdefghjkmnpqrstuvwxyz23456789', s = ''; for (var i = 0; i < (n || 6); i++) s += a.charAt(Math.floor(Math.random() * a.length)); return s; }
  function slug(s) { return lower(s).normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '').slice(0, 30); }
  function clicksBy(linkId) { return S.clicks.filter(function (c) { return String(c.link_id) === String(linkId); }); }
  function sparkHtml(list, days) {
    days = days || 30; var t = today(), b = [], mx = 1;
    for (var i = days - 1; i >= 0; i--) { var d = addDays(t, -i), n = list.filter(function (c) { return dayOf(c.at) === d; }).length; b.push([d, n]); if (n > mx) mx = n; }
    return '<div class="spark" role="img" aria-label="Clicks a day, last ' + days + ' days">' + b.map(function (x) { return '<i style="height:' + Math.max(2, Math.round(x[1] / mx * 100)) + '%" title="' + E(niceDate(x[0])) + ': ' + x[1] + '"></i>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function topOf(list, key, n) { var m = {}; list.forEach(function (c) { var k = c[key] || 'unknown'; m[k] = (m[k] || 0) + 1; }); return Object.keys(m).map(function (k) { return [k, m[k]]; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b[1] - a[1]; }).slice(0, n || 5); }
  function linksTabs() { return '<div class="row" style="margin:0 0 12px"><span class="muted">Short links for posts, emails and print. The link in our bio and its insights live in <a data-w225go="#studio/pages" style="cursor:pointer;font-weight:700;color:' + ROSE + '">Link pages</a>.</span></div>'; }
  function linksHtml() {
    if (S.sub === 'bio') { setTimeout(function () { go('#studio/pages'); }, 0); return ''; }
    var all = S.links.filter(function (l) { return !/^(bio|lp)-/.test(l.code); }), qq = lower(S.f.kq);
    var list = all.filter(function (l) { return (S.f.kall || l.active) && (!qq || (lower(l.code) + ' ' + lower(l.label) + ' ' + lower(l.url) + ' ' + lower(l.campaign)).indexOf(qq) >= 0); });
    var c30 = S.clicks, c7 = c30.filter(function (c) { return Date.now() - new Date(c.at).getTime() < 7 * 86400000; });
    var bio = S.links.filter(function (l) { return /^(bio|lp)-/.test(l.code); }).map(function (l) { return String(l.id); });
    var bioClicks = c30.filter(function (c) { return bio.indexOf(String(c.link_id)) >= 0; }).length;
    var kpis = '<div class="kpis"><div class="kpi"><b>' + num(c30.length) + '</b><span>clicks, 30 days</span><em>' + num(c7.length) + ' in the last 7</em></div><div class="kpi"><b>' + all.filter(function (l) { return l.active; }).length + '</b><span>live short links</span></div><div class="kpi" data-w225go="#studio/pages"><b>' + num(bioClicks) + '</b><span>link page clicks, 30 days</span></div>' +
      '<div class="kpi"><b style="font-size:17px">' + E((topOf(c30, 'device', 1)[0] || ['\u2013'])[0]) + '</b><span>most clicks from</span><em>' + E(topOf(c30, 'referrer_host', 2).map(function (x) { return x[0] + ' ' + x[1]; }).join(' \u00b7 ')) + '</em></div></div>';
    var mk = S.mk;
    var form = '<div class="card"><h3>' + I('plus') + ' Make a short link <small>anyone on staff</small></h3><div class="row" style="gap:8px"><input type="url" id="w225kurl" placeholder="https://welfareavenue.com/\u2026" style="flex:2;min-width:220px"><input type="text" id="w225klabel" placeholder="What it is (for us)" style="flex:1;min-width:150px"><input type="text" id="w225kcode" placeholder="Ending (optional)" style="width:150px" maxlength="40"><input type="text" id="w225kcamp" placeholder="Campaign (optional)" style="width:160px"><a class="btn225 sm pri" data-w225kmake="1"' + (S.busy.link ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('link') + ' Make it</a></div><p class="muted" style="margin:8px 0 0">The short link is ' + E(HUB) + '/l/<i>ending</i>. Posts make their own when Tracked links is on in the composer, with the UTM tags added.</p></div>';
    var rows = list.map(function (l) {
      var cl = clicksBy(l.id), p = l.post_id ? posts().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(l.post_id); })[0] : null;
      return '<div class="card" style="padding:12px 14px;' + (l.active ? '' : 'opacity:.6') + '"><div class="row" style="gap:10px;align-items:flex-start"><div style="flex:1;min-width:0"><b>' + E(l.label || l.code) + '</b>' + (l.platform ? ' ' + mark(l.platform) : '') + (l.campaign ? ' <span class="pill">' + E(l.campaign) + '</span>' : '') + (l.active ? '' : ' <span class="pill bad">off</span>') +
        '<div style="font-size:12.5px;margin-top:3px"><a href="' + E(shortUrl(l.code)) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="color:' + ROSE + ';font-weight:700">' + E(shortUrl(l.code).replace(/^https:\/\//, '')) + '</a> <span class="muted">\u2192 ' + E(clip(l.url, 90)) + '</span></div>' +
        (p ? '<div class="muted" style="font-size:12px;margin-top:2px;cursor:pointer" data-w225open="' + p.id + '">From the post: ' + E(clip(titleOf(p), 60)) + '</div>' : '') + '</div>' +
        '<div style="width:150px">' + sparkHtml(cl, 30) + '<div class="muted" style="font-size:11.5px;text-align:right">' + num(l.clicks || 0) + ' in all \u00b7 ' + cl.length + ' in 30 days</div></div>' +
        '<div class="row" style="gap:4px"><a class="btn225 sm" data-w225kcopy="' + E(l.code) + '" title="Copy">' + I('copy') + '</a>' + (mk ? '<a class="btn225 sm" data-w225kact="' + (l.active ? 'off' : 'on') + '|' + l.id + '">' + (l.active ? 'Switch off' : 'Switch on') + '</a>' : '') + '</div></div></div>';
    }).join('');
    return linksTabs() + kpis + '<div class="card"><h3>' + I('bolt') + ' Clicks a day <small>every short link, 30 days</small></h3>' + sparkHtml(c30, 30) + '</div>' + form +
      '<div class="row" style="margin:0 0 10px"><input type="search" data-w225kq placeholder="Search the links" value="' + E(S.f.kq || '') + '"><label class="muted" style="display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center"><input type="checkbox" data-w225kall' + (S.f.kall ? ' checked' : '') + '> Show the switched-off ones</label></div>' +
      (rows || '<div class="card"><div class="empty">' + (all.length ? 'Nothing matches.' : 'No short links yet.') + '</div></div>');
  }
  async function makeLink(url, label, code, campaign, extra) {
    url = String(url || '').trim(); if (!/^https?:\/\/\S+\.\S+/.test(url)) throw new Error('Paste a full address that starts with https://');
    code = String(code || '').trim(); if (code && !/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{3,40}$/.test(code)) throw new Error('The ending takes letters, numbers, - and _ (3 to 40)');
    if (code && /^(bio|lp)-/.test(code) && !(extra && extra.bio)) throw new Error('Endings that start with lp- are for the link pages');
    var tries = code ? 1 : 4;
    for (var i = 0; i < tries; i++) {
      var c = code || randCode(6);
      var r = await db.from('short_links').insert(Object.assign({ code: c, url: url, label: label || null, campaign: campaign || null, created_by: myName() || myEmail() }, extra && extra.row || {})).select('*').maybeSingle();
      if (!r.error) { S.links.unshift(r.data); return r.data; }
      if (String(r.error.code) !== '23505') throw r.error;
      if (code) throw new Error('That ending is taken: pick another');
    }
    throw new Error('Could not find a free ending, try again');
  }
  async function makeLinkFromForm() {
    var g = function (id) { var el = document.getElementById(id); return el ? el.value : ''; };
    S.busy.link = true;
    try { var l = await makeLink(g('w225kurl'), g('w225klabel').trim(), g('w225kcode'), g('w225kcamp').trim()); ['w225kurl', 'w225klabel', 'w225kcode', 'w225kcamp'].forEach(function (id) { var el = document.getElementById(id); if (el) el.value = ''; }); copyText(shortUrl(l.code)); toastS('Made and copied: ' + shortUrl(l.code).replace(/^https:\/\//, '')); }
    catch (x) { toastS(x.message || String(x)); }
    S.busy.link = false; paint();
  }
  async function linkActive(id, on) {
    try { var r = await db.from('short_links').update({ active: on }).eq('id', id); if (r.error) throw r.error; S.links.forEach(function (l) { if (String(l.id) === String(id)) l.active = on; }); toastS(on ? 'Switched on' : 'Switched off: the link now goes to the website'); }
    catch (x) { toastS('Could not change it: ' + (x.message || x)); }
    paint();
  }

  function connOf(k) { return (S.conn && S.conn.connections && S.conn.connections[k]) || {}; }
  function chips(list, cls) { return (list || []).map(function (x) { return '<span class="' + (cls || '') + '">' + E(x) + '</span>'; }).join(''); }
  function statusLine(k) {
    var m = modeOf(k), mm = MODE[m] || MODE.reminder, c = connOf(k);
    return '<small style="color:' + mm[1] + ';font-weight:700">' + E(mm[0]) + '</small>' + (c.name || c.handle ? ' <small>\u00b7 ' + E(c.handle ? '@' + c.handle : c.name) + '</small>' : '') + (c.connected_at ? ' <small>\u00b7 since ' + E(niceDate(dayOf(c.connected_at), { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' })) + '</small>' : '');
  }
  function hdr(k, sub, name) { var x = PLX[k] || ['Make.com', 'M', '#6D00CC']; return '<div class="hd"><div class="lg" style="background:' + x[2] + '">' + E(x[1]) + '</div><div><b>' + E(name || x[0]) + '</b>' + (sub || statusLine(k)) + '</div></div>'; }
  function metaCard() {
    var cx = cap() || {}, c = connOf('meta'), can = S.conn && S.conn.can_meta, granted = cx.granted || c.granted || [], missing = cx.missing || c.missing || [];
    var html = '<div class="card">' + hdr('instagram', '<small>Instagram and Facebook, through the Meta system user token</small>', 'Meta') +
      '<div class="row" style="gap:6px">' + ['instagram', 'facebook'].map(function (k) { var mm = MODE[modeOf(k)] || MODE.reminder; return mark(k) + '<small style="color:' + mm[1] + ';font-weight:700;margin-right:8px">' + E(mm[0]) + '</small>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
      (cx.token === false ? '<p style="color:' + FLAG + ';margin:0">No Meta token in the vault yet.</p>' : '') +
      (granted.length ? '<div class="chips">' + chips(granted) + chips(missing, 'miss') + '</div>' : '') +
      (missing.length ? '<p class="muted" style="margin:0;color:' + FLAG + '">Missing: ' + E(missing.join(', ')) + '. Comments' + (missing.some(function (x) { return /publish|manage_posts/.test(x); }) ? ', posting' : '') + ' and some formats wait for a token with these.   }

  function makeCard() {
    var c = connOf('make'), ch = S.conn && S.conn.can_change, li = modeOf('linkedin');
    var html = '<div class="card">' + hdr('make', '<small>Posts to the LinkedIn Page until LinkedIn approves the direct connection' + (li === 'make' ? ' \u00b7 <b style="color:' + TEAL + '">in use</b>' : '') + '</small>') +
      '<ol><li>In Make, a scenario starts with Webhooks, Custom webhook. Copy its address and save it below.</li><li>Press Send Make a sample, then in Make press Redetermine data structure (or run once) so it learns the fields.</li><li>Add a filter: secret equals the code below. Then LinkedIn, Create a company text post (or image post when image_url is filled), with text as the content.</li><li>Turn the scenario on. Send a test post if you want to see it on the Page.</li></ol>';
    if (ch) {
      html += '<div class="row" style="gap:6px"><input type="url" id="w225mkhook" autocomplete="off" placeholder="' + (c.saved ? 'Saved. Paste a new address to replace it' : 'https://hook.us2.make.com/\u2026') + '" style="flex:1;min-width:200px"><a class="btn225 sm" data-w225cx="make_hook">Save</a></div>' +
        (c.secret ? '<div style="font-size:12px">The filter code <code>' + E(c.secret) + '</code> <a data-w225ccopy="' + E(c.secret) + '" style="cursor:pointer">' + I('copy') + '</a></div>' : '') +
        '<div class="row" style="gap:6px">' + (c.saved ? '<a class="btn225 sm" data-w225cx="make_sample">Send Make a sample</a><a class="btn225 sm" data-w225cx="make_test">' + I('send') + ' Send a test post</a><span class="sp"></span><a class="btn225 sm bad" data-w225cx="disconnect|make">Forget it</a>' : '') + '</div>' +
        (c.sampled_at || c.tested_at ? '<p class="muted" style="margin:0">' + (c.sampled_at ? 'Sample sent ' + E(ago(c.sampled_at)) + '. ' : '') + (c.tested_at ? 'Test post ' + E(ago(c.tested_at)) + (c.tested_ok ? ', Make took it.' : ', Make refused it.') : '') + '</p>' : '');
    } else html += '<p class="muted" style="margin:0">' + (c.saved ? 'Set up.' : 'Not set up yet.') + ' A marketing lead or an admin changes it.</p>';
    return html + '</div>';
  }
  function remindCard() {
    var man = PORDER.filter(function (k) { return modeOf(k) === 'reminder'; });
    return '<div class="card">' + '<div class="hd"><div class="lg" style="background:' + GOLD + ';color:' + GREEN + '">' + I('clock') + '</div><div><b>Reminders</b><small>For every platform the Hub cannot post to yet</small></div></div>' +
      '<p style="margin:0;font-size:12.5px">At the post\u2019s time, its owner gets a Hub notification and a Slack message with the caption and the media. They post it in the app, then press Mark posted in the composer, so the calendar and the goals stay right.</p>' +
      '<p class="muted" style="margin:0">' + (man.length ? 'By reminder now: ' + E(man.map(function (k) { return PLX[k][0]; }).join(', ')) + '.' : 'Every platform posts on its own.') + ' People turn these off in their notification settings (Content Studio).</p>' +
      '<div class="row"><span class="sp"></span><a class="btn225 sm" data-w225cx="remind_preview">' + I('eye') + ' Preview a reminder</a></div>' +
      (S.keys.remind ? '<div style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:12px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.05);border-radius:10px;padding:10px">' + E(S.keys.remind) + '</div>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function connectHtml() {
    if (!S.conn) { loadConn().then(paint); return '<div class="card"><div class="empty">Checking the connections\u2026</div></div>'; }
    if (S.conn.error) return '<div class="card"><div class="empty">Could not read the connections: ' + E(S.conn.error) + ' <a data-w225cx="reload" style="cursor:pointer;font-weight:700">Try again</a></div></div>';
    var flash = S.keys.flash ? '<div class="card" style="background:' + (S.keys.flash.ok ? 'rgba(42,140,122,.10)' : 'rgba(179,64,47,.08)') + '">' + E(S.keys.flash.text) + '</div>' : '';
    return flash + '<div class="row" style="margin:0 0 12px"><span class="muted">Every platform posts straight from the Hub once it is connected. Until then, the Hub reminds the post\u2019s owner to post it by hand. Keys and tokens go into the vault and are never shown again.</span><span class="sp"></span><a class="btn225 sm" data-w225cx="reload">' + I('refresh') + ' Check again</a></div>' +
      '<div class="cx">' + metaCard() + connectExtra250() + appCard('threads') + appCard('youtube') + appCard('tiktok') + appCard('linkedin') + makeCard() + remindCard() + '</div>' +
      '<p class="muted" style="margin:14px 0 0">The setup doc (09/10/26) has every form, answer and email for these steps.</p>';
  }
  /* the platforms send people back to #studio/connections?connected=x or ?error= */
  function connFlash() {
    var h = location.hash || '', i = h.indexOf('?'); if (i < 0) return;
    var p = new URLSearchParams(h.slice(i + 1)), ok = p.get('connected'), er = p.get('error');
    if (ok) S.keys.flash = { ok: true, text: (PLX[ok] ? PLX[ok][0] : ok) + ' is connected. New posts go there directly.' };
    else if (er) S.keys.flash = { ok: false, text: er };
    S.conn = null;
    try { history.replaceState(null, '', location.pathname + location.search + h.slice(0, i)); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  async function connAct(what) {
    if (window.w277 && window.w277.connAct) return window.w277.connAct(what);   /* wave277 */
    var parts = String(what).split('|'), a = parts[0], k = parts[1];
    var val = function (id) { var el = document.getElementById(id); return el ? el.value.trim() : ''; };
    var clear = function (id) { var el = document.getElementById(id); if (el) el.value = ''; };
    try {
      if (a === 'reload') { S.conn = null; S.keys.flash = null; var w = W(); if (w) await w.loadCap(); paint(); return; }
      if (a === 'meta_check' || a === 'meta_save') {
        var tok = val('w225metatok'); if (!tok) { toastS('Paste the token first'); return; }
        S.busy.meta = true; paint();
        var r = await fn('social-connect', { action: 'meta_token', token: tok, check_only: a === 'meta_check' });
        S.keys.metaCheck = { granted: r.granted || [], missing: r.missing || [] };
        if (a === 'meta_save') { clear('w225metatok'); toastS('Replaced. ' + (r.missing && r.missing.length ? r.missing.length + ' permissions still missing' : 'Every permission is there')); S.conn = null; var w2 = W(); if (w2) await w2.loadCap(); }
        else toastS(r.missing && r.missing.length ? 'Missing ' + r.missing.length + ': ' + r.missing.join(', ') : 'Every permission is there');
        S.busy.meta = false; paint(); return;
      }
      if (a === 'save_app') {
        var id = val('w225cid-' + k), sec = val('w225csec-' + k); if (!id || !sec) { toastS('Paste both the ID and the secret'); return; }
        await fn('social-connect', { action: 'save_app', platform: k, id: id, secret: sec }); clear('w225cid-' + k); clear('w225csec-' + k);
        toastS('Saved in the vault'); await loadConn(); paint(); return;
      }
      if (a === 'start') { var s = await fn('social-connect', { action: 'start', platform: k }); if (s && s.url) location.href = s.url; return; }
      if (a === 'disconnect') {
        if (!confirm('Disconnect ' + (PLX[k] ? PLX[k][0] : 'Make') + '? Posts go back to reminders until it is connected again.')) return;
        await fn('social-connect', { action: 'disconnect', platform: k }); toastS('Disconnected'); await loadConn(); var w3 = W(); if (w3) await w3.loadCap(); paint(); return;
      }
      if (a === 'make_hook') { var u = val('w225mkhook'); if (!u) { toastS('Paste the webhook address'); return; } await fn('social-connect', { action: 'make_hook', url: u }); clear('w225mkhook'); toastS('Saved. Now send Make a sample'); await loadConn(); var w4 = W(); if (w4) await w4.loadCap(); paint(); return; }
      if (a === 'make_sample') { var m = await fn('social-connect', { action: 'make_sample' }); toastS(m.ok ? 'Make got the sample' : 'Make answered ' + m.status + ': is the scenario listening?'); await loadConn(); paint(); return; }
      if (a === 'make_test') {
        if (!confirm('This puts a real post on the Welfare Avenue LinkedIn Page: "Testing the connection between the Welfare Avenue Hub and our LinkedIn Page. Please ignore this post." Send it?')) return;
        var t = await fn('social-connect', { action: 'make_test', confirm: true }); toastS(t.ok ? 'Sent: check the LinkedIn Page in a minute' : 'Make answered ' + t.status); await loadConn(); paint(); return;
      }
      if (a === 'test_short') {
        var w5 = W(); var pre = { title: 'Test Short from the Hub', platforms: ['youtube'], caption: 'Testing the connection between the Welfare Avenue Hub and YouTube.', variants: { youtube: { title: 'Test from the Hub', privacy: 'unlisted', kids: false } } };
        toastS('Add a short vertical video, then Publish now. It goes up unlisted.');
        if (w5 && w5.compose) w5.compose(pre); return;
      }
      if (a === 'remind_preview') {
        var cand = posts().filter(function (p) { return ['draft', 'scheduled'].indexOf(p.status) >= 0 && (p.platforms || []).some(function (x) { return modeOf(x) === 'reminder'; }); }).sort(function (x, y) { return String(x.scheduled_at || '9').localeCompare(String(y.scheduled_at || '9')); })[0];
        if (!cand) { S.keys.remind = 'No planned post goes out by reminder. Make one for LinkedIn, TikTok or YouTube to see the message.'; paint(); return; }
        var pl = (cand.platforms || []).filter(function (x) { return modeOf(x) === 'reminder'; })[0];
        var rr = await fn('social-publish', { action: 'remind_test', post_id: cand.id, platform: pl });
        var plan = rr.plan || {}; S.keys.remind = 'To: ' + (plan.to || 'nobody (the post has no owner)') + '\n\n' + String(plan.slack || '').replace(/<([^|>]+)\|([^>]+)>/g, '$2').replace(/\*/g, ''); paint(); return;
      }
    } catch (x) { S.busy.meta = false; toastS((x.message || String(x))); paint(); }
  }

  /* ---------- the monthly report (printable) ---------- */
  function repMonth() { var m = /^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(S.sub) ? S.sub : (S.reportMonth || today().slice(0, 7)); S.reportMonth = m; return m; }
  function monthAdd(m, n) { var d = new Date(m + '-15T12:00:00'); d.setMonth(d.getMonth() + n); return ymd(d).slice(0, 7); }
  function monthName(m) { try { return new Date(m + '-15T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return m; } }
  function engOf(sp) { return Number(sp.engagement || (Number(sp.likes || 0) + Number(sp.comments || 0) + Number(sp.shares || 0) + Number(sp.saves || 0))); }
  function monthStats(m) {
    var inM = function (iso) { return iso && dayOf(iso).slice(0, 7) === m; };
    var out = posts().filter(function (p) { return inM(p.published_at || p.scheduled_at) && (['published', 'posted'].indexOf(p.status) >= 0 || Object.keys(p.results || {}).some(function (k) { return p.results[k] && (p.results[k].id || p.results[k].posted_at); })); });
    var sp = S.social.filter(function (x) { return inM(x.published_at) && PORDER.indexOf(x.platform) >= 0; });
    var per = {}; PORDER.forEach(function (k) { per[k] = { posts: 0, reach: 0, eng: 0, likes: 0, comments: 0, shares: 0, n: 0 }; });
    sp.forEach(function (x) { var r = per[x.platform]; r.n++; r.reach += Number(x.reach || x.views || 0); r.eng += engOf(x); r.likes += Number(x.likes || 0); r.comments += Number(x.comments || 0); r.shares += Number(x.shares || 0); });
    out.forEach(function (p) { (p.platforms || []).forEach(function (k) { if (per[k]) per[k].posts++; }); });
    var reach = 0, eng = 0; PORDER.forEach(function (k) { reach += per[k].reach; eng += per[k].eng; });
    var tags = {}; out.forEach(function (p) { (p.tags || []).forEach(function (t) { tags[t] = (tags[t] || 0) + 1; }); });
    var cms = S.comments.filter(function (c) { return !c.from_us && inM(c.commented_at); });
    var answered = cms.filter(function (c) { return c.status === 'resolved' || S.comments.some(function (k) { return k.from_us && k.platform === c.platform && k.parent_external_id === c.external_id; }); }).length;
    return { posts: out, social: sp, per: per, reach: reach, eng: eng, rate: reach ? eng / reach : NaN, tags: tags, comments: cms.length, answered: answered, top: sp.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return engOf(b) - engOf(a); }).slice(0, 5) };
  }
  function delta(a, b) { if (!isFinite(a) || !isFinite(b) || !b) return ''; var d = (a - b) / b; return '<em class="' + (d >= 0 ? 'ok' : 'bad') + '" style="font-style:normal;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:700;color:' + (d >= 0 ? TEAL : FLAG) + '">' + (d >= 0 ? '\u25b2 ' : '\u25bc ') + Math.abs(Math.round(d * 100)) + '% on ' + E(monthName(monthAdd(S.reportMonth, -1)).split(' ')[0]) + '</em>'; }
  function weeksIn(m) { var out = [], d = mondayOf(m + '-01'); while (d.slice(0, 7) <= m) { if (addDays(d, 6).slice(0, 7) >= m && addDays(d, 6) < today()) out.push(d); d = addDays(d, 7); } return out; }
  function goalWeeks(m) {
    var g = goals(), any = PORDER.some(function (k) { return Number(g[k] || 0) > 0; }); if (!any) return null;
    var wk = weeksIn(m), met = 0;
    wk.forEach(function (w) { var off = Math.round((new Date(w + 'T12:00:00') - new Date(mondayOf(today()) + 'T12:00:00')) / (7 * 86400000)); var gs = goalStatus(off), ok = true; PORDER.forEach(function (k) { if (gs[k].goal > 0 && gs[k].n < gs[k].goal) ok = false; }); if (ok) met++; });
    return { met: met, of: wk.length };
  }
  function reportClicks(m) {
    var key = 'clicks-' + m; if (S.keys[key]) return S.keys[key];
    S.keys[key] = { loading: true, n: null };
    var a = m + '-01T00:00:00', b = monthAdd(m, 1) + '-01T00:00:00';
    q(db.from('short_link_clicks').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).gte('at', new Date(a).toISOString()).lt('at', new Date(b).toISOString())).then(function (r) { S.keys[key] = { n: r.count == null ? null : r.count }; if (S.sec === 'report') paint(); });
    return S.keys[key];
  }
  function reportHtml() {
    var m = repMonth(), st = monthStats(m), prev = monthStats(monthAdd(m, -1)), gw = goalWeeks(m), cl = reportClicks(m), cur = today().slice(0, 7);
    var note = S.keys['note-' + m] || '';
    var bar = '<div class="row noprint" style="margin:0 0 12px"><a class="btn225 sm" data-w225go="#studio/report/' + monthAdd(m, -1) + '">\u2039 ' + E(monthName(monthAdd(m, -1))) + '</a>' + (m < cur ? '<a class="btn225 sm" data-w225go="#studio/report/' + monthAdd(m, 1) + '">' + E(monthName(monthAdd(m, 1))) + ' \u203a</a>' : '') + '<span class="sp"></span><a class="btn225 sm" data-w225rcopy="1">' + I('copy') + ' Copy a summary</a><a class="btn225 sm pri" data-w225print="1">' + I('print') + ' Print or save as PDF</a></div>';
    var k = function (v, l, d) { return '<div class="kpi"><b>' + v + '</b><span>' + E(l) + '</span>' + (d || '') + '</div>'; };
    var kpis = '<div class="kpis">' + k(st.posts.length, 'posts out', delta(st.posts.length, prev.posts.length)) + k(st.reach ? num(st.reach) : '\u2013', 'reach and views', delta(st.reach, prev.reach)) + k(st.eng ? num(st.eng) : '\u2013', 'likes, comments, shares, saves', delta(st.eng, prev.eng)) + k(pct(st.rate), 'engagement over reach') +
      k(cl.n == null ? '\u2013' : num(cl.n), 'link clicks') + k(st.comments ? Math.round(st.answered / st.comments * 100) + '%' : '\u2013', 'comments answered', '<em>' + st.comments + ' comments</em>') + (gw ? k(gw.met + '/' + gw.of, 'weeks every goal met') : '') + '</div>';
    var rows = PORDER.filter(function (x) { return st.per[x].posts || st.per[x].n; }).map(function (x) { var r = st.per[x]; return '<tr><td>' + mark(x) + E(PLX[x][0]) + '</td><td>' + r.posts + '</td><td>' + (r.reach ? num(r.reach) : '\u2013') + '</td><td>' + num(r.likes) + '</td><td>' + num(r.comments) + '</td><td>' + num(r.shares) + '</td><td>' + (r.reach ? pct(r.eng / r.reach) : '\u2013') + '</td></tr>'; }).join('');
    var table = '<h4>By platform</h4>' + (rows ? '<table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:12.5px" class="rt"><thead><tr style="text-align:left;color:' + MUTED + '"><th>Platform</th><th>Posts</th><th>Reach</th><th>Likes</th><th>Comments</th><th>Shares</th><th>Rate</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + rows + '</tbody></table>' : '<p class="muted">Nothing went out this month.</p>');
    var top = '<h4>The best posts</h4>' + (st.top.length ? st.top.map(function (x, i) { var u = x.thumb_cached || x.thumb_url; return '<div class="pl" style="cursor:default">' + (u ? '<img src="' + E(u) + '" alt="">' : '<div class="ph"></div>') + '<div style="min-width:0"><b><span style="font-family:' + HEAD + ';color:' + GOLD + ';margin-right:6px">' + (i + 1) + '</span>' + E(clip(x.caption || 'A post', 90)) + '</b><small>' + mark(x.platform) + ' ' + E(niceDate(dayOf(x.published_at), { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })) + ' \u00b7 ' + num(x.reach || x.views || 0) + ' reach \u00b7 ' + num(engOf(x)) + ' engagements</small></div></div>'; }).join('') : '<p class="muted">No results yet for this month.</p>');
    var tg = Object.keys(st.tags).sort(function (a, b) { return st.tags[b] - st.tags[a]; });
    var tags = tg.length ? '<h4>Themes</h4><div class="row" style="gap:6px">' + tg.map(function (t) { var g = tagsAll().filter(function (x) { return x.name === t; })[0]; return '<span class="pill"><span class="tagdot" style="background:' + E(g && g.color || PURPLE) + '"></span> ' + E(t) + ' \u00b7 ' + st.tags[t] + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '';
    var notes = '<h4>What we learned</h4><textarea data-w225rnote="' + m + '" rows="3" class="noprint" style="width:100%" placeholder="A line or two for the board or the team (kept until you leave the page)">' + E(note) + '</textarea>' + (note ? '<p class="printonly" style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13px">' + E(note) + '</p>' : '');
    return bar + '<div class="rep"><div class="row"><div><h2>Content report, ' + E(monthName(m)) + '</h2><span class="muted">Welfare Avenue \u00b7 Instagram, Facebook, Threads, LinkedIn, TikTok and YouTube \u00b7 made ' + E(niceDate(today(), { month: 'long', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' })) + '</span></div></div><div class="band"></div>' + kpis + '<div class="grid2"><div>' + top + '</div><div>' + table + tags + '</div></div>' + notes +
      '<p class="muted" style="margin:14px 0 0;font-size:11px">Reach and engagement come from the platforms (Analytics); posts out come from the Content Studio. Link clicks count the hub.welfareavenue.com/l short links. Change against the month before where both have numbers.</p></div>';
  }
  function reportSummary() {
    var m = repMonth(), st = monthStats(m), cl = reportClicks(m);
    var by = PORDER.filter(function (k) { return st.per[k].posts; }).map(function (k) { return PLX[k][0] + ' ' + st.per[k].posts; }).join(', ');
    return 'Content, ' + monthName(m) + ': ' + st.posts.length + ' posts out' + (by ? ' (' + by + ')' : '') + '; ' + num(st.reach) + ' reach and views; ' + num(st.eng) + ' engagements (' + pct(st.rate) + ' of reach)' + (cl.n != null ? '; ' + num(cl.n) + ' link clicks' : '') + (st.comments ? '; ' + st.answered + ' of ' + st.comments + ' comments answered' : '') + '.' + (st.top[0] ? ' Best post: ' + clip(st.top[0].caption || 'a post', 80) + ' on ' + PLX[st.top[0].platform][0] + ' (' + num(engOf(st.top[0])) + ' engagements).' : '') + (S.keys['note-' + m] ? '\n' + S.keys['note-' + m] : '');
  }
  function printReport() {
    document.body.classList.add('w225print');
    var done = function () { document.body.classList.remove('w225print'); window.removeEventListener('afterprint', done); };
    window.addEventListener('afterprint', done);
    setTimeout(function () { try { window.print(); } catch (e) { done(); } }, 60);
  }

  /* =====================================================================
     wave230 link pages (Steel, 09/10/26): "a linktree section of the studio with the ability to track insights on who
     clicking what link and make it similar to linktree pro but much better". His choices: anonymous insights with no
     cookies, a person named only when they sign up or give; several pages; smart links (schedules, featured, A/B tests,
     fallbacks); sign-up, volunteer and donation blocks; content blocks (Instagram, YouTube, events, impact, English and
     French); a tracked address per source and QR codes; a weekly summary (the linkpage function, Mondays).
     #studio/pages (the pages), #studio/pages/edit/<id>, #studio/pages/insights, #studio/pages/sources, #studio/pages/people.
     Data: link_pages, link_page_sources, link_page_events, wa230_conversions(), short_links (each link button), crm_forms.
     ===================================================================== */
