  var PLM = { facebook: ['Messenger', '#0866FF'], instagram: ['Instagram', '#C13584'] };
  function winOf(t) {
    if (!t.last_inbound_at) return { open: false, until: null };
    var until = new Date(new Date(t.last_inbound_at).getTime() + 24 * 3600000);
    return { open: until.getTime() > Date.now(), until: until };
  }
  function thName(t) { return t.participant_handle ? '@' + t.participant_handle : (t.participant_name || 'Someone'); }
  function limitNote250() {
    var r = M250.last && M250.last.result ? M250.last.result : null; if (!r) return '';
    var bits = [];
    if (r.instagram && (r.instagram.limited === 'needs_advanced_access' || /role on app|advanced access/i.test(String(r.instagram.error || '')))) bits.push('Instagram only shows the conversations Meta allows before App Review (instagram_manage_messages, Advanced Access). Until it is approved, answer most Instagram messages in Meta Business Suite.');
    if (r.facebook && r.facebook.error) bits.push('Messenger could not be read: ' + clip(r.facebook.error, 140));
    if (r.instagram && r.instagram.error && !/role on app|advanced access/i.test(String(r.instagram.error))) bits.push('Instagram could not be read: ' + clip(r.instagram.error, 140));
    return bits.length ? '<div class="card" style="background:rgba(220,165,39,.10)">' + I('warn') + ' ' + bits.map(E).join(' ') + ' <a href="https://business.facebook.com/latest/inbox/all" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="font-weight:700">Open Meta Business Suite</a></div>' : '';
  }
  function threadHtml250(t) {
    var msgs = M250.msgs[t.id];
    var mk = S.mk, pub = S.pub, w = winOf(t), r = M250.reply[t.id];
    var replies = lib().filter(function (x) { return x.kind === 'reply'; });
    var conv = !msgs ? '<div class="empty">Loading the conversation\u2026</div>' : !msgs.length ? '<div class="empty">No messages kept for this conversation yet.</div>' :
      '<div class="w250conv">' + msgs.map(function (m) {
        var att = (m.attachments || []).map(function (a) { return a.url ? '<a href="' + E(a.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + E(a.name || ('[' + a.type + ']')) + '</a>' : E(a.name || ('[' + a.type + ']')); }).join(' ');
        return '<div class="w250b' + (m.from_us ? ' us' : '') + '">' + E(m.text || '') + (att ? (m.text ? '<br>' : '') + att : '') + '<small>' + E(niceWhen(m.sent_at)) + (m.from_us && m.sent_by ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(m.sent_by) : '') + '</small></div>';
      }).join('') + '</div>';
    var win = w.open ? '<div class="w250win ok">' + I('clock') + ' You can answer here until ' + E(niceWhen(w.until.toISOString())) + ' (Meta allows 24 hours after their last message).</div>'
      : '<div class="w250win no">' + I('clock') + ' ' + (t.last_inbound_at ? 'More than 24 hours since their last message: Meta only lets a Page answer within 24 hours. ' : '') + 'Answer in <a href="https://business.facebook.com/latest/inbox/all" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="font-weight:700">Meta Business Suite</a>.</div>';
    var acts = mk ? '<div class="row" style="flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;margin-top:8px">' +
      (w.open && !r ? '<a class="btn225 sm pri" data-w250rp="' + t.id + '">' + I('chat') + ' Reply</a>' : '') +
      (t.status === 'open' ? '<a class="btn225 sm" data-w250act="dm_resolve|' + t.id + '">' + I('check') + ' Resolve</a>' : t.status === 'resolved' ? '<a class="btn225 sm" data-w250act="dm_reopen|' + t.id + '">Reopen</a>' : '') +
      '<select data-w250assign="' + t.id + '" aria-label="Assign" style="font-size:11.5px;padding:4px 8px;border-radius:999px"><option value="">' + (t.assigned_to ? 'Assigned: ' + E(t.assigned_to) : 'Assign\u2026') + '</option>' + members().filter(function (m) { return m.department === 'marketing' || m.department === 'exec'; }).map(function (m) { return '<option value="' + E(m.name) + '">' + E(m.name) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<span class="sp"></span>' +
      (pub ? (t.status === 'archived' ? '<a class="btn225 sm" data-w250act="dm_unarchive|' + t.id + '">Bring back</a>' : '<a class="btn225 sm bad" data-w250act="dm_archive|' + t.id + '" title="Meta keeps the conversation; this takes it out of the Hub\u2019s inbox">' + I('trash') + ' Remove</a>') : '') + '</div>' : '';
    var box = r ? '<div class="rbox" style="margin-top:8px"><textarea data-w250rtext="' + t.id + '" placeholder="Reply as Welfare Avenue" style="width:100%;min-height:84px">' + E(r.text || '') + '</textarea><div class="row" style="margin-top:6px">' +
      (replies.length ? '<select data-w250saved="' + t.id + '"><option value="">Saved replies\u2026</option>' + replies.map(function (x) { return '<option value="' + x.id + '">' + E(x.name) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' : '') +
      '<a class="btn225 sm" data-w250ai="' + t.id + '"' + (r.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('star') + (r.busy ? ' Writing\u2026' : ' Suggest a reply') + '</a><span class="sp"></span><a class="btn225 sm" data-w250rpx="' + t.id + '">Cancel</a><a class="btn225 sm pri" data-w250send="' + t.id + '"' + (r.sending ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('send') + (r.sending ? ' Sending\u2026' : ' Send') + '</a></div></div>' : '';
    return '<div class="card" data-w250conv="' + t.id + '"><div class="row" style="margin:0 0 6px"><b style="font-size:15px">' + E(thName(t)) + '</b><span class="muted">' + E((PLM[t.platform] || [t.platform])[0]) + (t.assigned_to ? ' \u00b7 for ' + E(t.assigned_to) : '') + (t.status !== 'open' ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(t.status) : '') + '</span><span class="sp"></span><a class="btn225 sm" data-w250close="1">' + I('x') + ' Close</a></div>' +
      conv + win + acts + box + '</div>';
  }
  function messagesHtml() {
    if (!S.mk && !S.pub) return '<div class="card"><div class="empty">Messages are for the marketing team and admins.</div></div>';
    if (!M250.loaded) { load250().then(paint); return '<div class="card"><div class="empty">Loading the messages\u2026</div></div>'; }
    var all = M250.threads, f = M250.f;
    var c = { open: all.filter(function (t) { return t.status === 'open'; }).length, resolved: all.filter(function (t) { return t.status === 'resolved'; }).length, archived: all.filter(function (t) { return t.status === 'archived'; }).length, all: all.filter(function (t) { return t.status !== 'archived'; }).length };
    var list = all.filter(function (t) {
      if (f === 'open' && t.status !== 'open') return false;
      if (f === 'resolved' && t.status !== 'resolved') return false;
      if (f === 'archived' && t.status !== 'archived') return false;
      if (f === 'all' && t.status === 'archived') return false;
      if (M250.pl && t.platform !== M250.pl) return false;
      if (M250.q && (lower(t.participant_name) + ' ' + lower(t.participant_handle) + ' ' + lower(t.last_snippet)).indexOf(lower(M250.q)) < 0) return false;
      return true;
    });
    var waiting = all.filter(function (t) { return t.status === 'open' && !t.last_from_us; });
    var closing = waiting.filter(function (t) { var w = winOf(t); return w.open && w.until.getTime() - Date.now() < 4 * 3600000; }).length;
    var last = M250.last;
    var head = '<div class="kpis"><div class="kpi"><b class="' + (waiting.length ? 'warn' : 'ok') + '">' + waiting.length + '</b><span>waiting for us</span></div>' +
      '<div class="kpi"><b class="' + (closing ? 'warn' : '') + '">' + closing + '</b><span>window closing</span><em>under 4 hours left to answer here</em></div>' +
      '<div class="kpi"><b>' + all.filter(function (t) { return t.replied_at && Date.now() - new Date(t.replied_at).getTime() < 7 * 86400000; }).length + '</b><span>answered from the Hub</span><em>last 7 days</em></div>' +
      '<div class="kpi"><b style="font-size:18px">' + E(last && last.at ? ago(last.at) : 'never') + '</b><span>last check</span><em><a data-w250sync="1" style="cursor:pointer;color:' + ROSE + ';font-weight:700">' + (M250.busy.sync ? 'Checking\u2026' : 'Check now') + '</a></em></div></div>' + limitNote250();
    var filters = '<div class="row" style="margin:0 0 12px"><span class="seg">' + [['open', 'Open'], ['resolved', 'Answered'], ['all', 'Everything'], ['archived', 'Removed']].map(function (x) { return '<a data-w250f="' + x[0] + '" class="' + (f === x[0] ? 'on' : '') + '">' + x[1] + '<span class="n">' + c[x[0]] + '</span></a>'; }).join('') + '</span>' +
      '<span class="seg"><a data-w250pl="" class="' + (!M250.pl ? 'on' : '') + '">All</a><a data-w250pl="facebook" class="' + (M250.pl === 'facebook' ? 'on' : '') + '">Messenger</a><a data-w250pl="instagram" class="' + (M250.pl === 'instagram' ? 'on' : '') + '">Instagram</a></span>' +
      '<span class="sp"></span><input type="search" data-w250q placeholder="Search messages" value="' + E(M250.q) + '"></div>';
    var rows = list.map(function (t) {
      var nm = thName(t), pm = PLM[t.platform] || ['', MUTED];
      return '<div class="w250th' + (String(M250.sel) === String(t.id) ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w250open="' + t.id + '"><div class="av">' + E(String(nm).replace(/^@/, '').charAt(0).toUpperCase() || '?') + '<i class="w225pm ok" style="background:' + pm[1] + '" title="' + E(pm[0]) + '">' + (t.platform === 'instagram' ? 'IG' : 'm') + '</i></div>' +
        '<div style="min-width:0"><b>' + E(nm) + '</b><div class="sn' + (t.unread && !t.last_from_us ? ' new' : '') + '">' + (t.last_from_us ? 'You: ' : '') + E(clip(t.last_snippet || '', 90)) + '</div></div>' +
        '<div class="tm">' + E(ago(t.last_message_at)) + (t.unread && !t.last_from_us ? '<br><span class="dot">' + t.unread + '</span>' : '') + '</div></div>';
    }).join('');
    var sel = M250.sel ? all.filter(function (t) { return String(t.id) === String(M250.sel); })[0] : null;
    var right = sel ? threadHtml250(sel) : '<div class="card"><div class="empty">' + (list.length ? 'Pick a conversation to read it and answer.' : '') + '</div></div>';
    var empty = all.length ? (f === 'open' ? 'Every message is answered.' : 'Nothing matches.') : 'No direct messages in the Hub yet. They arrive within 10 minutes of being sent, at once when Meta reports them.';
    return head + filters + '<div class="w250dm"><div class="card">' + (rows || '<div class="empty">' + E(empty) + '</div>') + '</div>' + right + '</div>';
  }
  async function dmAct250(action, id, extra) {
    try {
      await fn('social-dms', Object.assign({ action: action, id: Number(id) }, extra || {}));
      toastS({ dm_reply: 'Sent', dm_resolve: 'Marked answered', dm_reopen: 'Reopened', dm_assign: 'Assigned', dm_archive: 'Removed from the inbox', dm_unarchive: 'Back in the inbox', dm_read: 'Read' }[action] || 'Done');
      if (action === 'dm_reply') delete M250.reply[id];
      if (action === 'dm_archive') M250.sel = null;
      await load250(true); if (M250.sel) await loadMsgs250(M250.sel);
    } catch (x) { toastS((x.message || String(x))); if (M250.reply[id]) M250.reply[id].sending = false; }
    paint(); try { if (window.w153) window.w153.paint(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  async function sync250() {
    if (M250.busy.sync) return; M250.busy.sync = true; paint();
    try {
      var r = await fn('social-dms', { action: 'dm_sync', days: 14 });
      var m = (r && r.messages) || {}, added = Number((m.facebook || {}).added || 0) + Number((m.instagram || {}).added || 0);
      toastS(added ? added + ' new message' + (added === 1 ? '' : 's') : 'Checked: nothing new');
      await load250(true); if (M250.sel) await loadMsgs250(M250.sel);
    } catch (x) { toastS('Could not check: ' + (x.message || x)); }
    M250.busy.sync = false; paint(); try { if (window.w153) window.w153.paint(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  var DM_SYSTEM = 'You answer direct messages sent to Welfare Avenue on Messenger or Instagram. Welfare Avenue is a youth-powered charity in Montreal and Toronto that serves meals and builds community. Write one reply as Welfare Avenue: warm, specific, short (two or three sentences), plain words. Use their first name when you have it. Answer only with what the conversation says or with these: volunteering sign-ups at hub.welfareavenue.com/links, questions to info@welfareavenue.com. Never promise anything, never invent facts, dates or numbers. Reply in the language of their last message (Quebec French for French). No em dashes.';
  async function suggest250(id) {
    var t = M250.threads.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0]; if (!t) return;
    var r = M250.reply[id] = M250.reply[id] || { text: '' }; r.busy = true; paint();
    try {
      var convo = (M250.msgs[id] || []).slice(-12).map(function (m) { return (m.from_us ? 'WELFARE AVENUE: ' : 'THEM (' + (t.participant_name || t.participant_handle || 'someone') + '): ') + (m.text || '[attachment]'); }).join('\n');
      var res = await db.functions.invoke('llm', { body: { task: 'draft', caller: 'content-studio', max_tokens: 300, temperature: 0.5, system: DM_SYSTEM, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'PLATFORM: ' + t.platform + '\nCONVERSATION:\n' + clip(convo, 3000) }] } });
      if (res.error) throw res.error;
      var txt = ((res.data && res.data.content) || []).filter(function (x) { return x.type === 'text'; }).map(function (x) { return x.text; }).join(' ').replace(/\u2014/g, ',').trim();
      if (!txt) throw new Error('nothing came back');
      r.text = txt;
    } catch (x) { toastS('The Hub could not help this time: ' + (x.message || x)); }
    r.busy = false; paint();
  }

  /* ---------- Paid and organic ---------- */
  var PER = [['last7', '7 days', 7], ['last30', '30 days', 30], ['last90', '90 days', 90]];
  function money(v, cur) { v = Number(v) || 0; return (cur === 'CAD' || !cur ? '$' : '') + (v >= 1000 ? Math.round(v).toLocaleString('en-CA') : v.toFixed(2).replace(/\.00$/, '')) + (cur && cur !== 'CAD' ? ' ' + cur : ''); }
  var RESW = { link_click: 'link clicks', landing_page_view: 'landing page views', lead: 'leads', 'onsite_conversion.lead_grouped': 'leads', 'offsite_conversion.fb_pixel_lead': 'leads', 'onsite_conversion.messaging_conversation_started_7d': 'conversations', post_engagement: 'engagements', page_engagement: 'engagements', like: 'Page likes', video_view: 'video views', reach: 'people reached', purchase: 'purchases', donate_total: 'donations', none: 'results' };
  function adsHtml() {
    if (!M250.ads) { loadAds250().then(paint); return '<div class="card"><div class="empty">Reading the ad accounts\u2026</div></div>'; }
    var per = PER.filter(function (p) { return p[0] === M250.period; })[0] || PER[1], days = per[2], from = addDays(today(), -(days - 1));
    var accs = (M250.ads.accounts || []), cur = (accs[0] && accs[0].currency) || 'CAD';
    var paid = { spend: 0, reach: 0, impressions: 0, clicks: 0, results: 0 }, camps = [];
    accs.forEach(function (a) { var p = a[per[0]] || {}; if (p.error) return; ['spend', 'reach', 'impressions', 'clicks', 'results'].forEach(function (k) { paid[k] += Number(p[k] || 0); }); (p.campaigns || []).forEach(function (c) { camps.push(Object.assign({ account: a.name || a.id }, c)); }); });
    var org = S.social.filter(function (p) { return p.published_at && dayOf(p.published_at) >= from; });
    var o = { posts: org.length, reach: 0, views: 0, eng: 0 }; org.forEach(function (p) { o.reach += Number(p.reach || 0); o.views += Number(p.views || 0); o.eng += Number(p.engagement || ((p.likes || 0) + (p.comments || 0) + (p.shares || 0) + (p.saves || 0)) || 0); });
    var byDay = {}; M250.daily.forEach(function (r) { if (r.day >= from) byDay[r.day] = (byDay[r.day] || 0) + Number(r.spend || 0); });
    var dd = []; for (var i = days - 1; i >= 0; i--) dd.push(addDays(today(), -i)); var mx = Math.max.apply(null, dd.map(function (d) { return byDay[d] || 0; }).concat([0.01]));
    var bars = '<div class="w250bars" aria-label="Spend by day">' + dd.map(function (d) { var v = byDay[d] || 0; return '<i class="' + (v ? '' : 'z') + '" style="height:' + Math.max(2, Math.round(v / mx * 100)) + '%" title="' + E(niceDate(d)) + ': ' + E(money(v, cur)) + '"></i>'; }).join('') + '</div><div class="row muted" style="font-size:11px"><span>' + E(niceDate(from)) + '</span><span class="sp"></span><span>' + E(niceDate(today())) + '</span></div>';
    var cpr = paid.results ? paid.spend / paid.results : null;
    var head = '<div class="row" style="margin:0 0 12px"><span class="seg">' + PER.map(function (p) { return '<a data-w250per="' + p[0] + '" class="' + (M250.period === p[0] ? 'on' : '') + '">' + p[1] + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</span><span class="sp"></span>' +
      '<span class="muted" style="font-size:12px">' + (M250.ads.at ? 'Read ' + E(ago(M250.ads.at)) : 'Not read yet') + '</span><a class="btn225 sm" data-w250adsync="1"' + (M250.busy.ads ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('refresh') + (M250.busy.ads ? ' Reading\u2026' : ' Refresh') + '</a></div>';
    var orgCard = '<div class="card"><h4>Organic</h4><div class="w250tiles"><div><b>' + num(o.posts) + '</b><span>posts</span></div><div><b>' + num(o.reach) + '</b><span>reach (sum of posts)</span></div><div><b>' + num(o.eng) + '</b><span>engagements</span></div></div>' +
      '<p class="muted" style="margin:8px 0 0;font-size:12px">Instagram and Facebook posts published in the last ' + days + ' days, from Analytics. <a data-w225go="#socialstats" style="cursor:pointer;font-weight:700">Open Analytics</a></p></div>';
    var paidCard = '<div class="card"><h4>Paid</h4><div class="w250tiles"><div><b>' + E(money(paid.spend, cur)) + '</b><span>spent</span></div><div><b>' + num(paid.reach) + '</b><span>people reached</span></div><div><b>' + num(paid.results) + '</b><span>results' + (cpr ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(money(cpr, cur)) + ' each' : '') + '</span></div></div>' +
      bars + '<p class="muted" style="margin:6px 0 0;font-size:12px">' + num(paid.impressions) + ' impressions, ' + num(paid.clicks) + ' clicks. Read-only from Meta\u2019s ad accounts; the Hub never changes an ad.</p></div>';
    var table = camps.length ? '<div class="card"><b>Campaigns, last ' + days + ' days</b><div style="overflow-x:auto"><table class="rt" style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:12.5px;margin-top:8px"><tr><th align="left">Campaign</th><th align="right">Spent</th><th align="right">Reach</th><th align="right">Results</th><th align="right">Each</th></tr>' +
      camps.sort(function (x, y) { return y.spend - x.spend; }).map(function (cpn) { return '<tr><td style="white-space:normal">' + E(clip(cpn.name || cpn.id, 80)) + '<br><span class="muted">' + E(String(cpn.objective || '').replace(/^OUTCOME_/, '').replace(/_/g, ' ').toLowerCase()) + '</span></td><td align="right">' + E(money(cpn.spend, cur)) + '</td><td align="right">' + num(cpn.reach) + '</td><td align="right">' + num(cpn.results) + ' <span class="muted">' + E(RESW[cpn.result_type] || '') + '</span></td><td align="right">' + (cpn.results ? E(money(cpn.spend / cpn.results, cur)) : '\u2013') + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table></div></div>'
      : '<div class="card"><div class="empty">No ads ran in the last ' + days + ' days.' + (accs.length ? ' Lifetime spend: ' + accs.map(function (a) { return E(a.name || a.id) + ' ' + E(money(a.lifetime_spend, a.currency)); }).join(', ') + '.' : '') + '</div></div>';
    var err = M250.ads.error ? '<div class="card" style="background:rgba(179,64,47,.08)">Could not read the ad accounts: ' + E(clip(M250.ads.error, 160)) + '</div>' : '';
    return err + head + '<div class="w250cmp">' + orgCard + paidCard + '</div><div style="height:14px"></div>' + table +
      '<p class="muted" style="margin:6px 0 0">Ad accounts: ' + (accs.length ? accs.map(function (a) { return E(a.name && a.name !== String(a.id).replace('act_', '') ? a.name : a.id); }).join(', ') : 'none found') + '. <a href="https://adsmanager.facebook.com/adsmanager/manage/campaigns" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open Ads Manager</a></p>';
  }

  /* ---------- Connections: Meta live, and WhatsApp ---------- */
  function yes250(b, t, f) { return b ? '<span class="w250ok">' + E(t || 'Yes') + '</span>' : '<span class="w250no">' + E(f || 'No') + '</span>'; }
  function metaLiveCard250() {
    var adm = isAdmin();
    var m = M250.meta;
    var body;
    if (!adm) body = '<p class="muted" style="margin:0">Admins see the details here. Comments and messages arrive as they happen; the 10-minute check catches anything missed.</p>';
    else if (!m) body = '<p class="muted" style="margin:0">' + (M250.busy.meta ? 'Checking\u2026' : '<a data-w250cx="meta" style="cursor:pointer;font-weight:700">Check the live connection</a>') + '</p>';
    else if (m.error) body = '<p class="w250no" style="margin:0">' + E(m.error) + '</p>';
    else {
      var ps = m.page_subscription || {}, ev = m.events_7d || {}, evn = Object.keys(ev).reduce(function (s, k) { return s + ev[k]; }, 0);
      var forms = Array.isArray(m.lead_forms) ? m.lead_forms : [];
      body = '<dl class="w250kv">' +
        '<dt>Live updates</dt><dd>' + yes250(!!ps.subscribed, 'On: ' + (ps.fields || []).join(', '), 'Off') + '</dd>' +
        '<dt>Signed by Meta</dt><dd>' + yes250(!!m.app_secret, 'Checked', 'No app secret') + '</dd>' +
        '<dt>Last update</dt><dd>' + E(m.last_event ? ago(m.last_event) : 'none yet') + (evn ? ' \u00b7 ' + evn + ' in 7 days' : '') + '</dd>' +
        '<dt>Messenger</dt><dd>' + (m.messenger && m.messenger.readable ? '<span class="w250ok">Readable</span>' : yes250(false, '', clip((m.messenger || {}).error || 'Not readable', 80))) + '</dd>' +
        '<dt>Instagram DMs</dt><dd>' + (m.instagram_dm && m.instagram_dm.readable ? '<span class="w250ok">Readable</span>' : '<span class="w250wait">Limited until App Review</span>') + '</dd>' +
        '<dt>Lead forms</dt><dd>' + (forms.length ? forms.length + ' (' + E(forms.map(function (x) { return x.name; }).slice(0, 3).join(', ')) + ')' : 'None yet') + ': sign-ups go to Applicants received</dd>' +
        '<dt>Ad accounts</dt><dd>' + E((m.ad_accounts || []).map(function (a) { return a.name; }).join(', ') || 'none') + '</dd></dl>';
    }
    return '<div class="card"><div class="hd"><div class="lg" style="background:#0866FF">' + I('bolt') + '</div><div><b>Meta, live</b><small>Comments, messages, lead ads and paid results as they happen</small></div></div>' + body +
      (adm ? '<div class="row"><span class="sp"></span>' + (m && !m.error ? '<a class="btn225 sm" data-w250cx="resub">' + I('refresh') + ' Subscribe again</a>' : '') + '<a class="btn225 sm" data-w250cx="meta">' + I('refresh') + ' Check</a></div>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function waCard250() {
    var adm = isAdmin(), w = M250.wa;
    if (!w) return '<div class="card"><div class="hd"><div class="lg" style="background:#25D366">' + I('chat') + '</div><div><b>WhatsApp</b><small>Serving reminders and \u201cstill in?\u201d beside the texts</small></div></div><p class="muted" style="margin:0">' + (M250.busy.wa ? 'Checking\u2026' : '<a data-w250cx="wa" style="cursor:pointer;font-weight:700">Check WhatsApp</a>') + '</p></div>';
    if (w.error) return '<div class="card"><div class="hd"><div class="lg" style="background:#25D366">' + I('chat') + '</div><div><b>WhatsApp</b></div></div><p class="w250no" style="margin:0">' + E(w.error) + '</p></div>';
    var num0 = w.number || {}, acc = w.account || {}, tps = Array.isArray(w.templates) ? w.templates : [];
    var test = /555/.test(String(num0.display_phone_number || '')) || /test/i.test(String(num0.verified_name || ''));
    var tpl = tps.length ? tps.map(function (t) { return '<span class="' + (t.status === 'APPROVED' ? '' : 'miss') + '" title="' + E(t.rejected_reason || '') + '">' + E(t.name.replace(/^wa_/, '').replace(/_/g, ' ') + ' ' + t.language + ': ' + String(t.status || '').toLowerCase()) + '</span>'; }).join('') : '<span class="miss">no templates yet</span>';
    var ed = M250.waEdit || { mode: w.mode, channel: w.channel, tests: '' };

      '<label for="w250wtest">Test phones</label><input id="w250wtest" placeholder="' + (w.test_phones ? w.test_phones + ' saved; type to replace' : '514 555 0123, 438 555 0199') + '" value="' + E(ed.tests || '') + '"></div>' +
      '<div class="row"><a class="btn225 sm" data-w250cx="watest">' + I('send') + ' Send me a test</a><span class="sp"></span><a class="btn225 sm pri" data-w250cx="wasave">Save</a></div>' : '<p class="muted" style="margin:0">An admin changes these.</p>';
    return '<div class="card"><div class="hd"><div class="lg" style="background:#25D366">' + I('chat') + '</div><div><b>WhatsApp</b><small>Serving reminders and \u201cstill in?\u201d beside the texts</small></div></div>' +
      '<dl class="w250kv"><dt>Number</dt><dd>' + E(num0.display_phone_number || '\u2013') + (test ? ' <span class="w250wait">Meta\u2019s test number</span>' : '') + '</dd>' +
      '<dt>Business</dt><dd>' + E(String(acc.business_verification_status || '\u2013').replace(/_/g, ' ')) + '</dd>' +
      '<dt>Now</dt><dd>' + (w.mode === 'off' ? '<span class="w250wait">Off</span>' : w.mode === 'test' ? '<span class="w250wait">Test</span>' : '<span class="w250ok">Live</span>') + (w.channel === 'also' ? ', with the texts' : ', not with the texts') + '</dd></dl>' +
      '<div class="chips">' + tpl + '</div>' +
      '<p class="muted" style="margin:0;font-size:12px">The same hours as the texts (9 am to 7 pm), nobody who said STOP, YES or NO and the buttons confirm or decline. WhatsApp cannot post into groups. Going live needs Welfare Avenue\u2019s own number, the business verified and a payment method in the Meta app.</p>' + ctrl + '</div>';

  async function connAct250(a) {
    try {
      if (a === 'meta' || a === 'resub') {
        M250.busy.meta = true; paint();
        if (a === 'resub') { await fn('meta-events', { action: 'subscribe' }); await fn('meta-events', { action: 'app_subscribe' }); toastS('Subscribed again'); }
        M250.meta = await fn('meta-events', { action: 'status' });
      }
      if (a === 'wa') { M250.busy.wa = true; paint(); M250.wa = await fn('whatsapp-send', { action: 'status' }); }
      if (a === 'wasave') {
        var mode = (document.getElementById('w250wmode') || {}).value || 'off', chan = (document.getElementById('w250wchan') || {}).value || 'off', tests = String((document.getElementById('w250wtest') || {}).value || '').trim();
        if (mode === 'live' && !confirm('Live: WhatsApp messages go to volunteers whenever a confirmation or "still in?" text goes out. Turn it on?')) return;
        var rows = [{ key: 'sms_whatsapp_mode', value: mode }, { key: 'sms_whatsapp_channel', value: chan }];
        if (tests) rows.push({ key: 'sms_whatsapp_test_phones', value: tests.split(/[,;\n]+/).map(function (x) { return x.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(-10); }).filter(function (x) { return x.length === 10; }).join(',') });
        for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) { var r = await db.from('app_config').update({ value: rows[i].value }).eq('key', rows[i].key); if (r.error) throw r.error; }
        M250.waEdit = null; toastS('Saved'); M250.wa = await fn('whatsapp-send', { action: 'status' });
      }
      if (a === 'watest') {
        var w = M250.wa || {}, tp = (w.templates || []).filter(function (t) { return t.status === 'APPROVED'; })[0];
        var tpl = tp ? tp.name : 'hello_world';
        if (!confirm('Send one WhatsApp message (' + (tp ? tpl.replace(/^wa_/, '').replace(/_/g, ' ') : 'Meta\u2019s hello world') + ') to the first test phone?')) return;
        var t = await fn('whatsapp-send', { action: 'test', template: tpl, lang: tp && tp.language === 'fr' ? 'fr' : 'en' });
        toastS(t.ok ? 'Sent: check WhatsApp on the test phone' : 'Not sent: ' + (t.error || 'unknown'));
      }
    } catch (x) { toastS(x.message || String(x)); }
    M250.busy.meta = false; M250.busy.wa = false; paint();
  }
  function connectExtra250() { return metaLiveCard250() + waCard250(); }

  /* ---------- events ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!inApp(t)) return;
    var el = t.closest('[data-w250open],[data-w250f],[data-w250pl],[data-w250sync],[data-w250close],[data-w250rp],[data-w250rpx],[data-w250send],[data-w250ai],[data-w250act],[data-w250per],[data-w250adsync],[data-w250cx]'); if (!el) return;
    if (el.hasAttribute('disabled')) { e.preventDefault(); return; }
    var x;
    if ((x = el.getAttribute('data-w250open'))) { M250.sel = x; paint(); loadMsgs250(x).then(function () { paint(); var c = document.querySelector('#w225host .w250conv'); if (c) c.scrollTop = c.scrollHeight; }); var th = M250.threads.filter(function (z) { return String(z.id) === x; })[0]; if (th && th.unread && S.mk) fn('social-dms', { action: 'dm_read', id: Number(x) }).then(function () { th.unread = 0; paint(); try { if (window.w153) window.w153.paint(); } catch (er) { /* fine */ } }, function () { /* fine */ }); return; }
    if (el.hasAttribute('data-w250f')) { M250.f = el.getAttribute('data-w250f') || 'open'; lsSet('w250f', M250.f); paint(); return; }
    if (el.hasAttribute('data-w250pl')) { M250.pl = el.getAttribute('data-w250pl'); paint(); return; }
    if (el.hasAttribute('data-w250sync')) { sync250(); return; }
    if (el.hasAttribute('data-w250close')) { M250.sel = null; paint(); return; }
    if ((x = el.getAttribute('data-w250rp'))) { M250.reply[x] = M250.reply[x] || { text: '' }; paint(); var ta = document.querySelector('#w225host [data-w250rtext="' + x + '"]'); if (ta) ta.focus(); return; }
    if ((x = el.getAttribute('data-w250rpx'))) { delete M250.reply[x]; paint(); return; }
    if ((x = el.getAttribute('data-w250ai'))) { suggest250(x); return; }
    if ((x = el.getAttribute('data-w250send'))) { var rr = M250.reply[x]; if (!rr || !String(rr.text || '').trim()) { toastS('Write the reply first'); return; } rr.sending = true; paint(); dmAct250('dm_reply', x, { text: String(rr.text).trim() }); return; }
    if ((x = el.getAttribute('data-w250act'))) { var ap = x.split('|'); dmAct250(ap[0], ap[1]); return; }
    if ((x = el.getAttribute('data-w250per'))) { M250.period = x; lsSet('w250p', x); paint(); return; }
    if (el.hasAttribute('data-w250adsync')) { M250.busy.ads = true; paint(); fn('social-ads', { action: 'sync', days: 30 }).then(function () { return loadAds250(true); }).then(function () { toastS('The ad accounts are read again'); }, function (er) { toastS('Could not read them: ' + (er.message || er)); }).then(function () { M250.busy.ads = false; paint(); }); return; }
    if ((x = el.getAttribute('data-w250cx'))) { e.preventDefault(); connAct250(x); return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!inApp(t) || !t.matches) return;
    if (t.matches('[data-w250q]')) { M250.q = t.value; soon(); return; }
    if (t.matches('[data-w250rtext]')) { var id = t.getAttribute('data-w250rtext'); (M250.reply[id] = M250.reply[id] || {}).text = t.value; return; }
    if (t.matches('#w250wtest')) { M250.waEdit = M250.waEdit || { mode: (M250.wa || {}).mode, channel: (M250.wa || {}).channel }; M250.waEdit.tests = t.value; return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!inApp(t) || !t.matches) return;
    if (t.matches('[data-w250assign]')) { dmAct250('dm_assign', t.getAttribute('data-w250assign'), { to: t.value }); return; }
    if (t.matches('[data-w250saved]')) {
      var tid = t.getAttribute('data-w250saved'), sn = lib().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === t.value; })[0]; if (!sn) return;
      var th = M250.threads.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === tid; })[0], nm = th ? String(th.participant_name || th.participant_handle || '').split(' ')[0] : '';
      var r = M250.reply[tid] = M250.reply[tid] || {}; r.text = String(sn.body || '').replace(/\{name\}/g, nm || 'there');
      q(db.from('content_snippets').update({ uses: Number(sn.uses || 0) + 1 }).eq('id', sn.id)); paint(); return;
    }
    if (t.matches('#w250wmode,#w250wchan')) { M250.waEdit = M250.waEdit || { tests: '' }; M250.waEdit.mode = (document.getElementById('w250wmode') || {}).value; M250.waEdit.channel = (document.getElementById('w250wchan') || {}).value; return; }
  });

  document.head.appendChild(css262);
  var M262 = { loaded: 0, tags: [], posts: [], f: lsGet('w262hf') || 'new', tag: '', busy: {}, err: '' };
  async function load262(force) {
    if (M262.loaded && !force && Date.now() - M262.loaded < 60000) return;
    try {
      var a = await db.from('social_hashtag_watch').select('id, tag, active, added_by, added_at, last_checked_at, last_error').order('tag');
      if (a.error) throw a.error;
      var b = await db.from('social_hashtag_posts').select('id, tag, media_id, permalink, media_type, caption, posted_at, like_count, comments_count, source, first_seen_at, status, reviewed_by, reviewed_at, note').order('posted_at', { ascending: false }).limit(400);
      if (b.error) throw b.error;
      M262.tags = a.data || []; M262.posts = b.data || []; M262.err = '';
    } catch (e) { M262.err = (e && e.message) || String(e); }
    M262.loaded = Date.now();
  }
  function counts262() { return M262.posts.filter(function (p) { return p.status === 'new' || p.status === 'flagged'; }).length || null; }
  var HST = { new: 'New', ok: 'Looks fine', flagged: 'Needs attention', reported: 'Reported' };
  function hashtagsHtml() {
    if (!S.mk && !S.pub) return '<div class="card"><div class="empty">Hashtags are for the marketing team and admins.</div></div>';
    if (!M262.loaded) { load262().then(paint); return '<div class="card"><div class="empty">Loading the hashtags\u2026</div></div>'; }
    if (M262.err) return '<div class="card"><div class="empty">The hashtag watch is not set up yet (' + E(clip(M262.err, 120)) + ').</div></div>';
    var act = M262.tags.filter(function (t) { return t.active; });
    var last = act.map(function (t) { return t.last_checked_at; }).filter(Boolean).sort().pop();
    var all = M262.posts.filter(function (p) { return !M262.tag || p.tag === M262.tag; });
    var c = { new: 0, flagged: 0, ok: 0, reported: 0, all: all.length }; all.forEach(function (p) { c[p.status] = (c[p.status] || 0) + 1; });
    var head = '<div class="kpis"><div class="kpi"><b class="' + (c.new ? 'warn' : 'ok') + '">' + c.new + '</b><span>new, not looked at</span></div>' +
      '<div class="kpi"><b class="' + (c.flagged ? 'warn' : '') + '">' + c.flagged + '</b><span>need attention</span></div>' +
      '<div class="kpi"><b>' + c.reported + '</b><span>reported</span></div>' +
      '<div class="kpi"><b style="font-size:18px">' + E(last ? ago(last) : 'never') + '</b><span>last check</span><em><a data-w262hsync="1" style="cursor:pointer;color:' + ROSE + ';font-weight:700">' + (M262.busy.sync ? 'Checking\u2026' : 'Check now') + '</a></em></div></div>';
    var tags = '<div class="w262tags">' + M262.tags.map(function (t) {
      return '<span class="w262tag' + (t.active ? '' : ' off') + '" title="' + E(t.last_error ? 'Last check failed: ' + t.last_error : (t.active ? 'Watched' : 'Not watched')) + '"><a data-w262htag="' + E(t.tag) + '" style="cursor:pointer">#' + E(t.tag) + '</a>' + (t.last_error ? ' <small>check failed</small>' : '') +
        '<button type="button" data-w262hon="' + E(t.tag) + '|' + (t.active ? '0' : '1') + '" aria-label="' + (t.active ? 'Stop watching #' : 'Watch #') + E(t.tag) + '" title="' + (t.active ? 'Stop watching' : 'Watch again') + '">' + (t.active ? '&times;' : '+') + '</button></span>';
    }).join('') + '<span class="w262add"><input type="text" data-w262hq placeholder="#hashtag to watch" aria-label="A hashtag to watch" value="' + E(M262.add || '') + '"><a class="btn225 sm" data-w262hadd="1">' + I('plus') + ' Watch</a></span></div>';
    var filters = '<div class="row" style="margin:0 0 12px"><span class="seg">' + [['new', 'New'], ['flagged', 'Needs attention'], ['ok', 'Looks fine'], ['reported', 'Reported'], ['all', 'All']].map(function (x) { return '<a data-w262hf="' + x[0] + '" class="' + (M262.f === x[0] ? 'on' : '') + '">' + x[1] + '<span class="n">' + (c[x[0]] || 0) + '</span></a>'; }).join('') + '</span>' +
      (M262.tag ? '<span class="sp"></span><a class="btn225 sm" data-w262htag="">#' + E(M262.tag) + ' ' + I('x') + '</a>' : '') + '</div>';
    var list = all.filter(function (p) { return M262.f === 'all' || p.status === M262.f; });
    var rows = list.map(function (p) {
      var btn = function (st, l) { return '<a class="btn225 sm' + (p.status === st ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w262hrev="' + p.id + '|' + st + '"' + (M262.busy['r' + p.id] ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + l + '</a>'; };
      return '<div class="w262post' + (p.status === 'flagged' ? ' flagged' : '') + '"><div style="min-width:0"><div class="cap">' + E(clip(p.caption || '(no caption)', 400)) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="meta"><span class="w262st ' + E(p.status) + '">' + E(HST[p.status] || p.status) + '</span><span>#' + E(p.tag) + '</span><span>' + E(String(p.media_type || '').toLowerCase().replace('_album', '').replace('carousel', 'carousel')) + '</span>' +
        '<span>posted ' + E(p.posted_at ? ago(p.posted_at) : '?') + '</span><span>' + (p.like_count != null ? E(String(p.like_count)) + ' likes' : '') + (p.comments_count != null ? ', ' + E(String(p.comments_count)) + ' comments' : '') + '</span>' +
        (p.reviewed_by ? '<span>' + E(HST[p.status] || '') + ' by ' + E(String(p.reviewed_by).split('@')[0].replace('.', ' ')) + '</span>' : '') + (p.note ? '<span>' + E(clip(p.note, 120)) + '</span>' : '') + '</div></div>' +
        '<div class="acts">' + (p.permalink ? '<a class="btn225 sm" href="' + E(p.permalink) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + I('ext') + ' Open on Instagram</a>' : '') + btn('ok', 'Looks fine') + btn('flagged', 'Needs attention') + btn('reported', 'Reported') + '</div></div>';
    }).join('');
    var empty = M262.posts.length ? 'Nothing here.' : (act.length ? 'No public Instagram posts under ' + act.map(function (t) { return '#' + t.tag; }).join(', ') + ' yet. The Hub checks every 30 minutes.' : 'Add a hashtag to watch.');
    var note = '<p class="muted" style="margin:10px 2px 0;font-size:12px">Instagram only: Facebook and TikTok offer no hashtag search, so check those in their apps. Instagram shares public posts from the last 24 hours and its top posts; private accounts never show. To take a post down, open it on Instagram (the three dots, Report) or message the person, then mark it Reported here. Instagram lets us look up 30 different hashtags a week.</p>';
    return head + tags + filters + '<div class="card">' + (rows || '<div class="empty">' + E(empty) + '</div>') + '</div>' + note;
  }
  async function sync262() {
    M262.busy.sync = true; paint();
    try { var r = await fn('hashtag-watch', { action: 'sync' }); var n = ((r && r.tags) || []).reduce(function (s, t) { return s + (t.added || 0); }, 0); toastS(n ? n + (n === 1 ? ' new post' : ' new posts') + ' found' : 'Checked: nothing new'); }
    catch (x) { toastS('Could not check: ' + (x.message || x)); }
    M262.busy.sync = false; await load262(true); paint(); try { if (window.w153) window.w153.paint(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  async function setTag262(tag, on) {
    try { var r = await db.rpc('wa262_hashtag_set', { p_tag: tag, p_active: on }); if (r.error) throw r.error; M262.add = ''; toastS(on ? 'Watching #' + (r.data && r.data.tag || tag) + '. The first check runs now.' : 'Stopped watching #' + tag); }
    catch (x) { toastS(x.message || String(x)); return; }
    await load262(true); paint();
    if (on) sync262();
  }
  async function review262(id, st) {
    M262.busy['r' + id] = true; paint();
    try { var r = await db.rpc('wa262_hashtag_review', { p_id: Number(id), p_status: st, p_note: null }); if (r.error) throw r.error; M262.posts.forEach(function (p) { if (String(p.id) === String(id)) { p.status = st; p.reviewed_by = myEmail(); } }); }
    catch (x) { toastS(x.message || String(x)); }
    delete M262.busy['r' + id]; paint(); try { if (window.w153) window.w153.paint(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!inApp(t)) return;
    var el = t.closest('[data-w262hsync],[data-w262hon],[data-w262hadd],[data-w262hf],[data-w262htag],[data-w262hrev]'); if (!el) return;
    if (el.hasAttribute('disabled')) { e.preventDefault(); return; }
    var x;
    if (el.hasAttribute('data-w262hsync')) { if (!M262.busy.sync) sync262(); return; }
    if ((x = el.getAttribute('data-w262hon'))) { var p = x.split('|'); setTag262(p[0], p[1] === '1'); return; }
    if (el.hasAttribute('data-w262hadd')) { var inp = document.querySelector('#w225host [data-w262hq]'); var v = inp ? inp.value : ''; if (!String(v).replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9_]/g, '')) { toastS('Type a hashtag first'); return; } setTag262(v, true); return; }
    if (el.hasAttribute('data-w262hf')) { M262.f = el.getAttribute('data-w262hf') || 'new'; lsSet('w262hf', M262.f); paint(); return; }
    if (el.hasAttribute('data-w262htag')) { M262.tag = el.getAttribute('data-w262htag') || ''; paint(); return; }
    if ((x = el.getAttribute('data-w262hrev'))) { var q = x.split('|'); review262(q[0], q[1]); return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) { var i = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w225host [data-w262hq]'); if (i) M262.add = i.value; });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key !== 'Enter') return; var i = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w225host [data-w262hq]'); if (!i) return; e.preventDefault(); if (String(i.value).replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9_]/g, '')) setTag262(i.value, true); });

  /* ---------- the academy's page tours (wave122's engine; trainings row tour:contentstudio; tour:studio is the Email studio (wave202)) ---------- */
  function addTours() {
    try {
      var T = window.w122; if (!T || !T.tours) return false;
      var OPT = { opt: 1 };
      var St = function (sel, t, p, o) { var x = { s: sel, t: t, p: p }; if (o) Object.keys(o).forEach(function (k) { x[k] = o[k]; }); return x; };
      var Ct = function (t, p) { return { c: 1, t: t, p: p }; };
      if (!T.tours.contentstudio || !T.tours.contentstudio.__w225) T.tours.contentstudio = { __w225: true, tab: 'studio', icon: 'grid', hash: '#studio', steps: [
        St('#studio > .w118mast', 'Content Studio', 'Everything for our social media in one app: plan, approve, post, answer comments and see what worked, on Instagram, Facebook, Threads, LinkedIn, TikTok and YouTube.'),
        St('#w225host .kpis', 'At a glance', 'What is scheduled, what waits for approval, comments to answer, the weekly goal and the clicks on our links. Each number opens its page.', OPT),
        St('#w225host .grid2', 'The week ahead', 'The next seven days, anything that needs attention, the goals and how each platform posts.', OPT),
        Ct('The app menu', 'Calendar to plan and write posts; Queue and schedule for the posting times and goals; Approvals; Comments; Messages for the direct messages on Facebook and Instagram; Hashtags for public Instagram posts under our campaign hashtags; Library for templates, hashtags and saved replies; Link pages for our link in bio and campaign pages, with who clicks what; Short links; Analytics; Paid and organic for what our ads did beside our posts; Report; Connections.'),
        Ct('Messages', 'Direct messages to our Facebook Page and Instagram arrive here. The marketing team answers them within 24 hours of the person\u2019s last message (Meta\u2019s rule); after that, answer in Meta Business Suite. Marketing leads and admins can take a conversation out of the inbox.'),
        Ct('Approvals', 'Marketing leads and admins publish. Everyone else presses Send for approval in the composer; a lead approves it or sends it back with a note, and you hear about it in the Hub and on Slack.'),
        Ct('Reminders', 'Where the Hub cannot post yet (LinkedIn, TikTok or YouTube until they are connected), the post\u2019s owner gets a reminder at post time with the caption and the media. Post it in the app, then press Mark posted.')
      ] };
      var cc = T.tours.contentcal;
      if (cc && !cc.__w225) {
        cc.__w225 = true;
        cc.steps = (cc.steps || []).map(function (s) {
          if (s.s === '#contentcal .w116top') return St(s.s, 'Content calendar', 'Plan, approve and publish posts for Instagram, Facebook, Threads, LinkedIn, TikTok and YouTube. It lives in the Content Studio app.');
          if (s.s === '#contentcal .cap') return St(s.s, 'How we post', 'Which platforms the Hub posts to directly, and which ones send a reminder to post by hand. Connections in the Content Studio sets them up.', OPT);
          if (s.c && s.t === 'The composer') return Ct('The composer', 'Pick the platforms, write the caption (the Hub drafts it and tailors it for each platform), add images or a video with alt text, a first comment and tags. Preview it on each platform, then plan it, schedule it in the next free slot, or send it for approval.');
          if (s.s === '#contentcal .chips') return St(s.s, 'Filters', 'By platform, state, tag, and the posts waiting for approval.', OPT);
          return s;
        });
      }
      return true;
    } catch (e) { return false; }
  }

  /* ---------- paint and route ---------- */
  var KEEP = ['data-w262hq', 'data-w250q', 'data-w250rtext', 'data-w225iq', 'data-w225lq', 'data-w225kq', 'data-w225lf', 'data-w225bf', 'data-w225bsoc', 'data-w225rtext', 'data-w225rnote', 'data-w225blabel', 'data-w225burl', 'data-w225apnote', 'data-w230b', 'data-w230p', 'data-w230s', 'data-w230so', 'data-w230tc', 'data-w230ab', 'data-w230st'];
  function paint() {
    if (S.sec === 'calendar') return;
    var h = host(); if (!h) return;
    var a = document.activeElement, keep = null;
    if (a && h.contains(a)) { for (var i = 0; i < KEEP.length; i++) { if (a.hasAttribute(KEEP[i])) { keep = { attr: KEEP[i], val: a.getAttribute(KEEP[i]), s: a.selectionStart, e: a.selectionEnd }; break; } } }
    /* typed-but-unsent text survives a repaint */
    var held = {}; h.querySelectorAll('[data-w225apnote],[data-w225blabel],[data-w225burl]').forEach(function (el) { var k = ['data-w225apnote', 'data-w225blabel', 'data-w225burl'].filter(function (x) { return el.hasAttribute(x); })[0]; held[k + '=' + el.getAttribute(k)] = el.value; });
    h.querySelectorAll('input[id^="w225"],textarea[id^="w225"],select[id^="w225"],input[id^="w230"],select[id^="w230"]').forEach(function (el) { if (el.value) held['id=' + el.id] = el.value; });
    var html;
    if (!staffOk()) html = '<div class="card"><div class="empty">The Content Studio is for the staff.</div></div>';
    else if (S.err && !S.loaded) html = '<div class="card"><div class="empty">The Content Studio could not load (' + E(clip(S.err, 140)) + '). <a data-w225reload="1" style="cursor:pointer;font-weight:700">Try again</a></div></div>';
    else if (!S.loaded) html = window.w306 ? window.w306.skeleton(S.sec) : '<div class="card"><div class="empty">Loading the Content Studio\u2026</div></div>';   /* wave306: never a false empty state while loading */
    else {
      try {
        html = S.sec === 'queue' ? queueHtml() : S.sec === 'approvals' ? approvalsHtml() : S.sec === 'inbox' ? inboxHtml() : S.sec === 'messages' ? messagesHtml() : S.sec === 'hashtags' ? hashtagsHtml() : S.sec === 'ads' ? adsHtml() : S.sec === 'library' ? libraryHtml() : S.sec === 'pages' ? pagesHtml() : S.sec === 'links' ? linksHtml() : S.sec === 'report' ? reportHtml() : S.sec === 'connections' ? (window.w277 && window.w277.connectHtml ? window.w277.connectHtml() : connectHtml()) : S.sec === 'web' && window.w306 ? window.w306.web() : (window.w306 ? window.w306.today() : overviewHtml());   /* wave306 */
      } catch (e) { console.warn('wave225 paint', e); html = '<div class="card"><div class="empty">Something went wrong drawing this page: ' + E(e.message || e) + '</div></div>'; }
    }
    if (!(S.sec === 'pages' && S.sub === 'edit' && lpPatch(h, html))) h.innerHTML = html;
    Object.keys(held).forEach(function (k) { var p = k.split('='), el = h.querySelector('[' + p[0] + '="' + p.slice(1).join('=') + '"]'); if (el && held[k] !== el.value) el.value = held[k]; });
    if (keep) { try { var el = h.querySelector('[' + keep.attr + (keep.val ? '="' + keep.val + '"' : '') + ']'); if (el) { el.focus(); if (keep.s != null && el.setSelectionRange) el.setSelectionRange(keep.s, keep.e); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    if (window.w306) { try { if (S.loaded) window.w306.entered(S.sec); window.w306.tabs(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }   /* wave306 */
  }
  function route(hh) {
    var raw = String(hh || '').replace(/^#/, ''), qi = raw.indexOf('?'), path = qi >= 0 ? raw.slice(0, qi) : raw;
    var p = path.split('/'), sec = p[1] || 'overview', sub = p[2] || '';
    S.sec = SECTIONS.indexOf(sec) >= 0 ? sec : 'overview';
    S.sub = sub; S.sub2 = p[3] || '';
    if (S.sec === 'library') { if (LIBT.some(function (x) { return x[0] === sub; })) S.f.lib = sub; else S.sub = S.f.lib || 'templates'; if (S.libEdit && S.libEdit.tab !== S.sub) S.libEdit = null; }
    if (S.sec === 'connections' && qi >= 0) connFlash();
    if (S.sec === 'approvals' && qi >= 0) { try { var qa = new URLSearchParams(raw.slice(qi + 1)); S.apDo = qa.get('post') ? { post: qa.get('post'), do: qa.get('do') === 'back' ? 'back' : 'approve' } : null; } catch (e) { S.apDo = null; } }   /* wave241 */
    if (S.sec === 'library' && S.sub === 'partners' && qi >= 0) { try { var qp = new URLSearchParams(raw.slice(qi + 1)); if (qp.get('partner')) S.f.partner = qp.get('partner'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  }
  async function show(hh) {
    route(hh || location.hash);
    var h = host(); if (!h) return;
    paint();   /* wave306: draw at once (a skeleton the first time, what we have after) */
    await loadAll();
    if (S.sec === 'connections' && !S.conn) await loadConn();
    if (S.sec === 'pages') { try { await lpLoad(); } catch (e) { /* drawn below */ } }
    if (S.sec === 'messages') { try { await load250(); } catch (e) { /* drawn below */ } }   /* wave251 */
    if (S.sec === 'hashtags') { try { await load262(); } catch (e) { /* drawn below */ } }   /* wave262c */
    if (S.sec === 'ads') { try { await loadAds250(); } catch (e) { /* drawn below */ } }   /* wave253 */
    paint();
    try { if (window.w153 && window.w153.paint) window.w153.paint(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    if (S.sec === 'approvals' && S.apDo) { try { await applyApDo(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }   /* wave241 */
    if (S.sec === 'library' && S.sub === 'partners') { try { var hadP = !!S.partners; await loadPartnerPosts(); if (!hadP) paint(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  }
  function reload() { S.loaded = 0; S.conn = null; M250.loaded = 0; M250.ads = null; M250.meta = null; M250.wa = null; return show(location.hash); }

  /* ---------- small actions ---------- */
  function compose(pre) { var w = W(); if (w && w.compose) { w.compose(pre || null); return; } if (window.w121Compose) window.w121Compose(pre || null); else go('#contentcal/new'); }
  function openPost(id) { var w = W(); if (w && w.open && w.open(id)) return; go('#contentcal/' + id); }
  function localIso(d, at) { return new Date(d + 'T' + (at || '18:00') + ':00').toISOString(); }
  async function fillSlot(d, at, id) {
    try {
      var r = await db.from('content_posts').update({ scheduled_at: localIso(d, at), updated_by: myName() || myEmail(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', Number(id)); if (r.error) throw r.error;
      var w = W(); if (w) await w.load(true); toastS('Planned for ' + niceDate(d) + ', ' + at + '. Open it to schedule it.');
    } catch (x) { toastS('Could not plan it: ' + (x.message || x)); }
    paint();
  }
  async function removeSlot(id) {
    try { var r = await db.from('content_slots').delete().eq('id', Number(id)); if (r.error) throw r.error; var w = W(); if (w) await w.studio(); } catch (x) { toastS('Could not remove it: ' + (x.message || x)); }
    paint();
  }
  async function saveGoals() {
    var g = {}; document.querySelectorAll('#w225host [data-w225goal]').forEach(function (el) { var v = Math.max(0, Math.min(21, Math.round(Number(el.value) || 0))); g[el.getAttribute('data-w225goal')] = v; });
    try { var value = Object.assign({}, S.settings || {}, { goals: g }); var r = await db.rpc('studio_settings_save', { p_key: 'content_studio', p_value: value }); if (r.error) throw r.error; S.settings = value; toastS('Goals saved'); }
    catch (x) { toastS('Could not save the goals: ' + (x.message || x)); }
    paint();
  }
  async function syncInbox() {
    if (S.busy.sync) return; S.busy.sync = true; paint();
    try {
      var r = await fn('social-inbox', { action: 'sync', days: 45 });
      var rs = await Promise.all([q(db.from('social_comments').select('*').gte('commented_at', new Date(Date.now() - 90 * 86400000).toISOString()).order('commented_at', { ascending: false }).limit(1500)), q(db.from('hub_settings').select('value').eq('key', 'studio_inbox_sync').maybeSingle())]);
      if (rs[0].data) S.comments = rs[0].data; if (rs[1].data) S.inboxLast = rs[1].data.value;
      var added = 0; Object.keys((r && r.platforms) || {}).forEach(function (k) { added += Number(r.platforms[k].added || 0); }); toastS(added ? added + ' new comment' + (added === 1 ? '' : 's') : 'Checked: nothing new');
    } catch (x) { toastS('Could not check: ' + (x.message || x)); }
    S.busy.sync = false; paint(); try { if (window.w153) window.w153.paint(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }

  /* ---------- events ---------- */
  function inApp(t) { return !!(t && t.closest && t.closest('#w225host')); }
  var tT = null; function soon() { clearTimeout(tT); tT = setTimeout(paint, 200); }
  function arg(el, a) { return el.getAttribute(a) || ''; }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!inApp(t)) return;
    var el = t.closest('a,button,[data-w225open],[data-w225go],[data-w225lcol]'); if (!el) return;
    if (el.hasAttribute('disabled')) { e.preventDefault(); return; }
    var x;
    if (el.hasAttribute('href') && /^https?:/.test(el.getAttribute('href'))) return;
    if ((x = arg(el, 'data-w225open'))) { e.preventDefault(); openPost(x); return; }
    if ((x = arg(el, 'data-w225go'))) { e.preventDefault(); go(x); return; }
    if (el.hasAttribute('data-w225new')) { e.preventDefault(); compose(null); return; }
    if (el.hasAttribute('data-w225reload')) { reload(); return; }
    if ((x = arg(el, 'data-w225slotnew'))) { var sp = x.split('|'); compose({ date: sp[0], time: sp[1], platforms: (sp[2] || '').split(',').filter(Boolean) }); return; }

/* =====================================================================
   WA v306 the Content Studio, rebuilt around five areas (10/11/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (10/11/26, after testing it together): "the Content Studio could definitely use a revamp on the layout of it and its
   functionality ... reduce redundancy of different places and areas ... make sure that with our recent marketing and
   fundraising leader meeting and the past marketing meeting, all those updates are added", and "ensure you pull in
   everything important from the website analytics ... Really consider the user experience and the data that really
   matters and how you plan to present it."
   His choices: five areas instead of fourteen places, nothing removed and every old address still works.

   - The rail (parts153 APPS.studio): Today, Plan, Inbox, Results, Toolkit. An item lights for its own address and for the
     addresses in its `also` list (v153 isOn reads it), so #studio/queue lights Plan and #socialstats lights Results.
   - The area tabs (#w306tabs): one row under the masthead of every Studio page, the area's pages in order with their counts.
     It is not a second menu: the rail names the area, the tabs its pages.
       Plan: Calendar #contentcal, Queue #studio/queue, Approvals #studio/approvals
       Inbox: Comments #studio/inbox, Messages #studio/messages, Hashtags #studio/hashtags
       Results: Website #studio/web, Social #socialstats, Paid and organic #studio/ads, Report #studio/report
       Toolkit: Library #studio/library, Link in bio #studio/pages, Short links #studio/links, Connections #studio/connections
   - Today (#studio, replaces wave225's Overview through w225's paint): what needs me first (approvals, posts sent back,
     failures of the last 14 days, comments, messages, hashtags), the week as seven days with the goals, the campaign that is
     on (the drive's money, gifts and the website's donate clicks), and the pulse of the last 28 days (social reach, engaged
     website visitors, link clicks, website actions), each against the 28 days before.
   - Results > Website (#studio/web): web-insights v1 (GA4 read live, cached as totals). What a visit is worth, where people
     come from, what brings them in, what they do (donate clicks by campaign, sign-ups, forms sent), who they are (device,
     new or returning, city), with plain sentences that say what changed, and automated traffic called out.
   - Loading: skeletons until the Studio's data is in, never an empty state that is not true (the Overview used to say every
     platform was Reminder and the comments were not connected while it loaded). wave225's loadAll runs its loads at once.
   - Motion (Emil Kowalski's rules): the tab marker slides 200 ms ease-out between tabs of one area; a section's cards rise
     4 px and fade in once when the section changes, never on a repaint; buttons press to .97; nothing moves for people who
     ask for reduced motion; hover effects only where there is a pointer that hovers.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {

  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function num(v) { v = Number(v); if (!isFinite(v)) return '–'; var a = Math.abs(v); if (a >= 1e6) return (v / 1e6).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'M'; if (a >= 1e4) return Math.round(v / 1e3) + 'k'; if (a >= 1e3) return (v / 1e3).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'k'; return String(Math.round(v)); }
  function money(v) { v = Number(v || 0); return '$' + (v >= 1000 ? v.toLocaleString('en-CA', { maximumFractionDigits: 0 }) : String(Math.round(v))); }
  function pct(v, d) { if (!isFinite(v)) return '–'; return (v * 100).toFixed(d == null ? 0 : d) + '%'; }
  function dur(sec) { sec = Math.round(Number(sec) || 0); if (sec < 60) return sec + 's'; var m = Math.floor(sec / 60), s = sec % 60; return m + 'm' + (s ? ' ' + (s < 10 ? '0' : '') + s + 's' : ''); }
  function clip(s, n) { s = String(s || ''); return s.length > n ? s.slice(0, n - 1) + '…' : s; }
  function ymd(d) { return d.getFullYear() + '-' + ('0' + (d.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2) + '-' + ('0' + d.getDate()).slice(-2); }
  function addD(s, n) { var d = new Date(s + 'T12:00:00'); d.setDate(d.getDate() + n); return ymd(d); }

  function ic(n) { return '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.9" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">' + (IC[n] || IC.check) + '</svg>'; }
  function delta(cur, prev, opt) {
    opt = opt || {};
    if (prev === undefined) return '';
    if (prev == null || !isFinite(prev) || prev === 0) return cur ? '<span class="delta flat">new</span>' : '<span class="delta flat">–</span>';
    var d = (cur - prev) / prev; if (opt.lowerBetter) d = -d;
    var cls = Math.abs(d) < 0.03 ? 'flat' : d > 0 ? 'up' : 'dn';
    var shown = (cur - prev) / prev;
    return '<span class="delta ' + cls + '" title="Against the period before">' + (Math.abs(shown) < 0.03 ? '' : '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="12" height="12" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="round" aria-hidden="true">' + (shown > 0 ? IC.up : IC.down) + '</svg>') + Math.abs(Math.round(shown * 100)) + '%</span>';
  }
  function spark(vals, color) {
    vals = (vals || []).map(Number); if (vals.length < 2) return '';
    var w = 120, h = 28, mx = Math.max.apply(null, vals) || 1;
    var pts = vals.map(function (v, i) { return [i / (vals.length - 1) * w, h - 2 - v / mx * (h - 4)]; });
    var d = pts.map(function (p, i) { return (i ? 'L' : 'M') + p[0].toFixed(1) + ' ' + p[1].toFixed(1); }).join('');
    return '<svg class="spark" viewBox="0 0 ' + w + ' ' + h + '" preserveAspectRatio="none" aria-hidden="true"><path d="' + d + 'L' + w + ' ' + h + 'L0 ' + h + 'Z" fill="' + (color || ROSE) + '" opacity=".10"/><path d="' + d + '" fill="none" stroke="' + (color || ROSE) + '" stroke-width="1.6" vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg>';
  }

  /* ---------- the area tabs ---------- */
  var lastTab = null;
  function panelFor(h) { var p = path(h).split('/')[0]; return document.getElementById(p || 'studio'); }
  function tabs() {
    var h = hashNow(), a = areaOf(h);
    var old = document.getElementById('w306tabs');
    if (!a || !a.area.tabs.length) { if (old) old.remove(); lastTab = a ? a.area.k + ':-' : null; return; }
    var panel = panelFor(h); if (!panel) return;
    var c = {}; try { c = W2() && W2().counts ? W2().counts() : {}; } catch (e) { c = {}; }
    var html = a.area.tabs.map(function (t, i) { var n = t[2] ? c[t[2]] : null; return '<a href="' + E(t[0]) + '"' + (i === a.tab ? ' aria-current="page"' : '') + '>' + E(t[1]) + (n ? '<b class="tnum">' + E(n > 99 ? '99+' : n) + '</b>' : '') + '</a>'; }).join('') + '<span class="ind" aria-hidden="true"></span>';
    var nav = old;
    if (!nav || nav.parentNode !== panel) {
      if (nav) nav.remove();
      nav = document.createElement('nav'); nav.id = 'w306tabs'; nav.setAttribute('aria-label', a.area.l);
      var mast = panel.querySelector(':scope > .w118mast') || panel.querySelector(':scope > [class*="mast"]');
      if (mast && mast.nextSibling) panel.insertBefore(nav, mast.nextSibling); else if (mast) panel.appendChild(nav); else panel.insertBefore(nav, panel.firstChild);
    }
    var key = a.area.k + ':' + a.tab, sameArea = lastTab && lastTab.split(':')[0] === a.area.k;
    if (nav.getAttribute('data-h') !== html) { nav.innerHTML = html; nav.setAttribute('data-h', html); }
    nav.setAttribute('aria-label', a.area.l);
    var on = nav.querySelector('a[aria-current="page"]'), ind = nav.querySelector('.ind');
    if (on && ind) {
      nav.classList.toggle('noanim', !sameArea || reduce || lastTab === key);
      if (!sameArea) { ind.style.transform = 'translateX(' + on.offsetLeft + 'px)'; ind.style.width = on.offsetWidth + 'px'; }
      requestAnimationFrame(function () { ind.style.transform = 'translateX(' + on.offsetLeft + 'px)'; ind.style.width = on.offsetWidth + 'px'; try { if (on.scrollIntoView && nav.scrollWidth > nav.clientWidth) on.scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest', inline: 'nearest' }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } });
    }
    lastTab = key;
  }

  /* ---------- entering a section: cards rise once, never on a repaint ---------- */
  var lastSec = null;
  function entered(sec) {
    try { var st = document.getElementById('studio'); if (st) st.classList.toggle('w306today', sec === 'overview'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    var h = document.getElementById('w225host'); if (!h || reduce) { lastSec = sec; return; }
    if (lastSec === sec) return;
    lastSec = sec;
    h.classList.remove('w306enter'); void h.offsetWidth; h.classList.add('w306enter');
    clearTimeout(entered.t); entered.t = setTimeout(function () { h.classList.remove('w306enter'); }, 420);
  }

  /* ---------- skeletons ---------- */
  function sk(w, hgt, mb) { return '<div class="w306sk" style="width:' + w + ';height:' + hgt + 'px;margin:0 0 ' + (mb == null ? 10 : mb) + 'px"></div>'; }
  function skeleton(sec) {
    var tile = '<div class="w306skcard" style="padding:14px">' + sk('55%', 12) + sk('40%', 28, 8) + sk('100%', 22, 0) + '</div>';
    var card = function (lines) { var s = '<div class="w306skcard">' + sk('38%', 18, 16); for (var i = 0; i < lines; i++) s += sk(i % 3 === 2 ? '72%' : '100%', 40); return s + '</div>'; };
    if (sec === 'overview' || !sec) return '<div class="w306" aria-busy="true" aria-label="Loading">' + sk('34%', 30, 6) + sk('22%', 14, 18) + '<div class="grid"><div>' + card(3) + card(2) + '</div><div>' + card(2) + '<div class="pulse" style="grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr">' + tile + tile + '</div></div></div></div>';
    if (sec === 'web') return '<div class="w306" aria-busy="true" aria-label="Loading">' + '<div class="heroes">' + tile + tile + tile + tile + '</div>' + card(1) + '<div class="grid"><div>' + card(4) + '</div><div>' + card(3) + '</div></div></div>';
    return '<div class="w306" aria-busy="true" aria-label="Loading">' + card(4) + card(3) + '</div>';
  }

  /* ---------- Today ---------- */
  var T = { camp: null, campAt: 0, olderOpen: false };
  function loadCamp() {
    if (T.campBusy || Date.now() - T.campAt < 300000) return;   /* once every five minutes at most, answered or not */
    T.campBusy = true; T.campAt = Date.now();
    Promise.resolve((typeof db !== 'undefined' ? db : window.db) ? (typeof db !== 'undefined' ? db : window.db).rpc('www_campaign_totals', { p_slug: 'gift-basket-drive' }) : null).then(function (r) { T.camp = r && r.data ? r.data : null; }, function () { /* fine */ }).then(function () { T.campBusy = false; if (T.camp) repaint('overview'); });
  }
  function repaint(sec) { var w = W2(); if (!w) return; var s = w.state(); if (!sec || s.sec === sec) { try { w.paint(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
  function hello() { var h = new Date().getHours(); return h < 5 ? 'Good evening' : h < 12 ? 'Good morning' : h < 18 ? 'Good afternoon' : 'Good evening'; }
  function today() {
    var w = W2(), x = X(); if (!w || !x) return '';
    var S = w.state(), all = w.posts(), now = Date.now(), t = x.today(), me = x.myEmail();
    var first = String(x.myName() || '').split(' ')[0];
    loadCamp(); if (!WEB.r28) loadWeb(28);
    /* what needs me */
    var needs = [], older = [];
    var pend = all.filter(function (p) { return p.approval_status === 'pending' && ['draft', 'scheduled', 'failed'].indexOf(p.status) >= 0; });
    if (S.pub && pend.length) pend.forEach(function (p) { needs.push({ w: 1, ic: 'check', c: '', t: 'Approve “' + x.titleOf(p) + '”', s: 'From ' + String(p.submitted_by || 'the team').split('@')[0] + (p.scheduled_at ? '–' : num(dc)) + '</b><span>Give clicks on the site, 28 days</span></div></div>' +

    }
    /* the pulse: 28 days against the 28 before */
    var d28 = 28 * 86400000, reachC = 0, reachP = 0, sReach = new Array(28).fill(0);
    S.social.forEach(function (sp) { if (!sp.published_at) return; var age = now - new Date(sp.published_at).getTime(), v = Number(sp.reach || sp.views || 0); if (age < d28) { reachC += v; var ix = 27 - Math.floor(age / 86400000); if (ix >= 0 && ix < 28) sReach[ix] += v; } else if (age < 2 * d28) reachP += v; });
    var clicksC = S.clicks.filter(function (k) { return now - new Date(k.at).getTime() < d28; }).length, sClicks = new Array(28).fill(0);

    var r = WEB.r28, wt = r && r.totals, wc = wt && wt.cur, wp = wt && wt.prev;
    if (r && r.prev && r.prev.from <= '2026-11-09' && r.to >= '2026-10-02') wp = undefined;   /* the tracking gap (October 2 to November 9): no comparison */
    var acts = function (list) { return (list || []).filter(function (e) { return ['donate_click', 'form_sent', 'newsletter_signup'].indexOf(e.eventName) >= 0; }).reduce(function (s, e) { return s + (e.eventCount || 0); }, 0); };
    var tile = function (go, label, val, dl, sp) { return '<a class="tile" data-w225go="' + go + '"><span>' + label + '</span><b class="tnum">' + val + '</b>' + dl + (sp || '') + '</a>'; };

      tile('#studio/web', 'Engaged website visits', wc ? num(wc.engagedSessions) : (WEB.err28 ? '–' : '<span class="w306sk" style="display:inline-block;width:50px;height:24px;vertical-align:middle"></span>'), wc ? (wp === undefined ? '<span class="muted sm">tracking back since Nov 10</span>' : delta(wc.engagedSessions, wp && wp.engagedSessions)) : '', webDaily ? spark(webDaily, TEAL) : '') +
      tile('#studio/links', 'Short link clicks', num(clicksC), '', clicksC ? spark(sClicks, PURPLE) : '') +
      tile('#studio/web', 'Website actions', r ? num(acts(r.events && r.events.cur)) : '–', r ? '<span class="muted sm">gives, forms, sign-ups</span>' : '', '') +
      '</div></div>';
    /* the best post */
    var top = S.social.filter(function (sp) { return sp.published_at && now - new Date(sp.published_at).getTime() < d28; }).sort(function (a, b) { var ea = Number(a.engagement || 0) || (Number(a.likes || 0) + Number(a.comments || 0) + Number(a.shares || 0) + Number(a.saves || 0)), eb = Number(b.engagement || 0) || (Number(b.likes || 0) + Number(b.comments || 0) + Number(b.shares || 0) + Number(b.saves || 0)); return eb - ea; })[0];
    var topCard = top ? '<div class="card"><h3>Best post this month</h3><div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:64px minmax(0,1fr);gap:12px;align-items:center">' + ((top.thumb_cached || top.thumb_url) ? '<img src="' + E(top.thumb_cached || top.thumb_url) + '" alt="" loading="lazy" style="width:64px;height:64px;border-radius:12px;object-fit:cover">' : '<i style="width:64px;height:64px;border-radius:12px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.08)"></i>') +

'<p style="margin:12px 0 0"><a class="btn" href="' + E(top.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open it</a></p>' : '') + '</div>' : '';

      '<div class="grid"><div>' + needsCard + weekCard + '</div><div>' + campCard + pulse + topCard + '</div></div></div>';
