  /* ---------- Results > Website ---------- */
  var WEB = { range: 28, data: {}, busy: {}, err: {}, at: {}, r28: null, err28: null, live: null, liveAt: 0 };
  try { var sr = Number(localStorage.getItem('w306range')); if ([7, 28, 90, 365].indexOf(sr) >= 0) WEB.range = sr; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  function rangeDates(days) { var to = ymd(new Date()), from = addD(to, -(days - 1)); return [from, to]; }
  function call(body) { var w = W2(); if (w && w.x && w.x.fn) return w.x.fn('web-insights', body); return Promise.reject(new Error('The Studio is not ready')); }
  function loadWeb(days, fresh) {
    days = days || WEB.range; var k = String(days);
    if (WEB.busy[k]) return WEB.busy[k];
    if (!fresh && WEB.at[k] && Date.now() - WEB.at[k] < 60000) return Promise.resolve();   /* one try a minute, answered or not */
    WEB.at[k] = Date.now();
    var rd = rangeDates(days);
    WEB.busy[k] = call({ action: 'report', from: rd[0], to: rd[1], fresh: !!fresh }).then(function (j) {
      if (!j || j.error || !j.totals) throw new Error((j && j.error) || 'No answer from Google Analytics');
      WEB.data[k] = j; WEB.err[k] = null; if (days === 28) { WEB.r28 = j; WEB.err28 = null; }
    }).catch(function (e) { WEB.err[k] = (e && e.message) || String(e); if (days === 28) WEB.err28 = WEB.err[k]; }).then(function () { WEB.busy[k] = null; repaint('web'); repaint('overview'); });
    return WEB.busy[k];
  }
  function loadLive() {
    if (WEB.liveBusy || Date.now() - WEB.liveAt < 60000) return;
    WEB.liveBusy = true; WEB.liveAt = Date.now();
    call({ action: 'realtime' }).then(function (j) { WEB.live = j && j.ok ? j : null; }, function () { WEB.live = null; }).then(function () { WEB.liveBusy = false; if (WEB.live) repaint('web'); });
  }
  var CH = { 'Organic Search': ['Google and other searches', TEAL], 'Direct': ['Typed in or no source', '#8e9a96'], 'Organic Social': ['Social media posts', ROSE], 'Paid Social': ['Paid social', '#d06b9a'], 'Referral': ['Links on other sites', PURPLE], 'Paid Search': ['Paid search ads', GOLD], 'Email': ['Emails', '#3d7bb0'], 'Unassigned': ['Unknown', '#c4c9c6'], 'Organic Video': ['Video', '#e0574a'], 'Cross-network': ['Google campaigns', '#a07d2a'], 'AI Assistant': ['ChatGPT and other chat tools', '#5c8fa8'] };
  function chName(k) { return (CH[k] || [k])[0]; } function chColor(k) { return (CH[k] || [0, '#9aa6a2'])[1]; }
  var PAGE_NAMES = { '/': 'Home', '/fr/': 'Accueil (FR)', '/donate/': 'Donate', '/volunteer/': 'Volunteer', '/join/': 'Join our team', '/gift-basket-drive/': 'Holiday Gift Basket Drive', '/fr/paniers-cadeaux/': 'Paniers-cadeaux (FR)', '/about/': 'About', '/events/': 'Events', '/contact/': 'Contact', '/press/': 'Press', '/toronto/': 'Toronto', '/flag-football/': 'Flag football', '/privacy/': 'Privacy' };
  function pageName(p) { var k = String(p || '').split('?')[0]; return PAGE_NAMES[k] || PAGE_NAMES[k + '/'] || k; }
  function chartArea(daily, acts) {
    if (!daily || daily.length < 2) return '<div class="empty">Not enough days in this range to draw a line.</div>';
    var W = 680, H = 190, pl = 34, pr = 8, pt = 10, pb = 24, iw = W - pl - pr, ih = H - pt - pb;
    var mx = Math.max.apply(null, daily.map(function (d) { return d.sessions || 0; })) || 1;
    var step = Math.pow(10, Math.floor(Math.log10(mx))), nice = Math.ceil(mx / step) * step; if (nice / step <= 2) step = step / 2; mx = Math.max(nice, 1);
    var X = function (i) { return pl + i / (daily.length - 1) * iw; }, Y = function (v) { return pt + ih - v / mx * ih; };
    var line = function (k) { return daily.map(function (d, i) { return (i ? 'L' : 'M') + X(i).toFixed(1) + ' ' + Y(d[k] || 0).toFixed(1); }).join(''); };
    var grid = ''; for (var v = 0; v <= mx; v += step) grid += '<line x1="' + pl + '" x2="' + (W - pr) + '" y1="' + Y(v).toFixed(1) + '" y2="' + Y(v).toFixed(1) + '" stroke="rgba(16,63,57,.08)"/><text x="' + (pl - 6) + '" y="' + (Y(v) + 3.5).toFixed(1) + '" text-anchor="end" font-size="10" fill="' + MUTED + '">' + num(v) + '</text>';
    var lab = ''; var every = Math.max(1, Math.round(daily.length / 6));
    daily.forEach(function (d, i) { if (i % every === 0 || i === daily.length - 1) { var dt = new Date(d.day + 'T12:00:00'); lab += '<text x="' + X(i).toFixed(1) + '" y="' + (H - 6) + '" text-anchor="middle" font-size="10" fill="' + MUTED + '">' + dt.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }) + '</text>'; } });
    var dots = ''; var byDay = {}; (acts || []).forEach(function (a) { byDay[a.day] = (byDay[a.day] || 0) + (a.count || 0); });
    daily.forEach(function (d, i) { if (byDay[d.day]) dots += '<circle cx="' + X(i).toFixed(1) + '" cy="' + Y(d.sessions || 0).toFixed(1) + '" r="4" fill="' + GOLD + '" stroke="#FFFAF2" stroke-width="1.5"><title>' + byDay[d.day] + ' action' + (byDay[d.day] > 1 ? 's' : '') + ' on ' + d.day + '</title></circle>'; });
    var s = line('sessions'), e = line('engaged');
    return '<svg class="chart" viewBox="0 0 ' + W + ' ' + H + '" role="img" aria-label="Visits and engaged visits per day">' + grid +
      '<path d="' + s + 'L' + X(daily.length - 1).toFixed(1) + ' ' + Y(0) + 'L' + pl + ' ' + Y(0) + 'Z" fill="' + GREEN + '" opacity=".07"/>' +
      '<path d="' + s + '" fill="none" stroke="' + GREEN + '" stroke-opacity=".45" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linejoin="round"/>' +
      '<path d="' + e + 'L' + X(daily.length - 1).toFixed(1) + ' ' + Y(0) + 'L' + pl + ' ' + Y(0) + 'Z" fill="' + TEAL + '" opacity=".16"/>' +
      '<path d="' + e + '" fill="none" stroke="' + TEAL + '" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-linejoin="round"/>' + dots + lab + '</svg>' +
      '<div class="legend"><span><i style="background:' + GREEN + ';opacity:.5"></i>Visits</span><span><i style="background:' + TEAL + '"></i>Engaged visits (10 seconds or more, two pages, or an action)</span>' + (dots ? '<span><i style="background:' + GOLD + ';border-radius:50%"></i>A day with a gift click, a form sent or a sign-up</span>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function bot(r) { return (r.totalUsers || 0) >= 15 && (r.sessions || 0) >= 15 && (r.engaged == null || r.engaged === 0); }
  function web() {
    var k = String(WEB.range), r = WEB.data[k], err = WEB.err[k];
    if (!r && !WEB.busy[k]) loadWeb(WEB.range);
    loadLive();
    var seg = '<div class="seg" role="group" aria-label="Range">' + [[7, '7 days'], [28, '28 days'], [90, '90 days'], [365, '12 months']].map(function (o) { return '<button type="button" data-w306range="' + o[0] + '" aria-pressed="' + (WEB.range === o[0]) + '">' + o[1] + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
    var liveH = WEB.live ? '<span class="live" title="People on welfareavenue.com in the last 30 minutes who accepted cookies"><i></i><span class="tnum">' + num(WEB.live.active) + '</span> on the site now</span>' : '';
    var top = '<div class="bar1">' + seg + '<span class="sp"></span>' + liveH + '<button type="button" class="btn" data-w306fresh="1" aria-label="Read again from Google Analytics">' + ic('refresh').replace('<svg', '<svg width="15" height="15"') + 'Refresh</button></div>';
    if (err && !r) return '<div class="w306">' + top + '<div class="card"><div class="empty">The website numbers could not load: ' + E(clip(err, 200)) + '. <a data-w306fresh="1" style="cursor:pointer;color:' + ROSE + ';font-weight:700">Try again</a></div></div></div>';
    if (!r) return '<div class="w306">' + top + skeleton('web').replace('<div class="w306" aria-busy="true" aria-label="Loading">', '<div aria-busy="true" aria-label="Loading">') + '</div>';
    /* the new site went live on October 2 without the Google tag; it came back on November 10 (wave306 note) */
    var GAP = ['2026-10-02', '2026-11-09'], hits = function (a, b) { return a <= GAP[1] && b >= GAP[0]; };
    var gapCur = hits(r.from, r.to), gapPrev = r.prev && hits(r.prev.from, r.prev.to), noCompare = gapCur || gapPrev;
    var c = (r.totals && r.totals.cur) || {}, p = noCompare ? {} : ((r.totals && r.totals.prev) || {});
    var users = c.totalUsers || 0, engaged = c.engagedSessions || 0, sessions = c.sessions || 0;
    var perUser = users ? (c.userEngagementDuration || 0) / users : 0, perUserP = noCompare ? undefined : p.totalUsers ? (p.userEngagementDuration || 0) / p.totalUsers : null;
    var evC = {}, evP = {}; (r.events && r.events.cur || []).forEach(function (e) { evC[e.eventName] = e; }); (r.events && r.events.prev || []).forEach(function (e) { evP[e.eventName] = e; });
    var ev = function (n, m) { return (evC[n] && evC[n][m || 'eventCount']) || 0; };
    if (noCompare) evP = {};
    var actions = ev('donate_click') + ev('form_sent') + ev('newsletter_signup'), actionsP = ((evP.donate_click || {}).eventCount || 0) + ((evP.form_sent || {}).eventCount || 0) + ((evP.newsletter_signup || {}).eventCount || 0);
    var since = r.events_since || '2026-11-10', before = r.from < since;
    var heroes = '<div class="heroes">' +
      '<div class="hero main"><span>Engaged visits</span><b class="tnum">' + num(engaged) + '</b>' + delta(engaged, p.engagedSessions) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="hero"><span>Visitors</span><b class="tnum">' + num(users) + '</b>' + delta(users, p.totalUsers) + ' <span class="muted sm" style="display:inline">' + pct(c.engagementRate || 0) + ' engaged</span></div>' +
      '<div class="hero"><span>Time with us, per visitor</span><b class="tnum">' + dur(perUser) + '</b>' + delta(perUser, perUserP) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="hero"><span>Actions taken</span><b class="tnum">' + num(actions) + '</b>' + (actionsP ? delta(actions, actionsP) : '<span class="muted sm" style="display:inline">gives, forms, sign-ups</span>') + '</div></div>';
    /* what it says */
    var says = [];
    var chC = (r.channels && r.channels.cur || []).filter(function (x) { return (x.sessions || 0) > 0; }).sort(function (a, b) { return (b.sessions || 0) - (a.sessions || 0); }), chP = {}; if (!noCompare) (r.channels && r.channels.prev || []).forEach(function (x) { chP[x.sessionDefaultChannelGroup] = x; });
    if (!noCompare && p.engagedSessions) { var dd = (engaged - p.engagedSessions) / p.engagedSessions; if (Math.abs(dd) >= 0.1) says.push([dd > 0 ? 'g' : 'r', dd > 0 ? '↑' : '↓', 'Engaged visits are ' + (dd > 0 ? 'up ' : 'down ') + Math.abs(Math.round(dd * 100)) + '% on the ' + (WEB.range === 365 ? 'year' : WEB.range + ' days') + ' before (' + num(p.engagedSessions) + ' then, ' + num(engaged) + ' now).']); }
    var bestCh = chC.filter(function (x) { return (x.sessions || 0) >= Math.max(20, sessions * 0.03); }).map(function (x) { return { k: x.sessionDefaultChannelGroup, rate: (x.engagedSessions || 0) / (x.sessions || 1), n: x.sessions }; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.rate - a.rate; })[0];
    if (bestCh) says.push(['g', '★', chName(bestCh.k) + ' bring the most engaged visitors: ' + pct(bestCh.rate) + ' of those visits stay and look around.']);
    var soc = chC.filter(function (x) { return x.sessionDefaultChannelGroup === 'Organic Social' || x.sessionDefaultChannelGroup === 'Paid Social'; }).reduce(function (s, x) { return s + (x.sessions || 0); }, 0);
    says.push([soc / (sessions || 1) < 0.1 ? 'r' : 'n', '→', 'Social media sent ' + num(soc) + ' visit' + (soc === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' (' + pct(soc / (sessions || 1)) + ' of all). ' + (soc / (sessions || 1) < 0.1 ? 'Put a Studio short link or the link in bio in every post, so the site gets the people the posts reach.' : 'Keep linking the site from posts.')]);
    var bots = (r.places || []).filter(function (x) { return x.country !== 'Canada' && x.country !== 'United States' && (x.totalUsers || 0) >= 15 && (x.sessions || 0) <= (x.totalUsers || 0) * 1.05; });
    if (bots.length) says.push(['n', '!', num(bots.reduce(function (s, x) { return s + x.totalUsers; }, 0)) + ' visitors from ' + bots.map(function (x) { return x.city; }).join(', ') + ' came once and left at once: most likely automated traffic, not people. Engaged visits leave it out.']);
    var weakLp = (r.landing || []).filter(function (x) { return (x.sessions || 0) >= 10 && (x.engagedSessions || 0) / (x.sessions || 1) < 0.15; })[0];
    if (weakLp) says.push(['r', '↓', pageName(weakLp.landingPage) + ' starts ' + num(weakLp.sessions) + ' visits but ' + pct((weakLp.engagedSessions || 0) / (weakLp.sessions || 1)) + ' stay. ' + (/cart|shop|activity/.test(weakLp.landingPage) ? 'It is an address from the old site: worth a redirect.' : 'Check what people see first there.')]);
    var topClick = (r.clicks || []).filter(function (x) { return x.text; })[0];
    if (topClick) says.push(['g', '\u2605', 'The most clicked: \u201c' + topClick.text + '\u201d (' + num(topClick.count) + ' clicks) on ' + pageName(topClick.page) + '.']);
    if (before) says.push(['n', 'i', 'Gives, sign-ups and forms are counted from ' + new Date(since + 'T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'long', day: 'numeric' }) + ', and since then only visitors who accept cookies are counted at all.']);
    var saysCard = '<div class="card"><h3>What the numbers say</h3><ul class="says">' + says.slice(0, 5).map(function (s) { return '<li><i class="' + s[0] + '">' + E(s[1]) + '</i><span>' + E(s[2]) + '</span></li>'; }).join('') + '</ul></div>';
    var trend = '<div class="card"><h3>Visits, day by day<span class="sp"></span><small class="tnum">' + E(new Date(r.from + 'T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString('en-CA', r.from.slice(0, 4) !== r.to.slice(0, 4) ? { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' } : { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })) + ' to ' + E(new Date(r.to + 'T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' })) + '</small></h3>' + chartArea(r.daily, r.actions_daily) + '</div>';
    /* where they come from */
    var chMax = Math.max.apply(null, chC.map(function (x) { return x.sessions || 0; }).concat([1]));
    var chH = chC.slice(0, 8).map(function (x) { var k2 = x.sessionDefaultChannelGroup, eng = (x.engagedSessions || 0) / (x.sessions || 1), pr = chP[k2]; return '<div class="rw"><span class="nm">' + E(chName(k2)) + ' <span class="muted sm">' + pct(eng) + ' engaged</span></span><span class="vl tnum">' + num(x.sessions) + (pr ? ' ' + delta(x.sessions, pr.sessions) : '') + '</span><span class="tr"><i style="width:' + ((x.engagedSessions || 0) / chMax * 100).toFixed(1) + '%;background:' + chColor(k2) + '"></i><i style="width:' + (((x.sessions || 0) - (x.engagedSessions || 0)) / chMax * 100).toFixed(1) + '%;background:' + chColor(k2) + ';opacity:.3"></i></span></div>'; }).join('');
    var src = (r.sources || []).slice(0, 8).map(function (x) { return '<tr><td>' + E(x.sessionSource === '(direct)' ? 'Direct' : x.sessionSource) + '<small>' + E(x.sessionMedium === '(none)' ? '' : x.sessionMedium) + '</small></td><td class="tnum">' + num(x.sessions) + '</td><td class="tnum hm">' + pct((x.engagedSessions || 0) / (x.sessions || 1)) + '</td></tr>'; }).join('');
    var camps = (r.campaigns || []).filter(function (x) { return !/^\(/.test(x.sessionCampaignName || ''); }).slice(0, 6).map(function (x) { return '<tr><td>' + E(x.sessionCampaignName) + '<small>' + E(x.sessionSource) + '</small></td><td class="tnum">' + num(x.sessions) + '</td><td class="tnum hm">' + pct((x.engagedSessions || 0) / (x.sessions || 1)) + '</td></tr>'; }).join('');
    var whereCard = '<div class="card"><h3>Where visitors come from</h3><div class="rows">' + (chH || '<div class="empty">No visits in this range.</div>') + '</div>' +
      (src ? '<h3 style="margin-top:18px;font-size:16px">Top sources</h3><table class="t"><thead><tr><th>Source</th><th>Visits</th><th class="hm">Engaged</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + src + '</tbody></table>' : '') +
      '<h3 style="margin-top:18px;font-size:16px">Campaign links</h3>' + (camps ? '<table class="t"><thead><tr><th>Campaign</th><th>Visits</th><th class="hm">Engaged</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + camps + '</tbody></table>' : '<div class="empty">No tagged links brought visits yet. A Studio short link adds the campaign for you, so each post, email or poster shows up here by name. <a data-w225go="#studio/links" style="cursor:pointer;color:' + ROSE + ';font-weight:700">Make a short link</a></div>') + '</div>';
    /* what brings them in */
    var lpH = (r.landing || []).slice(0, 10).map(function (x) { var rate = (x.engagedSessions || 0) / (x.sessions || 1), t = (x.engagedSessions ? (x.userEngagementDuration || 0) / (x.totalUsers || 1) : 0); return '<tr><td>' + E(pageName(x.landingPage)) + (rate < 0.15 && (x.sessions || 0) >= 10 ? '<span class="tag bad">people leave</span>' : rate >= 0.6 && (x.sessions || 0) >= 5 ? '<span class="tag good">holds attention</span>' : '') + '<small>' + E(x.landingPage) + '</small></td><td class="tnum">' + num(x.sessions) + '</td><td class="tnum">' + pct(rate) + '</td><td class="tnum hm">' + dur(t) + '</td></tr>'; }).join('');
    var lpCard = '<div class="card"><h3>The pages people arrive on</h3>' + (lpH ? '<table class="t"><thead><tr><th>Page</th><th>Visits</th><th>Engaged</th><th class="hm">Time</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + lpH + '</tbody></table>' : '<div class="empty">No visits in this range.</div>') + '</div>';
    /* what they do */
    var opened = ev('signup_open'), formsSent = ev('form_sent'), gives = ev('donate_click'), news = ev('newsletter_signup');
    var fMax = Math.max(sessions, 1);
    var fs = function (label, n, sub) { return '<div class="fs"><span>' + E(label) + (sub ? '<small class="muted" style="display:block;font-size:11.5px">' + E(sub) + '</small>' : '') + '</span><span class="tr"><i style="width:' + Math.max(n ? 1.5 : 0, n / fMax * 100).toFixed(1) + '%"></i></span><b class="tnum">' + num(n) + '</b></div>'; };
    var funnel = '<div class="funnel">' + fs('Visits', sessions) + fs('Engaged', engaged, pct(engaged / (sessions || 1)) + ' of visits') + fs('Clicked Give or a sign-up', gives + opened) + fs('Sent a form or signed up', formsSent + news) + '</div>';
    var donH = (r.donate || []).sort(function (a, b) { return (b.clicks || 0) - (a.clicks || 0); }).map(function (x) { return '<tr><td>' + E(x.campaign === 'general' ? 'Donate (general)' : x.campaign === 'gift-basket-drive' ? 'Holiday Gift Basket Drive' : x.campaign) + '</td><td class="tnum">' + num(x.clicks) + '</td><td class="tnum hm">' + (x.value ? money(x.value) : '–') + '</td></tr>'; }).join('');
    var FN = { volunteer: 'Volunteer', benevole: 'Bénévole', 'benevole,volunteer': 'Volunteer', contact: 'Contact', join: 'Join our team', group: 'Group volunteering', groupe: 'Groupe', 'infolettre,newsletter': 'Newsletter', 'gift-drive-give': 'Drive: give', 'gift-drive-group': 'Drive: group', 'gift-drive-sponsor': 'Drive: sponsor', 'gift-drive-volunteer': 'Drive: volunteer' };
    var forms = {}; (r.forms || []).forEach(function (f) { var key = f.form || 'other'; forms[key] = forms[key] || { open: 0, submit: 0, sent: 0, news: 0 }; if (f.event === 'signup_open') forms[key].open += f.count; else if (f.event === 'form_submit') forms[key].submit += f.count; else if (f.event === 'form_sent') forms[key].sent += f.count; else if (f.event === 'newsletter_signup') forms[key].news += f.count; });
    var formH = Object.keys(forms).sort(function (a, b) { return (forms[b].sent + forms[b].news) - (forms[a].sent + forms[a].news); }).map(function (key) { var f = forms[key], s = f.sent + f.news; return '<tr><td>' + E(FN[key] || key.replace(/^\/|\/$/g, '') || 'Other') + '</td><td class="tnum hm">' + num(f.open + f.submit) + '</td><td class="tnum">' + num(s) + '</td></tr>'; }).join('');
    var doCard = '<div class="card"><h3>What visitors do</h3>' + funnel +
      '<h3 style="margin-top:18px;font-size:16px">Give clicks by campaign</h3>' + (donH ? '<table class="t"><thead><tr><th>Campaign</th><th>Clicks</th><th class="hm">Amounts picked</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + donH + '</tbody></table>' : '<div class="empty">' + (before ? 'Counted from November 10.' : 'No Give clicks yet in this range.') + ' Gifts themselves are counted in the Fundraising app from Stripe.</div>') +
      '<h3 style="margin-top:18px;font-size:16px">Forms</h3>' + (formH ? '<table class="t"><thead><tr><th>Form</th><th class="hm">Started</th><th>Sent</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + formH + '</tbody></table>' : '<div class="empty">No forms sent yet in this range.</div>') + '</div>';
    /* who */
    var devTot = (r.devices || []).reduce(function (s, x) { return s + (x.totalUsers || 0); }, 0) || 1;
    var devH = (r.devices || []).sort(function (a, b) { return (b.totalUsers || 0) - (a.totalUsers || 0); }).map(function (x) { var nm = x.deviceCategory === 'mobile' ? 'Phone' : x.deviceCategory === 'desktop' ? 'Computer' : x.deviceCategory === 'tablet' ? 'Tablet' : x.deviceCategory; return '<div class="rw"><span class="nm">' + E(nm) + ' <span class="muted sm">' + pct(x.engagementRate || 0) + ' engaged</span></span><span class="vl tnum">' + pct((x.totalUsers || 0) / devTot) + '</span><span class="tr"><i style="width:' + ((x.totalUsers || 0) / devTot * 100).toFixed(1) + '%;background:' + GREEN + '"></i></span></div>'; }).join('');
    var nv = {}; (r.returning || []).forEach(function (x) { nv[x.newVsReturning] = x.totalUsers || 0; });
    var retH = (nv['new'] || nv.returning) ? '<p class="sm" style="margin:12px 0 0"><b class="tnum">' + num(nv.returning || 0) + '</b> returning and <b class="tnum">' + num(nv['new'] || 0) + '</b> new visitors.</p>' : '';
    var placeH = (r.places || []).slice(0, 8).map(function (x) { var b = bots.indexOf(x) >= 0; return '<div class="rw"><span class="nm">' + E(x.city) + ' <span class="muted sm">' + E(x.country) + '</span>' + (b ? '<span class="tag info">automated</span>' : '') + '</span><span class="vl tnum">' + num(x.totalUsers) + '</span></div>'; }).join('');
    var whoCard = '<div class="card"><h3>Who visits</h3><div class="rows">' + devH + '</div>' + retH + (placeH ? '<h3 style="margin-top:18px;font-size:16px">Where they are</h3><div class="rows">' + placeH + '</div>' : '') + '</div>';
    var popH = (r.pages || []).slice(0, 8).map(function (x) { return '<tr><td>' + E(pageName(x.pagePath)) + '<small>' + E(clip(x.pageTitle, 60)) + '</small></td><td class="tnum">' + num(x.screenPageViews) + '</td><td class="tnum hm">' + dur((x.userEngagementDuration || 0) / (x.screenPageViews || 1)) + '</td></tr>'; }).join('');
    var popCard = popH ? '<div class="card"><h3>Most viewed pages</h3><table class="t"><thead><tr><th>Page</th><th>Views</th><th class="hm">Per view</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + popH + '</tbody></table></div>' : '';
    /* what people click, how far they read, the parts they see, the paths they take (analytics.js since 10/11/26) */
    var AREA = { menu: 'Menu', footer: 'Footer', popup: 'Pop-up form', page: 'Page' };
    var areaName = function (a) { return AREA[a] || (a ? a.replace(/-/g, ' ') : 'Page'); };
    var EVN = { donate_click: 'Give', signup_open: 'Sign-up', contact_click: 'Contact', language_switch: 'Language', link_click: 'Link', button_click: 'Button' };
    var clicks = (r.clicks || []).slice(0, 12);
    var clickH = clicks.map(function (x) { return '<tr><td>' + E(x.text || '(no words)') + '<span class="tag info">' + E(EVN[x.event] || x.event) + '</span><small>' + E(areaName(x.area)) + ' · ' + E(pageName(x.page)) + '</small></td><td class="tnum">' + num(x.count) + '</td><td class="tnum hm">' + num(x.people) + '</td></tr>'; }).join('');
    var areaTot = {}; (r.clicks || []).forEach(function (x) { var a = x.area === 'menu' || x.area === 'footer' || x.area === 'popup' ? x.area : 'page'; areaTot[a] = (areaTot[a] || 0) + (x.count || 0); });
    var areaSum = Object.keys(areaTot).reduce(function (s2, k2) { return s2 + areaTot[k2]; }, 0);
    var areaBar = areaSum ? '<div class="rw" style="margin:0 0 12px"><span class="nm muted sm">Where clicks happen</span><span class="vl"></span><span class="tr">' + ['page', 'menu', 'popup', 'footer'].filter(function (k2) { return areaTot[k2]; }).map(function (k2, i2) { return '<i title="' + E(areaName(k2)) + ' ' + pct(areaTot[k2] / areaSum) + '" style="width:' + (areaTot[k2] / areaSum * 100).toFixed(1) + '%;background:' + [GREEN, ROSE, GOLD, PURPLE][i2] + '"></i>'; }).join('') + '</span></div><div class="legend" style="margin:-6px 0 12px">' + ['page', 'menu', 'popup', 'footer'].filter(function (k2) { return areaTot[k2]; }).map(function (k2, i2) { return '<span><i style="background:' + [GREEN, ROSE, GOLD, PURPLE][i2] + '"></i>' + E(areaName(k2)) + ' ' + pct(areaTot[k2] / areaSum) + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '';
    var clickCard = '<div class="card"><h3>What people click</h3>' + (clickH ? areaBar + '<table class="t"><thead><tr><th>Clicked</th><th>Clicks</th><th class="hm">People</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + clickH + '</tbody></table>' : '<div class="empty">Every link and button on the site is counted from November 10, with where it sits on the page. The first clicks show here within a day.</div>') + '</div>';
    var pp = {}; (r.page_people || []).forEach(function (x) { pp[x.page] = x.people; });
    var depthBy = {}; (r.scroll || []).forEach(function (x) { (depthBy[x.page] = depthBy[x.page] || {})[x.pct] = x.people; });
    var depthRows = Object.keys(depthBy).map(function (pg) { var d0 = depthBy[pg], base = Math.max(pp[pg] || 0, d0[25] || 0, 1); return { pg: pg, base: base, d: [25, 50, 75, 100].map(function (m) { return Math.min(1, (d0[m] || 0) / base); }) }; }).filter(function (x) { return x.base >= 3; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.base - a.base; }).slice(0, 8);
    var secBy = {}; (r.sections || []).forEach(function (x) { (secBy[x.page] = secBy[x.page] || []).push(x); });
    var depthH = depthRows.map(function (x) {
      var half = x.d[1], end = x.d[3];
      var secs = (secBy[x.pg] || []).sort(function (a, b) { return (b.people || 0) - (a.people || 0); });
      var lost = secs.filter(function (s3) { return (s3.people || 0) / x.base < 0.25; }).slice(-2);
      return '<div style="padding:10px 0;border-top:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.08)"><div class="rw"><span class="nm"><b style="font-weight:600">' + E(pageName(x.pg)) + '</b> <span class="muted sm">' + num(x.base) + ' people</span>' + (half < 0.35 ? '<span class="tag bad">most stop early</span>' : end >= 0.5 ? '<span class="tag good">read to the end</span>' : '') + '</span><span class="vl"></span></div>' +
        '<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:4px;margin-top:6px">' + x.d.map(function (v, i3) { return '<div title="' + [25, 50, 75, 100][i3] + '% down the page: ' + pct(v) + '" style="height:26px;border-radius:6px;background:rgba(42,140,122,' + (0.08 + v * 0.8).toFixed(2) + ');display:grid;place-items:center;font:600 11px/1 inherit;color:' + (v > 0.55 ? '#FFFAF2' : GREEN) + '">' + pct(v) + '</div>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
        (lost.length ? '<small class="muted" style="display:block;margin-top:6px">Few people reach: ' + lost.map(function (s3) { return E(s3.section.replace(/-/g, ' ')); }).join(', ') + '</small>' : '') + '</div>';
    }).join('');
    var depthCard = '<div class="card"><h3>How far people read<span class="sp"></span><small>25 · 50 · 75 · 100% down the page</small></h3>' + (depthH || '<div class="empty">Scroll depth and the page sections people see are counted from November 10.</div>') + '</div>';
    var paths = (r.paths || []).slice(0, 10), pMax = Math.max.apply(null, paths.map(function (x) { return x.views || 0; }).concat([1]));
    var fromHome = (r.paths || []).filter(function (x) { return x.from === '/' || x.from === '/fr/'; }).slice(0, 5);
    var pathH = paths.map(function (x) { return '<div class="rw"><span class="nm">' + E(pageName(x.from)) + ' <span class="muted">→</span> <b style="font-weight:600">' + E(pageName(x.to)) + '</b></span><span class="vl tnum">' + num(x.views) + '</span><span class="tr"><i style="width:' + ((x.views || 0) / pMax * 100).toFixed(1) + '%;background:' + PURPLE + '"></i></span></div>'; }).join('');
    var pathCard = '<div class="card"><h3>Paths through the site</h3>' + (pathH ? '<div class="rows">' + pathH + '</div>' + (fromHome.length ? '<p class="sm" style="margin:12px 0 0"><b>From Home, people go to</b> ' + fromHome.map(function (x) { return E(pageName(x.to)) + ' <span class="muted tnum">(' + num(x.views) + ')</span>'; }).join(', ') + '.</p>' : '') : '<div class="empty">The moves from one page to the next show here as people use the new site (the old one was a single page app, so it never recorded them).</div>') + '</div>';
    var gapNote = noCompare ? '<p class="note">The new site had no tracking from October 2 to November 9 (fixed November 10), so ' + (gapCur ? 'this range is missing those weeks' : 'the period before has almost no data') + ' and no comparison is shown. ' + (WEB.range < 365 ? 'Pick 12 months to see the old site\u2019s year, or come back as the new numbers build up.' : '') + '</p>' : '';
    var note = gapNote + ((r.errors && r.errors.length) ? '<p class="note">Some numbers are not available yet from Google Analytics (new event details can take a day to appear).</p>' : '');
    var made = '<p class="muted sm" style="margin:4px 0 0">From Google Analytics, ' + (r.cached ? 'read ' : 'read just now') + (r.cached ? E(agoShort(r.made_at)) : '') + '. Visitors are counted only when they accept cookies on the site.</p>';
    return '<div class="w306">' + top + note + heroes + '<div class="grid"><div>' + saysCard + trend + whereCard + lpCard + pathCard + '</div><div>' + doCard + clickCard + depthCard + whoCard + popCard + '</div></div>' + made + '</div>';
  }
  function agoShort(iso) { var m = Math.round((Date.now() - new Date(iso).getTime()) / 60000); return m < 1 ? 'just now' : m < 60 ? m + ' min ago' : Math.round(m / 60) + ' h ago'; }

  /* ---------- clicks ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var el = t.closest('[data-w306range],[data-w306fresh]'); if (!el) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    if (el.hasAttribute('data-w306range')) { var v = Number(el.getAttribute('data-w306range')); if (v && v !== WEB.range) { WEB.range = v; try { localStorage.setItem('w306range', String(v)); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } repaint('web'); } return; }
    if (el.hasAttribute('data-w306fresh')) { if (WEB.busy[String(WEB.range)]) return; WEB.err[String(WEB.range)] = null; delete WEB.data[String(WEB.range)]; WEB.liveAt = 0; loadWeb(WEB.range, true); repaint('web'); }
  });
  document.addEventListener('toggle', function (e) { if (e.target && e.target.hasAttribute && e.target.hasAttribute('data-w306older')) T.olderOpen = e.target.open; }, true);

  /* ---------- the tabs follow the address; Studio pages outside wave225 (the calendar, the analytics) get them too ---------- */
  function onHash() { var a = areaOf(hashNow()); if (!a) { var o = document.getElementById('w306tabs'); if (o) o.remove(); return; } tabs(); setTimeout(tabs, 120); setTimeout(tabs, 600); }
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', onHash);
  if (document.readyState !== 'loading') setTimeout(onHash, 0); else document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', onHash);
  /* a repaint of the calendar or the analytics can drop the row: put it back, cheaply */
  setInterval(function () { var a = areaOf(hashNow()); if (a && a.area.tabs.length && !document.getElementById('w306tabs')) tabs(); }, 1500);

  window.w306 = { today: today, web: web, skeleton: skeleton, tabs: tabs, entered: entered, areaOf: areaOf, railAlso: railAlso, AREAS: AREAS, loadWeb: loadWeb, state: function () { return { WEB: WEB, T: T }; } };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v277 the Connections page (02/11/26)
   =====================================================================
   One list of every account the Hub posts to, read from the platform registry (studio_platforms, through social-connect
   status): a row per account in registry order (Instagram and Facebook share the Meta row), a status pill from the
   connection's health, the capability chips, and one action. Pressing a row opens its disclosure: who connected it and
   when, when it expires, scopes asked against granted, the console, the redirect address, the keys, and for LinkedIn the
   Make hook under Advanced. An attention strip lists what expires or fails. The Hub modal replaces confirm and prompt.

   wave225 keeps the page, the route and the state. It hands over through three hooks: its paint asks
   window.w277.connectHtml for the connections section, its connAct passes every action to window.w277.connAct, and its
   loadConn keeps the registry in S.platforms. wave277 reads that state through window.w225.state() and repaints with
   window.w225.paint(). Test: test/w277drive.js with test/fx_w277.json.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa277) return; window.__wa277 = true;
  var HEAD = "'Barlow Condensed','Arial Narrow','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  var BODY = "'Space Grotesk','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";

  var DAY = 86400000;
  var LINE_TEXT = 'Every account the Hub posts to, and what each one can carry.';
  var META = ['instagram', 'facebook'];

  /* wave277's own state: which row is open, which actions are running, the last pill text per row */
  var S277 = { open: null, busy: {}, pills: {}, held: null };

  /* ---------- small helpers (copied, not imported: wave225 keeps its own closure) ---------- */
  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function toastS(m) { try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(m); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function dateOf(iso) { if (!iso) return ''; try { return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return String(iso).slice(0, 10); } }
  function ago(iso) { if (!iso) return ''; var m = Math.round((Date.now() - new Date(iso).getTime()) / 60000); if (m < 1) return 'just now'; if (m < 60) return m + ' min ago'; var h = Math.round(m / 60); if (h < 24) return h + ' h ago'; var d = Math.round(h / 24); return d === 1 ? 'yesterday' : d + ' days ago'; }
  function who(email) {
    try { var m = ((typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' && DB_MEMBERS) || []).filter(function (x) { return x && x.email && String(x.email).toLowerCase() === String(email || '').toLowerCase(); })[0]; if (m && m.name) return m.name; } catch (e) { /* below */ }
    return String(email || '').split('@')[0].replace(/\./g, ' ').replace(/\b\w/g, function (c) { return c.toUpperCase(); }); }
  function fn(name, body) {
    var call = typeof window.__WA_INVOKE === 'function' ? Promise.resolve().then(function () { return window.__WA_INVOKE(name, { body: body }); }) : db.functions.invoke(name, { body: body });
    return Promise.resolve(call).then(function (r) {
      r = r || {};
      if (r.error) { var m = r.error.message || String(r.error); try { if (r.error.context && r.error.context.json) return r.error.context.json().then(function (j) { throw new Error((j && j.error) || m); }); } catch (x) { /* below */ } throw new Error(m); }
      if (r.data && r.data.error) throw new Error(r.data.error);
      return r.data;
    });
  }

  /* ---------- wave225's state, through its test hook ---------- */
  function w() { return window.w225 || null; }
  function St() { var x = w(); return x && x.state ? x.state() : null; }
  function paint() { var x = w(); if (x && x.paint) x.paint(); }
  function W121() { var x = w(); return x && x.W ? x.W() : (window.w121 || null); }

  async function loadCap() { var c = W121(); if (c && c.loadCap) { try { await c.loadCap(); } catch (e) { /* the page still reads */ } } }
  function conn() { var S = St(); return (S && S.conn) || null; }

  function canChange() { var c = conn(); return !!(c && c.can_change); }
  function canMeta() { var c = conn(); return !!(c && c.can_meta); }
  /* without the registry (status answered no platforms): wave225's own list, no chips, no steps */
  function fallbackPlatforms() {
    var x = w(), PLX = (x && x.PLX) || {}, ORD = (x && x.PORDER) || [];
    return ORD.filter(function (k) { return PLX[k]; }).map(function (k, i) {
      var meta = META.indexOf(k) >= 0, modes = { direct: 'Posts directly', reminder: 'Reminder to post by hand' };
      if (k === 'linkedin') modes.make = 'Posts through Make'; if (k === 'tiktok') modes.drafts = 'Lands in TikTok drafts';
      return { key: k, label: PLX[k][0], short: PLX[k][1], colour: PLX[k][2], sort: i + 1, auth: meta ? 'meta_token' : 'oauth', caps: {}, scopes: [], steps: [], modes: modes, active: true };
    });
  }
  function noRegistry() { var S = St(), c = conn(); return !!c && !c.error && !((S && S.platforms && S.platforms.length) || (c.platforms && c.platforms.length)); }
  function platforms() {
    var S = St(), list = (S && S.platforms && S.platforms.length ? S.platforms : (conn() && conn().platforms)) || [];
    if (!list.length && noRegistry()) list = fallbackPlatforms();
    return list.filter(function (p) { return p && p.key && p.active !== false; }).slice().sort(function (a, b) { return (a.sort || 0) - (b.sort || 0); });
  }
  function platOf(k) { return platforms().filter(function (p) { return p.key === k; })[0] || null; }
  function labelOf(k) { var p = platOf(k); return p ? p.label : k === 'meta' ? 'Meta' : k === 'make' ? 'the Make route' : k; }

  /* ---------- health and the pill ---------- */
  function daysTo(iso) { return Math.ceil((new Date(iso).getTime() - Date.now()) / DAY); }
  function healthOf(c) {
    if (!c || c.status !== 'connected') return 'not_connected';
    var h = c.health && c.health !== 'not_connected' ? c.health : 'ok';
    if (c.expires_at && daysTo(c.expires_at) <= 0) return 'expired';
    return h;
  }
  function expiresText(c) { var n = Math.max(1, daysTo(c.expires_at)); return 'Expires in ' + n + (n === 1 ? ' day' : ' days'); }
  function metaState() {
    var c = connOf('meta'), cx = metaCap(), missing = c.missing || cx.missing || [];
    if (c.token === false || (c.token == null && cx.token === false)) return 'none';
    return missing.length ? 'missing' : 'ok';
  }
  /* [text, tone]: tone is ok, warn, flag or quiet */
  function pillOf(k) {
    if (k === 'meta') { var m = metaState(); return m === 'none' ? ['Not connected', 'quiet'] : m === 'missing' ? ['Needs attention', 'flag'] : ['Connected', 'ok']; }
    var c = connOf(k), h = healthOf(c), p = platOf(k) || {};
    if (h === 'expired') return ['Expired', 'flag'];
    if (h === 'expiring') return [c.expires_at ? expiresText(c) : 'Expires soon', 'warn'];
    if (h === 'error') return ['Needs attention', 'flag'];
    if (c.mode === 'drafts' && (p.modes || {}).drafts) return ['To ' + p.label + ' drafts', 'ok'];
    if (c.mode === 'make' && (p.modes || {}).make) return ['Through Make', 'ok'];
    return h === 'not_connected' ? ['Not connected', 'quiet'] : ['Connected', 'ok'];
  }
  /* scopes the registry asks for against the ones the platform granted */
  function scopesOf(k) {
    var p = platOf(k), c = connOf(k); if (!p || c.status !== 'connected') return null;
    var asked = p.scopes || []; if (!asked.length) return null;
    if (!Array.isArray(c.scopes_granted)) return null;   /* not reported: nothing to compare */
    var got = c.scopes_granted;
    return { n: asked.length, m: asked.filter(function (s) { return got.indexOf(s) >= 0; }).length };
  }
  function attention() {
    return platforms().filter(function (p) { return p.auth === 'oauth'; }).map(function (p) {
      var c = connOf(p.key), h = healthOf(c);
      if (h === 'expiring') return { k: p.key, label: p.label, why: c.expires_at ? expiresText(c) : 'Expires soon', act: 'reconnect', btn: 'Reconnect' };
      if (h === 'expired') return { k: p.key, label: p.label, why: 'Expired', act: 'reconnect', btn: 'Reconnect' };
      if (h === 'error') return { k: p.key, label: p.label, why: c.last_error || 'The last check failed', act: 'health', btn: 'Check again' };
      return null;
    }).filter(Boolean);
  }

  function btn(act, k, text, cls, extra) {
    var busy = S277.busy[k + ':' + act];
    return '<button type="button" class="w277btn' + (cls ? ' ' + cls : '') + '" data-w277act="' + act + '" data-w277k="' + E(k) + '"' + (extra || '') + (busy ? ' disabled aria-busy="true"' : '') + '>' + E(text) + '</button>';
  }
  function chipsOf(caps) {
    caps = caps || {}; var out = [];
    if (caps.images && caps.max_images > 0) out.push('Photos ' + caps.max_images);
    if (caps.video) out.push('Video');
    if (caps.comments) out.push('Comments');
    if (caps.analytics) out.push('Analytics');
    return out.map(function (x) { return '<span>' + E(x) + '</span>'; }).join('');
  }
  function list(xs, cls) { return (xs || []).map(function (x) { return '<span' + (cls ? ' class="' + cls + '"' : '') + '>' + E(x) + '</span>'; }).join(''); }
  function pillHtml(k) {
    var p = pillOf(k), prev = S277.pills[k], fresh = prev && prev !== p[0];
    S277.pills[k] = p[0];
    return '<span class="w277pill ' + p[1] + (fresh ? ' fresh' : '') + '" data-w277pill="' + E(k) + '">' + E(p[0]) + '</span>';
  }

  /* the row's one action: Connect, Reconnect, or the chevron that says it opens */
  function actionOf(k) {
    if (k === 'meta') return canMeta() && metaState() === 'none' ? btn('connect', 'meta', 'Connect', 'gold') : CHEV;
    if (!canChange()) return CHEV;
    var c = connOf(k), h = healthOf(c), sc = scopesOf(k);
    if (h === 'not_connected') return btn('connect', k, 'Connect', 'gold');
    if (h === 'expiring' || h === 'expired' || h === 'error' || (sc && sc.m < sc.n)) return btn('reconnect', k, 'Reconnect', 'gold');
    return CHEV;
  }
  function rowHtml(k, marks, name, sub, caps) {
    var open = S277.open === k, act = actionOf(k), quiet = act === CHEV;
    return '<div class="w277item" role="listitem">'
      + '<div class="w277row' + (quiet ? ' quiet' : '') + '" data-w277row="' + E(k) + '" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-expanded="' + open + '" aria-controls="w277d-' + E(k) + '">'
      + '<span class="w277mk">' + marks + '</span>'
      + '<span class="w277nm"><b>' + E(name) + '</b>' + (sub ? '<small>' + E(sub) + '</small>' : '') + '</span>'
      + '<span class="w277pl">' + pillHtml(k) + '</span>'
      + '<span class="w277ch">' + chipsOf(caps) + '</span>'
      + '<span class="w277act">' + act + '</span></div>'
      + '<div class="w277disc' + (open ? ' open' : '') + '" id="w277d-' + E(k) + '" data-w277disc="' + E(k) + '"><div class="w277in"' + (open ? '' : ' inert') + '><div class="w277body">' + (k === 'meta' ? metaBody() : oauthBody(k)) + '</div></div></div></div>';
  }

  /* the Meta row's disclosure: the system user token and its permissions (the words of wave225's Meta card) */
  function metaBody() {
    var c = connOf('meta'), cx = metaCap(), S = St() || { keys: {}, busy: {} }, granted = c.granted || cx.granted || [], missing = c.missing || cx.missing || [];
    var h = '<p class="muted">Instagram and Facebook, through the Meta system user token.</p>';
    if (metaState() === 'none') h += '<p class="flag">No Meta token in the vault yet.</p>';
    if (granted.length || missing.length) h += '<div class="w277chips">' + list(granted) + list(missing, 'miss') + '</div>';
    if (missing.length) h += '<p class="flag">Missing: ' + E(missing.join(', ')) + '. Comments' + (missing.some(function (x) { return /publish|manage_posts/.test(x); }) ? ', posting' : '') + ' and some formats wait for a token with these. Section 2 of the setup doc says how to make it.</p>';
    else if (granted.length) h += '<p class="muted">Every permission the Studio uses is there.</p>';
    if (c.replaced_at) h += '<p class="muted">Replaced ' + E(ago(c.replaced_at)) + ' by ' + E(who(c.replaced_by)) + '.</p>';
    if (canMeta()) {
      h += '<div class="w277keys"><input type="password" id="w277metatok" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" aria-label="The new system user token" placeholder="Paste the new system user token">'
        + btn('meta_check', 'meta', 'Check it', 'sm', S.busy.meta ? ' disabled' : '') + btn('meta_save', 'meta', 'Replace', 'sm gold', S.busy.meta ? ' disabled' : '') + '</div>'
        + '<p class="muted">It goes straight into the vault. The Hub never shows it again.</p>'
        + (S.keys.metaCheck ? '<div class="w277chips">' + list(S.keys.metaCheck.granted) + list(S.keys.metaCheck.missing, 'miss') + '</div>' : '');
    } else h += '<p class="muted">An admin replaces the token.</p>';
    return h;
  }

  /* an OAuth platform's disclosure */
  function oauthBody(k) {
    var p = platOf(k) || {}, c = connOf(k), h = healthOf(c), sc = scopesOf(k), ch = canChange(), r = redirect(), out = '';
    if (h !== 'not_connected') {
      var facts = [];
      if (c.connected_at) facts.push('Connected ' + (c.connected_by ? 'by ' + who(c.connected_by) + ' ' : '') + 'on ' + dateOf(c.connected_at));
      if (c.expires_at) facts.push((h === 'expired' ? 'Expired on ' : 'Expires on ') + dateOf(c.expires_at));
      if (c.last_checked_at) facts.push('Checked ' + ago(c.last_checked_at));
      if (facts.length) out += '<div class="w277facts">' + facts.map(function (f) { return '<span>' + E(f) + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
    }
    if (c.last_error) out += '<p class="flag">' + E(c.last_error) + '</p>';
    if (sc) out += '<div class="w277line"><span>Asked for ' + sc.n + ', granted ' + sc.m + '</span>' + (sc.m < sc.n ? '<span class="muted">Some features wait for the rest.</span>' + (ch ? btn('reconnect', k, 'Reconnect', 'sm gold') : '') : '') + '</div>';
    if (h === 'not_connected' && (p.steps || []).length) out += '<ol>' + p.steps.map(function (s) { return '<li>' + E(s) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ol>';
    if (p.console_url) out += '<div class="w277line"><a class="w277btn sm" data-w277act="open_console" data-w277k="' + E(k) + '" href="' + E(p.console_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open the console</a>' + (p.console_note ? '<span class="muted">' + E(p.console_note) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>';
    out += '<div class="w277line"><span class="muted">Redirect address</span><code>' + E(r) + '</code>' + btn('copy_redirect', k, 'Copy', 'sm', ' data-w277v="' + E(r) + '"') + '</div>';
    if (k === 'threads') out += ['uninstall', 'delete'].map(function (ev) { var u = r + '?event=' + ev; return '<div class="w277line"><span class="muted">' + (ev === 'uninstall' ? 'Uninstall callback' : 'Delete callback') + '</span><code>' + E(u) + '</code>' + btn('copy_redirect', k, 'Copy', 'sm', ' data-w277v="' + E(u) + '"') + '</div>'; }).join('');
    if (ch) {
      out += (c.app_saved ? '<p class="muted">Keys saved' + (c.app_id ? ' (' + E(c.app_id) + ')' : '') + '. Paste new ones to replace them.</p>' : '')
        + '<div class="w277keys"><input type="text" id="w277id-' + E(k) + '" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" aria-label="' + E(p.label) + ' client ID" placeholder="' + (k === 'tiktok' ? 'Client key' : k === 'threads' ? 'Threads app ID' : 'Client ID') + '">'
        + '<input type="password" id="w277sec-' + E(k) + '" autocomplete="off" aria-label="' + E(p.label) + ' client secret" placeholder="' + (k === 'threads' ? 'Threads app secret' : 'Client secret') + '">'
        + btn('save_keys', k, 'Save keys', 'sm') + '</div>';
      out += '<div class="w277line">' + (h === 'not_connected' ? btn('connect', k, 'Connect', 'gold') : (sc && sc.m < sc.n ? '' : btn('reconnect', k, 'Reconnect', '')) + btn('disconnect', k, 'Disconnect', 'bad'))
        + (k === 'youtube' && h !== 'not_connected' ? btn('test_short', k, 'Send a test Short', '') : '') + '</div>';
    } else out += '<p class="muted">A marketing lead or an admin connects it.</p>';
    if (k === 'linkedin') out += makeBody();
    return out;
  }
  /* LinkedIn, Advanced: the Make hook that posts to the Page until LinkedIn approves the direct connection */
  function makeBody() {
    var m = connOf('make'), ch = canChange();
    var h = '<details class="w277adv"><summary>Advanced</summary><p class="muted">Until LinkedIn approves the direct connection, a Make scenario can post to the Page. Paste its webhook address here.</p>';
    if (ch) {
      h += '<div class="w277keys"><input type="url" id="w277mkhook" autocomplete="off" aria-label="Make webhook address" placeholder="' + (m.saved ? 'Saved. Paste a new address to replace it' : 'The Make webhook address') + '">' + btn('make_hook', 'make', 'Save', 'sm') + '</div>';
      if (m.secret) h += '<div class="w277line"><span class="muted">The filter code</span><code>' + E(m.secret) + '</code>' + btn('copy_redirect', 'make', 'Copy', 'sm', ' data-w277v="' + E(m.secret) + '"') + '</div>';
      if (m.saved) h += '<div class="w277line">' + btn('make_sample', 'make', 'Send Make a sample', 'sm') + btn('make_test', 'make', 'Send a test post', 'sm') + btn('disconnect', 'make', 'Forget it', 'sm bad') + '</div>';
      if (m.sampled_at || m.tested_at) h += '<p class="muted">' + (m.sampled_at ? 'Sample sent ' + E(ago(m.sampled_at)) + '. ' : '') + (m.tested_at ? 'Test post ' + E(ago(m.tested_at)) + (m.tested_ok ? ', Make took it.' : ', Make refused it.') : '') + '</p>';
    } else h += '<p class="muted">' + (m.saved ? 'Set up.' : 'Not set up yet.') + ' A marketing lead or an admin changes it.</p>';
    return h + '</details>';
  }

  function attnHtml() {
    var xs = attention(); if (!xs.length) return '';
    return '<div id="w277attn" role="region" aria-label="Needs attention">' + xs.map(function (x) {
      return '<div class="w277arow"><b>' + E(x.label) + '</b><span class="why">' + E(x.why) + '</span>' + (canChange() ? btn(x.act, x.k, x.btn, 'sm gold') : '') + '</div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function testHtml() {
    var S = St() || { keys: {} }, x = w(), byHand = platforms().filter(function (p) { return x && x.modeOf && x.modeOf(p.key) === 'reminder'; }).map(function (p) { return p.label; });
    return '<div class="w277card"><div class="w277test"><b>Reminders</b><span>' + (byHand.length ? 'By reminder now: ' + E(byHand.join(', ')) + '. ' : 'Every platform posts on its own. ') + 'At the time of the post, its owner gets the caption and the media to post by hand.</span>'
      + btn('remind_preview', 'remind', 'Preview a reminder', 'sm') + '</div>'
      + (S.keys.remind ? '<div class="w277pre">' + E(S.keys.remind) + '</div>' : '') + '</div>';
  }

  function connectHtml() {
    var S = St(); if (!S) return '';
    if (!S.conn) { loadConn().then(paint); return '<div id="w277conn" aria-busy="true"><div class="w277card"><div class="w277empty">Checking the connections.</div></div></div>'; }
    if (S.conn.error) return '<div id="w277conn"><div class="w277card"><div class="w277empty">Could not read the connections: ' + E(S.conn.error) + ' <a data-w277act="reload" data-w277k="" role="button" tabindex="0">Try again</a></div></div></div>';
    var ps = platforms(), meta = ps.filter(function (p) { return META.indexOf(p.key) >= 0; }), rows = [], metaDone = false;
    ps.forEach(function (p) {
      if (META.indexOf(p.key) >= 0) {
        if (metaDone) return; metaDone = true;
        rows.push(rowHtml('meta', meta.map(function (m) { return markOf(m, true); }).join(''), meta.map(function (m) { return m.label; }).join(' and '), 'Meta', p.caps));
      } else {
        var c = connOf(p.key);
        rows.push(rowHtml(p.key, markOf(p), p.label, c.handle ? '@' + String(c.handle).replace(/^@+/, '') : c.name || '', p.caps));
      }
    });
    var fl = S.keys.flash;
    return '<div id="w277conn">'
      + (fl ? '<div class="w277flash' + (fl.ok ? '' : ' bad') + '" role="status">' + E(fl.text) + '</div>' : '')
      + (noRegistry() ? '<p class="w277note" role="status">Platform details are unavailable right now; the accounts below still work.</p>' : '')
      + '<div class="w277bar"><span>Keys and tokens go into the vault and are never shown again.</span>' + btn('reload', '', 'Check again', 'sm') + '</div>'
      + attnHtml()
      + (rows.length ? '<div class="w277card" role="list">' + rows.join('') + '</div>' : '<div class="w277card"><div class="w277empty">No platforms are on yet. <a data-w277act="reload" data-w277k="" role="button" tabindex="0">Check again</a></div></div>')
      + testHtml() + extraHtml() + '</div>';
  }
  /* wave250's Meta live and WhatsApp cards keep their own markup and handlers (data-w250cx, in wave225) */
  function extraHtml() { var x = w(); if (!x || !x.connectExtra) return ''; try { var h = x.connectExtra(); return h ? '<div class="w277extra cx">' + h + '</div>' : ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } }

  /* ---------- ask: one clear sentence and a primary button, in the Hub modal (never confirm or prompt) ---------- */
  function ask(text, okLabel, danger) {
    return new Promise(function (resolve) {
      var done = false, prev = document.activeElement;
      var html = '<div id="w277modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="w277mt"><p id="w277mt">' + E(text) + '</p><div class="w277mb">'
        + '<button type="button" class="w277btn" data-w277cancel>Cancel</button>'
        + '<button type="button" class="w277btn ' + (danger ? 'bad' : 'gold') + '" data-w277ok>' + E(okLabel || 'Go ahead') + '</button></div></div>';
      var hub = typeof openModal === 'function' && typeof closeModal === 'function' && document.getElementById('modalBox') && document.getElementById('modalBack');
      var back, box;
      if (hub) { openModal(html); back = document.getElementById('modalBack'); box = document.getElementById('modalBox'); }
      else { back = document.createElement('div'); back.className = 'w277back'; back.innerHTML = html; document.body.appendChild(back); box = back; }
      function finish(v) {
        if (done) return; done = true;
        box.removeEventListener('click', onClick); back.removeEventListener('click', onBack); document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey, true);
        if (hub) { try { if (document.getElementById('w277modal')) closeModal(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } else if (back.parentNode) back.parentNode.removeChild(back);
        try { if (prev && prev.focus && document.contains(prev)) prev.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
        resolve(v);
      }
      function onClick(e) { var t = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-w277ok],[data-w277cancel]') : null; if (t) { e.preventDefault(); finish(t.hasAttribute('data-w277ok')); } }
      function onBack(e) { if (e.target === back) finish(false); }
      function onKey(e) { if (e.key === 'Escape') { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); finish(false); } }
      box.addEventListener('click', onClick); back.addEventListener('click', onBack); document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey, true);
      setTimeout(function () { var b = document.querySelector(danger ? '#w277modal [data-w277cancel]' : '#w277modal [data-w277ok]'); if (b) try { b.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 0);
    });
  }

  /* ---------- wiring: typed text survives a repaint, the masthead line, the disclosure, the actions ---------- */
  function hold() {
    var root = document.getElementById('w277conn'); if (!root) return;
    var vals = {}, a = document.activeElement, focus = null;
    root.querySelectorAll('input[id^="w277"]').forEach(function (el) { if (el.value) vals[el.id] = el.value; });
    if (a && root.contains(a) && a.id) focus = { id: a.id, s: a.selectionStart, e: a.selectionEnd };
    else if (a && root.contains(a) && a.hasAttribute('data-w277row')) focus = { row: a.getAttribute('data-w277row') };
    S277.held = { vals: vals, focus: focus };
    Promise.resolve().then(restore);
  }
  function restore() {
    var h = S277.held; S277.held = null; if (!h) return;
    Object.keys(h.vals).forEach(function (id) { var el = document.getElementById(id); if (el && !el.value) el.value = h.vals[id]; });
    try {
      var f = h.focus, el = f && (f.id ? document.getElementById(f.id) : document.querySelector('#w277conn [data-w277row="' + f.row + '"]'));
      if (el && document.activeElement !== el) { el.focus(); if (f.id && f.s != null && el.setSelectionRange) el.setSelectionRange(f.s, f.e); }
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function mastLine() {
    var m = document.querySelector('#studio > .w118mast p');
    if (m && m.textContent !== LINE_TEXT) m.textContent = LINE_TEXT;
  }
  function page() { hold(); var html = connectHtml(); Promise.resolve().then(mastLine); return html; }

  /* one disclosure open at a time; the DOM changes in place so the height can ease */
  function setOpen(k) {
    S277.open = k || null;
    document.querySelectorAll('#w277conn [data-w277row]').forEach(function (r) {
      var key = r.getAttribute('data-w277row'), on = key === S277.open, d = document.getElementById('w277d-' + key);
      r.setAttribute('aria-expanded', on ? 'true' : 'false');
      if (d) { d.classList.toggle('open', on); var inn = d.firstElementChild; if (inn) { if (on) inn.removeAttribute('inert'); else inn.setAttribute('inert', ''); } }
    });
  }
  function toggle(k) { setOpen(S277.open === k ? null : k); }
  function focusId(id) { setTimeout(function () { var el = document.getElementById(id); if (el) try { el.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 200); }
  function val(id) { var el = document.getElementById(id); return el ? el.value.trim() : ''; }
  function clear(id) { var el = document.getElementById(id); if (el) el.value = ''; }
  function copy(t) {
    try { if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) { navigator.clipboard.writeText(t).then(function () { toastS('Copied'); }, function () { toastS('Could not copy. Select it and copy by hand.'); }); return; } } catch (e) { /* below */ }
    toastS('Could not copy. Select it and copy by hand.');
  }
  async function refresh() { await loadConn(); await loadCap(); paint(); }

  /* every action, the wave225 names too ('save_app', 'start'), as '<action>|<key>' */
  async function connAct(what) {
    var parts = String(what || '').split('|'), a = parts[0], k = parts[1] || '', S = St(); if (!S) return;
    if (a === 'save_app') a = 'save_keys'; if (a === 'start') a = 'connect';
    var tag = k + ':' + a;
    try {
      if (a === 'toggle') { toggle(k); return; }
      if (a === 'open_console') return;
      if (a === 'copy_redirect') { copy(parts.slice(2).join('|') || redirect()); return; }
      if (a === 'reload') { S.conn = null; S.keys.flash = null; S.keys.metaCheck = null; await loadCap(); paint(); return; }
      if (a === 'health') {
        S277.busy[tag] = true; paint();
        var hr = await fn('social-connect', { action: 'health' });
        if (hr && hr.connections) { var keep = { can_change: S.conn.can_change, can_meta: S.conn.can_meta, redirect_uri: S.conn.redirect_uri }; Object.assign(S.conn, hr, keep); if (hr.platforms && hr.platforms.length) S.platforms = hr.platforms; }
        S277.busy[tag] = false; toastS('Checked again'); paint(); return;
      }
      if (a === 'connect' || a === 'reconnect') {
        if (k === 'meta') { setOpen('meta'); focusId('w277metatok'); return; }
        if (a === 'connect' && !connOf(k).app_saved) { setOpen(k); focusId('w277id-' + k); toastS('Save the ' + labelOf(k) + ' keys first, then Connect'); return; }
        S277.busy[tag] = true; paint();
        var s = await fn('social-connect', { action: 'start', platform: k });
        S277.busy[tag] = false;
        if (s && s.url) { location.href = s.url; return; }
        paint(); return;
      }
      if (a === 'save_keys') {
        var id = val('w277id-' + k), sec = val('w277sec-' + k); if (!id || !sec) { toastS('Paste both the ID and the secret'); return; }
        await fn('social-connect', { action: 'save_app', platform: k, id: id, secret: sec });
        clear('w277id-' + k); clear('w277sec-' + k);
        toastS('Saved in the vault'); await loadConn(); paint(); return;
      }
      if (a === 'disconnect') {
        var ok = await ask(k === 'make' ? 'Forget the Make route? LinkedIn posts go back to reminders until it is set up again.' : 'Disconnect ' + labelOf(k) + '? Posts go back to reminders until it is connected again.', k === 'make' ? 'Forget it' : 'Disconnect', true);
        if (!ok) return;
        await fn('social-connect', { action: 'disconnect', platform: k }); toastS('Disconnected'); await refresh(); return;
      }
      if (a === 'meta_check' || a === 'meta_save') {
        var tok = val('w277metatok'); if (!tok) { toastS('Paste the token first'); return; }
        S.busy.meta = true; paint();
        var r = await fn('social-connect', { action: 'meta_token', token: tok, check_only: a === 'meta_check' });
        S.keys.metaCheck = { granted: r.granted || [], missing: r.missing || [] };
        if (a === 'meta_save') { clear('w277metatok'); toastS('Replaced. ' + (r.missing && r.missing.length ? r.missing.length + ' permissions still missing' : 'Every permission is there')); S.busy.meta = false; await refresh(); return; }
        toastS(r.missing && r.missing.length ? 'Missing ' + r.missing.length + ': ' + r.missing.join(', ') : 'Every permission is there');
        S.busy.meta = false; paint(); return;
      }
      if (a === 'make_hook') {
        var u = val('w277mkhook'); if (!u) { toastS('Paste the webhook address'); return; }
        await fn('social-connect', { action: 'make_hook', url: u }); clear('w277mkhook'); toastS('Saved. Now send Make a sample'); await refresh(); return;
      }
      if (a === 'make_sample') { var m = await fn('social-connect', { action: 'make_sample' }); toastS(m && m.ok ? 'Make got the sample' : 'Make answered ' + (m && m.status) + '. Is the scenario listening?'); await loadConn(); paint(); return; }
      if (a === 'make_test') {
        if (!(await ask('This puts a real post on the Welfare Avenue LinkedIn Page: "Testing the connection between the Welfare Avenue Hub and our LinkedIn Page. Please ignore this post." Send it?', 'Send the test post'))) return;
        var t = await fn('social-connect', { action: 'make_test', confirm: true }); toastS(t && t.ok ? 'Sent. Check the LinkedIn Page in a minute.' : 'Make answered ' + (t && t.status)); await loadConn(); paint(); return;
      }
      if (a === 'test_short') {
        var c121 = W121(), pre = { title: 'Test Short from the Hub', platforms: ['youtube'], caption: 'Testing the connection between the Welfare Avenue Hub and YouTube.', variants: { youtube: { title: 'Test from the Hub', privacy: 'unlisted', kids: false } } };
        toastS('Add a short vertical video, then Publish now. It goes up unlisted.');
        if (c121 && c121.compose) c121.compose(pre); return;
      }
      if (a === 'remind_preview') {
        var x = w(), mode = function (p) { return x && x.modeOf ? x.modeOf(p) : 'reminder'; }, all = x && x.posts ? x.posts() : [];
        var cand = all.filter(function (p) { return ['draft', 'scheduled'].indexOf(p.status) >= 0 && (p.platforms || []).some(function (q) { return mode(q) === 'reminder'; }); }).sort(function (p, q) { return String(p.scheduled_at || '9').localeCompare(String(q.scheduled_at || '9')); })[0];
        if (!cand) { S.keys.remind = 'No planned post goes out by reminder. Plan one for a platform that is not connected to see the message.'; paint(); return; }
        var pl = (cand.platforms || []).filter(function (q) { return mode(q) === 'reminder'; })[0];
        var rr = await fn('social-publish', { action: 'remind_test', post_id: cand.id, platform: pl });
        var plan = (rr && rr.plan) || {}; S.keys.remind = 'To: ' + (plan.to || 'nobody (the post has no owner)') + '\n\n' + String(plan.slack || '').replace(/<([^|>]+)\|([^>]+)>/g, '$2').replace(/\*/g, ''); paint(); return;
      }

  async function loadMe() {
    var em = myEmail(); if (!em) return null;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('team_members').select('id,name,email,avatar_url,public_photo_url').ilike('email', em).eq('active', true).limit(1);

  function square(file, size) {
    return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
      var img = new Image(); var u = URL.createObjectURL(file);
      img.onload = function () {
        try {
          var s = Math.min(img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight), sx = (img.naturalWidth - s) / 2, sy = (img.naturalHeight - s) / 2;
          var c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = size; c.height = size;
          var x = c.getContext('2d'); x.fillStyle = '#FFFAF2'; x.fillRect(0, 0, size, size); x.drawImage(img, sx, sy, s, s, 0, 0, size, size);
          URL.revokeObjectURL(u);
          c.toBlob(function (b) { if (b) resolve(b); else reject(new Error('Could not encode the photo')); }, 'image/jpeg', 0.88);
        } catch (e) { reject(e); }
      };
      img.onerror = function () { URL.revokeObjectURL(u); reject(new Error('That file is not an image the browser can read')); };
      img.src = u;
    });
  }
  function picHtml(url) {
    if (url) return '<div class="pic"><img src="' + E(url) + '" alt=""></div>';
    return '<div class="pic logo"><img src="' + STO.replace('/avatars/', '/content-images/') + 'brand/logo-primary-cream-600.png" alt="Welfare Avenue" onerror="this.style.display=\'none\'"></div>';
  }
  function slotHtml(kind) {

      : 'Shows with your name in the team section of welfareavenue.com. Pick a clear, professional photo. Without one, the website uses your Hub photo, then our logo.';

      + '<label class="choose">Choose a photo<input type="file" accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp" data-w232file="' + kind + '"></label>'
      + (cur ? '<button type="button" class="rm" data-w232rm="' + kind + '">Remove it</button>' : '') + '<p>' + note + '</p></div>';
  }
  async function openPhotos() {

  function msg(t) { var m = document.querySelector('[data-w232msg]'); if (m) m.textContent = t || ''; }
  async function savePhotos(btn) {
    var me = S.me; if (!me) return;
    btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Saving...';
    try {
      var em = lower(me.email);
      for (var i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
        var kind = ['hub', 'website'][i], path = (kind === 'hub' ? 'u/' : 'w/') + em + '.jpg';
        if (S.pend[kind]) {
          var up = await db.storage.from(BUCKET).upload(path, S.pend[kind], { upsert: true, contentType: 'image/jpeg', cacheControl: '3600' });
          if (up && up.error) throw new Error('Upload failed: ' + (up.error.message || 'storage error'));
          var url = STO + path + '?v=' + Date.now();
          var r = await db.rpc('wa232_my_photo_set', { p_kind: kind, p_url: url });
          if (r && r.error) throw new Error(r.error.message || 'Could not save');
          if (kind === 'hub') me.avatar_url = url; else me.public_photo_url = url;
        } else if (S.rm[kind]) {

          if (kind === 'hub') me.avatar_url = ''; else me.public_photo_url = null;

    }
  }
  document.addEventListener('change', async function (e) {
