  /* ---------- the mileage on a reimbursement ---------- */
  async function rate() {
    if (S.rate != null) return S.rate;
    S.rate = 0.73;
    try { var r = await db.from('app_config').select('value').eq('key', 'mileage_rate_cad').maybeSingle(); var v = Number(r && r.data && r.data.value); if (isFinite(v) && v > 0 && v < 5) S.rate = v; } catch (e) { /* the default */ }
    return S.rate;
  }
  function money(n) { return '$' + (Math.round(n * 100) / 100).toFixed(2); }
  function mileHook() {
    var f = document.querySelector('form.w123form[data-w123form="reimbursement"]');
    if (!f || f.querySelector('.w232mile')) return;
    var amt = f.querySelector('input[type=number]:not([data-w232m])'); if (!amt) return;
    var lab = f.querySelector('label[for="' + amt.id + '"]') || amt;
    var box = document.createElement('details'); box.className = 'w232mile';
    box.innerHTML = '<summary>Driving? Work out the mileage</summary><div class="in">'
      + '<div><label>From</label><input type="text" data-w232m="from" placeholder="e.g. the storage unit"></div>'
      + '<div><label>To</label><input type="text" data-w232m="to" placeholder="e.g. Place Emilie-Gamelin"></div>'
      + '<div><label>Kilometres, one way</label><input type="number" min="0" step="0.1" inputmode="decimal" data-w232m="km"></div>'
      + '<div><label>&nbsp;</label><span class="tog"><input type="checkbox" data-w232m="round" checked> Round trip (there and back)</span></div>'
      + '<div class="sum" data-w232m="sum">Enter the kilometres.</div>'
      + '<p class="fine" data-w232m="rate">Rate: the CRA automobile allowance rate.</p></div>';
    lab.parentNode.insertBefore(box, lab);
    rate().then(function (r) { var el = box.querySelector('[data-w232m="rate"]'); if (el) el.textContent = 'Rate: ' + money(r) + ' a km (the CRA automobile allowance rate for 2026, first 5,000 km). It fills Total amount and adds a line to Items and prices; check both before you send.'; });
    box.addEventListener('input', function () { calc(f, box); });
    box.addEventListener('change', function () { calc(f, box); });
  }
  async function calc(f, box) {
    var r = await rate();
    var km = Number((box.querySelector('[data-w232m="km"]') || {}).value);
    var round = !!(box.querySelector('[data-w232m="round"]') || {}).checked;
    var from = ((box.querySelector('[data-w232m="from"]') || {}).value || '').trim(), to = ((box.querySelector('[data-w232m="to"]') || {}).value || '').trim();
    var sum = box.querySelector('[data-w232m="sum"]');
    if (!isFinite(km) || km <= 0) { if (sum) sum.textContent = 'Enter the kilometres.'; return; }
    var dist = Math.round(km * (round ? 2 : 1) * 10) / 10, total = Math.round(dist * r * 100) / 100;
    if (sum) sum.innerHTML = E(dist + ' km x ' + money(r)) + ' = <b>' + E(money(total)) + '</b>';
    var amt = f.querySelector('input[type=number]:not([data-w232m])');
    if (amt) { amt.value = total.toFixed(2); amt.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true })); amt.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true })); }
    var items = f.querySelector('textarea:not([name="__more"])');
    if (items) {
      var line = 'Mileage: ' + (from || to ? (from || '?') + ' to ' + (to || '?') + ', ' : '') + dist + ' km' + (round ? ' (round trip)' : '') + ' x ' + money(r) + ' = ' + money(total);
      var lines = String(items.value || '').split('\n').filter(function (l) { return !/^Mileage: /.test(l); });
      items.value = [line].concat(lines.filter(function (l) { return l.trim() !== ''; })).join('\n');
      items.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
    }
  }

  window.w232 = { openPhotos: openPhotos, rate: rate, state: function () { return { me: S.me, rate: S.rate }; } };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v234 Volunteer spots on each serving card, and one Community lead (Steel, 14/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel, 14/10/26: "I'd like to have a bar that shows how we are filling up the total amount of roles for volunteers for
   the event", and "Community Lead position and Community Talker is the same position - please combine the 2 and keep the
   name Community Lead" (one spot a serving, assigned by staff, not picked on the website).
   - Each upcoming serving on the Volunteer Manager's calendar shows its volunteer spots: a bar in two parts (confirmed in
     teal, signed up but not confirmed yet in gold) over every spot the serving needs, with "N of M volunteer spots filled"
     and how many wait to confirm. The spots are the serving page's: volunteer_role_guides.target per role, the serving's
     own spot counts (volunteer_events.spot_counts, wave232) first, and a partner's share as wave191o counts it (what they
     cover is filled by them; when they make the food no cooks are needed, when they bring it ready one driver). A role
     counts at most its spots, so ten servers never fill the drivers' spots. The bar's tooltip lists what is still open.
   - Community welcome (community_talker) is Community lead (community_debrief) everywhere: the pickers and labels in waves
     020, 119, 119b, 152, 171, 191, 209 and 218 changed in their sources; the database folds the old key into the new one
     (migration wa234_community_lead). Here a stray old key still counts as Community lead.
   Wrapped: waVolRenderCalendar (paints after the calendar, like 119 and 171). Reads event_roster, volunteer_role_guides,
   volunteer_events (spot_counts) and volunteer_event_partners for the upcoming servings only (staff RLS). ASCII only.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa234) return; window.__wa234 = true;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', GOLD = '#DCA527', TEAL = '#2A8C7A', MUTED = '#797773';
  var S = { key: '', at: 0, data: null, busy: null, labels: {} };
  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function today() { try { if (typeof waVolToday === 'function') return waVolToday(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); }
  function roleOf(r) { var k = lower(r) || 'volunteer'; return k === 'community_talker' ? 'community_debrief' : k; }
  function isOut(r) { var s = lower(r.status); return s === 'cancelled' || s === 'unavailable' || r.confirm_status === 'declined'; }
  function isConf(r) { return lower(r.status) === 'confirmed' || r.confirm_status === 'confirmed'; }
  var NAMES = { team_leader: ['team leader', 'team leaders'], cook: ['cook', 'cooks'], driver: ['driver', 'drivers'], server: ['server', 'servers'],
    dishwasher: ['dishwasher', 'dishwashers'], photographer: ['photographer', 'photographers'], videographer: ['videographer', 'videographers'], community_debrief: ['community lead', 'community leads'] };
  function nameOf(k, n) { var x = NAMES[k]; if (x) return n === 1 ? x[0] : x[1]; var l = lower(S.labels[k] || k.replace(/_/g, ' ')); return n === 1 ? l : l + 's'; }
  var ORDER = ['team_leader', 'cook', 'driver', 'server', 'dishwasher', 'photographer', 'videographer', 'community_debrief'];

  var css = document.createElement('style'); css.id = 'wa234css';
  css.textContent =
    '#volunteering .vl-ev .w234{margin:8px 0 6px}' +
    '#volunteering .vl-ev .w234 .tr{height:8px;border-radius:999px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.10);display:flex;overflow:hidden}' +
    '#volunteering .vl-ev .w234 .tr i{display:block;height:100%}' +
    '#volunteering .vl-ev .w234 .tr i.c{background:' + TEAL + '}' +
    '#volunteering .vl-ev .w234 .tr i.w{background:' + GOLD + '}' +
    '#volunteering .vl-ev .w234 .t{font-size:12.5px;color:' + GREEN + ';margin-top:5px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.35}' +
    '#volunteering .vl-ev .w234 .t span{color:' + MUTED + ';font-weight:500}' +
    '#volunteering .vl-ev .w234.full .t{color:#1c6b5d}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- the spots of one serving ---------- */
  function spotsOf(evId, D) {
    var t = {};
    (D.guides || []).forEach(function (g) { var k = roleOf(g.role); if (g.target != null && Number(g.target) > 0 && k !== 'volunteer') t[k] = Math.max(t[k] || 0, Number(g.target)); });
    if (!Object.keys(t).length) t = { team_leader: 1, cook: 4, driver: 2, server: 8, dishwasher: 1, photographer: 1, community_debrief: 1 };
    var sc = D.spots[evId];
    if (sc && typeof sc === 'object') Object.keys(sc).forEach(function (k) { var n = Number(sc[k]); if (isFinite(n) && n >= 0) t[roleOf(k)] = Math.round(n); });
    var P = D.partners[evId];
    if (P) {
      if (P.food && P.food !== 'ours') t.cook = 0;
      if (P.food === 'they_bring' && (t.driver || 0) > 1) t.driver = 1;
    }
    return t;
  }
  function fillOf(evId, D) {
    var T = spotsOf(evId, D), P = D.partners[evId], cov = (P && P.covers) || {};
    var conf = {}, wait = {}, pform = {};
    (D.roster[evId] || []).forEach(function (r) {
      if (isOut(r)) return;
      var k = roleOf(r.role); if (!(k in T)) return;
      if ((r.detail || {}).source === 'partner form') { pform[k] = (pform[k] || 0) + 1; return; }
      if (isConf(r)) conf[k] = (conf[k] || 0) + 1; else wait[k] = (wait[k] || 0) + 1;
    });
    var total = 0, filled = 0, waiting = 0, open = [];
    Object.keys(T).forEach(function (k) {
      var need = T[k] || 0; if (!need) return;
      var theirs = (k === 'team_leader' || k === 'community_debrief') ? 0 : Math.max(Number(cov[roleOf(k)] || cov[k] || 0) || 0, pform[k] || 0);
      var f = Math.min(need, (conf[k] || 0) + theirs);
      var w = Math.min(need - f, wait[k] || 0);
      total += need; filled += f; waiting += w;
      if (need - f - w > 0) open.push([k, need - f - w]);
    });
    open.sort(function (a, b) { return ORDER.indexOf(a[0]) - ORDER.indexOf(b[0]); });
    return { total: total, filled: filled, waiting: waiting, open: open };
  }
  function barHtml(f) {
    if (!f || !f.total) return '';
    var pc = Math.round(f.filled / f.total * 100), pw = Math.round(f.waiting / f.total * 100);
    if (pc + pw > 100) pw = 100 - pc;
    var full = f.filled >= f.total;
    var tip = full ? 'Every spot is filled' : f.open.length ? 'Still open: ' + f.open.map(function (x) { return x[1] + ' ' + nameOf(x[0], x[1]); }).join(', ') : 'Every spot has someone signed up';
    var line = full ? 'All ' + f.total + ' volunteer spots filled'
      : f.filled + ' of ' + f.total + ' volunteer spots filled' + (f.waiting ? ' <span>&middot; ' + f.waiting + ' to confirm</span>' : '');
    return '<div class="w234' + (full ? ' full' : '') + '" title="' + E(tip) + '" data-k="' + f.total + ':' + f.filled + ':' + f.waiting + ':' + E(tip) + '">'
      + '<div class="tr" role="img" aria-label="' + E(f.filled + ' of ' + f.total + ' volunteer spots filled, ' + f.waiting + ' waiting to confirm') + '">'
      + (pc ? '<i class="c" style="width:' + pc + '%"></i>' : '') + (pw ? '<i class="w" style="width:' + pw + '%"></i>' : '') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="t">' + line + '</div></div>';
  }

  /* ---------- data, once for the upcoming servings on the page ---------- */
  function upcoming() {
    var t = today();
    return ((typeof WA_VOL_EVENTS !== 'undefined' ? WA_VOL_EVENTS : []) || []).filter(function (e) {
      return e && (e.kind || 'service') === 'service' && e.event_date >= t && e.status !== 'cancelled';
    });
  }
  async function load(force) {
    var evs = upcoming(); if (!evs.length) return null;
    var ids = evs.map(function (e) { return e.id; }).sort(function (a, b) { return a - b; });
    var key = ids.join(',');
    if (!force && S.data && S.key === key && Date.now() - S.at < 20000) return S.data;
    if (S.busy) return S.busy;
    S.busy = (async function () {
      try {
        var res = await Promise.all([
          db.from('event_roster').select('event_id,role,status,confirm_status,detail').in('event_id', ids),
          db.from('volunteer_role_guides').select('role,target,label_en'),
          db.from('volunteer_events').select('id,spot_counts').in('id', ids),
          db.from('volunteer_event_partners').select('event_id,covers,food').in('event_id', ids)
        ]);
        if (res[0].error || res[1].error) return null;
        var D = { roster: {}, guides: res[1].data || [], spots: {}, partners: {} };
        (res[0].data || []).forEach(function (r) { (D.roster[r.event_id] = D.roster[r.event_id] || []).push(r); });
        D.guides.forEach(function (g) { if (g.label_en) S.labels[g.role] = g.label_en; });
        ((res[2] && res[2].data) || []).forEach(function (x) { if (x.spot_counts) D.spots[x.id] = x.spot_counts; });
        ((res[3] && !res[3].error && res[3].data) || []).forEach(function (x) { D.partners[x.event_id] = x; });
        S.data = D; S.key = key; S.at = Date.now();
        return D;
      } catch (e) { return null; } finally { S.busy = null; }
    })();
    return S.busy;
  }

  /* ---------- paint: in place, only where it changed ---------- */
  function decorate(D) {
    var sec = document.getElementById('volunteering'); if (!sec || !D) return;
    var t = today();
    sec.querySelectorAll('.vl-ev[data-vlev]').forEach(function (el) {
      var id = Number(el.getAttribute('data-vlev'));
      var e = upcoming().filter(function (x) { return x.id === id; })[0];
      var old = el.querySelector('.w234');
      if (!e || e.event_date < t) { if (old) old.remove(); return; }
      var html = barHtml(fillOf(id, D));
      var box = document.createElement('div'); box.innerHTML = html; var nu = box.firstChild;
      if (old && nu && old.getAttribute('data-k') === nu.getAttribute('data-k')) return;
      if (old) old.remove();
      if (!nu) return;
      var at = el.querySelector('.vl-bar') || el.querySelector('.vl-nums');
      if (at) el.insertBefore(nu, at);
      else { var code = el.querySelector('.vl-code'); if (code) code.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', nu); else el.appendChild(nu); }
    });
  }
  async function paint(force) {
    var sec = document.getElementById('volunteering'); if (!sec || !sec.querySelector('.vl-ev[data-vlev]')) return;
    var D = await load(force); decorate(D);
  }

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */
  if (typeof waVolRenderCalendar === 'function') {
    var __cal234 = waVolRenderCalendar;
    waVolRenderCalendar = function () { var out = __cal234.apply(this, arguments); setTimeout(function () { paint(true); }, 160); return out; };
  }
  var pend = null;
  try {
    new MutationObserver(function (muts) {
      for (var i = 0; i < muts.length; i++) {
        var a = muts[i].addedNodes;
        for (var j = 0; j < a.length; j++) {
          var n = a[j]; if (!n || n.nodeType !== 1 || (n.classList && n.classList.contains('w234'))) continue;
          if ((n.matches && n.matches('.vl-ev[data-vlev]')) || (n.querySelector && n.querySelector('.vl-ev[data-vlev]'))) {
            if (!pend) pend = setTimeout(function () { pend = null; paint(false); }, 200);
            return;
          }
        }
      }
    }).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  } catch (e) { /* the calendar wrapper still paints */ }

  window.w234 = { state: function () { return S; }, fillOf: function (id) { return S.data ? fillOf(id, S.data) : null; }, paint: paint };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v239 The volunteer roles set right (Steel, 14/10/26 marketing meeting; built 15/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: the Volunteers list's filters for photographers, servers, community leads and drivers must work; "we should
   text the people who signed up as photographers to see if they are still in", saying it is Welfare Avenue; a videographer
   role; website photographer applicants in Applicants received (crm_form_apply step 7c, wa239_roles_pool).
   - The Volunteers list (Volunteering > Volunteers): the chips are the roles in a fixed order (Everyone, Team leaders,
     Cooks, Drivers, Servers, Dishwashers, Photographers, Videographers, Community lead), counted from each person's roles
     and role tags; the other tags (recurring volunteer, cabm, applied 2026-04...) moved behind a Tag select. A role and a
     tag filter together; the search still narrows the rows. A role chip tells the person that role's count.
   - Still in?: with a role chip on, leaders, HR and admins see "Text: still in?". It lists the people shown (those with a
     phone), each ticked, with the text they will get ("Hi <first>, it's Welfare Avenue! A while back you signed up to
     volunteer with us as a photographer. Are you still up for it? Tell us here, one tap: <link> (or reply YES or NO).
     Reply STOP to stop texts."; French for French contacts). wa239_pool_send queues one text a person (not again within 14
     days; no phone, STOP and the test phones skipped; sms_mode decides) and quo-send drains inside the 9 to 19 window,
     else the tick sends at 9. The link opens the /c/ page's Still in? screen (Yes, count me in / Not any more, a note);
     a YES or NO reply counts too (wa171_inbound). The list shows the answer in a Still in? column (Yes 3 Oct, No, Asked
     12 Oct, no answer) and filters by it (Said yes, Said no, No answer yet).
   Wrapped: waVolRenderPeople (final; wave26's version is replaced, its search, flags and edit link kept). Reads
   volunteer_people (already loaded), wa239_pool_list(); writes through wa239_pool_send only. ASCII only.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa239) return; window.__wa239 = true;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', GOLD = '#DCA527', TEAL = '#2A8C7A', MUTED = '#797773', FLAG = '#B3402F';
  var ROLE_ORDER = ['team_leader', 'cook', 'driver', 'server', 'dishwasher', 'photographer', 'videographer', 'community_debrief'];
  var ONE = { team_leader: 'team leader', cook: 'cook', driver: 'driver', server: 'server', dishwasher: 'dishwasher', photographer: 'photographer',
    videographer: 'videographer', community_debrief: 'community lead' };
  var W = { tag: '', pool: '', poolRows: null, poolAt: 0, poolBusy: null, can: null, canAt: 0, busy: false };
  window.__wa239state = W;
  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return '&#' + c.charCodeAt(0) + ';'; }); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function roleOf(r) { var k = lower(r); return k === 'community_talker' ? 'community_debrief' : k; }
  function isRole(k) { return ROLE_ORDER.indexOf(roleOf(k)) >= 0; }
  function label(k) { try { return waVolRoleLabel(k); } catch (e) { return k; } }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function toastS(t) { try { toast(t); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function dayWords(iso) { if (!iso) return ''; var d = new Date(iso); if (isNaN(d)) return ''; try { return d.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return String(iso).slice(0, 10); } }
  function first(p) { return String((p && p.full_name) || '').trim().split(/\s+/)[0] || 'there'; }
  function digits(p) { var out = ''; ((p && p.phones) || []).some(function (x) { var d = String(x || '').replace(/\D/g, '').replace(/^1(?=\d{10}$)/, ''); if (d.length === 10) { out = d; return true; } return false; }); return out; }
  function roleSet(p) { var set = {}; ((p && p.roles) || []).forEach(function (r) { set[roleOf(r)] = 1; }); ((p && p.tags) || []).forEach(function (r) { if (isRole(r)) set[roleOf(r)] = 1; }); return set; }
  function tagSet(p) { var set = {}; ((p && p.tags) || []).forEach(function (r) { if (!isRole(r)) set[String(r)] = 1; }); return set; }

  var css = document.createElement('style'); css.id = 'wa239css';

    '#volunteering .w239pool{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;align-items:center;margin:-4px 0 12px}' +

    '#modalBox .w239list{max-height:260px;overflow:auto;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.15);border-radius:10px;padding:8px 10px;margin:8px 0;background:#FFFDF8}' +

  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- who may text (leaders, HR, admins); the database decides anyway ---------- */
  function can() {
    if (W.can && Date.now() - W.canAt < 60000) return Promise.resolve(W.can);
    var out = { text: false };
    return Promise.all([db.rpc('is_wa_admin'), db.rpc('is_wa_leader'), db.rpc('wa_is_hr'), db.rpc('wa256_can_text')]).then(function (r) {   /* wave258: and text_staff */
      out.text = !!(r[0] && r[0].data === true) || !!(r[1] && r[1].data === true) || !!(r[2] && r[2].data === true) || !!(r[3] && r[3].data === true);
      W.can = out; W.canAt = Date.now(); return out;
    }).catch(function () { W.can = out; W.canAt = Date.now(); return out; });
  }
  /* ---------- the latest Still in? check per person ---------- */
  function pool(force) {
    if (!force && W.poolRows && Date.now() - W.poolAt < 30000) return Promise.resolve(W.poolRows);
    if (W.poolBusy) return W.poolBusy;
    W.poolBusy = db.rpc('wa239_pool_list').then(function (r) {
      var rows = (r && Array.isArray(r.data)) ? r.data : [];
      var by = {}; rows.forEach(function (x) { by[String(x.person_id)] = x; });
      W.poolRows = by; W.poolAt = Date.now(); W.poolBusy = null; return by;
    }).catch(function () { W.poolBusy = null; W.poolRows = W.poolRows || {}; W.poolAt = Date.now(); return W.poolRows; });
    return W.poolBusy;
  }
  function poolOf(p) { return (W.poolRows && W.poolRows[String(p.id)]) || null; }
  function poolWord(x) {
    if (!x) return '';
    if (x.answer === 'yes') return '<span class="w239a yes" title="Said yes on ' + E(dayWords(x.answered_at)) + (x.via === 'reply' ? ' (by reply)' : '') + (x.note ? ': ' + E(x.note) : '') + '">Yes \u00b7 ' + E(dayWords(x.answered_at)) + (x.note ? ' *' : '') + '</span>';
    if (x.answer === 'no') return '<span class="w239a no" title="Said no on ' + E(dayWords(x.answered_at)) + (x.via === 'reply' ? ' (by reply)' : '') + (x.note ? ': ' + E(x.note) : '') + '">No \u00b7 ' + E(dayWords(x.answered_at)) + (x.note ? ' *' : '') + '</span>';
    var st = String(x.status || 'queued');
    var how = st === 'sent' ? 'no answer yet' : st === 'queued' ? 'text waiting to go' : st === 'dry_run' ? 'dry run' : st === 'skipped' ? 'not sent (' + (x.error || 'skipped') + ')' : st === 'failed' ? 'the text failed' : st;
    return '<span class="w239a ask" title="Asked by ' + E(x.asked_by || 'the Hub') + ' on ' + E(dayWords(x.asked_at)) + ' as ' + E(ONE[x.role] || x.role) + '">Asked ' + E(dayWords(x.asked_at)) + ', ' + E(how) + '</span>';
  }
  function poolState(x) { if (!x) return ''; if (x.answer === 'yes') return 'yes'; if (x.answer === 'no') return 'no'; return 'asked'; }

  /* ---------- the list ---------- */
  function shown() {
    var q = lower(WA26_Q), qd = q.replace(/\D/g, '');
    return WA_VOL_PEOPLE.filter(function (p) {
      if (WA_VOL_ROLEFILTER && !roleSet(p)[roleOf(WA_VOL_ROLEFILTER)]) return false;
      if (W.tag && !tagSet(p)[W.tag]) return false;
      if (W.pool && poolState(poolOf(p)) !== W.pool) return false;
      return true;
    });
  }
  function render() {
    var roleCounts = {}, tagCounts = {}, poolCounts = { yes: 0, no: 0, asked: 0 };
    WA_VOL_PEOPLE.forEach(function (p) {
      Object.keys(roleSet(p)).forEach(function (r) { roleCounts[r] = (roleCounts[r] || 0) + 1; });
      Object.keys(tagSet(p)).forEach(function (t) { tagCounts[t] = (tagCounts[t] || 0) + 1; });
      var s = poolState(poolOf(p)); if (s) poolCounts[s]++;
    });
    if (WA_VOL_ROLEFILTER) WA_VOL_ROLEFILTER = roleOf(WA_VOL_ROLEFILTER);

      }).join('');
    var tags = Object.keys(tagCounts).sort(function (a, b) { return a.localeCompare(b); });
    var tagSel = '<select id="w239tag" aria-label="Tag"><option value="">Any tag</option>' + tags.map(function (t) { return '<option value="' + E(t) + '"' + (W.tag === t ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(t) + ' (' + tagCounts[t] + ')</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>';

    waVolPaint(html);

  }
  if (typeof waVolRenderPeople === 'function') {

  /* ---------- Text: still in? ---------- */
  function sample(role) {
    var r = ONE[role] || label(role).toLowerCase(), art = /^[aeiou]/.test(r) ? 'an ' : 'a ';
    return 'Hi <first name>, it\u2019s Welfare Avenue! A while back you signed up to volunteer with us as ' + art + r + '. Are you still up for it? Tell us here, one tap: hub.welfareavenue.com/c/<their link> (or reply YES or NO). Reply STOP to stop texts.';
  }
  function askModal() {
    if (!WA_VOL_ROLEFILTER) { toastS('Pick a role chip first (photographers, servers...), then Text: still in?'); return; }
    var role = roleOf(WA_VOL_ROLEFILTER);
    var people = shown().filter(function (p) {
      var tr = document.querySelector('#volunteering tr[data-p26="' + p.id + '"]');
      return (!tr || tr.style.display !== 'none') && digits(p);
    });
    var noPhone = shown().length - people.length;
    if (!people.length) { toastS('Nobody shown has a phone number.'); return; }
    var recent = people.filter(function (p) { var x = poolOf(p); return x && x.asked_at && Date.now() - new Date(x.asked_at).getTime() < 14 * 86400000; });
    openModal('<h3 style="margin:0 0 6px">Text: still in?</h3>'
      + '<p class="read" style="font-size:13px;margin:0 0 8px">One text each to the ' + E(label(role).toLowerCase()) + ' shown, asking whether they are still up for volunteering. They answer with one tap on their page, or by replying YES or NO; the answers show in the Still in? column.</p>'
      + '<div class="w239pre">' + E(sample(role)) + '</div>'
      + '<p class="w239note">French contacts get the French text. Sent now between 9 and 19 (Toronto), otherwise at 9 tomorrow. Nobody who texted STOP, and nobody asked in the last 14 days' + (recent.length ? ' (' + recent.length + ' here)' : '') + '. Texts follow the sms_mode switch (test mode: the test phones only).</p>'
      + '<div class="w239list" id="w239list">' + people.map(function (p) {
        var x = poolOf(p), st = poolState(x);
        return '<label><input type="checkbox" data-w239p="' + p.id + '"' + (st === 'no' ? '' : ' checked') + '> ' + E(p.full_name || '') + ' <em>' + E(digits(p).replace(/(\d{3})(\d{3})(\d{4})/, '$1-$2-$3')) + (st === 'yes' ? ' \u00b7 said yes ' + E(dayWords(x.answered_at)) : st === 'no' ? ' \u00b7 said no ' + E(dayWords(x.answered_at)) + ' (unticked)' : st === 'asked' ? ' \u00b7 asked ' + E(dayWords(x.asked_at)) : '') + '</em></label>';
      }).join('') + '</div>'
      + (noPhone ? '<p class="w239note">' + noPhone + ' of the people shown have no phone number and get nothing.</p>' : '')
      + '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w239send">Send to <span id="w239n">' + people.filter(function (p) { return poolState(poolOf(p)) !== 'no'; }).length + '</span></button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w239cancel">Cancel</button></div>');
    var box = document.getElementById('w239list');
    if (box) box.addEventListener('change', function () { var n = box.querySelectorAll('input:checked').length; var el = document.getElementById('w239n'); if (el) el.textContent = String(n); });
  }
  function send() {
    if (W.busy) return;
    var role = roleOf(WA_VOL_ROLEFILTER);
    var ids = [].map.call(document.querySelectorAll('#w239list input:checked'), function (i) { return Number(i.getAttribute('data-w239p')); }).filter(function (n) { return isFinite(n) && n > 0; });
    if (!ids.length) { toastS('Nobody is ticked.'); return; }
    var b = document.getElementById('w239send'); if (b) { b.disabled = true; b.textContent = 'Sending\u2026'; }
    W.busy = true;
    db.rpc('wa239_pool_send', { p_role: role, p_people: ids }).then(function (r) {
      W.busy = false;
      if (r && r.error) { toastS(r.error.message || 'That did not go through.'); if (b) { b.disabled = false; b.textContent = 'Send'; } return; }
      var d = (r && r.data) || {};
      var parts = [];
      if (d.reason) parts.push(d.reason + ' (sms_mode ' + (d.mode || 'off') + ')');
      else parts.push((d.queued || 0) + ' text' + (d.queued === 1 ? '' : 's') + (d.window_open ? ' on their way' : ' queued for 9 am'));
      if (d.asked_recently) parts.push(d.asked_recently + ' asked in the last 14 days');
      if (d.opted_out) parts.push(d.opted_out + ' opted out');
      if (d.no_phone) parts.push(d.no_phone + ' without a phone');
      if (d.held_by_test_mode) parts.push(d.held_by_test_mode + ' held by test mode');
      toastS(parts.join(', ') + '.');
      try { closeModal(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      pool(true).then(function () { if (WA_VOL_VIEW === 'people') render(); });
    }).catch(function (e) { W.busy = false; toastS('That did not go through: ' + ((e && e.message) || 'offline')); if (b) { b.disabled = false; b.textContent = 'Send'; } });
  }

  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var ask = t.closest('#volunteering [data-w239ask]'); if (ask) { e.preventDefault(); askModal(); return; }
    var pf = t.closest('#volunteering [data-w239pool]');
    if (pf) { e.preventDefault(); var v = pf.getAttribute('data-w239pool'); W.pool = W.pool === v ? '' : v; render(); return; }
    if (t.closest('#w239send')) { e.preventDefault(); send(); return; }
    if (t.closest('#w239cancel')) { e.preventDefault(); try { closeModal(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } return; }
  }, true);
})();
/* =====================================================================

  }

/* =====================================================================
   WA v242 The notification centre (Steel, 14/10/26 marketing meeting; built 15/10/26; v2 the same day, wave245:
   "The notifications need to be even more robust and descriptive")
   =====================================================================
   Settings > Notifications lists every kind of notification the Hub sends a person, read from the registry
   hub_notification_kinds (wave245), in groups: each with its title, when it fires, a Hub switch and a Slack switch
   (wa_notify_prefs.prefs.kinds[kind] = {hub, slack}, saved through wa242_prefs_set), and under Details what the message

   reach nobody says so. Recent: the person's own last messages from hub_notifications_sent (sent, skipped and why,
   failed and why). What the Hub posts in Slack channels is listed for the record, without switches. It takes the place
   of wave116j's checkbox list and wave219's "where they arrive" radios and announcement checkbox (hidden once mounted).
   The task reminders row shows what is due today (wa242_due_mine) and Send me today's reminder (wa242_remind_me).

   wa242_due_mine; writes through wa242_prefs_set, wa242_remind_me and the notify function (sample) only. Staff only.

  ];
  var W = { loaded: false, loading: null, channel: 'both', disabled: [], kinds: {}, devices: null, due: null, dueAt: 0, busy: false, sending: false, at: 0,
            reg: null, regAt: 0, recent: null, recentAt: 0, open: {}, sampling: '', slack: null };
  window.__wa242state = W;
  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return '&#' + c.charCodeAt(0) + ';'; }); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function myEmail() { try { return lower(USER && USER.email); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function toastS(t) { try { toast(t); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function deviceCount() {
    if (W.devices != null) return W.devices;
    try { var s = window.__wa219 && window.__wa219.state(); if (s && typeof s.devices === 'number') return s.devices; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    return 0;
  }
  /* can Slack reach the person? true when the Hub knows their Slack account (wa_slack_ids); null when it will look it up by email on the first message */
  function slackOn() { return W.slack === true ? true : null; }
  var GRP_ORDER = ['work', 'mentions', 'studio', 'volunteers', 'people', 'loop', 'announce', 'channels'];
  function kinds() {
    var gi = function (g) { var i = GRP_ORDER.indexOf(g); return i < 0 ? 50 : i; };
    return (W.reg && W.reg.length ? W.reg : FALLBACK).filter(function (k) { return k.active !== false; }).slice().sort(function (a, b) { return (gi(a.grp) - gi(b.grp)) || ((a.sort || 0) - (b.sort || 0)) || String(a.key).localeCompare(String(b.key)); });
  }
  function switchable() { return kinds().filter(function (k) { return !k.channel_only; }); }
  function kindOf(key) { var out = null; kinds().forEach(function (k) { if (k.key === key) out = k; }); return out; }

  /* ---------- the person's choices, the registry, the log ---------- */
  async function load(force) {
    if (W.loading) return W.loading;
    if (W.loaded && !force && Date.now() - W.at < 15000) return Promise.resolve();
    W.loading = (async function () {
      try {
        var r = await db.from('wa_notify_prefs').select('disabled, prefs').eq('email', myEmail()).maybeSingle();
        var d = r && r.data;
        W.disabled = (d && Array.isArray(d.disabled)) ? d.disabled.slice() : [];
        var p = (d && d.prefs && typeof d.prefs === 'object') ? d.prefs : {};
        W.channel = (p.channel === 'hub' || p.channel === 'slack') ? p.channel : 'both';
        W.kinds = (p.kinds && typeof p.kinds === 'object') ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(p.kinds)) : {};
      } catch (e) { /* keep what we have */ }
      try { var dv = await db.from('hub_push_subs').select('id').eq('email', myEmail()); W.devices = (dv && Array.isArray(dv.data)) ? dv.data.length : 0; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      if (!W.reg || Date.now() - W.regAt > 300000) {
        try { var rg = await db.from('hub_notification_kinds').select('*').eq('active', true).order('sort'); if (rg && Array.isArray(rg.data) && rg.data.length) { W.reg = rg.data; W.regAt = Date.now(); } } catch (e) { /* the fallback */ }
      }
      if (W.slack == null) { try { var sl = await db.from('wa_slack_ids').select('slack_id').eq('email', myEmail()).maybeSingle(); W.slack = !!(sl && sl.data && sl.data.slack_id); } catch (e) { /* unknown */ } }
      W.loaded = true; W.at = Date.now();
    })();
    try { await W.loading; } finally { W.loading = null; }
  }
  async function loadRecent(force) {
    if (W.recent && !force && Date.now() - W.recentAt < 30000) return;
    try { var r = await db.from('hub_notifications_sent').select('id, kind, channel, title, body, url, outcome, sent_at').eq('email', myEmail()).order('sent_at', { ascending: false }).limit(30); if (r && Array.isArray(r.data)) { W.recent = r.data; W.recentAt = Date.now(); } } catch (e) { W.recent = W.recent || []; }
    paint();
  }
  /* each kind's state: the saved switch, else the old rules (the channel, the disabled list, a device on) */
  function stateOf(k) {
    var ex = W.kinds[k.key];
    var off = W.disabled.indexOf(k.key) >= 0;
    var hub = k.hub && (ex && typeof ex.hub === 'boolean' ? ex.hub : (W.channel !== 'slack' && !off));
    var slack = k.slack && (ex && typeof ex.slack === 'boolean' ? ex.slack : (!off && !(W.channel === 'hub' && deviceCount() > 0 && k.key !== 'request_update' && k.key !== 'mail_for_you')));
    return { hub: !!hub, slack: !!slack };
  }
  function allKinds() {
    var out = {};
    switchable().forEach(function (k) { var s = stateOf(k); out[k.key] = { hub: k.hub ? s.hub : true, slack: k.slack ? s.slack : true }; });
    return out;
  }
  /* would anything reach the person for this kind right now? */
  function reach(k) {
    var s = stateOf(k);
    var hubOk = k.hub && s.hub && deviceCount() > 0;
    var slackOk = k.slack && s.slack && slackOn() !== false;
    if (hubOk || slackOk) return '';
    if (!k.hub && k.slack) return s.slack ? '' : 'Switched off: this kind reaches you nowhere.';
    if (k.hub && !k.slack) return s.hub ? 'No device of yours has Hub notifications on yet (turn them on above), so this reaches you nowhere.' : 'Switched off: this kind reaches you nowhere.';
    if (!s.hub && !s.slack) return 'Switched off on both: this kind reaches you nowhere.';
    if (s.hub && !s.slack) return 'Hub only, and no device of yours has Hub notifications on yet: turn them on above, or switch Slack on.';
    return '';
  }
  async function flip(key, ch) {
    var k = kindOf(key);
    if (!k || k.channel_only || W.busy) return;
    var cur = stateOf(k); var next = { hub: cur.hub, slack: cur.slack };
    next[ch] = !cur[ch];
    var all = allKinds(); all[key] = { hub: k.hub ? next.hub : true, slack: k.slack ? next.slack : true };
    W.kinds = all; W.busy = true; paint();
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('wa242_prefs_set', { p_kinds: all });
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      var d = r && r.data;
      if (d && d.kinds) W.kinds = d.kinds;
      if (d && Array.isArray(d.disabled)) W.disabled = d.disabled;
      toastS(k.title.split(' (')[0].split(':')[0] + ': ' + (ch === 'hub' ? 'Hub' : 'Slack') + (next[ch] ? ' on' : ' off'));
    } catch (e) {
      toastS('Could not save that: ' + (e && e.message ? e.message : 'try again'));
      await load(true);
    }
    W.busy = false; paint();
  }

  /* ---------- the day's tasks, the samples ---------- */
  async function loadDue(force) {
    if (W.due && !force && Date.now() - W.dueAt < 60000) return;
    try { var r = await db.rpc('wa242_due_mine'); if (r && !r.error) { W.due = r.data || null; W.dueAt = Date.now(); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    paint();
  }
  async function remindMe() {
    if (W.sending) return;
    W.sending = true; paint();
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('wa242_remind_me');
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      var d = (r && r.data) || {};
      toastS(d.sent ? 'Sent: look in the Hub and on Slack as you chose above' : (d.why || 'Nothing sent'));
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not send it: ' + (e && e.message ? e.message : 'try again')); }
    W.sending = false; paint();
    setTimeout(function () { loadRecent(true); }, 2500);
  }
  async function sample(key) {
    if (W.sampling) return;
    var k = kindOf(key); if (!k || k.channel_only) return;
    W.sampling = key; paint();
    try {
      var r = await db.functions.invoke('notify', { body: { event: 'sample', kind: key } });
      if (r && r.error) throw new Error(String(r.error.message || r.error));
      var d = (r && r.data) || {};
      if (d.error) throw new Error(String(d.error));
      var bits = [];
      if (d.slack) bits.push('Slack: ' + words(String(d.slack)));
      if (d.hub) bits.push('Hub: ' + words(String(d.hub)));
      toastS('Sample of "' + k.title.split(':')[0] + '". ' + (bits.join('. ') || 'Sent.'));
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not send the sample: ' + (e && e.message ? e.message : 'try again')); }
    W.sampling = ''; paint();
    setTimeout(function () { loadRecent(true); }, 2500);
  }
  /* an outcome, in words */
  function words(o) {
    o = String(o || '');
    if (/^sent \(Hub instead/.test(o)) return 'no Slack account, so it went to the Hub';
    if (/^sent/.test(o)) return 'sent';
    if (/opted out|switched off|Hub off|Slack off/i.test(o)) return 'switched off here';
    if (/Hub only|hub_only/i.test(o)) return 'Hub only (your choice)';
    if (/no device/i.test(o)) return 'no device with Hub notifications on';
    if (/no Slack|no_slack|no Slack account/i.test(o)) return 'no Slack account found';
    if (/no roster email|no_email/i.test(o)) return 'no email on the roster';
    if (/^failed/i.test(o)) return 'failed: ' + o.replace(/^failed:?\s*/i, '');
    if (/^skipped:?\s*/i.test(o)) return o.replace(/^skipped:?\s*/i, '');
    return o;
  }
  function dueWords() {
    var d = W.due; if (!d) return '';
    if (d.member === false) return 'You are not on the team list yet, so there are no tasks of yours to remind you of.';
    var n = function (k) { return Array.isArray(d[k]) ? d[k].length : 0; };
    var t = n('due_today'), o = n('overdue'), m = n('due_tomorrow');
    if (!t && !o && !m) return 'Nothing of yours is due today or tomorrow, and nothing is overdue.';
    var bits = [];
    if (t) bits.push('<b>' + t + '</b> due today');
    if (m) bits.push('<b>' + m + '</b> due tomorrow');
    if (o) bits.push('<b>' + o + '</b> overdue');
    return 'Right now: ' + bits.join(', ') + '. <a data-w242go="mypage">Open My page</a>';
  }
  function ago(iso) {
    var t = new Date(iso).getTime(); if (isNaN(t)) return '';
    var s = Math.max(0, (Date.now() - t) / 1000);
    if (s < 90) return 'just now';
    if (s < 3600) return Math.round(s / 60) + ' min ago';
    if (s < 86400) return Math.round(s / 3600) + ' h ago';
    try { return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short' }); } catch (e) { return String(iso).slice(0, 10); }
  }

  /* ---------- paint ---------- */
  function sw(k, ch, on) {
    if (!k[ch]) return '<span class="w242na" title="Not on ' + (ch === 'hub' ? 'the Hub' : 'Slack') + ' for this kind">\u2014</span>';
    return '<span class="sw' + (on ? ' on' : '') + (W.busy ? ' busy' : '') + '" data-w242sw="' + k.key + '|' + ch + '" role="switch" aria-checked="' + (on ? 'true' : 'false') + '" aria-label="' + E(k.title) + ': ' + (ch === 'hub' ? 'Hub' : 'Slack') + '"><i></i></span>';
  }
  function rowHtml(k) {
    var s = stateOf(k); var open = !!W.open[k.key]; var warn = reach(k);
    var extra = '';
    if (k.key === 'task_due') {
      extra += '<div class="w242due">' + (W.due ? dueWords() : 'Looking at your tasks\u2026') + (W.due && W.due.member !== false ? ' <button type="button" class="w242btn" data-w242="remind"' + (W.sending ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (W.sending ? 'Sending\u2026' : 'Send me today\u2019s reminder') + '</button>' : '') + '</div>';
    }
    var details = '';
    if (open) {
      details = '<div class="w242more">'
        + (k.says ? '<div><b>What it says</b> ' + E(k.says) + '</div>' : '')
        + (k.example_title || k.example_body ? '<div class="w242ex"><span class="w242exh">' + E(k.example_title || 'Example') + '</span>' + E(k.example_body || '') + '</div>' : '')
        + '<div><b>Who gets it</b> ' + E(k.audience || 'everyone') + (k.sender ? ' <span class="w242dim">(sent by ' + E(k.sender) + ')</span>' : '') + '</div>'
        + '<div class="w242act"><button type="button" class="w242btn" data-w242sample="' + k.key + '"' + (W.sampling ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (W.sampling === k.key ? 'Sending the sample\u2026' : 'Send me a sample') + '</button><span class="w242dim">A real message to you, through the switches as they stand.</span></div>'
        + '</div>';
    }
    return '<div class="w242row' + (open ? ' open' : '') + '" data-w242row="' + k.key + '"><div class="w242lab">'
      + '<a class="w242t" data-w242open="' + k.key + '">' + E(k.title) + ' <i class="w242chev">' + (open ? '\u25be' : '\u25b8') + '</i></a>'
      + (k.blurb ? '<div class="w242when">' + E(k.blurb) + '</div>' : '')
      + extra + (warn ? '<div class="w242warn">' + E(warn) + '</div>' : '') + details
      + '</div><div class="w242c">' + sw(k, 'hub', s.hub) + '</div><div class="w242c">' + sw(k, 'slack', s.slack) + '</div></div>';
  }
  function statusHtml() {
    var dev = deviceCount(); var sl = slackOn();
    var a = dev ? 'Hub notifications are on for ' + dev + (dev === 1 ? ' device' : ' devices') + ' of yours.' : 'No device of yours has Hub notifications on yet (Turn on, above).';
    var b = sl === true ? 'Slack messages come to your DMs (the Hub knows your Slack account).' : 'Slack messages go to your DMs: the Hub finds your account by email the first time, or you pick it under Connections.';
    return '<span>' + E(a) + ' ' + E(b) + '</span>';
  }
  function recentHtml() {
    var rows = W.recent;
    var h = '<div class="w242rhead"><b>Recent</b><span>What the Hub sent you lately, and whether it landed.</span><a data-w242="refresh">Refresh</a></div>';
    if (!rows) return h + '<div class="w242dim">Loading\u2026</div>';
    if (!rows.length) return h + '<div class="w242dim">Nothing sent to you yet. Open a kind above and press Send me a sample.</div>';
    return h + '<div class="w242recent">' + rows.map(function (r) {
      var k = kindOf(r.kind); var ok = /^sent/.test(String(r.outcome || ''));
      return '<div class="w242r' + (ok ? '' : ' skip') + '"><span class="w242rt">' + E(ago(r.sent_at)) + '</span><span class="w242rk">' + E(k ? k.title.split(':')[0] : r.kind) + ' \u00b7 ' + (r.channel === 'hub' ? 'Hub' : r.channel === 'slack' ? 'Slack' : E(r.channel)) + '</span>'
        + '<span class="w242ro">' + E(words(r.outcome)) + '</span>'
        + (r.title || r.body ? '<span class="w242rb">' + E(r.title ? r.title + (r.body ? ': ' : '') : '') + E(String(r.body || '').slice(0, 160)) + (r.url ? ' <a href="' + E(String(r.url).indexOf('#') === 0 ? r.url : (String(r.url).indexOf('/#') === 0 ? r.url.slice(1) : r.url)) + '">Open</a>' : '') + '</span>' : '')
        + '</div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function html() {
    var groups = [], byG = {};
    switchable().forEach(function (k) { if (!byG[k.grp]) { byG[k.grp] = { title: k.grp_title, rows: [] }; groups.push(k.grp); } byG[k.grp].rows.push(k); });
    var chans = kinds().filter(function (k) { return k.channel_only; });
    var out = '<div class="w242head"><div class="w242lab"><b>What reaches you, and where</b>' + statusHtml() + '</div><div class="w242c">Hub</div><div class="w242c">Slack</div></div>';
    groups.forEach(function (g) { out += '<div class="w242grp">' + E(byG[g].title) + '</div>' + byG[g].rows.map(rowHtml).join(''); });
    if (chans.length) {
      out += '<div class="w242grp">' + E(chans[0].grp_title || 'Also, in Slack channels') + ' <span class="w242dim">(for everyone; no switch)</span></div>'
        + chans.map(function (k) { return '<div class="w242row chan"><div class="w242lab"><span class="w242t">' + E(k.title) + '</span>' + (k.blurb ? '<div class="w242when">' + E(k.blurb) + '</div>' : '') + '</div><div class="w242c w242na">\u2014</div><div class="w242c w242na">#</div></div>'; }).join('');
    }
    out += '<div class="note w242note">Each switch saves by itself. A kind switched off on both stays quiet. The weekly digest, the serving texts and the announcements\u2019 Slack posts and emails are not per person and are not touched here.</div>';
    out += '<div class="w242rec">' + recentHtml() + '</div>';
    return out;
  }
  function host() {
    var box = document.getElementById('modalBox'); if (!box) return null;
    var root = box.querySelector('.w116js'); if (!root) return null;
    var tab = root.querySelector('.tabs a.on'); if (!tab || tab.getAttribute('data-jtab') !== 'notifications') return null;
    return root;
  }
  function paint() {
    try {
      var root = host(); if (!root) return;
      var el = document.getElementById('w242set');
      if (!el) {
        el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w242set';
        var after = document.getElementById('w219set');
        if (after && after.nextElementSibling && after.nextElementSibling.classList.contains('note') && after.nextElementSibling.classList.contains('w219what')) after = after.nextElementSibling;
        if (!after) { var tabs = root.querySelector('.tabs'); after = tabs; }
        if (after && after.nextSibling) root.insertBefore(el, after.nextSibling); else root.appendChild(el);
      }
      var h = html();
      if (el.__h !== h) { el.innerHTML = h; el.__h = h; }
      tidy(root);
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  /* the older controls for the same choices, out of the way */
  function tidy(root) {
    try {
      var labels = root.querySelectorAll(':scope > label.chk');
      for (var i = 0; i < labels.length; i++) { var inp = labels[i].querySelector('input[data-jev]'); if (inp) labels[i].style.display = 'none'; }
      var w = root.querySelector('#w219set .w219where'); if (w) w.style.display = 'none';
      var a = root.querySelector('#w219set .w219ann'); if (a) a.style.display = 'none';
      var n = root.querySelector('.note.w219what'); if (n && !n.__w242) { n.__w242 = true; n.innerHTML = n.innerHTML.replace('Which of these reach you. On Slack they come to your DMs', 'Slack messages go to your DMs'); }
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function mount() {
    if (!staff() || !host()) return;
    paint();
    load().then(function () { paint(); });
    loadDue(); loadRecent();
  }
  var prevAfter = window.w138AfterPaint;
  window.w138AfterPaint = function (tab) {
    var r;
    try { if (typeof prevAfter === 'function') r = prevAfter.apply(this, arguments); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    if (tab === 'notifications') { setTimeout(mount, 0); setTimeout(paint, 400); setTimeout(paint, 1500); }
    return r;
  };
  try {
    if (window.__wa219 && typeof window.__wa219.paintSettings === 'function' && !window.__wa219.paintSettings.__w242) {
      var ps = window.__wa219.paintSettings;
      var wrapped = function () { var r = ps.apply(this, arguments); try { if (host()) { W.devices = null; paint(); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return r; };
      wrapped.__w242 = true; window.__wa219.paintSettings = wrapped;
    }
  } catch (e) { /* fine */ }

  /* ---------- clicks ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var s = t.closest('#w242set [data-w242sw]');
    if (s) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); var p = s.getAttribute('data-w242sw').split('|'); flip(p[0], p[1]); return; }
    var o = t.closest('#w242set [data-w242open]');
    if (o) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); var k = o.getAttribute('data-w242open'); W.open[k] = !W.open[k]; paint(); return; }
    var sm = t.closest('#w242set [data-w242sample]');
    if (sm) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); sample(sm.getAttribute('data-w242sample')); return; }
    var b = t.closest('#w242set [data-w242]');
    if (b) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); var what = b.getAttribute('data-w242'); if (what === 'remind') remindMe(); else if (what === 'refresh') { W.recent = null; paint(); loadRecent(true); } return; }
    var g = t.closest('#w242set [data-w242go]');
    if (g) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); try { if (typeof closeModal === 'function') closeModal(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } location.hash = '#mypage'; }
  }, true);

  var css = document.createElement('style'); css.id = 'wa242css';
  css.textContent =
    '#w242set{margin:6px 0 14px}' +
    '#w242set .w242head,#w242set .w242row{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 64px 64px;gap:8px;align-items:center}' +
    '#w242set .w242head{padding:0 0 6px;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.14)}' +

    '#w242set .w242row{padding:8px 0;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.08);align-items:start}' +

    '#w242set .w242chev{font-style:normal;color:' + MUTED + ';font-size:11px;margin-left:2px}' +

    '#w242set .w242more b{color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:700}' +
    '#w242set .w242ex{padding:8px 10px;border-left:3px solid ' + GOLD + ';background:rgba(255,250,242,.7);border-radius:0 8px 8px 0;font-size:12.5px}' +
    '#w242set .w242exh{display:block;font-weight:700;color:' + GREEN + ';margin-bottom:2px}' +
    '#w242set .w242act{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap}' +
    '#w242set .w242dim{color:' + MUTED + ';font-size:12px}' +
