/* =====================================================================
   WA v244 Two roles at most, and the coordinator places people who picked two (Steel, 15/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel, 15/10/26: "I want to limit people from being able to sign up to two or more positions ... it can get difficult
   for our team to kind of decide where to put them." Asked where someone who picks two goes: "Wait for coordinator to
   choose". The forms allow 2 roles (crm_forms max 2, form-submit v8); one role picked lands in that role as before, two
   land on the roster as role 'volunteer' with detail.role_choices (crm_form_apply, migration wa244_two_roles), and their
   7-day confirmation text waits until the coordinator chooses (wa171_queue_confirms).
   - The serving's roster board (wave119): a card waiting for a role says "Picked Driver or Server" with a button for each
     choice; one click moves the card (the board's own Move, so the change log, the toast and the counts are the same), and
     the column's head says what the cards are waiting for.
   - The serving cards' spots bar (wave234): "N to place" when people wait for a role.
   Dishwasher is off the website and the forms but stays here: the board, Plan the roster and the bench still offer it.
   Reads only what waves 119 and 234 already hold (WA_VOL_DETAIL, window.w234.state()). ASCII only.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa244) return; window.__wa244 = true;
  var GOLD = '#DCA527', INK = '#103F39';
  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' ? isStaff() : true; } catch (e) { return true; } }
  function one(r) { var l = r; try { l = waVolRoleLabel(r); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return String(l || r).replace(/_/g, ' ').replace(/s$/, ''); }
  function choicesOf(r) { var c = r && r.detail && r.detail.role_choices; return lower(r && r.role) === 'volunteer' && Array.isArray(c) && c.length > 1 ? c.map(String) : null; }
  function gone(r) { var s = lower(r.status); return s === 'cancelled' || s === 'unavailable' || r.confirm_status === 'declined'; }
  function detail() { try { return (WA_VOL_DETAIL && WA_VOL_DETAIL.id === WA_VOL_EVENT) ? WA_VOL_DETAIL : null; } catch (e) { return null; } }

  var css = document.createElement('style'); css.id = 'wa244css';
  css.textContent =
    '#w119 .pc .w244{display:block;margin-top:6px;padding:6px 7px;border-radius:9px;background:rgba(220,165,39,.16);font-size:11.5px;line-height:1.35;color:' + INK + '}' +
    '#w119 .pc .w244 b{display:inline;font-size:11.5px;padding:0}' +
    '#w119 .pc .w244 .bt{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:5px;margin-top:5px}' +
    '#w119 .pc .w244 button{font:inherit;font-weight:700;font-size:11.5px;border:1px solid ' + INK + ';background:#FFFAF2;color:' + INK + ';border-radius:999px;padding:3px 9px;cursor:pointer}' +
    '#w119 .pc .w244 button:hover,#w119 .pc .w244 button:focus-visible{background:' + INK + ';color:#FFFAF2}' +
    '#w119 .pc .w244 small{display:block;margin-top:4px;color:#5b5a57;font-size:10.5px}' +
    '#w119 .rc .w244h{display:block;font-size:11px;color:#5b5a57;margin:-2px 0 6px;line-height:1.35}' +
    '#volunteering .vl-ev .w234 .t .w244p{color:' + GOLD + ';font-weight:700;filter:brightness(.8)}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- the roster board: cards waiting for a role ---------- */
  function cardLine(r, ch) {
    var names = ch.map(function (k) { return one(k); });
    return '<span class="w244" data-w244="' + E(r.id + ':' + ch.join(',')) + '">Picked <b>' + E(names.join(' or ')) + '</b>' +
      (staff() && !gone(r) ? '<span class="bt">' + ch.map(function (k) { return '<button type="button" data-w244pick="' + E(k) + '" data-w244id="' + E(r.id) + '" title="Place ' + E(r.person_name || 'them') + ' as ' + E(one(k).toLowerCase()) + '">Make ' + E(one(k).toLowerCase()) + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</span>' : '') +
      '<small>Their confirmation text waits until a role is chosen.</small></span>';
  }
  function decorateBoard() {
    var D = detail(); var board = document.querySelector('#w119 .board'); if (!D || !board) return;
    var rows = {}; (D.roster || []).forEach(function (r) { rows[String(r.id)] = r; });
    board.querySelectorAll('.pc[data-w119card]').forEach(function (pc) {
      var r = rows[pc.getAttribute('data-w119card')]; var ch = r ? choicesOf(r) : null;
      var old = pc.querySelector('.w244');
      if (!ch) { if (old) old.remove(); return; }
      var key = r.id + ':' + ch.join(',');
      if (old && old.getAttribute('data-w244') === key) return;
      if (old) old.remove();
      var at = pc.querySelector('.sel2'); var box = document.createElement('div'); box.innerHTML = cardLine(r, ch);
      if (at) pc.insertBefore(box.firstChild, at); else pc.appendChild(box.firstChild);
    });
    var col = board.querySelector('.rc[data-w119role="volunteer"]');
    if (col) {
      var waiting = (D.roster || []).filter(function (r) { return choicesOf(r) && !gone(r); }).length;
      var h = col.querySelector('.w244h');
      if (waiting && !h) { var hd = col.querySelector('.h'); if (hd) hd.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend', '<span class="w244h">Picked two roles on the form: choose one for each.</span>'); }
      if (!waiting && h) h.remove();
    }
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var b = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('#w119 [data-w244pick]') : null; if (!b) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
    var id = b.getAttribute('data-w244id'), role = b.getAttribute('data-w244pick');
    var sel = document.querySelector('#w119 select[data-w119mv="' + id + '"]');
    if (!sel) return;
    if (!Array.prototype.some.call(sel.options, function (o) { return o.value === role; })) { var o = document.createElement('option'); o.value = role; o.textContent = 'To ' + one(role).toLowerCase(); sel.appendChild(o); }
    sel.value = role; sel.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
  }, true);

  /* ---------- the serving cards: N to place ---------- */
  function decorateBars() {
    var st = null; try { st = window.w234 && window.w234.state ? window.w234.state() : null; } catch (e) { st = null; }
    var D = st && st.data; if (!D || !D.roster) return;
    document.querySelectorAll('#volunteering .vl-ev[data-vlev]').forEach(function (el) {
      var t = el.querySelector('.w234 .t'); if (!t) return;
      var n = (D.roster[Number(el.getAttribute('data-vlev'))] || []).filter(function (r) { return choicesOf(r) && !gone(r); }).length;
      var sp = t.querySelector('.w244p');
      if (!n) { if (sp) sp.remove(); return; }
      var txt = ' \u00b7 ' + n + ' to place';
      if (sp) { if (sp.textContent !== txt) sp.textContent = txt; return; }
      t.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<span class="w244p" title="Picked two roles: the coordinator chooses one">' + E(txt) + '</span>');
    });
  }

  /* ---------- wiring: after the board or the bars change ---------- */
  var pend = null;
  function soon() { if (pend) return; pend = setTimeout(function () { pend = null; try { decorateBoard(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } try { decorateBars(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 120); }
  try {
    new MutationObserver(function (muts) {
      for (var i = 0; i < muts.length; i++) {
        var a = muts[i].addedNodes;
        for (var j = 0; j < a.length; j++) {
          var n = a[j]; if (!n || n.nodeType !== 1) continue;
          if (n.classList && (n.classList.contains('w244') || n.classList.contains('w244h') || n.classList.contains('w244p'))) continue;
          if ((n.matches && (n.matches('.board, .pc, .w234, .vl-ev, #w119') || (n.querySelector && n.querySelector('.board, .pc, .w234'))))) { soon(); return; }
        }
      }
    }).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  } catch (e) { /* fine */ }

  window.w244 = { paint: function () { decorateBoard(); decorateBars(); }, choicesOf: choicesOf };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v256 the Volunteer Manager's Phone page (15/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (15/10/26): "The volunteer manager needs to have a page where the phone and messages can be used. Similar to the
   text volunteers pop up. That should also be a page in itself. I'm also curious if phone calls itself can be done there."

   #volunteering/phone (the Volunteer Manager's rail, People > Phone; the classic page's Phone chip): every number that
   texted the ops line (438) 808-7754, was texted from it, called it or was called from it, in one list with what waits
   for a person (texts nobody answered, missed calls nobody returned), and the conversation with texts and calls in one
   timeline.
   - Texts: the same conversations as Find volunteers > Conversations (wave191e/h/i/j: wa191_recruit_inbox,
     wa191_recruit_thread, quo-send thread for what was typed in the Quo app, wa191_text_reply, wa191_text_done,
     wa191_text_roster for their place on the next servings), over 60 days instead of 21. New text (wa256_phone_lookup: a
     volunteer or CRM contact by name or number, or any number typed in) and Text a group (wave23's Text volunteers window).
     Texts go only between 9 am and 7 pm Montreal (the queue waits otherwise) and never to a number that texted STOP.
   - Calls: Quo's API reads calls but cannot place one. Call opens the Quo app with the number filled in (a tel: link: the
     Quo desktop app on a computer where Quo is the calling app, the Quo or phone app on a phone), and the call goes out
     from the ops line when the Quo app has it selected. quo-calls (cron wa-quo-calls, every 10 minutes, and Check now)
     copies the ops line's calls into quo_calls: missed calls, answered calls and their length, voicemails and Quo's call
     summaries (summaries and voicemail transcripts for leaders, HR and admins only: wa256_phone_calls). A missed call
     waits until someone calls or texts back, or presses No call back needed (wa256_call_done).
   - Who: every staff member who opens the Volunteer Manager reads; leaders, HR and admins text, reply and mark (as
     everywhere texts go out; the database checks again).
   The page re-reads the list every 30 seconds while it is open (and the open conversation with it). */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var GREEN = '#103F39', CREAM = '#FFFAF2', GOLD = '#DCA527', FLAG = '#B3402F', TEAL = '#2A8C7A', INK = '#1F201E', MUTED = '#797773';
  var BODY = "'Space Grotesk','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif", HEAD = "'Barlow Condensed','Arial Narrow','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  var OPS = '(438) 808-7754', OPS_TEL = '+14388087754';
  var DAYS = 60;
  var KIND = { recruit: 'Invitation', confirm: 'Confirmation text', signup: 'Sign-up text', itinerary: 'Itinerary', survey: 'Survey', ack: 'Thank-you', reply: 'Reply', manual: 'Text volunteers', reconfirm: 'Still in?', inbound: '' };
  var P = { inbox: null, at: 0, ph: null, thread: null, quo: null, calls: null, filter: 'all', q: '', drafts: {}, names: {}, can: false, win: true, synced: null,
    newOpen: false, look: null, lookQ: '', lookT: null, timer: null, loading: false, setting: null, checking: false, lastN: null };

  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function T(m) { try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(m); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function digits(s) { var d = String(s || '').replace(/\D/g, ''); return d.length >= 10 ? d.slice(-10) : ''; }
  function phf(d) { d = digits(d); return d ? '(' + d.slice(0, 3) + ') ' + d.slice(3, 6) + '-' + d.slice(6) : ''; }
  function initials(n) { var p = String(n || '?').trim().split(/\s+/); return (((p[0] || '')[0] || '') + ((p[1] || '')[0] || '')).toUpperCase() || '?'; }
  function hue(n) { var h = 0; n = String(n || ''); for (var i = 0; i < n.length; i++) h = (h * 31 + n.charCodeAt(i)) % 360; return h; }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function here() { try { return typeof WA_VOL_VIEW !== 'undefined' && WA_VOL_VIEW === 'phone' && /^#volunteering\/phone/.test(location.hash || ''); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function ms(x) { var t = new Date(x).getTime(); return isFinite(t) ? t : 0; }
  function when(ts) {
    if (!ts) return '';
    try {
      var d = new Date(ts), now = new Date(), day = 86400000;
      var tz = { timeZone: 'America/Toronto' };
      var ds = d.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', tz), ns = now.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', tz);
      if (ds === ns) return d.toLocaleTimeString('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto', hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' });
      var y = new Date(now.getTime() - day).toLocaleDateString('en-CA', tz);
      if (ds === y) return 'Yesterday';
      if (now.getTime() - d.getTime() < 6 * day) return d.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto', weekday: 'short' });
      return d.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' });
    } catch (e) { return ''; }
  }
  function whenLong(ts) { try { return new Date(ts).toLocaleString('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto', weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric', hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' }); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function dur(s) { s = Number(s) || 0; if (s < 60) return s + ' s'; var m = Math.floor(s / 60), r = s % 60; return m + ' min' + (r ? ' ' + r + ' s' : ''); }
  function ago(ts) { var m = Math.round((Date.now() - ms(ts)) / 60000); if (!ts || !isFinite(m)) return 'not yet'; if (m < 1) return 'just now'; if (m < 60) return m + ' min ago'; var h = Math.round(m / 60); return h < 24 ? h + ' h ago' : Math.round(h / 24) + ' days ago'; }
  function fmtDay(d) { try { return new Date(String(d).slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00Z').toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { timeZone: 'UTC', weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return String(d || ''); } }
  function roleName(r) { var m = { team_leader: 'Team leader', cook: 'Cook', driver: 'Driver', server: 'Server', dishwasher: 'Dishwasher', photographer: 'Photographer', videographer: 'Videographer', community_debrief: 'Community lead' }; return m[r] || (r ? String(r).replace(/_/g, ' ') : ''); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').toLowerCase(); }
  var ICO = {
    phone: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.9" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M7.2 3.8 9.6 4.4l1.2 3.9-1.9 1.4a11 11 0 0 0 5.4 5.4l1.4-1.9 3.9 1.2.6 2.4c.2.8-.3 1.6-1.1 1.8C11 20.2 3.8 13 5.4 4.9c.2-.8 1-1.3 1.8-1.1z"/></svg>',
    missed: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.9" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M7.2 3.8 9.6 4.4l1.2 3.9-1.9 1.4a11 11 0 0 0 5.4 5.4l1.4-1.9 3.9 1.2.6 2.4c.2.8-.3 1.6-1.1 1.8C11 20.2 3.8 13 5.4 4.9c.2-.8 1-1.3 1.8-1.1z"/><path d="m15 3.5 5 5M20 3.5l-5 5"/></svg>',
    text: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.9" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4.5 5.5h15v10h-9l-4 3.5v-3.5h-2z"/></svg>',
    vm: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.9" stroke-linecap="round" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="7" cy="13" r="3.5"/><circle cx="17" cy="13" r="3.5"/><path d="M7 16.5h10"/></svg>'
  };

  /* ---------- data ---------- */
  async function loadInbox(force) {
    if (!force && P.inbox && Date.now() - P.at < 25000) return P.inbox;
    if (P.loading) return P.inbox; P.loading = true;
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('wa256_phone_inbox', { p_days: DAYS });
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      var d = (r && r.data) || {};
      P.can = d.can === true; P.win = d.window !== false; P.synced = d.synced_at || null;
      P.inbox = merge(d.texts || [], d.calls || []); P.at = Date.now();
    } catch (e) { if (!P.inbox) P.inbox = []; P.err = String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 120); }
    P.loading = false;
    bumpRail();
    return P.inbox;
  }
  /* one row a number: the texts' row (wa191_recruit_inbox) and the calls' row side by side; the latest of the two leads */
  function merge(texts, calls) {
    var by = {};
    (texts || []).forEach(function (t) { if (!t || !t.ph) return; by[t.ph] = { ph: t.ph, n: t.n || null, p: t.p || null, t: t }; });
    (calls || []).forEach(function (c) { if (!c || !c.ph) return; var x = by[c.ph] || (by[c.ph] = { ph: c.ph, n: c.n || null, p: c.p || null }); x.c = c; if (!x.n && c.n) x.n = c.n; if (!x.p && c.p) x.p = c.p; });
    var out = Object.keys(by).map(function (k) {
      var x = by[k], ta = x.t ? ms(x.t.at) : 0, ca = x.c ? ms(x.c.at) : 0;
      x.at = Math.max(ta, ca); x.lastIs = ca > ta ? 'call' : 'text';
      x.waitT = !!(x.t && x.t.wait); x.openT = (x.t && x.t.open) || (x.waitT ? 1 : 0);
      x.missed = (x.c && x.c.open) || 0;
      x.wait = x.waitT || x.missed > 0;
      return x;
    });
    out.sort(function (a, b) { return (b.wait - a.wait) || (b.at - a.at); });
    return out;
  }
  function waitingCount() { var n = 0; (P.inbox || []).forEach(function (x) { if (x.wait) n++; }); return n; }
  function bumpRail() {
    var n = waitingCount();
    if (n === P.lastN) return; P.lastN = n;
    try { if (window.w153 && window.w153.state && window.w153.state().app === 'volunteer' && typeof window.w153.paint === 'function') window.w153.paint(); } catch (e) { /* the count shows on the next paint */ }
  }
  function toConv() {
    /* on a phone the conversation takes the screen: bring its box to the top */
    try { if (window.innerWidth < 760) { var c = root() && root().querySelector('.conv'); if (c) c.scrollIntoView({ block: 'start' }); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  async function openThread(ph) {
    P.ph = digits(ph); P.thread = null; P.quo = null; P.calls = null; P.newOpen = false;
    paint(); toConv();
    await loadThread(true);
  }
  async function loadThread(withQuo) {
    var ph = P.ph; if (!ph) return;
    var a = db.rpc('wa191_recruit_thread', { p_phone: ph }), b = db.rpc('wa256_phone_calls', { p_phone: ph });
    try { var r = await a; if (r && r.error) throw r.error; if (P.ph === ph) P.thread = r.data || { ph: ph, messages: [] }; }
    catch (e) { if (P.ph === ph && !P.thread) P.thread = { ph: ph, messages: [] }; }
    try { var c = await b; if (P.ph === ph) P.calls = (c && !c.error && c.data) || []; } catch (e) { if (P.ph === ph) P.calls = []; }
    if (P.ph !== ph) return; paintThread();
    if (!withQuo || !P.can) return;
    try {
      var q = await db.functions.invoke('quo-send', { body: { action: 'thread', phone: ph } });
      if (q && !q.error && q.data && q.data.messages && P.ph === ph && ph === (q.data.phone || ph)) { P.quo = q.data; paintThread(); }
    } catch (e) { /* the Hub's copy is enough */ }
  }
  /* the Hub's copy of the texts, then what was typed in the Quo app since the last copy, then the calls: one timeline */
  function timeline() {
    var t = (P.thread && P.thread.messages) || [], seen = {};
    t.forEach(function (m) { if (m.qid) seen[m.qid] = 1; });
    var out = t.map(function (m) { return { k: 'text', dir: m.dir, body: m.body, at: m.at, kind: m.kind, st: m.st, by: m.by, err: m.err, ev: m.ev }; });
    ((P.quo && P.quo.messages) || []).forEach(function (q) {
      if (seen[q.id]) return;
      if (q.dir === 'in' && t.some(function (m) { return m.dir === 'in' && String(m.body || '').trim() === String(q.text || '').trim(); })) return;
      if (q.dir === 'out' && t.some(function (m) { return m.dir === 'out' && String(m.body || '').trim() === String(q.text || '').trim() && Math.abs(ms(m.at) - ms(q.at)) < 180000; })) return;
      out.push({ k: 'text', dir: q.dir, body: q.text, at: q.at, kind: q.dir === 'out' ? 'quo' : 'inbound', st: q.status, quo: true });
    });
    (P.calls || []).forEach(function (c) { out.push({ k: 'call', dir: c.dir, at: c.at, c: c }); });
    out.sort(function (a, b) { return ms(a.at) - ms(b.at); });
    return out;
  }

  /* ---------- the page ---------- */
  function shown() {
    var q = lower(P.q).trim(), qd = String(P.q || '').replace(/\D/g, '');
    return (P.inbox || []).filter(function (x) {
      if (P.filter === 'wait' && !x.wait) return false;
      if (P.filter === 'missed' && !(x.c && x.c.missed)) return false;
      if (P.filter === 'texts' && !x.t) return false;
      if (P.filter === 'calls' && !x.c) return false;
      if (q && !(lower(x.n).indexOf(q) >= 0 || (qd.length >= 3 && x.ph.indexOf(qd) >= 0))) return false;
      return true;
    });
  }
  function lastLine(x) {
    if (x.lastIs === 'call' && x.c) {
      var c = x.c;
      if (c.dir === 'in' && !c.ans) return '<span class="ic miss">' + ICO.missed + '</span>' + (c.vm ? 'Missed call \u00b7 voicemail' : 'Missed call');
      return '<span class="ic">' + ICO.phone + '</span>' + (c.dir === 'in' ? 'Incoming call' : c.ans ? 'Outgoing call' : 'Outgoing call \u00b7 no answer') + (c.ans && c.dur ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(dur(c.dur)) : '');
    }
    var t = x.t || {};
    return (t.dir === 'out' ? 'You: ' : '') + E(t.last || '');
  }
  function rowHtml(x) {
    var nm = x.n || phf(x.ph);
    var badges = '';
    if (x.missed) badges += '<i class="b miss" title="' + x.missed + ' missed call' + (x.missed === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' to return">' + ICO.missed + (x.missed > 1 ? x.missed : '') + '</i>';
    if (x.waitT) badges += '<i class="dot" title="Their text waits for an answer"></i>';
    return '<button class="cv' + (P.ph === x.ph ? ' on' : '') + (x.wait ? ' wt' : '') + '" data-w256conv="' + E(x.ph) + '">'
      + '<span class="av" style="background:hsl(' + hue(x.n || x.ph) + ',36%,40%)">' + E(x.n ? initials(x.n) : '#') + '</span>'
      + '<span class="who"><b>' + E(nm) + '</b><span>' + lastLine(x) + '</span></span>'
      + '<span class="tm">' + E(when(new Date(x.at).toISOString())) + '<span class="bs">' + badges + '</span></span></button>';
  }
  function counts() {
    var c = { wait: 0, missed: 0, texts: 0 };
    (P.inbox || []).forEach(function (x) { if (x.waitT) c.texts++; if (x.missed) c.missed++; if (x.wait) c.wait++; });
    return c;
  }
  function headHtml() {
    var c = counts();
    var stat = '<div class="st">'
      + '<button class="sc' + (c.texts ? ' hot' : '') + '" data-w256f="wait"><b>' + c.texts + '</b><span>text' + (c.texts === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' waiting</span></button>'
      + '<button class="sc' + (c.missed ? ' hot' : '') + '" data-w256f="missed"><b>' + c.missed + '</b><span>missed call' + (c.missed === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' to return</span></button>'
      + '<span class="sc q"><span>Calls checked ' + E(ago(P.synced)) + '</span>' + (P.can ? '<button class="lnk" data-w256go="check"' + (P.checking ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (P.checking ? 'Checking\u2026' : 'Check now') + '</button>' : '') + '</span>'
      + '</div>';
    var acts = '<div class="acts">'
      + (P.can ? '<button class="w191btn gold" data-w256go="new">New text</button><button class="w191btn ghost" data-w256go="group">Text a group</button>' : '')
      + '<button class="w191btn quiet" data-w256go="how">How calls work</button></div>';
    return '<div class="hd"><div class="tt"><h2>Phone</h2><p>Texts and calls on the ops line <b>' + OPS + '</b>, in one place. ' + (P.win ? 'Texts send now.' : 'It is outside 9 am to 7 pm in Montreal: texts wait and go at 9 am.') + '</p></div>' + acts + '</div>' + stat;
  }
  function listHtml() {
    var segs = [['all', 'All'], ['wait', 'Waiting'], ['missed', 'Missed calls'], ['texts', 'Texts'], ['calls', 'Calls']];
    var top = '<div class="lt"><div class="seg" role="tablist">' + segs.map(function (s) { return '<button role="tab" data-w256f="' + s[0] + '" class="' + (P.filter === s[0] ? 'on' : '') + '" aria-selected="' + (P.filter === s[0]) + '">' + s[1] + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
      + '<input type="search" data-w256q placeholder="Search a name or number" value="' + E(P.q) + '" aria-label="Search a name or number"></div>';
    var list = P.inbox == null ? '<div class="empty">Loading the phone\u2026</div>' : (function () {
      var s = shown();
      if (!s.length) return '<div class="empty">' + (P.filter === 'wait' ? 'Nothing waits for a person. ' : P.filter === 'missed' ? 'No missed calls to return. ' : 'Nothing here in the last ' + DAYS + ' days. ') + '</div>';
      return s.map(rowHtml).join('');
    })();
    return '<div class="cl">' + top + '<div class="ls">' + list + '</div></div>';
  }
  function newHtml() {
    var ph = digits(P.lookQ);
    var res = P.look == null ? '' : !P.look.length ? '<div class="empty sm">No one with a phone by that name or number.' + (ph ? '' : '') + '</div>'
      : P.look.map(function (x) { return '<button class="lk" data-w256pick="' + E(x.ph) + '"><span class="av" style="background:hsl(' + hue(x.n || x.ph) + ',36%,40%)">' + E(initials(x.n)) + '</span><span class="who"><b>' + E(x.n || x.phf) + '</b><span>' + E(x.phf) + (x.k === 'p' ? ' \u00b7 volunteer' : ' \u00b7 in the CRM') + (x.r && x.r.length ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(x.r.map(roleName).join(', ')) : '') + '</span></span></button>'; }).join('');
    return '<div class="nw"><div class="th"><button class="bk" data-w256go="back" aria-label="Back">\u2039</button><span><b>New text</b><span>From the ops line ' + OPS + '</span></span></div>'
      + '<div class="nb"><label for="w256look">To</label><input id="w256look" type="search" data-w256look autocomplete="off" placeholder="A volunteer\u2019s name, or a phone number" value="' + E(P.lookQ) + '">'
      + (ph ? '<button class="w191btn" data-w256pick="' + ph + '">Text ' + E(phf(ph)) + '</button>' : '')
      + '<div class="lks">' + res + '</div>'
      + '<p class="gn">To text many people at once (a serving\u2019s roster, a role, everyone), use <button class="lnk" data-w256go="group">Text a group</button>. To invite people to a serving, use Find volunteers on the serving.</p></div></div>';
  }
  function callCard(m) {
    var c = m.c, inn = c.dir === 'in', missed = inn && !c.ans;
    var t = missed ? (c.ai ? 'Answered by Quo\u2019s assistant' : 'Missed call') : inn ? 'Incoming call' : c.ans ? 'Outgoing call' : 'Outgoing call, no answer';
    var meta = [whenLong(c.at), c.ans && c.dur ? dur(c.dur) : '', missed && c.done ? 'marked: no call back needed' : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ');
    var extra = '';
    if (c.vm) extra += '<div class="cx"><span class="ic">' + ICO.vm + '</span><span><b>Voicemail' + (c.vms ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(dur(c.vms)) : '') + '</b>' + (c.vmt ? '<span>' + E(c.vmt) + '</span>' : P.can ? '<span>Listen to it in the Quo app.</span>' : '') + '</span></div>';
    if (c.summary) extra += '<div class="cx"><span class="ic">' + ICO.text + '</span><span><b>Quo\u2019s summary</b><span>' + E(c.summary).replace(/\n/g, '<br>') + '</span>' + (c.next ? '<span class="nx"><b>Next steps:</b> ' + E(c.next).replace(/\n/g, '<br>') + '</span>' : '') + '</span></div>';
    return '<div class="m call ' + (inn ? 'in' : 'out') + '"><div class="cc' + (missed && !c.ai ? ' miss' : '') + '"><span class="ic">' + (missed ? ICO.missed : ICO.phone) + '</span><span class="ct"><b>' + E(t) + '</b><span>' + E(meta) + '</span></span></div>' + extra + '</div>';
  }
  function placeOf(r) { var s = lower(r.st), c = lower(r.cs); if (s === 'confirmed' || c === 'confirmed') return 'yes'; if (s === 'unavailable' || s === 'declined' || c === 'declined' || s === 'cancelled') return 'no'; return ''; }
  function placesHtml(th) {
    var sv = th.servings || []; if (!sv.length || !staff()) return '';
    var who = th.p ? '' : th.cn ? '<p class="srn">The CRM knows this number as <b>' + E(th.cn) + '</b>. Adding them to a serving gives them a volunteer record.</p>'
      : '<p class="srn"><label for="w256rname">Not in the volunteer directory yet.</label><input id="w256rname" type="text" data-w256name maxlength="80" autocomplete="off" placeholder="Their name, to add them" value="' + E(P.names[th.ph] || '') + '"></p>';
    var busy = P.setting != null;
    var rows = sv.map(function (s) {
      var r = s.row, pl = r ? placeOf(r) : '';
      var what = r ? [roleName(r.role), pl === 'yes' ? 'coming' : pl === 'no' ? 'can\u2019t make it' : 'waiting'] : ['not on the roster'];
      var day = '<span class="d"><b>' + E(fmtDay(s.d)) + '</b><span>' + E(what.filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ')) + '</span></span>';
      if (!r) return '<div class="sr">' + day + '<button class="w191btn ghost sm" data-w256set="' + s.e + '|Confirmed"' + (busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Add as coming</button></div>';
      return '<div class="sr">' + day + '<span class="seg sm">'
        + '<button data-w256set="' + s.e + '|' + (pl === 'yes' ? 'Waiting For Response' : 'Confirmed') + '"' + (pl === 'yes' ? ' class="on"' : '') + (busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Coming</button>'
        + '<button data-w256set="' + s.e + '|' + (pl === 'no' ? 'Waiting For Response' : 'Unavailable') + '"' + (pl === 'no' ? ' class="on"' : '') + (busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Can\u2019t make it</button></span></div>';
    }).join('');
    return '<details class="srv"' + (P.srvOpen ? ' open' : '') + '><summary>Their next servings</summary>' + who + rows + '</details>';
  }
  function threadHtml() {
    if (P.newOpen) return newHtml();
    if (!P.ph) return '<div class="empty big"><span class="ic">' + ICO.phone + '</span><b>Pick a conversation</b><span>Texts and calls with each number show together. ' + (P.can ? 'Or start a New text.' : '') + '</span></div>';
    var th = P.thread || { ph: P.ph, messages: [] };
    var row = (P.inbox || []).filter(function (x) { return x.ph === P.ph; })[0] || {};
    var nm = th.n || th.cn || row.n; var name = nm || phf(P.ph);
    var head = '<div class="th"><button class="bk" data-w256go="back" aria-label="Back">\u2039</button><span class="av" style="background:hsl(' + hue(nm || P.ph) + ',36%,40%)">' + E(nm ? initials(nm) : '#') + '</span>'
      + '<span class="nm"><b>' + E(name) + '</b><span>' + (nm ? E(phf(P.ph)) : 'Unknown number') + (th.p || row.p ? ' \u00b7 <a href="#volunteering/people" data-w256person="' + (th.p || row.p) + '">volunteer record</a>' : th.cn ? ' \u00b7 in the CRM' : '') + '</span></span>'
      + '<a class="w191btn call" href="tel:+1' + E(P.ph) + '" data-w256call="' + E(P.ph) + '" title="Opens the Quo app with this number">' + ICO.phone + '<span>Call</span></a></div>';
    var items = P.thread ? timeline() : null;
    var body = items == null ? '<div class="empty">Loading\u2026</div>' : !items.length ? '<div class="empty">No texts or calls yet. ' + (P.can && !th.out ? 'Write the first text below.' : '') + '</div>'
      : items.map(function (m) {
        if (m.k === 'call') return callCard(m);
        var lab = m.dir === 'out' ? (m.quo || m.by === 'Quo app' ? 'Typed in Quo' : (KIND[m.kind] || '') + (m.kind === 'reply' && m.by ? ' \u00b7 ' + String(m.by).split('@')[0] : '')) : '';
        var st = m.dir === 'out' && m.st === 'queued' ? (P.win ? 'Sending\u2026' : 'Waits for 9 am') : m.st === 'failed' ? 'Not delivered' + (m.err ? ': ' + m.err : '') : m.st === 'skipped' ? 'Not sent' + (m.err ? ': ' + m.err : '') : '';
        return '<div class="m ' + (m.dir === 'in' ? 'in' : 'out') + '"><div class="bub ' + (m.dir === 'in' ? 'in' : 'out') + (m.st === 'failed' || m.st === 'skipped' ? ' bad' : '') + '">' + E(m.body || '').replace(/\n/g, '<br>') + '</div><div class="mt">' + E([lab, m.ev, when(m.at), st].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ')) + '</div></div>';
      }).join('');
    /* what waits: their text, a missed call; a reply typed in Quo in the last minutes already answers */
    var lines = '';
    if (P.can && items) {
      var tIn = 0, tMe = 0; items.forEach(function (m) { if (m.k !== 'text') return; var t = ms(m.at); if (m.dir === 'in') tIn = Math.max(tIn, t); else if (m.quo || m.kind === 'reply' || m.kind === 'manual') tMe = Math.max(tMe, t); });
      if (row.waitT && !(tMe && tMe > tIn)) lines += '<div class="wl"><span><i class="dot"></i>' + (row.openT > 1 ? row.openT + ' texts wait' : 'Their text waits') + ' for an answer</span><button class="lnk" data-w256go="textdone">No reply needed</button></div>';
      if (row.missed) lines += '<div class="wl miss"><span>' + ICO.missed + (row.missed > 1 ? row.missed + ' missed calls' : 'A missed call') + ' to return</span><span><a class="lnk" href="tel:+1' + E(P.ph) + '" data-w256call="' + E(P.ph) + '">Call back</a><button class="lnk" data-w256go="calldone">No call back needed</button></span></div>';
    }
    var foot = th.out ? '<div class="note warn">They texted STOP, so the Hub cannot text them. If they text START to the ops line, texts work again. You can still call.</div>'
      : !P.can ? '<div class="note">Only leaders, HR, admins and the volunteer team can text from the Hub. Quo has the same conversation on the ops line.</div>'
      : lines + '<div class="rp"><textarea rows="2" placeholder="Text from the ops line" data-w256ta>' + E(P.drafts[P.ph] || '') + '</textarea><button class="w191btn" data-w256go="send">' + (P.win ? 'Send' : 'Send at 9 am') + '</button></div>';
    return head + placesHtml(th) + '<div class="msgs" id="w256msgs">' + body + '</div>' + foot;
  }
  function root() { return document.getElementById('w256'); }
  function paint() {
    var host = document.getElementById('volunteering'); if (!host || !here()) return;
    var el = root();
    if (!el) {
      if (typeof waVolPaint === 'function') waVolPaint('<div id="w256" class="w256"></div>'); else host.innerHTML = '<div id="w256" class="w256"></div>';
      el = root(); if (!el) return;
    }
    keepFocus(function () {
      el.innerHTML = headHtml() + '<div class="conv' + (P.ph || P.newOpen ? ' has' : '') + '">' + listHtml() + '<div class="thr" id="w256thr">' + threadHtml() + '</div></div>';
    });
    scrollEnd();
  }
  function paintThread() {
    var t = document.getElementById('w256thr'); if (!t) { paint(); return; }
    keepFocus(function () { t.innerHTML = threadHtml(); var c = root() && root().querySelector('.conv'); if (c) c.classList.toggle('has', !!(P.ph || P.newOpen)); });
    scrollEnd();
  }
  function paintList() {
    var r = root(); if (!r) { paint(); return; }
    var ls = r.querySelector('.ls'); var hd = r.querySelector('.st');
    keepFocus(function () {
      var tmp = document.createElement('div'); tmp.innerHTML = headHtml();
      var oldHd = r.querySelector('.hd'); if (oldHd && tmp.querySelector('.hd')) oldHd.replaceWith(tmp.querySelector('.hd'));
      if (hd && tmp.querySelector('.st')) hd.replaceWith(tmp.querySelector('.st'));
      if (ls) { var t2 = document.createElement('div'); t2.innerHTML = listHtml(); var nl = t2.querySelector('.ls'); if (nl) ls.replaceWith(nl); }
      r.querySelectorAll('[data-w256f]').forEach(function (b) { if (b.closest('.seg')) { b.classList.toggle('on', b.getAttribute('data-w256f') === P.filter); } });
    });
  }
  /* a redraw keeps the box being typed in, and where the cursor was */
  function keepFocus(fn) {
    var ae = document.activeElement, key = null, s = 0, e = 0;
    if (ae && ae.getAttribute) ['data-w256ta', 'data-w256q', 'data-w256look', 'data-w256name'].forEach(function (k) { if (ae.getAttribute(k) !== null) key = k; });
    if (key) { try { s = ae.selectionStart; e = ae.selectionEnd; } catch (x) { /* fine */ } }
    fn();
    if (key) { var n = root() && root().querySelector('[' + key + ']'); if (n) { try { n.focus(); n.setSelectionRange(s, e); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } } }
  }
  function scrollEnd() { var m = document.getElementById('w256msgs'); if (m && !m.__w256) { m.__w256 = 1; m.scrollTop = m.scrollHeight; } }

  async function render() {
    if (!staff()) return;
    paint();
    await loadInbox(true);
    if (!here()) return;
    paint();
    if (P.ph && !P.thread) loadThread(true);
    tick();
  }
  function tick() {
    clearInterval(P.timer);
    P.timer = setInterval(async function () {
      if (!here() || document.hidden) return;
      await loadInbox(true); if (!here()) return; paintList();
      if (P.ph && !P.newOpen) { var before = JSON.stringify([P.thread, P.calls]); await loadThread(false); if (JSON.stringify([P.thread, P.calls]) === before) { /* nothing new */ } }
    }, 30000);
  }

  /* ---------- actions ---------- */
  async function send(btn) {
    var ta = root() && root().querySelector('[data-w256ta]'); if (!ta) return; var v = ta.value.trim(); if (!v) { ta.focus(); return; }
    btn.disabled = true;
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('wa191_text_reply', { p_phone: P.ph, p_body: v }); if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      ta.value = ''; delete P.drafts[P.ph]; T(r.data && r.data.sends_now ? 'Sent from the ops line' : 'Queued: it goes at 9 am');
      setTimeout(function () { loadThread(true); }, r.data && r.data.sends_now ? 2500 : 300);
      loadInbox(true).then(paintList);
    } catch (e) { T('Could not send: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 140)); }
    btn.disabled = false;
  }
  async function textDone(btn) {
    btn.disabled = true;
    try { var r = await db.rpc('wa191_text_done', { p_phone: P.ph }); if (r && r.error) throw r.error; T('Marked as needing no reply'); await loadInbox(true); paintList(); paintThread(); }
    catch (e) { btn.disabled = false; T('Could not save: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 120)); }
  }
  async function callDone(btn) {
    btn.disabled = true;
    try { var r = await db.rpc('wa256_call_done', { p_phone: P.ph }); if (r && r.error) throw r.error; T('Marked: no call back needed'); await loadInbox(true); await loadThread(false); paintList(); }
    catch (e) { btn.disabled = false; T('Could not save: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 120)); }
  }
  async function check(btn) {
    if (P.checking) return; P.checking = true; paintList();
    try {
      var r = await db.functions.invoke('quo-calls', { body: { action: 'sync' } });
      var d = (r && r.data) || {};
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error; if (d.error) throw new Error(d.error);
      T(d.seen ? 'Calls checked: ' + d.seen + ' from Quo' : 'Calls checked: nothing new');
    } catch (e) { T('Could not check the calls: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 120)); }
    P.checking = false; await loadInbox(true); paintList(); if (P.ph) loadThread(false);
  }
  async function setPlace(evId, status) {
    var th = P.thread; if (!th || P.setting != null) return;
    var name = String(P.names[th.ph] || '').trim();
    if (!th.p && !th.cn && name.length < 2) { T('Type their name first: this number is not in the volunteer directory yet.'); var ni = root() && root().querySelector('[data-w256name]'); if (ni) ni.focus(); return; }
    P.setting = evId; P.srvOpen = true; paintThread();
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('wa191_text_roster', { p_phone: th.ph, p_event: evId, p_status: status, p_name: name || null, p_role: null });
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error; var d = r.data || {};
      var who = String(d.name || 'They').split(' ')[0];
      T(status === 'Confirmed' ? who + ' is coming on ' + fmtDay(d.date) + (d.added ? ' (added to the roster)' : '') : status === 'Unavailable' ? who + ' can\u2019t make it on ' + fmtDay(d.date) : who + ' is back to waiting for ' + fmtDay(d.date));
      if (d.new_person) delete P.names[th.ph];
      try { WA_VOL_LOADED = false; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    } catch (e) { T('Could not save: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 120)); }
    P.setting = null; await loadThread(false); loadInbox(true).then(paintList);
  }
  function how() {
    var tel = 'https://support.quo.com/core-concepts/calling/click-to-call';
    try {
      openModal('<div class="w256how"><h3>Calls from the Hub</h3>'
        + '<p class="m-sub">Quo can tell the Hub about calls, but it cannot start one from a web page. So <b>Call</b> hands the number to the Quo app, and the call goes out from the ops line ' + OPS + '.</p>'
        + '<ol><li><b>On a computer:</b> install the Quo desktop app and make it the computer\u2019s calling app (Windows: Default apps, TEL; Mac: FaceTime settings, Default for calls). Then Call opens Quo with the number filled in: press Call there. <a href="' + tel + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Quo\u2019s steps</a></li>'
        + '<li><b>On a phone:</b> Call opens the phone\u2019s dialer or Quo. To call from the ops line, choose Quo (on Android it can be the default dialer).</li>'
        + '<li><b>In Quo, pick the Welfare Phone line</b> (' + OPS + ') before calling, so volunteers see the number they know.</li></ol>'
        + '<p class="m-sub">Every call on the ops line shows here within 10 minutes (or press Check now): missed calls, answered calls and how long, voicemails, and Quo\u2019s summary when Quo makes one. A missed call waits on this page until someone calls or texts back.</p>'

  function lookup(q) {

      paintThread();
    }, 280);
  }

/* =====================================================================
   WA v257 Where everyone is placed, and placing them (15/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (15/10/26): "the calendar should be its own page. And once you click into a serving on the calendar, it opens
   this upcoming serving page." "For the people section of volunteer manager, like the volunteers page ... I can't see
   what ... upcoming events each individual is signed up for, which doesn't give me an idea of where they are placed."
   "In applicants received, I can't really open ... their profile up and assign them to an event. ... when we get the
   application, we can assign them to an event and they get the notification on text and email ... Once I assign her to
   an event or a few ... that confirmation goes to [her] ... please confirm ... we can have her confirmed early." "For
   applicants received and volunteers, I should be able to see when their upcoming volunteering dates are, if they've
   confirmed to them or not ... it needs to be easier to assign them to events rather than just keep them as an applicant
   that's received."

   - Calendar, its own page (#volunteering/calendar; the rail: Servings > Servings, Calendar): the month, bigger, each
     serving with its spots filled; a click opens the serving. #volunteering (Servings) keeps This week, the next serving
     and every upcoming serving, without the month.
   - Upcoming servings on each person: Volunteers (under the name) and Applicants received (on the card): each upcoming
     serving they are on, with the role and where it stands (Confirmed; Signed up, not confirmed; Placed, waiting to
     confirm; Role to choose; Can't make it), each opening the serving. Volunteers adds On a serving / Not placed yet.
   - Assign to servings (Volunteers: Assign on the row; Applicants received: the card's gold button): the next ten
     servings with the role's open spots and where the person already is; one role for all or per serving; Tell them by
     text and email (leaders, HR, admins) with the exact text first (wa257_preview, rolled back); from an application,
     it moves to Welcomed with the dates. wa257_assign places them (Waiting For Response, confirm_early: their page lets
     them confirm at once), texts once (kind signup; 9 am to 7 pm; STOP) and emails once (volunteer_assigned). */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var GREEN = '#103F39', CREAM = '#FFFAF2', GOLD = '#DCA527', FLAG = '#B3402F', TEAL = '#2A8C7A', INK = '#1F201E', MUTED = '#797773';
  var BODY = "'Space Grotesk','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif", HEAD = "'Barlow Condensed','Arial Narrow','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  var ROLES = [['server', 'Server'], ['cook', 'Cook'], ['driver', 'Driver'], ['dishwasher', 'Dishwasher'], ['team_leader', 'Team leader'], ['community_debrief', 'Community lead'], ['photographer', 'Photographer'], ['videographer', 'Videographer']];
  var U = { at: 0, by: null, evs: [], loading: null, filter: '' };
  var A = { p: null, app: null, opt: null, picked: {}, role: '', roles: {}, text: true, email: true, welcome: true, prev: null, prevT: null, busy: false, seq: 0 };

  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function T(m) { try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(m); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').toLowerCase(); }
  function roleName(k) { k = lower(k); if (k === 'community_talker') k = 'community_debrief'; var x = ROLES.filter(function (r) { return r[0] === k; })[0]; return x ? x[1] : (k === 'volunteer' ? 'Volunteer' : k ? k.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + k.slice(1).replace(/_/g, ' ') : ''); }
  function todayIso() { var d = new Date(), p = function (n) { return (n < 10 ? '0' : '') + n; }; return d.getFullYear() + '-' + p(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + p(d.getDate()); }
  function dayTxt(iso) { try { return new Date(String(iso).slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return String(iso || ''); } }
  function msgOf(e) { return String((e && (e.message || e.details)) || e || '').replace(/^.*?ERROR:\s*/, '').slice(0, 160); }
  function here(sub) { var h = String(location.hash || ''); return sub === '' ? /^#volunteering\/?$/.test(h) : h.indexOf('#volunteering/' + sub) === 0; }

  /* ---------- where people are placed: the upcoming servings' rosters, once for the page ---------- */
  function upcomingEvents() {
    var t = todayIso();
    return ((typeof WA_VOL_EVENTS !== 'undefined' ? WA_VOL_EVENTS : []) || []).filter(function (e) {
      return e && (e.kind || 'service') === 'service' && String(e.event_date) >= t && e.status !== 'cancelled' && e.status !== 'completed';
    }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.event_date) < String(b.event_date) ? -1 : 1; });
  }
  async function loadPlaces(force) {
    if (!force && U.by && Date.now() - U.at < 30000) return U.by;
    if (U.loading) return U.loading;
    U.loading = (async function () {
      try {
        /* the servings themselves (not only the calendar's list), so every upcoming serving counts */
        var evs = [];
        try {
          var q = await db.from('volunteer_events').select('id,code,title,event_date,status,kind').gte('event_date', todayIso()).order('event_date', { ascending: true });
          evs = ((q && q.data) || []).filter(function (e) { return (e.kind || 'service') === 'service' && e.status !== 'cancelled' && e.status !== 'completed' && String(e.event_date) >= todayIso(); });
        } catch (e) { evs = []; }
        if (!evs.length) evs = upcomingEvents();
        U.evs = evs;
        var by = {};
        if (evs.length) {
          var r = await db.from('event_roster').select('id,event_id,person_id,role,status,confirm_status,detail').in('event_id', evs.map(function (e) { return e.id; }));
          var ev = {}; evs.forEach(function (e) { ev[e.id] = e; });
          ((r && r.data) || []).forEach(function (x) {
            if (!x.person_id || !ev[x.event_id] || lower(x.status) === 'cancelled') return;
            (by[x.person_id] = by[x.person_id] || []).push({ id: x.id, e: ev[x.event_id], role: x.role, status: x.status, cs: x.confirm_status, d: x.detail || {} });
          });
          Object.keys(by).forEach(function (k) { by[k].sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.e.event_date) < String(b.e.event_date) ? -1 : 1; }); });
        }
        U.by = by; U.at = Date.now();
      } catch (e) { U.by = U.by || {}; }
      U.loading = null;
      return U.by;
    })();
    return U.loading;
  }
  function stateOf(x) {
    var s = lower(x.status), c = lower(x.cs);
    if (s === 'confirmed' || c === 'confirmed') return ['ok', 'Confirmed'];
    if (s === 'unavailable' || s === 'declined' || c === 'declined') return ['no', 'Can\u2019t make it'];
    if (lower(x.role) === 'volunteer' && x.d && x.d.role_choices) return ['wait', 'Role to choose'];
    if (s === 'tbd') return ['wait', 'Signed up, not confirmed'];
    if (s === 'waiting for response') return ['wait', x.d && (x.d.confirm_early || x.d.source === 'assigned') ? 'Placed, waiting to confirm' : x.d && x.d.source === 'recruit' ? 'Invited, no answer yet' : 'Waiting to confirm'];
    return ['wait', x.status || 'On the list'];
  }
  function placesHtml(pid, compact) {
    var rows = (U.by && U.by[pid]) || [];
    if (!rows.length) return '<div class="w257up none"><span class="w257lbl">Upcoming servings</span><span class="w257no">Not on a serving yet</span></div>';
    return '<div class="w257up"><span class="w257lbl">Upcoming servings</span>' + rows.map(function (x) {
      var st = stateOf(x); var role = lower(x.role) === 'volunteer' && x.d && x.d.role_choices ? (x.d.role_choices || []).map(roleName).join(' or ') : roleName(x.role);
      return '<a class="w257pl ' + st[0] + '" href="#volunteering/e/' + x.e.id + '" title="' + E((x.e.code || '') + (x.e.title ? ' \u00b7 ' + x.e.title : '') + ': open the serving') + '"><b>' + E(dayTxt(x.e.event_date)) + '</b><span>' + E(role) + '</span><i>' + E(st[1]) + '</i></a>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }

  /* ---------- Volunteers: the row's servings and Assign; On a serving / Not placed yet ---------- */
  function decoratePeople() {
    if (!here('people')) return;
    var sec = document.getElementById('volunteering'); if (!sec) return;
    var rows = sec.querySelectorAll('tr[data-p26]'); if (!rows.length) return;
    if (!U.by) { loadPlaces(false).then(decoratePeople); return; }
    var on = 0, off = 0;
    rows.forEach(function (tr) {
      var pid = tr.getAttribute('data-p26'); var has = !!((U.by[pid] || []).length); if (has) on++; else off++;
      tr.classList.toggle('w257hide', (U.filter === 'on' && !has) || (U.filter === 'off' && has));
      if (tr.getAttribute('data-w257') === String(U.at)) return;
      tr.setAttribute('data-w257', String(U.at));
      var td = tr.querySelector('td'); if (!td) return;
      var old = td.querySelector('.w257up'); if (old) old.remove();
      td.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', placesHtml(pid, true));
      if (staff()) {
        var last = tr.querySelector('td:last-child');
        if (last && !last.querySelector('[data-w257assign]')) last.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', '<button type="button" class="w257as" data-w257assign="' + E(pid) + '">Assign</button>');
      }
    });
    var bar = sec.querySelector('.w257bar');
    var html = '<span class="w257lbl">Placed</span>' + [['', 'Everyone'], ['on', 'On a serving (' + on + ')'], ['off', 'Not placed yet (' + off + ')']].map(function (x) {
      return '<span class="fchip' + (U.filter === x[0] ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w257f="' + x[0] + '">' + E(x[1]) + '</span>';
    }).join('');
    if (!bar) {
      var anchor = sec.querySelector('.w239bar') || sec.querySelector('.w26search');
      if (!anchor) return;
      bar = document.createElement('div'); bar.className = 'w257bar'; anchor.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', bar);
    }
    if (bar.__h !== html) { bar.innerHTML = html; bar.__h = html; }
  }

  /* ---------- Applicants received: the card's servings and Assign to servings ---------- */
  function appOf(id) { try { return ((window.w209 && window.w209.state().list) || []).filter(function (a) { return String(a.id) === String(id); })[0] || null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function decorateApplicants() {
    if (!here('applicants')) return;
    var cards = document.querySelectorAll('#volunteering li.w209app[data-w209id]'); if (!cards.length) return;
    if (!U.by) { loadPlaces(false).then(decorateApplicants); return; }
    cards.forEach(function (li) {
      var a = appOf(li.getAttribute('data-w209id')); if (!a || !a.person_id) return;
      if (li.getAttribute('data-w257') === String(U.at) && li.querySelector('.w257up')) return;
      li.setAttribute('data-w257', String(U.at));
      var old = li.querySelector('.w257up'); if (old) old.remove();
      var acts = li.querySelector('.w209acts');
      var html = placesHtml(a.person_id);
      if (acts) acts.insertAdjacentHTML('beforebegin', html); else li.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html);
      if (acts && staff() && !acts.querySelector('[data-w257assign]')) acts.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', '<button type="button" class="w197b sm pri w257go" data-w257assign="' + E(a.person_id) + '" data-w257app="' + E(a.id) + '">Assign to servings</button>');
    });
  }

  /* ---------- Calendar, its own page; Servings without the month ---------- */
  function sectionMode() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('volunteering'); if (!sec) return;
    var mode = here('calendar') ? 'cal' : here('') ? 'home' : '';
    if (sec.getAttribute('data-w257') !== mode) { if (mode) sec.setAttribute('data-w257', mode); else sec.removeAttribute('data-w257'); }
    var m = sec.querySelector('.w118mast');
    if (m && mode) {
      var h1 = m.querySelector('h1'), p = m.querySelector(':scope > p');
      var want = mode === 'cal' ? ['Calendar', 'Every serving, month by month. Click one to open it.'] : ['Servings', 'This week and every upcoming serving'];
      if (h1 && h1.textContent !== want[0]) h1.textContent = want[0];
      if (p && p.textContent !== want[1]) p.textContent = want[1];
    }
    /* the classic chips: Servings and Calendar */
    var sub = sec.querySelector('.vl-sub');
    if (sub && !sub.querySelector('[data-w257cal]')) {
      var c0 = sub.querySelector('[data-vl="calendar"]');
      if (c0) { c0.textContent = 'Servings'; c0.classList.toggle('on', mode === 'home'); c0.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend', '<a class="fchip' + (mode === 'cal' ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w257cal="1" href="#volunteering/calendar">Calendar</a>'); }
    }
    if (mode === 'cal') calChips();
  }
  /* each serving on the month: its code and spots filled (wave234's numbers) */
  function calChips() {
    var cal = document.getElementById('w119cal'); if (!cal) return;
    cal.querySelectorAll('.ev[data-vlev]').forEach(function (el) {
      var f = null; try { f = window.w234 && window.w234.fillOf ? window.w234.fillOf(Number(el.getAttribute('data-vlev'))) : null; } catch (e) { f = null; }
      var e = ((typeof WA_VOL_EVENTS !== 'undefined' ? WA_VOL_EVENTS : []) || []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === el.getAttribute('data-vlev'); })[0] || {};
      var line = (e.code || '') + (f && f.total ? ' \u00b7 ' + f.filled + '/' + f.total + ' filled' + (f.waiting ? ', ' + f.waiting + ' to confirm' : '') : '');
      var s = el.querySelector('.w257ln');
      if (!s) { el.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<span class="w257ln"></span>'); s = el.querySelector('.w257ln'); }
      if (s.textContent !== line) s.textContent = line;
    });
  }

  /* ---------- Assign to servings ---------- */
  async function openAssign(pid, appId) {
    A.p = Number(pid); A.app = appId ? Number(appId) : null; A.opt = null; A.picked = {}; A.roles = {}; A.prev = null; A.busy = false; A.welcome = !!appId;
    try { openModal('<div class="w257m" id="w257m"><p class="m-sub">Loading their servings\u2026</p></div>'); } catch (e) { return; }
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('wa257_assign_options', { p_person: A.p }); if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      A.opt = r.data || {};
    } catch (e) { var m = document.getElementById('w257m'); if (m) m.innerHTML = '<p class="m-sub">Could not load: ' + E(msgOf(e)) + '</p><div class="m-foot"><button class="btn" data-w257x>Close</button></div>'; return; }
    var pr = (A.opt.person || {}), mine = (A.opt.roles || pr.roles || []).map(lower).filter(function (k) { return ROLES.some(function (x) { return x[0] === k; }); });
    /* the role they are already on for an upcoming serving, else the first role they applied for */
    var onRole = ((A.opt.servings || []).filter(function (s) { return s.mine && ROLES.some(function (x) { return x[0] === lower(s.mine.role); }); })[0] || {}).mine;
    A.role = (onRole && lower(onRole.role)) || mine[0] || 'server';
    (A.opt.servings || []).forEach(function (s) {
      if (s.mine && !/^(confirmed)$/i.test(s.mine.status || '') && lower(s.mine.cs) !== 'confirmed' && !/^unavailable$/i.test(s.mine.status || '')) A.picked[s.id] = true;
    });
    A.text = !!A.opt.can_tell && !!pr.phone; A.email = !!A.opt.can_tell && !!pr.email;
    paintAssign(); schedPreview();
  }
  function openOf(s, role) {
    var need = Number((s.spots || {})[role] || 0), have = Number((s.filled || {})[role] || 0);
    if (s.mine && lower(s.mine.role) === role) have = Math.max(0, have - 1);
    return { need: need, have: have, open: need - have };
  }
  function roleFor(s) { return A.roles[s.id] || A.role; }
  function assignHtml() {
    var o = A.opt || {}, pr = o.person || {}, sv = o.servings || [];
    var mineRoles = (pr.roles || []).map(lower);
    var n = Object.keys(A.picked).filter(function (k) { return A.picked[k]; }).length;
    var roleSel = '<div class="w257roles" role="group" aria-label="Role">' + ROLES.map(function (r) {
      return '<button type="button" data-w257role="' + r[0] + '" class="' + (A.role === r[0] ? 'on' : '') + '" aria-pressed="' + (A.role === r[0]) + '">' + E(r[1]) + (mineRoles.indexOf(r[0]) >= 0 ? ' <small>applied</small>' : '') + '</button>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
    var list = sv.length ? sv.map(function (s) {
      var role = roleFor(s), op = openOf(s, role), st = s.mine ? stateOf({ role: s.mine.role, status: s.mine.status, cs: s.mine.cs, d: { role_choices: s.mine.choices } }) : null;
      var confirmed = st && st[0] === 'ok';
      var spot = op.need ? (op.open > 0 ? op.open + ' of ' + op.need + ' ' + roleName(role).toLowerCase() + ' spots open' : 'all ' + op.need + ' ' + roleName(role).toLowerCase() + ' spots taken') : 'no ' + roleName(role).toLowerCase() + ' spot on this serving';
      return '<label class="w257sv' + (A.picked[s.id] ? ' on' : '') + (op.open <= 0 ? ' full' : '') + '"><input type="checkbox" data-w257pick="' + s.id + '"' + (A.picked[s.id] ? ' checked' : '') + '>'
        + '<span class="d"><b>' + E(dayTxt(s.date)) + '</b><span>' + E([s.code, s.title, s.start].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ')) + '</span></span>'
        + '<span class="sp ' + (op.open > 0 ? 'ok' : 'no') + '">' + E(spot) + '</span>'
        + (s.mine ? '<span class="mn ' + st[0] + '">Already on: ' + E(roleName(s.mine.role)) + ', ' + E(st[1].toLowerCase()) + (confirmed && A.picked[s.id] ? ' (stays confirmed)' : '') + '</span>' : '')
        + (A.picked[s.id] ? '<select data-w257rrole="' + s.id + '" aria-label="Role on ' + E(dayTxt(s.date)) + '">' + ROLES.map(function (r) { return '<option value="' + r[0] + '"' + (role === r[0] ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(r[1]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' : '')
        + '</label>';
    }).join('') : '<p class="m-sub">No upcoming servings.</p>';
    var can = !!o.can_tell;
    var tell = '<div class="w257tell"><b>Tell them</b>'
      + '<label><input type="checkbox" data-w257t="text"' + (A.text ? ' checked' : '') + (can && pr.phone ? '' : ' disabled') + '> Text ' + (pr.phone ? E(pr.phone.replace(/(\d{3})(\d{3})(\d{4})/, '($1) $2-$3')) : '<i>(no phone number)</i>') + '</label>'
      + '<label><input type="checkbox" data-w257t="email"' + (A.email ? ' checked' : '') + (can && pr.email ? '' : ' disabled') + '> Email ' + (pr.email ? E(pr.email) : '<i>(no email)</i>') + '</label>'
      + (A.app ? '<label><input type="checkbox" data-w257t="welcome"' + (A.welcome ? ' checked' : '') + '> Mark the application Welcomed</label>' : '')
      + (can ? '' : '<p class="m-sub">Only leaders, HR, admins and the volunteer team can text or email volunteers: they will be placed without a message.</p>') + '</div>';
    var pv = A.prev, prevBox = '';
    if (n) {
      prevBox = '<div class="w257pv">' + (pv === null ? '<p class="m-sub">Working out what they will get\u2026</p>' : pv && pv.error ? '<p class="m-sub warn">' + E(pv.error) + '</p>'
        : '<div class="ln">' + E(pv.to_confirm ? (pv.to_confirm + ' serving' + (pv.to_confirm === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' to confirm') : 'Nothing new to confirm') + (pv.already_confirmed ? ' \u00b7 ' + pv.already_confirmed + ' already confirmed' : '') + '</div>'
          + (A.text && pv.body ? '<div class="bub">' + E(pv.body) + '</div><div class="ln sm">Text: ' + E(pv.text) + '</div>' : '')
          + (A.email && pv.to_confirm ? '<div class="ln sm">Email to ' + E(pv.email_to || '') + ': ' + E(pv.email) + ' (\u201cYou\u2019re on the team\u201d, with the dates and a Confirm button)</div>' : '')
          + '<div class="ln sm">Their page lets them confirm right away, whatever the date.</div>') + '</div>';
    }
    var go = n ? (A.text || A.email ? 'Place on ' + n + ' serving' + (n === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' and tell them' : 'Place on ' + n + ' serving' + (n === 1 ? '' : 's')) : 'Pick a serving';
    return '<div class="w257m" id="w257m"><h3>Place ' + E(pr.name || 'them') + ' on servings</h3>'
      + '<p class="m-sub">Applied for: ' + E(((pr.roles || []).map(roleName).join(', ')) || 'no role on file') + '. Pick the role, then the servings.</p>'
      + roleSel + '<div class="w257svs">' + list + '</div>' + tell + prevBox
      + '<div class="m-foot"><button class="btn ghost" data-w257x>Cancel</button><button class="btn" data-w257do' + (n && !A.busy ? '' : ' disabled') + '>' + E(A.busy ? 'Placing\u2026' : go) + '</button></div></div>';
  }
  function paintAssign() {
    var m = document.getElementById('w257m'); if (!m) return;
    var box = document.getElementById('modalBox'), st = box ? box.scrollTop : 0;
    var t = document.createElement('div'); t.innerHTML = assignHtml(); m.replaceWith(t.firstChild);
    if (box) box.scrollTop = st;
  }
  function picked() { return Object.keys(A.picked).filter(function (k) { return A.picked[k]; }).map(Number); }
  function rolesArg() { var o = {}; picked().forEach(function (id) { if (A.roles[id] && A.roles[id] !== A.role) o[String(id)] = A.roles[id]; }); return Object.keys(o).length ? o : null; }
  function schedPreview() { clearTimeout(A.prevT); if (!picked().length) { A.prev = null; paintAssign(); return; } A.prevT = setTimeout(preview, 350); }
  async function preview() {
    var my = ++A.seq; A.prev = null; paintAssign();
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('wa257_preview', { p_person: A.p, p_events: picked(), p_role: A.role, p_roles: rolesArg() });
      if (my !== A.seq) return; if (r && r.error) throw r.error; A.prev = r.data || {};
    } catch (e) { if (my !== A.seq) return; A.prev = { error: 'Could not preview: ' + msgOf(e) }; }
    paintAssign();
  }
  async function doAssign() {
    if (A.busy || !picked().length) return;
    A.busy = true; paintAssign();
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('wa257_assign', { p_person: A.p, p_events: picked(), p_role: A.role, p_text: !!A.text, p_email: !!A.email, p_app: A.app && A.welcome ? A.app : null, p_roles: rolesArg() });
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      var d = r.data || {}, nm = String(((A.opt || {}).person || {}).name || 'They').split(' ')[0];
      var told = [d.text && d.text !== 'not asked' ? 'text: ' + d.text : '', d.email && d.email !== 'not asked' ? 'email: ' + d.email : ''].filter(Boolean).join('; ');
      T(nm + ' is on ' + picked().length + ' serving' + (picked().length === 1 ? '' : 's') + (told ? ' (' + told + ')' : ''));
      try { closeModal(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      try { WA_VOL_LOADED = false; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      await loadPlaces(true);
      try { if (A.app && window.w209 && window.w209.load) { await window.w209.load(); if (window.w209.paint) window.w209.paint(); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      try { var st234 = window.w234 && window.w234.state ? window.w234.state() : null; if (st234) st234.at = 0; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      decoratePeople(); decorateApplicants();
    } catch (e) { A.busy = false; paintAssign(); T('Could not place them: ' + msgOf(e)); }
  }

  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var b;
    if ((b = t.closest('[data-w257assign]'))) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); openAssign(b.getAttribute('data-w257assign'), b.getAttribute('data-w257app')); return; }
    if ((b = t.closest('[data-w257f]'))) { U.filter = b.getAttribute('data-w257f'); var bar = document.querySelector('.w257bar'); if (bar) bar.__h = ''; document.querySelectorAll('#volunteering tr[data-p26]').forEach(function (tr) { tr.removeAttribute('data-w257'); }); decoratePeople(); return; }
    if (t.closest('[data-w257x]')) { try { closeModal(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } return; }
    if (t.closest('[data-w257do]')) { doAssign(); return; }
    if ((b = t.closest('#w257m [data-w257role]'))) { A.role = b.getAttribute('data-w257role'); A.roles = {}; paintAssign(); schedPreview(); return; }
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.getAttribute || !t.closest || !t.closest('#w257m')) return;
    var k;
    if ((k = t.getAttribute('data-w257pick'))) { A.picked[k] = t.checked; if (!t.checked) delete A.roles[k]; paintAssign(); schedPreview(); return; }
    if ((k = t.getAttribute('data-w257rrole'))) { A.roles[k] = t.value; paintAssign(); schedPreview(); return; }
    if ((k = t.getAttribute('data-w257t'))) { if (k === 'text') A.text = t.checked; else if (k === 'email') A.email = t.checked; else A.welcome = t.checked; paintAssign(); return; }
  });

  /* ---------- wiring: one quiet watcher on the Volunteering section (ignores its own nodes) ---------- */
  var pend = null;
  function run() { pend = null; try { sectionMode(); decoratePeople(); decorateApplicants(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function schedule() { if (pend) return; pend = setTimeout(run, 60); }
  function own(m) {
    var nodes = Array.prototype.slice.call(m.addedNodes || []).concat(Array.prototype.slice.call(m.removedNodes || []));
    return nodes.length && nodes.every(function (n) { return n.nodeType === 1 && (/\bw257/.test(n.className || '') || (n.querySelector && n.querySelector('.w257up') && n.classList && n.classList.contains('w257up'))); });
  }
  function watch() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('volunteering'); if (!sec || sec.getAttribute('data-w257mo')) return !!sec;
    sec.setAttribute('data-w257mo', '1');
    try { new MutationObserver(function (muts) { for (var i = 0; i < muts.length; i++) { if (!own(muts[i])) { schedule(); return; } } }).observe(sec, { childList: true, subtree: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    return true;
  }
  if (!watch()) { var tries = 0, iv = setInterval(function () { if (watch() || ++tries > 60) clearInterval(iv); }, 500); }

  }

  function teamOfHash() {

  function reqsFor(d) {
    try { return (window.w123 && window.w123.items) ? window.w123.items(d, 'open') : []; } catch (e) { return []; }
  }
  function reqBlock(d) {
    var items = reqsFor(d);
    var reqs = items.filter(function (it) { return it.k === 'r'; }), sugs = items.filter(function (it) { return it.k === 's'; });
    if (!reqs.length) return '';
    var shown = reqs.slice(0, 4);
    return '<div class="w116card" id="w258req" data-d="' + E(d) + '" data-n="' + reqs.length + '"><h3>Asked of ' + E(label(d)) + ' (' + reqs.length + ') <span class="lnk" data-w116go="#requests/team/' + E(d) + '">open the team inbox</span></h3>'
      + '<p class="w258sub">What other teams asked ' + E(label(d)) + ' for, in their meetings or through Requests. Open one to move it along.</p>'
      + shown.map(function (it) {
        var r = it.x || {};
        var need = r.needed_by || null, late = need && String(need).slice(0, 10) < todayKey();
        var who = String(r.requested_by || '').replace(/^Meeting AI \(accepted by (.+)\)$/, '$1 (from a meeting)') || 'someone';
        var meta = ['from ' + E(who), E(ago(r.created_at))];
        if (need) meta.push('<span class="' + (late ? 'late' : '') + '">needed by ' + E(day(need)) + '</span>');
        var st = String(r.status || 'requested'); st = st.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + st.slice(1);
        return '<div class="w258rr" data-w258r="' + E(r.id) + '" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-label="' + E(r.title) + '"><div class="tx"><div class="t">' + E(r.title) + '</div><div class="m">' + meta.join(' &middot; ') + '</div></div><span class="w258pill">' + E(st) + '</span></div>';
      }).join('')
      + (reqs.length > shown.length || sugs.length ? '<p class="w258more">' + (reqs.length > shown.length ? 'And ' + (reqs.length - shown.length) + ' more. ' : '') + (sugs.length ? sugs.length + (sugs.length === 1 ? ' ask' : ' asks') + ' the Hub heard in meetings ' + (sugs.length === 1 ? 'waits' : 'wait') + ' to be accepted. ' : '') + '<a data-w116go="#requests/team/' + E(d) + '">Open the team inbox</a></p>' : '')
      + '</div>';
  }
  var REQV = { loading: false };
  function placeReq(host, d) {
    var cur = document.getElementById('w258req');
    if (!staff()) { if (cur) cur.remove(); return; }
    /* wave287: not on the Overview, where the Team inbox card holds the same requests with more actions; Agenda and Tasks keep it */
    var c0 = W116cur(); if (c0 && c0.d === d && c0.page === 'overview') { if (cur) cur.remove(); return; }
    if (window.w123 && !window.w123.loaded()) {
      if (!REQV.loading) { REQV.loading = true; Promise.resolve(window.w123.load()).then(function () { REQV.loading = false; schedule(); }, function () { REQV.loading = false; }); }
      return;

    var seg = host.querySelector(':scope > .w116seg');
    var html = reqBlock(d);

    if (!html) { if (cur) cur.remove(); return; }
    if (cur && cur.parentNode === host && cur.getAttribute('data-v') === ver && (!seg || cur.nextElementSibling === seg)) return;
    if (cur) cur.remove();

    if (seg) host.insertBefore(el, seg); else host.insertBefore(el, host.firstChild);
  }
  function fixFundraising(host) {
    var c = W116cur(); if (!c || c.d !== 'fundraising') return;
    /* the page's tabs: Grants, beside the Events calendar */
    var seg = host.querySelector(':scope > .w116seg');
    if (seg && canSee('grants') && !seg.querySelector('[data-w116go="#grants"]')) {
      var a = document.createElement('a'); a.setAttribute('data-w116go', '#grants'); a.textContent = 'Grants'; seg.appendChild(a);
    }
    /* Overview: the totals without grants */
    if (c.page === 'overview') {
      var rows = window.w116TasksFor ? window.w116TasksFor('fundraising', null, null) : [];
      var tdy = todayKey(), since = new Date(Date.now() - 30 * 86400000).toISOString();
      var open = rows.filter(function (t) { return !t.done; });
      var vals = { open: open.length, overdue: open.filter(function (t) { return t.due_date && t.due_date < tdy; }).length, done: rows.filter(function (t) { return t.done && ((t.closed_at && t.closed_at >= since) || (!t.closed_at && t.updated_at && t.updated_at >= since)); }).length };
      var tiles = host.querySelectorAll('.w116kpis > .w116kpi');
      Array.prototype.forEach.call(tiles, function (k, i) {
        var q = k.getAttribute('data-w116q'), key = q === 'overdue' ? 'overdue' : q === 'done' ? 'done' : (i === 0 && !q ? 'open' : null);
        if (!key) return; var b = k.querySelector('b'); if (b && b.textContent !== String(vals[key])) b.textContent = String(vals[key]);
        if (key === 'overdue') k.classList.toggle('warn', vals.overdue > 0);
      });
    }
    /* Tasks: the Grants group becomes a pointer */
    var slot = document.getElementById('w116tasks');
    if (slot && c.page === 'tasks') {
      ['bgrants', 'wGrants'].forEach(function (k) {
        var g = slot.querySelector('.w116g[data-g="' + k + '"]'); if (!g) return;
        var p = document.createElement('div'); p.className = 'w258ptr'; p.setAttribute('data-w258ptr', '1');
        p.innerHTML = '<span class="dot"></span><span>Grants has its own page now: <a data-w116go="#grants">open Grants</a></span>';
        g.parentNode.replaceChild(p, g);
      });
    }
  }

  function statsHtml() {

    var soon = rows.filter(function (t) { return t.due_date && t.due_date >= tdy && t.due_date <= wkKey; }).length;
    var none = rows.filter(function (t) { return !t.due_date; }).length;
    return '<span><b>' + rows.length + '</b>open</span><span><b class="' + (over ? 'warn' : '') + '">' + over + '</b>overdue</span><span><b>' + soon + '</b>due this week</span><span><b>' + none + '</b>with no date</span>';
  }
