/* =====================================================================
   WA v258 The volunteer texts, clear and checked (15/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (15/10/26, after the operations meeting): "all of them should be able to text"; "Clarify what automated texts are
   going out to the volunteers so that we know going forward what [and] when"; confirmations "a week and a half before the
   event"; "the Thursday before"; "they need to check a box saying they must cancel four days before ... All the roles
   should say that"; "the cooks should have been able to choose their recipe before then". The meeting: Ibrahim checks
   the texts went ("Are there some logs?"), the rows copied from the old sheet are checked by their owners, Mary owns
   cooks, drivers and team leaders, Terry servers, the dishwasher and the photographer.

   - The serving page: a Texts strip under the stages: every automatic text for this serving with its date (Confirmation
     10 days before at 10 am, Monday reminder, the last day to cancel, Thursday check-in, Itinerary, Thank-you and survey)
     and how many went; Text log; My roles; Role owners. More > Text log too.
   - Text log (wa258_text_log): each person, their answer (and the 4-day box), every text the Hub sent them with what Quo
     says happened to it (delivered, waiting for 9 am, failed and why, skipped), their replies; filters (Not delivered, Not
     answered, My roles); Check delivery now (quo-send v7 delivery); Text the ones not answered (the serving's Text to
     confirm).
   - The roster cards: "4-day rule agreed", "Cancelled late", the last text and its delivery, "No phone number", and on a
     row copied from the old sheet "From the old sheet: check" with Keep (wa258_sheet_keep) or Remove (the card's own Take
     off). My roles dims the other roles' columns.
   - Applicants received: My roles in the role filter (parts209), and on each card who last texted them from the Hub
     (wa258_contacted), so two people do not text the same person. Volunteers rows too.
   - Role owners (app_config volunteer_role_owners; leaders and admins change it).
   Texting itself (Ibrahim, Mary, Terry): wa256_can_text and quo-send v7; the client checks in 171, 191 and 239 ask it.
   ASCII only. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (window.__wa258v) return; window.__wa258v = true;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', CREAM = '#FFFAF2', GOLD = '#DCA527', FLAG = '#B3402F', TEAL = '#2A8C7A', MUTED = '#797773';
  var S = { owners: null, ownersAt: 0, ownersBusy: null, mineOn: false, log: {}, logAt: {}, logBusy: {}, cont: {}, contAt: 0, contBusy: null, filter: 'all', checking: false };
  try { S.mineOn = localStorage.getItem('wa258mine') === '1'; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  var ROLES = [['team_leader', 'Team leader'], ['cook', 'Cook'], ['driver', 'Driver'], ['server', 'Server'], ['dishwasher', 'Dishwasher'], ['photographer', 'Photographer'], ['videographer', 'Videographer'], ['community_debrief', 'Community lead']];
  var KIND = { confirm: 'Confirmation', reminder: 'Monday reminder', checkin: 'Thursday check-in', signup: 'Sign-up text', recruit: 'Invitation', reconfirm: 'Still in?', itinerary: 'Itinerary', survey: 'Survey', ack: 'Thank-you', manual: 'Text', reply: 'Reply' };

  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function T(m) { try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(m); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').toLowerCase(); }
  function msgOf(e) { return String((e && (e.message || e.details)) || e || '').replace(/^.*?ERROR:\s*/, '').slice(0, 160); }
  function roleName(k) { k = lower(k); if (k === 'community_talker') k = 'community_debrief'; var x = ROLES.filter(function (r) { return r[0] === k; })[0]; if (x) return x[1]; try { return waVolRoleLabel(k); } catch (e) { return k; } }
  function dayTxt(iso) { try { return new Date(String(iso).slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return String(iso); } }
  function hm(h) { var m = String(h || '').match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/); if (!m) return ''; var n = Number(m[1]); return (n % 12 || 12) + (m[2] === '00' ? '' : ':' + m[2]) + (n < 12 ? ' am' : ' pm'); }
  function whenTxt(ts) {
    if (!ts) return ''; var d = new Date(ts); if (isNaN(d)) return '';
    return d.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }) + ' ' + d.toLocaleTimeString('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto', hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' }).replace(/\./g, '').toLowerCase();
  }
  function evId() { try { return (typeof WA_VOL_EVENT !== 'undefined' && WA_VOL_EVENT) ? Number(WA_VOL_EVENT) : null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function detail() { try { return (WA_VOL_DETAIL && WA_VOL_DETAIL.id === WA_VOL_EVENT) ? WA_VOL_DETAIL : null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function onServing() { return /^#volunteering\/e\/\d+/.test(String(location.hash || '')) && !!document.getElementById('w119'); }
  function here(sub) { return String(location.hash || '').indexOf('#volunteering/' + sub) === 0; }
  function gone(r) { var s = lower(r.status); return s === 'cancelled' || s === 'unavailable' || r.confirm_status === 'declined'; }
  function nameOf(email) { var n = S.owners && S.owners.names && S.owners.names[lower(email)]; return n ? String(n).split(' ')[0] : String(email || '').split('@')[0]; }

  /* ---------- data ---------- */
  function mine() { return (S.owners && S.owners.mine) || []; }
  async function loadOwners(force) {
    if (!staff()) return null;
    if (!force && S.owners && Date.now() - S.ownersAt < 120000) return S.owners;
    if (S.ownersBusy) return S.ownersBusy;
    S.ownersBusy = (async function () {
      try { var r = await db.rpc('wa258_role_owners'); if (r && !r.error && r.data) { S.owners = r.data; S.ownersAt = Date.now(); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      S.ownersBusy = null; return S.owners;
    })();
    return S.ownersBusy;
  }
  async function loadLog(id, force) {
    if (!id || !staff()) return null;
    if (!force && S.log[id] && Date.now() - (S.logAt[id] || 0) < 30000) return S.log[id];
    if (S.logBusy[id]) return S.logBusy[id];
    S.logBusy[id] = (async function () {
      try { var r = await db.rpc('wa258_text_log', { p_event: id }); if (r && !r.error && r.data) { S.log[id] = r.data; S.logAt[id] = Date.now(); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      S.logBusy[id] = null; return S.log[id] || null;
    })();
    return S.logBusy[id];
  }
  async function loadContacted(ids, force) {
    ids = (ids || []).filter(function (x) { return x; }).map(Number);
    var want = ids.filter(function (x) { return !(String(x) in S.cont); });
    if (!force && !want.length && Date.now() - S.contAt < 60000) return S.cont;
    if (S.contBusy) return S.contBusy;
    S.contBusy = (async function () {
      try {
        var r = await db.rpc('wa258_contacted', { p_people: ids.slice(0, 400) });
        if (r && !r.error) { ids.forEach(function (x) { S.cont[String(x)] = null; }); Object.keys(r.data || {}).forEach(function (k) { S.cont[k] = r.data[k]; }); S.contAt = Date.now(); }
      } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      S.contBusy = null; return S.cont;
    })();
    return S.contBusy;
  }

  /* ---------- words ---------- */
  function textLabel(x) {
    var k = KIND[x.kind] || x.kind;
    if (x.kind === 'manual' || x.kind === 'reply') k = (x.kind === 'reply' ? 'Reply' : 'Text') + (x.by && x.by.indexOf('@') > 0 ? ' from ' + nameOf(x.by) : x.by === 'Quo app' ? ' from the Quo app' : '');
    var st = x.status === 'queued' ? ['wait', 'waiting to send'] : x.status === 'failed' ? ['bad', 'failed' + (x.error ? ': ' + x.error : '')] : x.status === 'skipped' ? ['mute', 'skipped' + (x.error ? ': ' + x.error : '')]
      : x.delivery === 'delivered' ? ['ok', 'delivered'] : (x.delivery === 'undelivered' || x.delivery === 'failed') ? ['bad', 'not delivered'] : ['', 'sent'];
    return { cls: st[0], txt: k + ': ' + st[1], at: whenTxt(x.at) };
  }
  function lastAuto(lr) {
    var auto = (lr && lr.texts || []).filter(function (x) { return ['confirm', 'reminder', 'checkin', 'signup', 'recruit', 'reconfirm'].indexOf(x.kind) >= 0; });
    return auto.length ? auto[auto.length - 1] : null;
  }
  function answerOf(r) {
    if (lower(r.status) === 'confirmed' || r.cs === 'confirmed') return ['ok', 'Confirmed' + (r.rule ? ', 4-day rule agreed' : r.via === 'reply' ? ' by text' : '')];
    if (lower(r.status) === 'unavailable' || r.cs === 'declined') return ['bad', 'Can\u2019t make it' + (r.late ? ' (late)' : '')];
    return ['wait', 'Not answered yet'];
  }
  function schedLine(s, L) {
    var n = 0; (L.rows || []).forEach(function (r) { (r.texts || []).forEach(function (x) { if (x.kind === s.kind && x.status === 'sent') n++; }); });
    var at = String(s.at || ''), d = at.slice(0, 10), t = at.slice(11, 16);
    var past = d < new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    var state = !s.on ? 'off' : n ? n + ' sent' : s.kind === 'deadline' ? '' : past ? 'none sent' : 'planned';
    return { label: s.label, when: dayTxt(d) + (t ? ', ' + hm(t) : ''), who: s.who, state: state, on: s.on, done: n > 0, kind: s.kind };
  }

  /* ---------- the serving page: the Texts strip ---------- */
  function stripHtml(L) {
    var sch = (L.schedule || []).map(function (s) { return schedLine(s, L); });
    var own = S.owners && S.owners.owners ? groupOwners(S.owners.owners) : '';
    return '<div class="w258strip" data-w258="strip"><div class="hd"><b>Texts to volunteers</b><span>When texts are on (Messages to volunteers, below): 9 am to 7 pm, from the ops line. Each one once.</span></div>'
      + '<ol class="sch">' + sch.map(function (x) {
        return '<li class="' + (x.kind === 'deadline' ? 'dl' : x.done ? 'done' : !x.on ? 'off' : '') + '" title="' + E(x.who) + '"><b>' + E(x.label) + '</b><span>' + E(x.when) + '</span>' + (x.state ? '<i>' + E(x.state) + '</i>' : '') + '</li>';
      }).join('') + '</ol>'
      + '<div class="ft">' + (own ? '<span class="own">' + own + '</span>' : '')
      + '<span class="bt"><button type="button" class="w258b pri" data-w258go="log">Text log</button>'
      + (mine().length ? '<button type="button" class="w258b' + (S.mineOn ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w258go="mine" aria-pressed="' + S.mineOn + '">My roles</button>' : '')
      + '<button type="button" class="w258b" data-w258go="owners">Role owners</button></span></div></div>';
  }
  function groupOwners(o) {
    var by = {}; Object.keys(o || {}).forEach(function (k) { var e = lower(o[k]); (by[e] = by[e] || []).push(roleName(k).toLowerCase() + 's'); });
    return Object.keys(by).map(function (e) { return '<em>' + E(nameOf(e)) + '</em>: ' + E(by[e].join(', ')); }).join(' \u00b7 ');
  }
  /* wave273: what they told us on their page, on their card */
  function answerRows(r) {
    var d = r.detail || {}, c = r.confirm || {}, role = String(r.role || '').toLowerCase(), rows = [];
    function add(k, v) { if (v !== undefined && v !== null && String(v).trim() !== '') rows.push([k, String(v)]); }
    if (role === 'cook') {
      var loc = c.cook_location === 'home' ? 'Pick-up' : c.cook_location === 'drop off' ? 'Drop-off' : (d.pickup_dropoff && d.pickup_dropoff !== 'TBD' ? d.pickup_dropoff : '');
      if (!window.w304) add('Food', loc);   /* wave304: editable on the card instead */
      if (loc === 'Pick-up' && !window.w304) add('Address', [c.address || d.address, c.postal_code].filter(Boolean).join(', ') + (c.neighbourhood ? ' (' + c.neighbourhood + ')' : ''));
      var rc = (S.recipes || {})[String(r.recipe_id)]; add('Cooking', rc || d.type_of_food);
      var ing = c.ingredients === 'need' ? 'Needs them sent' : c.ingredients === 'own' ? 'Using their own' : d.ingredients_delivered === 'Yes' ? 'Needs them sent' : d.ingredients_delivered === 'No' ? 'Using their own' : '';
      if (!window.w304) add('Ingredients', ing);
      if (!window.w304 && Array.isArray(c.delivery_windows) && c.delivery_windows.length) add('Delivery days', c.delivery_windows.map(function (k) { return { wed: 'Wed', thu: 'Thu', fri: 'Fri', sun: 'Sun' }[k] || k; }).join(', '));
      if (c.stay_to_serve === true) add('Stays to serve', 'Yes'); else if (c.stay_to_serve === false) add('Stays to serve', 'No');
    } else if (role === 'driver') {
      add('Car', [c.vehicle, c.model].filter(Boolean).join(' ') || (S.cars || {})[String(r.person_id)]);
    } else if (role === 'server') {
      add('Bringing', c.donations);
    } else if (role === 'photographer' || role === 'videographer') {
      add('Will share', c.photos_share);
    } else if (role === 'community_debrief') {
      if (c.briefed_at) add('Briefing', 'Read');
    }
    if (c.guide_read === true) add('Guide', 'Read');
    add('Their note', c.note);
    return rows;
  }
  function answersHtml(r) {
    if (gone(r)) return '';
    var c = r.confirm || {}, rows = answerRows(r), by = c.via === 'reply' ? 'by text' : c.via === 'form' ? 'by the team' : c.via === 'link' ? 'on their page' : '';
    var head = '';
    if (r.confirm_status === 'confirmed' || c.attending === 'yes') head = 'Said yes ' + by + (c.at ? ', ' + new Date(c.at).toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }) : '');
    if (head && c.via === 'reply' && !rows.length) head += ': no details yet';
    if (!head && !rows.length) return '';
    return '<div class="w273a">' + (head ? '<p class="h">' + E(head) + '</p>' : '') + rows.map(function (x) { return '<p><span>' + E(x[0]) + '</span> ' + E(x[1]) + '</p>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  async function loadAnswerNames(rosters) {
    if (S.ansBusy) return; S.ansBusy = true;
    try {
      if (!S.recipes) { var a = await db.from('recipes').select('id,name'); S.recipes = {}; ((a && a.data) || []).forEach(function (x) { S.recipes[String(x.id)] = x.name; }); }
      var ids = rosters.filter(function (r) { return String(r.role || '').toLowerCase() === 'driver' && r.person_id; }).map(function (r) { return r.person_id; });
      S.cars = S.cars || {};
      var todo = ids.filter(function (i) { return !(String(i) in S.cars); });
      if (todo.length) { var b = await db.from('volunteer_people').select('id,car_type').in('id', todo); todo.forEach(function (i) { S.cars[String(i)] = ''; }); ((b && b.data) || []).forEach(function (x) { S.cars[String(x.id)] = x.car_type || ''; }); }
    } catch (e) { /* the card shows what it has */ }
    S.ansBusy = false; soon();
  }
  function cardTags(r, lr) {
    var d = r.detail || {}, c = r.confirm || {}, out = [];
    if (c.cancel_rule_ok === true || c.cancel_rule_ok === 'true') out.push('<span class="w258t ok" title="Ticked on their page: tell us at least 4 days before">4-day rule agreed</span>');
    if (c.late_cancel) out.push('<span class="w258t bad">Cancelled late</span>');
    if (lr && !lr.partner && !lr.phone && !gone(r)) out.push('<span class="w258t bad">No phone number</span>');
    var la = lastAuto(lr); if (la) { var l = textLabel(la); out.push('<span class="w258t ' + l.cls + '" title="' + E(l.at) + '">' + E(l.txt.replace(/: (.{40}).+$/, ': $1\u2026')) + '</span>'); }
    var sheet = d.source === 'sheet' && !d.sheet_checked && !d.partner && !gone(r);
    var h = answersHtml(r) + (window.w304 ? window.w304.html(r) : '') + out.join('');   /* wave304: a cook's food and ingredients */
    if (sheet) h += '<span class="w258sheet">From the old sheet: check' + (staff() ? '<span class="bt"><button type="button" data-w258keep="' + r.id + '" title="Keep ' + E(r.person_name || 'them') + ' on this serving">Keep</button><button type="button" data-w258rm="' + r.id + '" title="Take ' + E(r.person_name || 'them') + ' off this serving">Remove</button></span>' : '') + '</span>';
    return h;
  }
  function decorateServing() {
    if (!onServing() || !staff()) return;
    var id = evId(); if (!id) return;
    var L = S.log[id];
    if (!L || Date.now() - (S.logAt[id] || 0) > 30000) { loadLog(id).then(function (x) { if (x) soon(); }); if (!L) return; }
    if (!S.owners) { loadOwners().then(function (x) { if (x) soon(); }); }
    /* the strip, under the stages */
    var bar = document.querySelector('#w119 .w191bar'), head = bar && bar.parentElement && bar.parentElement.id !== 'w119' ? bar.parentElement : bar;   /* after the green header */
    var strip = document.querySelector('#w119 .w258strip');
    var html = stripHtml(L);
    if (head) {
      if (!strip) { var w = document.createElement('div'); w.innerHTML = html; strip = w.firstChild; head.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', strip); strip.__h = html; }
      else if (strip.__h !== html) { strip.outerHTML = html; strip = document.querySelector('#w119 .w258strip'); if (strip) strip.__h = html; }
    }
    /* More > Text log */
    var menu = document.querySelector('#w119 .acts .menu');
    if (menu && !menu.querySelector('[data-w258go="log"]')) {
      var t171 = menu.querySelector('[data-w191go="text171"]');
      var b = document.createElement('button'); b.setAttribute('data-w258go', 'log'); b.textContent = 'Text log';
      if (t171) t171.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', b); else menu.appendChild(b);
    }
    /* the cards */
    var D = detail(); if (!D) return;
    var rows = {}; (D.roster || []).forEach(function (r) { rows[String(r.id)] = r; });
    if (!S.recipes || (D.roster || []).some(function (r) { return String(r.role || '').toLowerCase() === 'driver' && r.person_id && !(S.cars && String(r.person_id) in S.cars); })) loadAnswerNames(D.roster || []);
    var lrs = {}; (L.rows || []).forEach(function (r) { lrs[String(r.id)] = r; });
    document.querySelectorAll('#w119 .pc[data-w119card]').forEach(function (pc) {
      var k = pc.getAttribute('data-w119card'), r = rows[k]; if (!r) return;
      var h = cardTags(r, lrs[k]);
      var box = pc.querySelector('.w258c');
      if (!h) { if (box) box.remove(); return; }
      if (box && box.__h === h) return;
      if (!box) { box = document.createElement('div'); box.className = 'w258c'; pc.appendChild(box); }
      box.innerHTML = h; box.__h = h;
    });
    /* My roles: the other roles' columns dimmed */
    var mi = mine(), on = S.mineOn && mi.length > 0;
    document.querySelectorAll('#w119 .rc[data-w119role]').forEach(function (rc) {
      var role = lower(rc.getAttribute('data-w119role')); var yours = mi.indexOf(role) >= 0;
      rc.classList.toggle('w258dim', on && !yours);
      var hd = rc.querySelector('.h'); var chip = rc.querySelector('.w258yours');
      if (yours && hd && !chip) hd.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<span class="w258yours">Yours</span>');
      if (!yours && chip) chip.remove();
    });
  }

  /* ---------- the Text log ---------- */
  function counts(L) {
    var c = { all: 0, undel: 0, noans: 0, mine: 0 }, mi = mine();
    (L.rows || []).forEach(function (r) {
      if (r.partner) return; c.all++;
      if (bad(r)) c.undel++;
      if (answerOf(r)[0] === 'wait') c.noans++;
      if (mi.indexOf(lower(r.role)) >= 0) c.mine++;
    });
    return c;
  }
  function bad(r) {
    if (!r.phone) return true;
    var la = lastAuto(r); if (!la) return answerOf(r)[0] === 'wait';
    return la.status === 'failed' || la.status === 'skipped' || la.delivery === 'undelivered' || la.delivery === 'failed';
  }
  function logHtml(L) {
    var ev = L.event || {}, c = counts(L), mi = mine();
    var rows = (L.rows || []).filter(function (r) {
      if (r.partner) return false;
      if (S.filter === 'undel') return bad(r);
      if (S.filter === 'noans') return answerOf(r)[0] === 'wait';
      if (S.filter === 'mine') return mi.indexOf(lower(r.role)) >= 0;
      return true;
    });
    var sch = (L.schedule || []).map(function (s) { return schedLine(s, L); });
    var chips = [['all', 'Everyone', c.all], ['undel', 'Not delivered or no phone', c.undel], ['noans', 'Not answered', c.noans]].concat(mi.length ? [['mine', 'My roles', c.mine]] : []);
    return '<div class="w258m" id="w258m"><h3>Text log: ' + E(dayTxt(ev.date)) + (ev.code ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(ev.code) : '') + '</h3>'
      + '<p class="m-sub">Every text the Hub sent for this serving, what Quo says happened to it, and who answered. Texts go between 9 am and 7 pm' + (L.mode && L.mode !== 'live' ? ' (texts are in ' + E(L.mode) + ' mode)' : '') + '. Anyone who has to cancel must tell us ' + E(L.notice_days || 4) + ' days before.</p>'
      + '<ol class="w258sch">' + sch.map(function (x) { return '<li class="' + (x.kind === 'deadline' ? 'dl' : x.done ? 'done' : !x.on ? 'off' : '') + '"><b>' + E(x.label) + '</b><span>' + E(x.when) + '</span><em>' + E(x.who) + '</em>' + (x.state ? '<i>' + E(x.state) + '</i>' : '') + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ol>'
      + '<div class="w258f">' + chips.map(function (x) { return '<button type="button" class="fchip' + (S.filter === x[0] ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w258f="' + x[0] + '">' + E(x[1]) + ' (' + x[2] + ')</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
      + (rows.length ? '<ul class="w258list">' + rows.map(function (r) {
        var a = answerOf(r);
        var tx = (r.texts || []).map(function (x) { var l = textLabel(x); return '<span class="w258t ' + l.cls + '" title="' + E(l.at) + '">' + E(l.txt) + (l.at ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(l.at) : '') + '</span>'; }).join('');
        return '<li><div class="who"><b>' + E(r.name || 'Unnamed') + '</b><span>' + E(roleName(r.role)) + (r.source === 'sheet' ? ' \u00b7 from the old sheet' : '') + '</span></div>'
          + '<span class="w258t ' + a[0] + '">' + E(a[1]) + '</span>'
          + '<div class="tx">' + (tx || (r.phone ? '<span class="w258t mute">No text yet</span>' : '')) + (!r.phone ? '<span class="w258t bad">No phone number</span>' : '') + (r.replies ? '<span class="w258t">' + r.replies + ' repl' + (r.replies === 1 ? 'y' : 'ies') + '</span>' : '') + '</div></li>';
      }).join('') + '</ul>' : '<p class="m-sub">Nobody here.</p>')
      + '<div class="m-foot"><button type="button" class="btn" data-w258go="check"' + (S.checking ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (S.checking ? 'Checking with Quo\u2026' : 'Check delivery now') + '</button>'
      + (L.can_text && c.noans ? '<button type="button" class="btn" data-w258go="text">Text the ones not answered</button>' : '')
      + '<button type="button" class="btn primary" data-w258x>Close</button></div></div>';
  }
  async function openLog() {
    var id = evId(); if (!id) return;
    S.filter = 'all';
    try { openModal('<div class="w258m" id="w258m"><p class="m-sub">Loading the texts\u2026</p></div>'); } catch (e) { return; }
    await loadOwners();
    var L = await loadLog(id, true);
    paintLog(L);
  }
  function paintLog(L) {
    var m = document.getElementById('w258m'); if (!m) return;
    if (!L) { m.innerHTML = '<p class="m-sub">Could not load the texts.</p><div class="m-foot"><button class="btn" data-w258x>Close</button></div>'; return; }
    var w = document.createElement('div'); w.innerHTML = logHtml(L); m.replaceWith(w.firstChild);
  }
  async function checkDelivery() {
    var id = evId(); if (!id || S.checking) return;
    S.checking = true; paintLog(S.log[id]);
    try {
      var r = await db.functions.invoke('quo-send', { body: { action: 'delivery', event_id: id } });
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      var n = (r && r.data && r.data.checked) || 0;
      T(n ? 'Checked ' + n + ' text' + (n === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' with Quo' : 'Every text already has its final status');
    } catch (e) { T('Could not check: ' + msgOf(e)); }
    S.checking = false;
    var L = await loadLog(id, true); paintLog(L); soon();
  }

  /* ---------- Role owners ---------- */
  async function openOwners() {
    var o = await loadOwners(true);
    if (!o) { T('Could not load the role owners'); return; }
    var mem = []; try { mem = (DB_MEMBERS || []).filter(function (m) { return m && m.email; }); } catch (e) { mem = []; }
    var can = !!o.can_edit;
    var rows = ROLES.map(function (r) {
      var cur = lower((o.owners || {})[r[0]] || '');
      var opts = '<option value="">Nobody yet</option>' + mem.map(function (m) { return '<option value="' + E(lower(m.email)) + '"' + (lower(m.email) === cur ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(m.name || m.email) + '</option>'; }).join('')
        + (cur && !mem.some(function (m) { return lower(m.email) === cur; }) ? '<option value="' + E(cur) + '" selected>' + E(cur) + '</option>' : '');
      return '<label class="w258own"><span>' + E(r[1]) + 's</span>' + (can ? '<select data-w258own="' + r[0] + '">' + opts + '</select>' : '<b>' + E(cur ? nameOf(cur) : 'Nobody yet') + '</b>') + '</label>';
    }).join('');
    openModal('<div class="w258m" id="w258o"><h3>Role owners</h3><p class="m-sub">Who finds and texts the volunteers for each role. Their My roles shows only those roles on Applicants received and the serving page, and they hear when someone in their role cancels.' + (can ? '' : ' Leaders and admins change it.') + '</p>'
      + '<div class="w258owns">' + rows + '</div><div class="m-foot">' + (can ? '<button type="button" class="btn primary" data-w258go="ownsave">Save</button>' : '') + '<button type="button" class="btn" data-w258x>Close</button></div></div>');
  }
  async function saveOwners() {
    var p = {}; document.querySelectorAll('#w258o select[data-w258own]').forEach(function (s) { if (s.value) p[s.getAttribute('data-w258own')] = s.value; });
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('wa258_role_owners_set', { p: p }); if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      S.owners = r.data; S.ownersAt = Date.now(); T('Role owners saved'); closeModal(); soon(); repaintApplicants();
    } catch (e) { T('Could not save: ' + msgOf(e)); }
  }

  /* ---------- Applicants received and Volunteers: who last texted them ---------- */
  function contLine(pid) {
    var c = S.cont[String(pid)]; if (!c) return '';
    return '<p class="w258ct">Texted by ' + E(String(c.name || '').split(' ')[0] || 'the team') + ' ' + E(whenTxt(c.at)) + (c.kind && c.kind !== 'manual' && c.kind !== 'reply' ? ' (' + E((KIND[c.kind] || c.kind).toLowerCase()) + ')' : '') + '</p>';
  }
  function decoratePeople() {
    if (!staff()) return;
    var apps = here('applicants') ? document.querySelectorAll('#volunteering li.w209app[data-w209id]') : [];
    var vols = here('people') ? document.querySelectorAll('#volunteering tr[data-p26]') : [];
    if (!apps.length && !vols.length) return;
    var list = []; try { list = (window.w209 && window.w209.state().list) || []; } catch (e) { list = []; }
    var byApp = {}; list.forEach(function (a) { byApp[String(a.id)] = a; });
    var ids = [];
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(apps, function (li) { var a = byApp[li.getAttribute('data-w209id')]; if (a && a.person_id) ids.push(a.person_id); });
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(vols, function (tr) { var p = Number(tr.getAttribute('data-p26')); if (p) ids.push(p); });
    var missing = ids.filter(function (x) { return !(String(x) in S.cont); });
    if (missing.length || Date.now() - S.contAt > 60000) { if (!S.contBusy) loadContacted(ids, Date.now() - S.contAt > 60000).then(function () { soon(); }); if (missing.length === ids.length) return; }
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(apps, function (li) {
      var a = byApp[li.getAttribute('data-w209id')]; if (!a || !a.person_id) return;
      put(li, contLine(a.person_id), li.querySelector('.w209acts'));
    });
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(vols, function (tr) {
      var td = tr.querySelector('td'); if (!td) return;
      put(td, contLine(tr.getAttribute('data-p26')), null);
    });
  }
  function put(host, html, before) {
    var old = host.querySelector('.w258ct');
    if (!html) { if (old) old.remove(); return; }
    if (old && old.outerHTML === html) return;
    if (old) old.remove();
    if (before) before.insertAdjacentHTML('beforebegin', html); else host.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html);
  }
  var repainted = false;
  function repaintApplicants() {
    try { if (here('applicants') && window.w209 && window.w209.paint) { var sel = document.getElementById('w209role'); if (sel && mine().length && !sel.querySelector('option[value="__mine"]')) window.w209.paint(); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function applicantsMine() {
    if (!here('applicants') || !staff()) return;
    if (!S.owners) { loadOwners().then(function (o) { if (o && !repainted) { repainted = true; repaintApplicants(); } }); return; }
    repaintApplicants();
  }

  /* ---------- clicks ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var b = t.closest('[data-w258go]');
    if (b) {

/* =====================================================================
   WA v259 The Volunteer Manager refresh (15/10/26, late evening)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (15/10/26, 23:15): "the UI is just getting really messy. So take a look at Volunteer Manager, review everything
   ... and consider some sort of refresh so that navigation, applications, the phone, everything makes more sense."
   The review (claude/15-10-26 - Volunteer Manager refresh, proposal and the text schedule): two navigations that
   disagreed, six ways to text, a page per people list, "event" and "serving" mixed. This wave is parts A and C of
   the proposal; the serving page (B) and one placement window (D) follow.

   - Five places, the same on the rail (computer) and the chip row (phone), in the same words and order:
     Servings, Calendar | People, Messages | Setup. Every old address still opens (#volunteering/applicants,
     /signoffs, /sop, /recipes, /inventory, /phone; /messages is an alias of /phone).
   - People (#volunteering/people): one page, three tabs under the title: Volunteers, New applicants (n), Sign-offs (n).
     The tabs are wave239/257's Volunteers, wave209's Applicants received and wave195's Sign-offs, unchanged inside.
   - Messages (#volunteering/phone): wave256's page under its right name (the ops line: every conversation, Text a
     group, calls). The app bar's Text volunteers still opens the group text window.
   - Setup (#volunteering/setup, new): The SOP, Recipes and Inventory (as cards that open them); the usual spot counts
     (volunteer_role_guides.target, as wave256's "Make these the usual counts"); Role owners (wave258); Texts: the
     schedule for the next servings in plain words with its switches (wa259_text_setup, wa259_text_switch), Text
     settings (wave171) and Text log hints. Things touched once a season, out of the daily rail.
   - The rail marks the right place for every address (People for applicants and sign-offs; Setup for the SOP, recipes,
     inventory). Words: "serving" everywhere "event" meant one (020, 106, 119b); "Dishwashers" (209, 218); the Text
     settings window reads the real confirmation days.
   Reads only what the earlier waves expose (window.w209, w195, w256, w258, WA_VOL_VIEW). ASCII only. */

  function setupHtml() {
    var st = S.setup || {}, can = !!st.can_text, next = st.next || [], ev = next[0];
    var card = function (id, title, body, foot) { return '<section class="w259card" id="' + id + '"><h3>' + E(title) + '</h3>' + body + (foot ? '<div class="ft">' + foot + '</div>' : '') + '</section>'; };
    var h = '<div class="w259setup">';
    /* the three reference pages */
    h += '<div class="w259grid">'
      + '<a class="w259tile" href="#volunteering/sop"><b>The SOP</b><span>The master checklist every serving starts from, in six stages.</span></a>'
      + '<a class="w259tile" href="#volunteering/recipes"><b>Recipes</b><span>What the cooks make, with the recipe links.</span></a>'
      + '<a class="w259tile" href="#volunteering/inventory"><b>Inventory</b><span>The kit in storage, counted and reordered.</span></a></div>';
    /* texts */
    var sched = ev ? scheduleRows(ev) : [];
    var rows = sched.map(function (r) {
      return '<li class="' + (r.k === 'deadline' ? 'dl' : '') + (r.on ? '' : ' off') + '"><div class="w"><b>' + E(r.l) + '</b><span>' + E(dayTxt(r.when)) + (r.at ? ', ' + E(r.at) : '') + '</span></div><p>' + E(r.who) + '</p>'
        + (r.sw ? '<label class="sw"><input type="checkbox" data-w259sw="' + r.sw + '"' + (r.on ? ' checked' : '') + (can ? '' : ' disabled') + '><span>' + (r.on ? 'On' : 'Off') + '</span></label>' : '') + '</li>';
    }).join('');
    var others = next.slice(1).map(function (e) { return dayTxt(e.date) + ': confirmations ' + dayTxt(addDays(e.date, -num('sms_confirm_days', 10))) + ', reminder ' + dayTxt(addDays(e.date, -num('sms_reminder_days', 6))) + ', last day to cancel ' + dayTxt(addDays(e.date, -num('cancel_notice_days', 4))) + ', check-in ' + dayTxt(addDays(e.date, -num('sms_checkin_days', 3))); }).join('. ');
    h += card('w259texts', 'Texts to volunteers',
      '<p class="lead">Every automatic text, from the ops line 438-808-7754, between ' + E(hour12(num('sms_window_start', 9))) + ' and ' + E(hour12(num('sms_window_end', 19))) + ' Toronto time, once per person per serving. ' + (st.sms_mode && st.sms_mode !== 'live' ? '<b style="color:' + FLAG + '">Texts are in ' + E(st.sms_mode) + ' mode.</b> ' : '') + (ev ? 'For the next serving, <b>' + E(dayTxt(ev.date, true)) + '</b>' + (ev.code ? ' (' + E(ev.code) + ')' : '') + ':' : 'No upcoming serving.') + '</p>'
      + (sched.length ? '<ol class="w259sched">' + rows + '</ol>' : '')
      + (others ? '<p class="m">Then ' + E(others) + '.</p>' : '')
      + '<p class="m">Not on a schedule: the sign-up text the moment a form puts someone on a serving; the text and email when the team places someone (Assign to servings); invitations from Find volunteers; Still in?; the thank-you after a YES or NO reply. A confirmed volunteer who says they can\u2019t come on their page tells the role\u2019s owner and the volunteer team at once.</p>'
      + '<div class="w259days">' + [['sms_confirm_days', 'Confirmation, days before'], ['sms_reminder_days', 'Reminder, days before'], ['sms_checkin_days', 'Check-in, days before'], ['cancel_notice_days', 'Days\u2019 notice to cancel']].map(function (x) {
        return '<label><span>' + E(x[1]) + '</span><input type="number" min="1" max="30" value="' + E(num(x[0], 0) || '') + '" data-w259d="' + x[0] + '"' + (can ? '' : ' disabled') + '></label>';
      }).join('') + (can ? '<button type="button" class="w259b pri" data-w259go="days">Save the days</button>' : '<span class="m">Leaders, HR, admins and the volunteer team change these.</span>') + '</div>',
      '<button type="button" class="w259b" data-w171set="1">Text settings</button><span class="m">The mode, the test phone, each role\u2019s page, the survey and the itineraries.</span><span class="m">Who got what: open a serving, then <b>Text log</b>.</span>');
    /* the usual spot counts */
    var usual = st.usual || {};
    h += card('w259spots', 'The usual spot counts',
      '<p class="lead">How many of each role a serving needs unless it sets its own (a serving\u2019s More menu, Spot counts).</p><div class="w259spots">' + ROLES.map(function (r) {
        return '<label><span>' + E(r[1]) + '</span><input type="number" min="0" max="60" value="' + E(usual[r[0]] == null ? '' : usual[r[0]]) + '" data-w259sp="' + r[0] + '"' + (can ? '' : ' disabled') + '></label>';
      }).join('') + '</div>',
      can ? '<button type="button" class="w259b pri" data-w259go="spots">Save the usual counts</button>' : '<span class="m">Leaders, HR, admins and the volunteer team change these.</span>');
    /* role owners */
    h += card('w259owners', 'Role owners',
      '<p class="lead">Who finds and texts the volunteers for each role; their My roles shows those roles first and they hear when someone in their role cancels.</p>' + ownersLine(),
      '<button type="button" class="w259b" data-w259go="owners">Role owners</button>');
    h += '</div>';
    return h;
  }
  function ownersLine() {
    var o = null; try { o = window.w258v && window.w258v.owners && window.w258v.owners(); } catch (e) { o = null; }
    if (!o || !o.owners) return '<p class="m" id="w259ownl">Loading\u2026</p>';
    var by = {}; Object.keys(o.owners).forEach(function (k) { var e = String(o.owners[k] || '').toLowerCase(); (by[e] = by[e] || []).push((ROLES.filter(function (r) { return r[0] === k; })[0] || [k, k])[1].toLowerCase()); });
    return '<p class="m" id="w259ownl">' + Object.keys(by).map(function (e) { var n = (o.names && o.names[e]) || e; return '<b>' + E(String(n).split(' ')[0]) + '</b>: ' + E(by[e].join(', ')); }).join(' \u00b7 ') + '</p>';
  }
  async function renderSetup() {
    try { if (typeof waVolPaint === 'function') waVolPaint('<p class="vl-empty">Loading the setup\u2026</p>'); } catch (e) { return; }
    await loadSetup(true);
    try { if (window.w258v && window.w258v.loadOwners) await window.w258v.loadOwners(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    if (view() !== 'setup') return;
    if (S.err && !S.setup) { waVolPaint('<p class="vl-empty">Could not load the setup: ' + E(S.err) + '</p>'); return; }
    waVolPaint(setupHtml());
    masthead();
  }
  async function saveSpots() {
    var val = {}, usual = (S.setup && S.setup.usual) || {}, changed = [];
    document.querySelectorAll('#w259spots [data-w259sp]').forEach(function (inp) { var k = inp.getAttribute('data-w259sp'), n = Math.max(0, Math.min(60, Math.round(Number(inp.value)))); if (isFinite(n) && inp.value !== '') { val[k] = n; if (n !== Number(usual[k] || 0)) changed.push(k); } });
    if (!changed.length) { T('These are already the usual counts'); return; }
    for (var i = 0; i < changed.length; i++) {
      var k = changed[i]; var r = await db.from('volunteer_role_guides').update({ target: val[k] }).eq('role', k);
      if (r && r.error) { T('Could not save ' + k + ': ' + r.error.message); return; }
    }
    try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'volunteer_role_guides', entity_id: 'target', field: 'usual_spot_counts', old_value: JSON.stringify(usual), new_value: JSON.stringify(val), changed_by: (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || 'hub' }); } catch (e) { /* audit only */ }
    try { var st234 = window.w234 && window.w234.state ? window.w234.state() : null; if (st234) st234.at = 0; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    T('The usual counts are saved'); S.setupAt = 0; renderSetup();
  }
  async function saveDays() {
    var inputs = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('#w259texts [data-w259d]')), n = 0;
    for (var i = 0; i < inputs.length; i++) {
      var k = inputs[i].getAttribute('data-w259d'), v = String(Math.round(Number(inputs[i].value)) || '');
      if (!v || v === String(num(k, 0))) continue;
      try { var r = await db.rpc('wa259_text_switch', { p_key: k, p_value: v }); if (r && r.error) throw r.error; S.setup = r.data; S.setupAt = Date.now(); n++; } catch (e) { T('Could not save: ' + msgOf(e)); return; }
    }
    T(n ? 'Saved: the schedule below is updated' : 'Nothing changed'); if (n) renderSetup();
  }
  async function flip(sw, box) {
    box.disabled = true;
    try { var r = await db.rpc('wa259_text_switch', { p_key: sw, p_value: box.checked ? 'on' : 'off' }); if (r && r.error) throw r.error; S.setup = r.data; S.setupAt = Date.now(); T((sw === 'sms_reminder_on' ? 'Monday reminder' : 'Thursday check-in') + (box.checked ? ' on' : ' off')); renderSetup(); }
    catch (e) { box.checked = !box.checked; box.disabled = false; T('Could not change it: ' + msgOf(e)); }
  }

  /* ---------- the page title for the places wave191's map does not know ---------- */
  var TITLES = { setup: ['Setup', 'The SOP, recipes, inventory, the usual spot counts, role owners and the text schedule'], phone: ['Messages', 'The ops line: every conversation, group texts and calls'] };
  function masthead() {
    var v = view(); var t = TITLES[v]; if (!t) return;
    var sec = document.getElementById('volunteering'); if (!sec) return;
    var m = sec.querySelector('.w118mast'); if (!m) return;
    var h1 = m.querySelector('h1'), p = m.querySelector(':scope > p');
    if (h1 && h1.textContent !== t[0]) h1.textContent = t[0];
    if (p && p.textContent !== t[1]) p.textContent = t[1];
  }

  /* ---------- clicks ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var b = t.closest('[data-w259go]'); if (!b) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    var go = b.getAttribute('data-w259go');
    if (go === 'spots') saveSpots();
    else if (go === 'days') saveDays();
    else if (go === 'owners') { try { if (window.w258v && window.w258v.openOwners) window.w258v.openOwners(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } }
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.getAttribute) return;
    var sw = t.getAttribute('data-w259sw'); if (sw) flip(sw, t);
  }, true);

  /* ---------- styles ---------- */
  var css = document.createElement('style'); css.id = 'wa259css';
  css.textContent =
    /* on a computer the page title already says where you are: the card's own repeated header goes */
    '@media (min-width:901px){body.w153app.w153volunteer #volunteering > .card > h2:first-child,body.w153app.w153volunteer #volunteering > .card > h2:first-child + p.read,body.w153app.w153volunteer #volunteering > .card > h2:first-child + p.read + p.read{display:none;}}' +
    '.w259tabs{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:4px;margin:-4px 0 14px;border-bottom:2px solid rgba(16,63,57,.12);}' +
    '.w259tabs a{font-family:' + HEAD + ';font-weight:600;font-size:17px;letter-spacing:.4px;text-transform:uppercase;color:' + MUTED + ';text-decoration:none;padding:8px 14px 9px;margin-bottom:-2px;border-bottom:3px solid transparent;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;}' +
    '.w259tabs a:hover{color:' + GREEN + ';}.w259tabs a.on{color:' + GREEN + ';border-bottom-color:' + GOLD + ';}' +
    '.w259tabs a em{font-family:inherit;font-style:normal;font-size:12px;letter-spacing:0;background:' + GOLD + ';color:' + GREEN + ';border-radius:999px;padding:1px 7px;}' +
    '.w259setup{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:16px;}' +
    '.w259grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(200px,1fr));gap:10px;}' +
    '.w259tile{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px;padding:14px 16px;border-radius:14px;background:' + GREEN + ';color:' + CREAM + ';text-decoration:none;}.w259tile b{font-family:' + HEAD + ';font-size:20px;letter-spacing:.4px;text-transform:uppercase;}.w259tile span{font-size:12.5px;opacity:.85;}.w259tile:hover{background:#0c312c;}' +
    '.w259card{padding:16px 18px;border-radius:14px;background:' + CREAM + ';border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.14);}.w259card h3{font-family:' + HEAD + ';font-weight:600;font-size:22px;letter-spacing:.4px;text-transform:uppercase;color:' + GREEN + ';margin:0 0 6px;}' +
    '.w259card .lead{margin:0 0 10px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3c4a47;}.w259card .m{font-size:12.5px;color:' + MUTED + ';margin:8px 0 0;}.w259card .ft{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px 14px;align-items:center;margin-top:12px;}.w259card .ft .m{margin:0;}' +

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */
  /* /messages is /phone */
  function alias() { if (/^#volunteering\/messages/.test(location.hash || '')) { try { history.replaceState(null, '', '#volunteering/phone'); } catch (e) { location.hash = '#volunteering/phone'; return; } try { WA_VOL_VIEW = 'phone'; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } if (typeof waVolRender === 'function') waVolRender(); } }
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', alias);
  var pend = null;
  function soon() { if (pend) return; pend = setTimeout(function () { pend = null; try { fixRail(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } try { fixChips(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } try { placeTabs(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } try { masthead(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 80); }
  function arm() {
    if (typeof waVolRender === 'function' && !waVolRender.__w259) {
      var vr = waVolRender;
      waVolRender = async function () {
        if (view() === 'setup') { if (!staff()) { WA_VOL_VIEW = 'calendar'; return await vr.apply(this, arguments); } await renderSetup(); soon(); return; }
        var out = await vr.apply(this, arguments); soon(); return out;
      };
      waVolRender.__w259 = true;
    }
    railGroups();
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') { var sa = startApp; startApp = async function () { var r = await sa.apply(this, arguments); try { arm(); alias(); soon(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return r; }; }
  else arm();
  try {
    new MutationObserver(function (muts) {
      for (var i = 0; i < muts.length; i++) {
        var a = muts[i].addedNodes;
        for (var j = 0; j < a.length; j++) {
          var n = a[j]; if (!n || n.nodeType !== 1) continue;
          if (n.classList && (n.classList.contains('w259tabs') || n.hasAttribute('data-w259chip'))) continue;
          if (n.id === 'w153rail' || n.id === 'w153bar' || n.closest && (n.closest('#w153rail') || n.closest('#w153bar') || n.closest('#volunteering'))) { soon(); return; }
        }
      }
    }).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', soon);

  window.w259 = { peopleBadge: peopleBadge, setup: function () { return S; }, renderSetup: renderSetup, paint: soon, placeOf: placeOf };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v260 Train to unlock, and the Dashboard for the admins (16/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (16/10/26): "nobody should have access to the executive dashboard", then "Admins yes, add Shima";
   and "people need to do trainings for each page and or app before they start using it. Can we prevent
   individuals going on to different tools and pages with an indication that they first must do a training
   to unlock the page". His choices: the lock applies to everyone but the admins (current staff included);
   what unlocks a page is that page's tour.

   1. The Executive dashboard (page overview, and the Delivery numbers section dashboard) opens for the admins
      (hub_roles role admin) and the people in app_config dashboard_people (Shima first), whatever the access
      profile says. window.w260.trim() takes overview and dashboard out of wa40Allowed() for everyone else (wave40's
      list, and wave127's profile wrapper calls it last), so the menu, the Academy's tour list and every gate that
      reads wa40Allowed agree. A refused page (wave40's showPanel) lands on w260.landing(): Home, or the Board
      overview for directors, never the Dashboard; an address that was the Dashboard (#overview, #decisions,
      #dashboard) says so in one line, "The Dashboard is for the admins." A decision link (#decision/<id>, from
      search) opens the decision's team page at its decisions instead. wave130's notice lands on the same page.
   2. Train to unlock: a page with an active page tour (trainings tour:<key>) shows a lock card in its place until
      the person has finished that tour (training_progress done, the Academy's own record): the page's name, what it
      is for, "Take the n-minute tour to unlock it", Start the tour, Back to Home. Start the tour shows the page and
      runs its tour on it (wave122's engine, which records completion exactly as from the Academy); stopping midway
      locks the page again. Locked pages carry a small lock in the menu and still open (to the card). Never locked:
      Home, the Avenue Academy, people pages, Needs me, the sign-in and the must-answer pop-ups (they sit above the
      page), and any page without an active tour or whose tour the person cannot take. Admins never see a lock.
      The map of page to tour is app_config page_gate_map (built-in default below); the switch is page_gate
      (on/off), the people let through are page_gate_exempt. Every page arrival goes through showPanel, so menu
      clicks, links, notifications and deep links follow the same rule.
   3. Admin, Page tours (#adminpanel/tours): the switch, the people let through, who opens the Dashboard, and every
      person against every page tour (done, locked, or a page they cannot open), from wa260_page_tours(). Changes go
      through wa260_gate_set() (admins only, logged in change_log).
   Data: build/data/wa260_page_gate.sql. Ask the Hub is unchanged. ASCII only.
   ===================================================================== */

    + '.w260note{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;margin:0 0 14px;padding:11px 14px;border-radius:12px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.07);border-left:4px solid ' + GREEN + ';font-size:14px;color:' + INK + ';}'

  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- helpers ---------- */
  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function toastS(m) { try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(m); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function mine() { try { return (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE) || null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function known() { return !!mine(); }
  function role() { var m = mine(); return (m && m.role) || ''; }
  function isAdmin() { return role() === 'admin'; }
  function myEmail() { var m = mine(); var e = (m && m.email) || ''; if (!e) { try { e = (USER && USER.email) || ''; } catch (x) { e = ''; } } return lower(e); }
  function staffMode() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function allowed() { try { return (typeof wa40Allowed === 'function' && wa40Allowed()) || []; } catch (e) { return []; } }
  function rootOf(h) { var s = String(h == null ? location.hash : h); try { s = decodeURIComponent(s); } catch (e) { /* keep */ } return s.replace(/^#/, '').split('/')[0]; }
  function W() { return window.w122 || null; }
  function svg(n) {
    var P = {
      lock: '<rect x="5" y="10.5" width="14" height="10" rx="2.2"/><path d="M8.5 10.5V8a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 7 0v2.5"/>',
      play: '<path d="M8 5.5v13l10.5-6.5z"/>'
    };
    return '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">' + (P[n] || '') + '</svg>';
  }

  /* ---------- 1. who opens the Dashboard ---------- */
  function dashOk() {
    if (isAdmin()) return true;
    var em = myEmail(); if (!em) return false;
    return (S.cfg.dash || []).indexOf(em) >= 0;
  }
  function trim(a) {
    if (!Array.isArray(a) || !known()) return a;
    if (dashOk()) {
      var out = a.slice(); ['overview', 'dashboard'].forEach(function (t) { if (out.indexOf(t) < 0) out.push(t); }); return out;
    }
    return a.filter(function (t) { return t !== 'overview' && t !== 'dashboard'; });
  }
  /* Home for staff; directors (no Home in the board view) get the Board overview */
  function home() {
    var a = allowed();
    if (staffMode() && a.indexOf('mypage') >= 0) return 'mypage';
    var order = ['mypage', 'governance', 'committees', 'calendar', 'orgchart'];   /* wave272: directors land on Home */
    for (var i = 0; i < order.length; i++) if (a.indexOf(order[i]) >= 0 && document.getElementById(order[i])) return order[i];
    return 'mypage';
  }
  function landing() { return dashOk() ? 'overview' : home(); }
  function homeLabel() { var h = home(); return h === 'mypage' ? 'Home' : (NAMES[h] || 'Home'); }

  /* a decision's team (the same reading of the area as wave258's team decisions) */
  function areaDept(area) {
    var s = lower(area);
    var M = { 'ops mtl': 'opsmtl', 'program development': 'progdev', 'ops to': 'opsto', 'marketing': 'marketing', 'fundraising': 'fundraising', 'grants': 'fundraising', 'events': 'fundraising', 'finance': 'fundraising', 'hr': 'hr', 'board': 'board' };
    if (M[s]) return M[s];
    if (s.indexOf('program dev') >= 0) return 'progdev';
    if (s.indexOf('montreal') >= 0) return 'opsmtl';
    if (s.indexOf('toronto') >= 0) return 'opsto';
    if (s.indexOf('fund') >= 0 || s.indexOf('grant') >= 0 || s.indexOf('financ') >= 0 || s.indexOf('event') >= 0) return 'fundraising';
    if (s.indexOf('market') >= 0) return 'marketing';
    if (s.indexOf('human') >= 0 || s === 'hr') return 'hr';
    if (s.indexOf('board') >= 0) return 'board';
    return null;
  }
  function decisionTeam(id) {
    try {
      var list = typeof DB_DECISIONS !== 'undefined' ? DB_DECISIONS : [];
      var d = list.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id) || String(x.ref) === String(id); })[0];
      var t = d ? areaDept(d.area) : null;
      return t && allowed().indexOf(t) >= 0 ? t : null;
    } catch (e) { return null; }
  }
  /* a registered page (the Board overview) draws itself on its own route, which a fallback skips: draw it */
  function landed(id) {
    try {
      if (rootOf() === id) return;
      var pg = null; ((window.WA_NAV && WA_NAV.pages) || []).forEach(function (x) { if (x && x.id === id) pg = x; });
      if (pg && typeof pg.onShow === 'function') setTimeout(function () { try { if (activePanel() && activePanel().id === id) pg.onShow(id); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 0);
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  /* wave40's showPanel calls this for a page the person may not open */
  function fallback(id) {
    try {
      if (dashOk()) return 'overview';
      var parts = String(decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '')).replace(/^#/, '').split('/');
      var root = parts[0];
      if (root === 'decision' && parts[1]) {
        var t = decisionTeam(parts[1]);
        if (t) { S.decGo = { team: t, id: parts[1], at: Date.now() }; setTimeout(goDecision, 0); return home(); }
      }
      if (root && DASH_ROOTS.indexOf(root) >= 0) { S.refused = Date.now(); if (!S.cfgLoaded) S.early = location.hash; }
      var h = home(); landed(h); return h;
    } catch (e) { return 'mypage'; }
  }
  function goDecision() {
    var g = S.decGo; if (!g) return; S.decGo = null;
    location.hash = '#team/' + g.team;
    var tries = 0;
    (function look() {
      var h = document.getElementById('w116host'); var x = h && h.querySelector('.w116dec');
      var card = x ? (x.closest('.w116card') || x) : null;
      if (card) { try { card.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return; }
      if (++tries < 16) setTimeout(look, 250);
    })();
  }
  function showNote() {
    var p = activePanel(); if (!p) return;
    var el = document.getElementById('w260note');
    if (!el) { el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w260note'; el.className = 'w260note'; el.setAttribute('role', 'status'); el.innerHTML = '<span>The Dashboard is for the admins.</span><button type="button" data-w260nx="1" aria-label="Close">&times;</button>'; }
    var mast = p.querySelector(':scope > .w118mast');
    if (mast) { if (mast.nextElementSibling !== el) mast.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', el); }
    else if (p.firstElementChild !== el) p.insertBefore(el, p.firstChild);
    el.setAttribute('data-for', p.id);
  }
  function noteTidy() {
    var el = document.getElementById('w260note'); if (!el) return;
    var p = activePanel(); if (!p || el.getAttribute('data-for') !== p.id || !p.contains(el)) el.remove();
  }

  /* ---------- 2. train to unlock ---------- */
  function activePanel() { return document.querySelector('main > .panel.active') || document.querySelector('.panel.active'); }
  function gateMap() { return S.cfg.map || MAP_DEFAULT; }
  function exempt() { return (S.cfg.exempt || []).indexOf(myEmail()) >= 0; }
  function gateApplies() {
    if (!S.cfg.gate || !known() || isAdmin() || exempt()) return false;
    if (role() === 'blocked') return false;
    /* directors on the board view cannot read the Academy (trainings are staff rows), so they are never asked */
    if (!staffMode()) return false;
    var w = W(); if (!w || !w.A || !w.A.loaded || w.A.err) return false;
    return true;
  }
  function tourFor(id) {
    var key = gateMap()[id]; if (!key || NEVER.indexOf(id) >= 0) return null;
    var w = W(); var tr = w.A.byKey && w.A.byKey['tour:' + key];
    if (!tr || tr.active === false || !w.tours || !w.tours[key]) return null;
    try { if (!w.tourAvail(key)) return null; } catch (e) { return null; }
    return { key: key, tr: tr };
  }
  function lockedFor(id) {
    if (!gateApplies()) return null;
    var t = tourFor(id); if (!t) return null;
    try { if (W().isDone(t.tr.id)) return null; } catch (e) { return null; }
    return t;
  }
  function tourRunning() { try { var st = W() && W().state(); return !!(st && st.T); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function unlockAll() {
    var c = document.getElementById('w260lock'); if (c) c.remove();
    document.querySelectorAll('.panel.w260hid').forEach(function (p) { p.classList.remove('w260hid'); });
  }
  function pageName(id) {
    if (id === 'team' || (window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.order && WA_TEAMS.order.indexOf(id) >= 0)) {
      var d = id;
      if (id === 'team') { var m = String(location.hash || '').match(/^#team\/([a-z]+)/); if (m) d = m[1]; else { try { if (window.W116 && W116.cur && W116.cur.d) d = W116.cur.d; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
      var lab = (window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.labels && WA_TEAMS.labels[d]) || (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || '';
      return lab || 'Team pages';
    }
    if (NAMES[id]) return NAMES[id];
    try { var b = document.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="' + id + '"]'); var t = b ? String(b.textContent || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim() : ''; if (t) return t; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    return 'This page';
  }
  function firstSentence(s) { s = String(s || '').trim(); var m = s.match(/^.*?[.!?](\s|$)/); return (m ? m[0] : s).trim(); }
  function minutes(tr) { var n = 0; try { n = Number(W().minutesOf(tr)) || Number(tr.minutes) || 2; } catch (e) { n = Number(tr.minutes) || 2; } return Math.max(1, Math.round(n)); }
  function cardHtml(id, t) {
    var n = minutes(t.tr);
    var what = firstSentence(t.tr.description) || 'What this page is for, in a few short steps.';
    var started = false; try { var r = (W().A.prog || []).filter(function (p) { return String(p.training_id) === String(t.tr.id) && lower(p.person_name) === lower(W().me().name); })[0]; started = !!(r && r.status !== 'done' && (r.started_at || r.pct)); } catch (e) { started = false; }
    return '<div class="w260in" role="region" aria-label="' + E(pageName(id)) + ' is locked">'
      + '<div class="w260ic">' + svg('lock') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w260k">Take the tour first</div>'
      + '<h2>' + E(pageName(id)) + '</h2>'
      + '<p class="w260what">' + E(what) + '</p>'
      + '<p class="w260how"><b>Take the ' + n + '-minute tour to unlock it.</b> It walks you through the page, section by section. Once you finish, the page stays open for you.</p>'
      + '<div class="w260acts"><button type="button" class="w260go" data-w260start="' + E(t.key) + '">' + svg('play') + 'Start the tour</button>'
      + '<a class="w260home" data-w260home="1" href="#' + E(home()) + '">Back to ' + E(homeLabel()) + '</a></div>'
      + '<p class="w260small">' + (started ? 'You started this tour before. Start it again and go through to the end to open the page.' : 'Every page of the Hub has a short tour like this one. You can also take them all in the Avenue Academy, under Page tours.') + '</p>'
      + '</div>';
  }
  function lock(p, t) {
    p.classList.add('w260hid');
    document.querySelectorAll('.panel.w260hid').forEach(function (x) { if (x !== p) x.classList.remove('w260hid'); });
    var c = document.getElementById('w260lock');
    if (!c) { c = document.createElement('section'); c.id = 'w260lock'; }
    if (c.parentNode !== p.parentNode || c.nextElementSibling !== p) p.parentNode.insertBefore(c, p);
    var k = p.id + ':' + t.key + ':' + home();
    if (c.getAttribute('data-k') !== k) { c.innerHTML = cardHtml(p.id, t); c.setAttribute('data-k', k); }
    c.setAttribute('data-page', p.id);
  }
  function guard() {
    try {
      var p = activePanel(); var id = p ? p.id : '';
      document.body.classList.toggle('w260nodash', known() && !dashOk());
      /* the Dashboard reached some other way (the first paint before the roles were in, an old link) */
      if (known() && !dashOk() && (id === 'overview' || id === 'dashboard') && typeof showPanel === 'function') {
        if (DASH_ROOTS.indexOf(rootOf()) >= 0) S.refused = Date.now();
        var h = home(); if (h !== id && document.getElementById(h)) { showPanel(h); landed(h); return; }
      }
      if (S.refused && Date.now() - S.refused < 4000) {
        S.refused = 0; showNote();
        /* the address says where the person is (as wave130 does for a refused page) */
        try {
          if (DASH_ROOTS.indexOf(rootOf()) >= 0 && history.replaceState) {
            history.replaceState(null, '', location.pathname + location.search + '#' + id);
          }
        } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      } else noteTidy();
      if (!p || tourRunning() || S.touring) { unlockAll(); return; }
      var t = lockedFor(id);
      if (!t) { unlockAll(); return; }
      lock(p, t);
    } catch (e) { unlockAll(); }
  }
  function paintMenu() {
    try {
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab[data-tab]').forEach(function (b) {
        var id = b.getAttribute('data-tab');
        var want = lockedFor(id) ? '1' : '';
        var cur = b.getAttribute('data-w260lock') || '';
        if (want !== cur) { if (want) { b.setAttribute('data-w260lock', '1'); b.setAttribute('title', 'Take its tour to unlock it'); } else { b.removeAttribute('data-w260lock'); if (b.getAttribute('title') === 'Take its tour to unlock it') b.removeAttribute('title'); } }
      });
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function startFromCard(key) {
    var w = W(); if (!w) return;
    S.touring = key; unlockAll();
    try { w.startTour(key); } catch (e) { /* below */ }
    setTimeout(function () {
      if (!tourRunning()) { S.touring = null; guard(); toastS('The tour could not start here. Try it from the Avenue Academy, under Page tours.'); }
      else S.touring = null;
    }, 700);
  }
  window.addEventListener('w122tour', function (e) {
    var d = (e && e.detail) || {};
    if (d.kind === 'start') { unlockAll(); return; }
    setTimeout(function () { S.touring = null; guard(); paintMenu(); }, 0);
  });
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var b = t.closest('[data-w260start]'); if (b) { e.preventDefault(); startFromCard(b.getAttribute('data-w260start')); return; }
    if (t.closest('[data-w260nx]')) { var n = document.getElementById('w260note'); if (n) n.remove(); return; }
  });

  /* ---------- the settings ---------- */
  function parseList(v) { try { var a = JSON.parse(v); return Array.isArray(a) ? a.map(lower).filter(Boolean) : []; } catch (e) { return String(v || '').split(/[\s,;]+/).map(lower).filter(Boolean); } }
  async function loadCfg() {
    try {
      var r = await db.from('app_config').select('key, value').in('key', ['dashboard_people', 'page_gate', 'page_gate_exempt', 'page_gate_map']);
      if (!r || r.error) return;
      (r.data || []).forEach(function (x) {
        if (x.key === 'dashboard_people') S.cfg.dash = parseList(x.value);
        if (x.key === 'page_gate') S.cfg.gate = !/^(off|false|0|"off")$/i.test(String(x.value || '').trim());
        if (x.key === 'page_gate_exempt') S.cfg.exempt = parseList(x.value);
        if (x.key === 'page_gate_map') { try { var m = JSON.parse(x.value); if (m && typeof m === 'object' && !Array.isArray(m)) S.cfg.map = m; } catch (e2) { /* the built-in map */ } }
      });
      S.cfgLoaded = true;
    } catch (e) { /* the defaults hold */ }
  }

  /* ---------- tour words for people the Dashboard is not for ---------- */
  function tourWords() {
    try {
      var w = W(); if (!w || !w.tours || dashOk()) return;
      var ch = w.tours.chrome; if (ch && Array.isArray(ch.steps)) ch.steps.forEach(function (st) { if (st && st.p && st.p.indexOf('the Executive dashboard and the Calendar') >= 0) st.p = st.p.replace('Home, the Executive dashboard and the Calendar at the top', 'Home, the Avenue Academy, Team Requests and the Calendar at the top'); });
      var tm = w.tours.team; if (tm && Array.isArray(tm.steps)) tm.steps.forEach(function (st) { if (st && st.p && st.p.indexOf('Decide them on the Executive dashboard.') >= 0) st.p = st.p.replace('Decide them on the Executive dashboard.', 'The executives record what was decided.'); });
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }

  (function addSection() {

  function onTours() { return /^#adminpanel\/tours/.test(location.hash || ''); }
  function cardEl() {

    if (!c) { c = document.createElement('div'); c.className = 'card'; c.id = 'w260card'; c.innerHTML = '<div id="w260a"></div>'; panel.appendChild(c); }

  }
  async function loadAdmin() {
    if (S.adm.busy) return; S.adm.busy = true; S.adm.err = '';
    try { var r = await db.rpc('wa260_page_tours'); if (r && r.error) throw r.error; S.adm.rows = (r && r.data) || []; }
    catch (e) { S.adm.err = String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 160); }
    S.adm.busy = false; paintAdmin();
  }
  function TL(k) {
    var w = W(); var tr = w && w.A && w.A.byKey && w.A.byKey['tour:' + k];
    var t = tr ? String(tr.title || '').replace(/^Page tour:\s*/i, '') : k;
    return t.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + t.slice(1);
  }
  function summary(rows) {
    var people = {}, per = {};
    rows.forEach(function (r) {
      if (r.exempt) return;
      if (r.can_open && !r.done) { people[r.email] = (people[r.email] || 0) + 1; per[r.tour_key] = (per[r.tour_key] || 0) + 1; }
    });
    var all = {}; rows.forEach(function (r) { all[r.email] = 1; });
    var most = Object.keys(per).sort(function (a, b) { return per[b] - per[a] || (a < b ? -1 : 1); });
    return { locked: Object.keys(people).length, total: Object.keys(all).length, per: per, most: most };
  }
  function paintAdmin() {
    var c = cardEl(); if (!c) return;
    var host = document.getElementById('w260a'); if (!host) return;
    var rows = S.adm.rows;
    var gateOn = S.cfg.gate;
    var html = '<p class="w260a-top">Every page with a tour opens once the person has finished that tour (the Avenue Academy keeps the record). Admins never see a lock. Home, the Avenue Academy, people pages and Needs me are always open.</p>'
      + '<div class="w260a-row"><h4>Train to unlock is <span class="' + (gateOn ? 'w260a-on' : 'w260a-off') + '">' + (gateOn ? 'on' : 'off') + '</span><small>' + (gateOn ? 'Pages stay locked until their tour is done.' : 'Nobody sees a lock. The tours still count in the Academy.') + '</small></h4>'
      + '<button type="button" class="w260a-btn' + (gateOn ? '' : ' pri') + '" data-w260set="gate"' + (S.adm.saving ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (gateOn ? 'Turn it off' : 'Turn it on') + '</button></div>';
    var staffOpts = function (skip) {
      var ppl = []; try { ppl = (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []).filter(function (m) { return m && m.active !== false && m.email; }); } catch (e) { ppl = []; }

        .map(function (m) { return '<option value="' + E(lower(m.email)) + '">' + E(m.name) + '</option>'; }).join('');
    };
    html += '<div class="w260a-row"><h4>Who opens the Executive dashboard<small>The admins always do. Add anyone else here.</small></h4><div style="flex:1 1 260px">'
      + (S.cfg.dash.length ? S.cfg.dash.map(function (em) { return '<span class="w260a-pill">' + E(nameOf(em)) + ' <button type="button" title="Take off" aria-label="Take ' + E(nameOf(em)) + ' off" data-w260dash-rm="' + E(em) + '">&times;</button></span>'; }).join('') : '<span style="color:' + MUTED + ';font-size:13px">Only the admins.</span>')
      + '<div style="margin-top:8px;display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap"><select data-w260dash-pick="1"><option value="">Add a person</option>' + staffOpts(S.cfg.dash) + '</select>'
      + '<button type="button" class="w260a-btn" data-w260dash-add="1"' + (S.adm.saving ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Add</button></div></div></div>';
    html += '<div class="w260a-row"><h4>Let someone through<small>They open every page without its tour. Use it sparingly, for a test account or a short stay.</small></h4><div style="flex:1 1 260px">'
      + (S.cfg.exempt.length ? S.cfg.exempt.map(function (em) { return '<span class="w260a-pill">' + E(nameOf(em)) + ' <button type="button" title="Take off" aria-label="Take ' + E(nameOf(em)) + ' off" data-w260ex-rm="' + E(em) + '">&times;</button></span>'; }).join('') : '<span style="color:' + MUTED + ';font-size:13px">Nobody.</span>')
      + '<div style="margin-top:8px;display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap"><select data-w260ex-pick="1"><option value="">Add a person</option>' + staffOpts(S.cfg.exempt) + '</select>'
      + '<button type="button" class="w260a-btn" data-w260ex-add="1"' + (S.adm.saving ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Add</button></div></div></div>';
    if (!rows) html += '<p class="w260a-sum">' + (S.adm.err ? 'The page tours could not load: ' + E(S.adm.err) : 'Loading who has taken which tour...') + '</p>';
    else {
      var sm = summary(rows);
      var tours = []; rows.forEach(function (r) { if (tours.indexOf(r.tour_key) < 0) tours.push(r.tour_key); });
      tours.sort(function (a, b) { return (sm.per[b] || 0) - (sm.per[a] || 0) || (TL(a) < TL(b) ? -1 : 1); });
      var people = []; var byP = {};
      rows.forEach(function (r) { if (!byP[r.email]) { byP[r.email] = { email: r.email, name: r.name, role: r.role, exempt: r.exempt, cells: {} }; people.push(byP[r.email]); } byP[r.email].cells[r.tour_key] = r; });
      people.forEach(function (p) { p.open = 0; p.done = 0; tours.forEach(function (k) { var x = p.cells[k]; if (x && x.can_open) { p.open++; if (x.done) p.done++; } }); });
      people.sort(function (a, b) { return (a.done / Math.max(1, a.open)) - (b.done / Math.max(1, b.open)) || String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); });
      html += '<p class="w260a-sum"><b>' + sm.locked + ' of ' + sm.total + '</b> people (admins aside) will see at least one lock on their next visit' + (gateOn ? '' : ' once the lock is back on') + '. The pages most of them still have to tour:</p>'
        + '<div class="w260a-most">' + sm.most.slice(0, 8).map(function (k) { return '<span class="w260a-pill">' + E(TL(k)) + ' ' + sm.per[k] + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
        + '<div class="w260a-wrap"><table class="w260a-m"><thead><tr><th class="p" style="vertical-align:bottom">Person</th>' + tours.map(function (k) { return '<th title="' + E(TL(k)) + '"><span>' + E(TL(k)) + '</span></th>'; }).join('') + '</tr></thead><tbody>'
        + people.map(function (p) {
          return '<tr><td class="p"><b>' + E(p.name) + '</b><small>' + p.done + ' of ' + p.open + ' toured' + (p.exempt ? ', let through' : '') + '</small>'
            + '<br><button type="button" class="w260a-x" data-w260ex-' + (p.exempt ? 'rm' : 'one') + '="' + E(p.email) + '">' + (p.exempt ? 'Lock again' : 'Let through') + '</button></td>'
            + tours.map(function (k) { var x = p.cells[k]; if (!x || !x.can_open) return '<td class="c-na" title="Cannot open this page">&middot;</td>'; return x.done ? '<td class="c-done" title="Toured">&#10003;</td>' : '<td class="c-lock" title="Locked until the tour is done">' + svg('lock').replace('<svg ', '<svg width="13" height="13" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" ') + '</td>'; }).join('') + '</tr>';
        }).join('') + '</tbody></table></div>'
        + '<p class="w260a-leg">&#10003; toured &nbsp; lock: not yet, the page is locked &nbsp; &middot; the person cannot open that page. From the access profiles, so a page someone was given by hand may show as &middot;.</p>';
    }
    if (host.__h !== html) { host.innerHTML = html; host.__h = html; }
  }
  function nameOf(em) {
    try { var m = (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []).filter(function (x) { return lower(x.email) === lower(em); })[0]; if (m && m.name) return m.name; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    return String(em || '').split('@')[0];
  }
  async function save(key, value) {
    if (S.adm.saving) return; S.adm.saving = true; paintAdmin();
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('wa260_gate_set', { p_key: key, p_value: value });
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      if (key === 'page_gate') S.cfg.gate = value === 'on';
      if (key === 'page_gate_exempt') S.cfg.exempt = value.slice();
      if (key === 'dashboard_people') S.cfg.dash = value.slice();
      toastS('Saved.');
      S.adm.rows = S.adm.rows ? S.adm.rows.map(function (x) { return Object.assign({}, x, { exempt: S.cfg.exempt.indexOf(lower(x.email)) >= 0 }); }) : S.adm.rows;
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not save: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 120)); }
    S.adm.saving = false; paintAdmin(); try { if (typeof wa40Enforce === 'function') wa40Enforce(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } guard(); paintMenu();
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest || !t.closest('#w260card') || !isAdmin()) return;
    var b;
    if ((b = t.closest('[data-w260set="gate"]'))) { save('page_gate', S.cfg.gate ? 'off' : 'on'); return; }
    if ((b = t.closest('[data-w260dash-rm]'))) { var em = b.getAttribute('data-w260dash-rm'); save('dashboard_people', S.cfg.dash.filter(function (x) { return x !== em; })); return; }
    if ((b = t.closest('[data-w260dash-add]'))) { var s = document.querySelector('#w260card [data-w260dash-pick]'); var v = s && s.value; if (!v) { toastS('Pick a person first.'); return; } save('dashboard_people', S.cfg.dash.concat([v])); return; }
    if ((b = t.closest('[data-w260ex-rm]'))) { var em2 = b.getAttribute('data-w260ex-rm'); save('page_gate_exempt', S.cfg.exempt.filter(function (x) { return x !== em2; })); return; }
    if ((b = t.closest('[data-w260ex-one]'))) { var em3 = b.getAttribute('data-w260ex-one'); if (S.cfg.exempt.indexOf(em3) < 0) save('page_gate_exempt', S.cfg.exempt.concat([em3])); return; }
    if ((b = t.closest('[data-w260ex-add]'))) { var s2 = document.querySelector('#w260card [data-w260ex-pick]'); var v2 = s2 && s2.value; if (!v2) { toastS('Pick a person first.'); return; } save('page_gate_exempt', S.cfg.exempt.concat([v2])); return; }
  });

  };

   them".

   - The menu (parts134): your team sits open under Teams; the other teams fold under "Other teams (n)" (open by default for
     admins); The Brain, Other Tools and Organization fold under More. Folds open by themselves on one of their pages and
     remember a click on this device. Computers only: the phone's Menu sheet lists every page.

   - Home (this segment): what matters first (your counts, what needs you, your tasks and your team's requests, your
     meetings, announcements); the rest (who is online, In the loop, Ask the Hub ideas, Updates for you, time commitments,

     what sits under More) with a one-minute tour (trainings tour:whatsnew). It waits for the must-answer pop-ups.

    + '#w262new .apps{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;margin-top:8px;}'
