    + '#w262new .app:hover{border-color:' + GREEN + ';}'
    + '#w262new .app:focus-visible,#w262new button:focus-visible{outline:2px solid ' + GOLD + ';outline-offset:2px;}'
    + '#w262new .app .ai{width:26px;height:26px;border-radius:8px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;color:' + CREAM + ';}'
    + '#w262new .app .ai svg{width:15px;height:15px;}'
    + '#w262new .app.more{background:transparent;font-weight:500;}'
    + '#w262new .sub{font-size:12px;color:' + MUTED + ';margin:8px 0 0;}'
    + '#w262new .ft{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;padding:12px 22px 16px;border-top:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.10);}'
    + '#w262new .go{background:' + GOLD + ';color:' + INK + ';border:0;border-radius:10px;padding:10px 18px;font:inherit;font-weight:700;font-size:14px;cursor:pointer;}'
    + '#w262new .ok{background:none;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.25);color:' + GREEN + ';border-radius:10px;padding:9px 16px;font:inherit;font-weight:600;font-size:13.5px;cursor:pointer;}'
    + '#w262new .ft small{margin-left:auto;font-size:12px;color:' + MUTED + ';}'
    + '@media (max-width:640px){#w262new{padding:0;align-items:stretch;}#w262new .dlg{max-height:100vh;border-radius:0;}#w262new .ft small{margin-left:0;flex-basis:100%;}}';
  var N = { shown: false, tries: 0, timer: null };

  function w122ok() { return !!(window.w122 && window.w122.tours); }
  function tourRow() { try { return window.w122.A.byKey['tour:' + TOUR] || null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function tourDone() { try { var t = tourRow(); return !!t && window.w122.isDone(t.id); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function seen() { return readLS(KEY_SEEN) === '1' || tourDone(); }
  function onHome() { var h = String(location.hash || ''); return !h || h === '#' || /^#mypage(\/|$)/.test(h); }
  function phone() { return window.innerWidth <= 900; }
  function vis(el) { if (!el) return false; var r = el.getBoundingClientRect(); return r.width > 0 && r.height > 0 && getComputedStyle(el).display !== 'none'; }
  /* never on top of another window: the Loop's Welcome back, the meeting pop-up, the due-date check, a modal, a running tour */
  function busy() {
    if (document.getElementById('w213pop') || vis(document.getElementById('w238loop')) || vis(document.getElementById('w177pop'))) return true;
    var m = document.getElementById('modalBack'); if (m && m.style.display === 'flex') return true;
    if (document.querySelector('.w122tp') || document.getElementById('w122sheet') && vis(document.getElementById('w122sheet'))) return true;
    return false;
  }
  function apps() {
    var A = window.WA_APP; if (!A || typeof A.yours !== 'function') return { yours: [], more: [], all: true };
    try { return A.yours(); } catch (e) { return { yours: [], more: [], all: true }; }
  }
  function appBtn(id, more) {
    var A = window.WA_APP, a = A && A.apps && A.apps[id]; if (!a) return '';
    return '<button type="button" class="app' + (more ? ' more' : '') + '" data-w262app="' + E(id) + '" title="' + E(a.blurb || '') + '"><span class="ai" style="background:' + E(a.color) + '">' + (A.icon ? A.icon(a.ic) : '') + '</span>' + E(a.label) + '</button>';
  }
  function moreLabels() {
    var out = [];
    ['tools', 'org'].forEach(function (k) {
      var g = document.querySelector('#tabBar > .tab-group[data-w42g="' + k + '"]'); if (!g) return;
      Array.prototype.forEach.call(g.querySelectorAll('.tab'), function (t) {
        if (t.style.display === 'none' || t.getAttribute('data-w73hide')) return;
        var l = ''; t.childNodes.forEach(function (n) { if (n.nodeType === 3 || (n.nodeType === 1 && !n.classList.contains('tabdot') && !n.classList.contains('w134n'))) l += n.textContent; });
        l = l.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); if (l) out.push(l);
      });
    });
    return out;
  }
  function listWords(a) { return a.length > 1 ? a.slice(0, -1).join(', ') + ' and ' + a[a.length - 1] : (a[0] || ''); }
  function otherCount() {
    return Array.prototype.filter.call(document.querySelectorAll('#tabBar .w42d.w262other'), function (row) {
      return row.style.display !== 'none' && Array.prototype.some.call(row.querySelectorAll('.tab'), function (t) { return t.style.display !== 'none'; });
    }).length;
  }
  function summary() {
    var f = (window.w134 && window.w134.folds) ? window.w134.folds() : null;
    var mine = f && f.mine ? teamLabel(f.mine) : '', others = otherCount(), more = moreLabels(), ap = apps();
    return { mine: mine, others: others, more: more, apps: ap };
  }
  function html() {
    var s = summary(), n = first(), items = [];
    var menu = 'Home, Team Requests, the Calendar and the Avenue Academy stay at the top. '
      + (s.mine ? 'Your team, ' + s.mine + ', sits open under Teams with its own pages. ' : '')
      + (s.others ? 'The other ' + (s.others === 1 ? 'team is' : s.others + ' teams are') + ' folded under Other teams, one click away. ' : '')
      + (s.more.length ? listWords(s.more) + ' are under More.' : '');
    if (phone()) menu = 'On a phone, the bar at the bottom has Home, the Calendar, Ask, My team and Menu. Menu lists every page you can open, with Find a page at the top.';
    items.push(['Your menu is shorter', menu]);
    items.push(['Home shows what matters first', 'Your counts, what needs you, your tasks and your team\u2019s requests come first, then your meetings and the announcements. In the loop, updates, time off and the rest wait behind Show everything on Home, at the bottom.']);
    var ap = s.apps, appsHtml = '';
    if (ap.yours.length || ap.more.length) {
      appsHtml = '<div class="apps">' + ap.yours.map(function (id) { return appBtn(id, false); }).join('') + '</div>'
        + (ap.more.length ? '<p class="sub">More apps you can open:</p><div class="apps">' + ap.more.map(function (id) { return appBtn(id, true); }).join('') + '</div>' : '');
      items.push([ap.all ? 'The apps you can open' : 'Your apps come first', (ap.all ? 'Every app is yours. ' : (phone() ? 'The apps your team uses come first in Menu; the others follow. ' : 'The apps your team uses sit at the top of Apps (bottom of the menu); the others follow under More apps. ')) + 'Click one to open it now.', appsHtml]);
    }
    items.push(['Ask the Hub finds pages for you', 'Ask where something is (where are the passwords? where do I find grants?) and the answer shows the page with Open. If your access does not open it yet, Request access sends the ask to the admins.']);
    items.push(['Clearing your tasks works for long lists', 'When tasks are overdue, due today or have no date, the Hub asks you to close them or pick a new date. Long sections now have All in this section: Done or Next meeting, and saving a big list no longer fails.']);
    return '<div class="dlg" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="w262h">'
      + '<div class="hd"><small>What changed in the Hub</small><h2 id="w262h">' + E(n ? n + ', the Hub got simpler' : 'The Hub got simpler') + '</h2>'
      + '<p>After Monday\u2019s leadership meeting we trimmed what you see first. Nothing was taken away: a few things moved one click further.</p>'
      + '<button type="button" class="x" data-w262x="1" aria-label="Close">&times;</button></div>'
      + '<div class="bd">' + items.map(function (it, i) { return '<div class="it"><span class="n">' + (i + 1) + '</span><div><h3>' + E(it[0]) + '</h3><p>' + E(it[1]) + '</p>' + (it[2] || '') + '</div></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="ft"><button type="button" class="go" data-w262tour="1">Take the 1-minute tour</button><button type="button" class="ok" data-w262ok="1">Got it</button>'
      + '<small>The tour stays in the Avenue Academy under Hub page tours.</small></div></div>';
  }
  function closeNew() {
    var el = document.getElementById('w262new'); if (el) el.remove();
    writeLS(KEY_SEEN, '1');
    document.removeEventListener('keydown', escNew, true);
  }
  function escNew(e) { if (e.key === 'Escape' && document.getElementById('w262new')) { e.stopPropagation(); closeNew(); } }
  function showNew() {
    if (document.getElementById('w262new')) return;
    var el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w262new'; el.innerHTML = html();
    el.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var t = e.target, b;
      if (t === el || t.closest('[data-w262x]') || t.closest('[data-w262ok]')) { closeNew(); return; }
      if (t.closest('[data-w262tour]')) { closeNew(); startTour(); return; }
      if ((b = t.closest('[data-w262app]'))) {
        closeNew();
        var id = b.getAttribute('data-w262app');
        try { if (window.WA_APP && window.WA_APP.open) window.WA_APP.open(id); } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
      }
    });
    document.body.appendChild(el);
    document.addEventListener('keydown', escNew, true);
    N.shown = true;
    try { var g = el.querySelector('[data-w262tour]'); if (g) g.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function startTour() {
    if (!w122ok()) return;
    if (!onHome()) location.hash = '#mypage';
    try { window.w122.startTour(TOUR); } catch (e) { toastS('The tour is in the Avenue Academy, under Hub page tours.'); }
  }
  /* wait for Home, the person's data and the must-answer pop-ups; give up quietly after a while */
  function tryNew() {
    clearTimeout(N.timer);
    if (N.shown || !staff()) return;
    try { if (typeof MODE !== 'undefined' && MODE === 'board') return; } catch (e) { return; }
    if (seen()) return;
    if (N.tries++ > 180) return;
    var ready = w122ok() && window.w122.A && window.w122.A.loaded;
    if (!onHome() || busy() || (!ready && N.tries < 20)) { N.timer = setTimeout(tryNew, 1500); return; }
    if (seen()) return;
    showNew();
  }

  /* ---------- the tour ---------- */
  function tourSteps() {
    var s = summary(), ap = s.apps;
    var appLine = ap.all ? 'Every app you can open is here: the Hub, ' + listWords(ap.yours.map(function (id) { return (window.WA_APP.apps[id] || {}).label; }).filter(Boolean)) + '.'
      : 'Apps opens your team\u2019s apps first' + (ap.yours.length ? ' (' + listWords(ap.yours.map(function (id) { return (window.WA_APP.apps[id] || {}).label; }).filter(Boolean)) + ')' : '') + ', then More apps' + (ap.more.length ? ' (' + listWords(ap.more.map(function (id) { return (window.WA_APP.apps[id] || {}).label; }).filter(Boolean)) + ')' : '') + '.';
    var all = [
      { c: 1, t: 'What changed', p: 'A one-minute look at what moved for you. Nothing was taken away: a few things are one click further. Use the arrow keys or the buttons, and Esc to stop.' },
      { s: '#tabBar > .tab[data-tab="mypage"]', t: 'Home', p: 'Home leads with what matters to you: your counts, what needs you, your tasks and your team\u2019s requests, then your meetings and the announcements.', opt: 1 },
      { s: '#w163bar', t: 'Your menu on a phone', p: 'Home, the Calendar, Ask, My team and Menu. Menu lists every page you can open, with your apps and Find a page at the top.', opt: 1 },
      { s: '#tabBar .w42d[data-w262mine]', t: 'Your team', p: (s.mine || 'Your team') + ' sits open under Teams with its own pages: Overview, Agenda, Tasks and the tools your team uses.', opt: 1 },
      { s: '#tabBar .w262tt', t: 'Other teams', p: 'Every other team is folded here. Open it when you need another team\u2019s page; the Hub remembers your choice on this computer.', opt: 1 },
      { s: '#tabBar .w262fold', t: 'More', p: (s.more.length ? listWords(s.more) : 'The Brain and the other tools') + ' live under More. It opens with a click, and by itself when you are on one of those pages.', opt: 1 },
      { s: '#w153dock .w153grid', t: 'Your apps', p: appLine, opt: 1 },
      { s: '#searchWrap', t: 'Search everything', p: 'Looking for something you cannot see in the menu? Type a page, a task, a person or a meeting here and jump straight to it.', opt: 1 },
      { s: '#w262homebar', t: 'Show everything on Home', p: 'In the loop, Updates for you, time commitments, Slack notifications and time off wait here. One click shows them, another hides them again.', opt: 1 },
      { c: 1, t: 'You are set', p: 'Every page still has its own tour in the Avenue Academy, and Ask the Hub (bottom right) can find anything for you.' }
    ];
    /* on a phone the side menu is closed: the bar at the bottom and its Menu sheet do that job */
    var deskOnly = ['#tabBar > .tab[data-tab="mypage"]', '#tabBar .w42d[data-w262mine]', '#tabBar .w262tt', '#tabBar .w262fold', '#w153dock .w153grid'];
    return phone() ? all.filter(function (st) { return deskOnly.indexOf(st.s) < 0; }) : all.filter(function (st) { return st.s !== '#w163bar'; });
  }
  function registerTour() {
    if (!w122ok() || window.w122.tours[TOUR]) return;
    window.w122.tours[TOUR] = { tab: 'mypage', icon: 'compass', hash: '#mypage', steps: tourSteps };
  }

  /* ---------- Ask the Hub finds pages (Steel, 21/10/26) ----------
     "I also want ask the hub to support with navigation when people ask for information. If the page they are looking
     for is something they don't have access to they should be able to request access."
     When a question asks where something is (where, find, open, take me, link, page, how do I get to...), the answer gets a
     "Where to find it" card: up to three pages that match, each with where it sits in the menu and Open, or, for a page the
     person's access does not open, Request access (the same wa_access_requests row and admin ping as Request more access on
     Home, wave110; "Requested" once sent). The model's answer is untouched; the card is drawn under it. No new data. */
  var NAV = [
    ['mypage', 'Home', '#mypage', 'home my page my tasks dashboard home page'],
    ['training', 'Avenue Academy', '#training', 'academy training trainings tours tour course courses onboarding modules learn'],
    ['overview', 'Executive dashboard', '#overview', 'executive dashboard exec dashboard kpis kpi decisions numbers'],
    ['requests', 'Team Requests', '#requests', 'requests request team requests ask another team'],
    ['calendar', 'Calendar', '#calendar', 'calendar schedule meetings dates'],
    ['brain', 'The Brain', '#brain', 'brain the brain how work flows map'],
    ['importantinfo', 'Important info', '#importantinfo', 'important info passwords password logins login accounts credentials links key contacts'],
    ['bank', 'The Task Bank', '#bank', 'task bank bank parked tasks later'],
    ['agendas', 'Agendas', '#agendas', 'agendas agenda meeting agenda'],
    ['documents', 'Document finder', '#documents', 'documents document docs files drive google drive find a document'],
    ['transcripts', 'Transcript archive', '#transcripts', 'transcripts transcript recordings recording loom meeting notes minutes'],
    ['photos', 'Photo library', '#photos', 'photos photo pictures images library'],
    ['orgchart', 'Org chart', '#orgchart', 'org chart organization chart who reports reporting structure staff list'],
    ['ourstory', 'Our story', '#ourstory', 'our story story history mission'],
    ['hubmail', 'Hub inbox', '#hubmail', 'hub inbox inbox hub mail emails to the hub'],
    ['opsmtl', 'Operations Montreal', '#team/opsmtl', 'operations montreal montreal ops mtl operations'],
    ['progdev', 'Program Development', '#team/progdev', 'program development programs progdev'],
    ['opsto', 'Operations Toronto', '#team/opsto', 'operations toronto toronto ops toronto team'],
    ['marketing', 'Marketing, Branding & Web', '#team/marketing', 'marketing team branding web marketing page'],
    ['fundraising', 'Fundraising team page', '#team/fundraising', 'fundraising team fundraising page'],
    ['hr', 'Human Resources team page', '#team/hr', 'human resources team hr team'],
    ['board', 'Board team page', '#team/board', 'board team board and governance team'],
    ['governance', 'Board overview', '#governance', 'board overview board meetings governance directors'],
    ['committees', 'Committees', '#committees', 'committees committee'],
    ['volunteering', 'Volunteer Manager', '#volunteering', 'volunteer manager volunteers volunteer servings serving roster sunday serving texts sign ups signups'],
    ['crm', 'CRM', '#crm', 'crm contacts contact donors donor email campaigns email studio supporters gifts'],
    ['partners', 'Partners', '#partners', 'partners partner sponsors list collaborators organizations'],
    ['fund', 'Fundraising app', '#fund', 'fundraising app fundraising events donor program sponsorship'],
    ['grants', 'Grants', '#fund/grants', 'grants grant funding funders applications grant writer'],
    ['fundevents', 'Events calendar', '#fundevents', 'events calendar fundraising events'],
    ['studio', 'Content Studio', '#studio', 'content studio studio social media posts posting queue approvals comments messages dms hashtags hashtag link pages short links'],
    ['contentcal', 'Content calendar', '#contentcal', 'content calendar post calendar social calendar'],
    ['socialstats', 'Analytics', '#socialstats', 'analytics insights stats statistics reach followers engagement'],
    ['hrcentre', 'HR centre', '#hrcentre', 'hr centre hr center hiring recruitment applicants candidates onboarding time off vacation policies offer letters'],
    ['finances', 'Finance', '#finances', 'finance finances budget budgets expenses reimbursement reimbursements ledger accounting invoices'],
    ['orggoals', 'Goals and the Strategy Library', '#orggoals', 'goals strategy strategic plan organizational goals strategy library'],
    ['projects', 'Projects', '#projects', 'projects project'],
    ['adminpanel', 'Admin centre', '#adminpanel', 'admin admin centre access permissions roles people and access']
  ];
  var NAV_INTENT = /\b(where|find|finding|open|opening|go to|take me|get to|navigate|link to|link for|which page|what page|the page|a page|tab|section|menu|how (do|can) i (get|see|reach|open|find|use|access)|show me|access to|can't see|cannot see|can not see|don't see|do not see|where's)\b/i;
  var NAV_SEEN = { q: '', reqs: null };
  /* words too general to point at one page on their own */
  var NAV_STOP = ['page', 'find', 'team', 'list', 'link', 'links', 'home', 'open', 'where', 'library', 'calendar', 'notes', 'people', 'events', 'access'];
  function navFold(s) { return ' ' + String(s || '').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9 ]+/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim() + ' '; }
  function navAllowed(id) {
    try { var al = (typeof wa40Allowed === 'function' && wa40Allowed()) || []; if (al.indexOf(id) >= 0) return true; } catch (e) { /* below */ }
    var t = document.querySelector('#tabBar .tab[data-tab="' + id + '"]');
    return !!t && t.style.display !== 'none';
  }
  function navMatches(q) {
    if (!q || !NAV_INTENT.test(q)) return [];
    var f = navFold(q), out = [];
    NAV.forEach(function (p) {
      var best = 0;
      /* every phrase of two or more words, then single words, in the page's word list */
      var words = p[3].split(' '), n = words.length;
      for (var len = 4; len >= 1; len--) {
        for (var i = 0; i + len <= n; i++) {
          var ph = words.slice(i, i + len).join(' ');
          if (len === 1 && (ph.length < 4 || NAV_STOP.indexOf(ph) >= 0)) continue;
          if (f.indexOf(' ' + ph + ' ') >= 0 || (len === 1 && f.indexOf(' ' + ph + 's ') >= 0)) { best = Math.max(best, len * 10 + ph.length); }
        }
      }
      if (f.indexOf(' ' + navFold(p[1]).trim() + ' ') >= 0) best = Math.max(best, 100 + p[1].length);
      if (best) out.push({ id: p[0], label: p[1], hash: p[2], score: best });
    });
    out.sort(function (a, b) { return b.score - a.score; });
    var top = out.length ? out[0].score : 0;
    out = out.filter(function (x) { return x.score >= top * 0.7; });
    /* "content calendar" means the Content calendar, not also the Calendar */
    out = out.filter(function (x) { return !out.some(function (y) { return y !== x && y.score >= 100 && navFold(y.label).indexOf(navFold(x.label)) >= 0 && y.label.length > x.label.length; }); });
    return out.slice(0, 3);
  }
  function navWhere(id) {
    var t = document.querySelector('#tabBar .tab[data-tab="' + id + '"]');
    try { if (window.WA_APP && typeof window.WA_APP.owner === 'function') { var a = window.WA_APP.owner(id); if (a && a !== 'hub' && window.WA_APP.apps[a]) return 'Apps, ' + window.WA_APP.apps[a].label; } } catch (e) { /* below */ }
    if (!t) return '';
    var row = t.closest('.w42d'), main = row ? row.querySelector(':scope > .tab') : null;
    if (row && main && main !== t) return 'Teams, ' + (main.textContent || '').trim();
    if (row) return 'Teams';
    var g = t.closest('.tab-group'), k = g ? g.getAttribute('data-w42g') : '';
    if (k === 'tools') return 'More, Other Tools';
    if (k === 'org') return 'More, Organization';
    if (k === 'admin') return 'Admin';
    if (k === 'board') return 'Board';
    return 'the top of the menu';
  }
  async function navRequests() {
    if (NAV_SEEN.reqs) return NAV_SEEN.reqs;
    try {
      var em = String((typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || '').toLowerCase();
      var r = await db.from('wa_access_requests').select('wanted, status').eq('email', em).order('id', { ascending: false }).limit(40);
      NAV_SEEN.reqs = (r && r.data) || [];
    } catch (e) { NAV_SEEN.reqs = []; }
    return NAV_SEEN.reqs;
  }
  async function navCard(d, q) {
    if (!d || d.getAttribute('data-w262nav')) return;
    var ms = navMatches(q); if (!ms.length) return;
    d.setAttribute('data-w262nav', '1');
    var reqs = await navRequests();
    var waiting = {}; reqs.forEach(function (r) { if (r.status === 'new' || r.status === 'pending' || !r.status) waiting[r.wanted] = 1; });
    var card = document.createElement('div'); card.className = 'w262nav'; card.setAttribute('data-w198skip', '');
    card.innerHTML = '<b>Where to find it</b>' + ms.map(function (m) {
      var ok = navAllowed(m.id), where = ok ? navWhere(m.id) : '';
      var act = ok ? '<a class="go w226in" href="' + E(m.hash) + '" data-w262go="' + E(m.hash) + '">Open</a>'
        : (waiting['tab:' + m.id] ? '<em class="wait">Requested, waiting for an admin</em>'
          : '<button type="button" class="ask" data-w262ask="' + E(m.id) + '">Request access</button>');
      return '<div class="it"><div><div class="nm">' + E(m.label) + '</div><small>' + E(ok ? (where ? 'In the menu: ' + where : 'Open to you') : 'Not in your access yet') + '</small></div>' + act + '</div>';
    }).join('');
    card.setAttribute('data-q', q.slice(0, 200));
    d.classList.add('w262has'); d.appendChild(card);
    var box = document.getElementById('wacMsgs'); if (box) box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;
  }
  async function navAsk(btn) {
    var id = btn.getAttribute('data-w262ask'), card = btn.closest('.w262nav'), q = card ? card.getAttribute('data-q') || '' : '';
    var em = String((typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || '').toLowerCase();
    var name = (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && (USER.name || USER.email)) || em;
    var role = (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role) || 'member';
    var label = (NAV.filter(function (p) { return p[0] === id; })[0] || [id, id])[1];
    btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Sending\u2026';
    try {
      var ins = await db.from('wa_access_requests').insert({ email: em, name: name, role_now: role, wanted: 'tab:' + id, reason: q ? 'Asked Ask the Hub: ' + q.slice(0, 240) : 'From Ask the Hub' });
      if (ins && ins.error) throw ins.error;
      try { await db.functions.invoke('notify', { body: { event: 'change_request_text', text: ':key: Access request from ' + name + ' (' + em + '): tab:' + id + ' (' + label + '), from Ask the Hub. Review in Admin > Access requests.' } }); } catch (e) { /* best-effort ping, as on Home */ }
      NAV_SEEN.reqs = (NAV_SEEN.reqs || []).concat([{ wanted: 'tab:' + id, status: 'new' }]);
      var s = document.createElement('em'); s.className = 'wait'; s.textContent = 'Requested, waiting for an admin'; btn.replaceWith(s);
      toastS('Request sent to the admins: ' + label);
    } catch (e) { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Request access'; toastS('Could not send the request. Try again, or use Request more access on Home.'); }
  }
  var navCss = ''
    + '#waChatPanel .wac-m.bot.w262has{flex-wrap:wrap;}'
    + '#waChatPanel .wac-m.bot .w262nav{flex:1 0 100%;margin-left:42px;max-width:calc(100% - 42px);}'
    + '#waChatPanel .w262nav{margin:8px 0 2px;padding:10px 12px;border-radius:12px;background:' + CREAM + ';border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.14);font-size:13px;max-width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;}'
    + '#waChatPanel .w262nav > b{display:block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:1.2px;text-transform:uppercase;color:' + MUTED + ';margin-bottom:4px;}'
    + '#waChatPanel .w262nav .it{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:6px 0;border-top:1px dashed rgba(16,63,57,.12);}'
    + '#waChatPanel .w262nav .it:first-of-type{border-top:none;}'
    + '#waChatPanel .w262nav .it > div{flex:1;min-width:0;}'
    + '#waChatPanel .w262nav .nm{display:block;font-weight:700;color:' + GREEN + ';}'
    + '#waChatPanel .w262nav small{display:block;color:' + MUTED + ';font-size:11.5px;}'
    + '#waChatPanel .w262nav .go,#waChatPanel .w262nav .ask{flex:0 0 auto;border-radius:999px;padding:5px 12px;font:inherit;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;text-decoration:none;}'
    + '#waChatPanel .w262nav .go{background:' + GREEN + ';color:' + CREAM + ';border:none;}'
    + '#waChatPanel .w262nav .ask{background:' + GOLD + ';color:' + INK + ';border:none;}'
    + '#waChatPanel .w262nav .ask[disabled]{opacity:.6;cursor:default;}'
    + '#waChatPanel .w262nav .wait{font-style:normal;flex:0 0 auto;font-size:11.5px;color:#6b4d05;background:rgba(220,165,39,.18);border-radius:999px;padding:4px 10px;}';
  /* the question as it is sent (wave38's input and button), then the answer as it lands (as waves 117 and 226 do) */
  function navNote() { var i = document.getElementById('w38in'); if (i && i.value.trim()) NAV_SEEN.q = i.value.trim(); }
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Enter' && e.target && e.target.id === 'w38in') { navWire(); navNote(); } }, true);
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target;
    navWire();   /* the chat panel can be drawn after the Hub starts */
    if (t && t.closest && t.closest('#w38send')) { navNote(); return; }
    var b = t && t.closest && t.closest('[data-w262ask]'); if (b) { e.preventDefault(); navAsk(b); return; }
    var g = t && t.closest && t.closest('[data-w262go]');
    if (g && window.innerWidth < 900) { var p = document.getElementById('waChatPanel'); if (p) p.style.display = 'none'; }
  }, true);
  var navObs = false;
  function navWire() {
    var m = document.getElementById('wacMsgs'); if (!m || navObs || typeof MutationObserver === 'undefined') return;
    navObs = true;
    new MutationObserver(function (recs) {
      recs.forEach(function (r) {
        r.addedNodes.forEach(function (n) {
          if (!n.classList || !n.classList.contains('bot')) return;
          var txt = (n.textContent || '').trim();
          if (/^Thinking\.\.\.$/.test(txt) || n.classList.contains('w117think')) return;
          var q = NAV_SEEN.q; NAV_SEEN.q = '';
          if (q) navCard(n, q);
        });
      });
    }).observe(m, { childList: true });
  }

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */
  if (!document.getElementById('wa262css')) {
    var st = document.createElement('style'); st.id = 'wa262css'; st.textContent = homeCss + newCss + navCss; document.head.appendChild(st);
  }
  registerTour();
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa262 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      var r = await __sa262.apply(this, arguments);
      try { registerTour(); applyHome(); navWire(); } catch (e) { console.warn('wave262 boot', e); }
      N.timer = setTimeout(tryNew, 2500);
      return r;
    };
  }
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () {
    /* the pop-up belongs to Home: leaving Home puts it away (unseen), so it comes back on Home */
    if (!onHome() && document.getElementById('w262new')) { var el = document.getElementById('w262new'); el.remove(); document.removeEventListener('keydown', escNew, true); N.shown = false; }
    if (onHome()) { try { applyHome(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } if (!N.shown && !seen()) { N.tries = Math.min(N.tries, 150); clearTimeout(N.timer); N.timer = setTimeout(tryNew, 1200); } } });

  /* ---------- test hook ---------- */
  window.w262 = { nav: navMatches, navWire: navWire, home: applyHome, homeAll: homeAll, show: showNew, close: closeNew, tryNew: tryNew, seen: seen, summary: summary, steps: tourSteps, state: function () { return N; } };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v261 The Holiday Gift Basket Drive's forms, the CRM's form groups, and Gifts collected (16/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (16/10/26): "I think we should have a new volunteer application form via the hub so that we can collect them on
   that page as a pop-up form, to donate gifts and essentials, to volunteer with us. I don't want it to bring people to a
   different page. I want it to pop up a form for each of those actions that they can take. Same thing for companies and
   sponsors and schools and groups. The forms in the CRM need to be maybe grouped. We can maybe group forms for different
   purposes, like volunteers." And, about the drive page's gift counter: "this should be editable".

   1. The website (welfareavenue.com/gift-basket-drive/, /fr/paniers-cadeaux/): four pop-up forms from the CRM (build/www/
      patches/261_drive_popups.py, assets/forms.js), the gift counter (assets/drive.js). Data: build/data/wa261_drive_forms.sql.
   2. The CRM's Forms tab (parts152/i_forms.js): the form groups (crm_forms.purpose) as chips and sections; the editor picks
      the group; saving keeps what the editor does not show (a multi's max, the options' descriptions).
   3. This segment: Gifts collected on the drive's event in the Fundraising app (the wave124 sheet, openFund calls
      w261.slot and w261.fill): the count toward the gift goal (what the website shows: received batches and confirmed
      pledges), the pledges that wait for a Confirm (a pledge from the website form waits, so the public number never grows
      from an unverified submission), every batch, and Add gifts, Edit, Confirm, Mark received, Remove. Reads wa261_gifts,
      writes wa261_gift_save (both check wa_fund_ok() inside: people who can open the Fundraising app). Money stays in the
      CRM's gifts. The campaign of an event is app_config donate_campaigns' fund_event_id. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (window.__wa261) return; window.__wa261 = true;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', CREAM = '#FFFAF2', GOLD = '#DCA527', INK = '#1F201E', MUTED = '#797773', FLAG = '#B3402F', SAND = '#F5ECD9';
  var MON = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'];
  var S = { cfg: null, cfgP: null, data: {}, form: null, confirmDel: null, busy: false, err: '', msg: '' };
  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;'); }
  function num(n) { try { return Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('en-CA'); } catch (e) { return String(n || 0); } }
  function day(d) { var m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/.exec(String(d || '')); return m ? Number(m[3]) + ' ' + MON[Number(m[2]) - 1] + ' ' + m[1] : ''; }
  function today() { var d = new Date(); try { return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto', year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit' }).format(d); } catch (e) { return d.toISOString().slice(0, 10); } }
  function client() { try { return typeof db !== 'undefined' ? db : null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' ? !!isStaff() : true; } catch (e) { return false; } }

  /* the campaigns, from app_config donate_campaigns (staff read app_config): which one is this event's */
  function loadCfg() {
    if (S.cfg) return Promise.resolve(S.cfg);
    if (S.cfgP) return S.cfgP;
    var c = client(); if (!c) return Promise.resolve({});
    S.cfgP = Promise.resolve(c.from('app_config').select('value').eq('key', 'donate_campaigns').maybeSingle()).then(function (r) {
      var v = r && r.data ? r.data.value : null; try { v = typeof v === 'string' ? JSON.parse(v) : v; } catch (e) { v = null; }
      S.cfg = v && typeof v === 'object' ? v : {}; return S.cfg;
    }).catch(function () { S.cfg = {}; return S.cfg; });
    return S.cfgP;
  }
  function slugFor(eventId) {
    var c = S.cfg || {}, out = null;
    Object.keys(c).forEach(function (k) { if (c[k] && String(c[k].fund_event_id) === String(eventId)) out = k; });
    return out;
  }
  function slot(f) {
    if (!f || !f.id || !staff()) return '';
    return '<div class="w261g" data-w261ev="' + E(f.id) + '" hidden></div>';
  }
  function box() { return document.querySelector('#w124sheet .w261g'); }
  function load(slug) {
    var c = client(); if (!c) return Promise.resolve(null);
    return Promise.resolve(c.rpc('wa261_gifts', { p_slug: slug })).then(function (r) {
      if (!r || r.error || !r.data) return null;
      S.data[slug] = r.data; return r.data;
    }).catch(function () { return null; });
  }
  function fill(f) {
    var b = box(); if (!b) return Promise.resolve();
    S.form = null; S.confirmDel = null; S.err = ''; S.msg = '';
    return loadCfg().then(function () {
      var slug = slugFor(f.id), b2 = box();
      if (!slug || !b2 || b2.getAttribute('data-w261ev') !== String(f.id)) { if (b2) b2.remove(); return; }
      b2.setAttribute('data-w261slug', slug);
      return load(slug).then(function (d) { var b3 = box(); if (!b3) return; if (!d) { b3.remove(); return; } paint(); });
    });
  }
  function rowHtml(g) {
    var waiting = g.status === 'pledged' && !g.confirmed_at;
    var chip = g.status === 'received' ? ['Received' + (g.received_on ? ' ' + day(g.received_on) : ''), GREEN] : waiting ? ['Pledged, waiting to confirm', FLAG] : ['Pledged, confirmed' + (g.received_on ? ', expected ' + day(g.received_on) : ''), GOLD];
    var acts = [];
    if (waiting) acts.push('<button type="button" class="w261b" data-w261do="confirm" data-id="' + E(g.id) + '">Confirm</button>');
    if (g.status === 'pledged') acts.push('<button type="button" class="w261b ghost" data-w261do="receive" data-id="' + E(g.id) + '">Mark received</button>');
    acts.push('<button type="button" class="w261b ghost" data-w261do="edit" data-id="' + E(g.id) + '">Edit</button>');
    acts.push('<button type="button" class="w261b ghost" data-w261do="remove" data-id="' + E(g.id) + '">Remove</button>');
    var del = S.confirmDel === String(g.id) ? '<div class="w261cf" role="alert">Remove this batch of ' + num(g.quantity) + '? It leaves the count on the website too.' +
      '<span><button type="button" class="w261b" data-w261do="removeok" data-id="' + E(g.id) + '">Remove it</button><button type="button" class="w261b ghost" data-w261do="keep">Keep it</button></span></div>' : '';
    return '<li class="w261r' + (waiting ? ' wait' : '') + '" data-w261row="' + E(g.id) + '"><div class="w261rt"><b>' + num(g.quantity) + '</b><span>' + E(g.what || 'Gifts') + '</span>' +
      '<i class="w261chip" style="--c:' + chip[1] + '">' + E(chip[0]) + '</i></div>' +
      ((g.given_by || g.note) ? '<p class="w261rm">' + (g.given_by ? 'From ' + E(g.given_by) : '') + (g.given_by && g.note ? ' \u00b7 ' : '') + (g.note ? E(g.note) : '') + '</p>' : '') +
      (S.form && S.form.id === String(g.id) ? formHtml(g) : '<div class="w261ra">' + acts.join('') + '</div>') + del + '</li>';
  }
  function formHtml(g) {
    var isNew = !g, v = g || { quantity: '', what: '', given_by: '', received_on: today(), status: 'received', note: '', confirmed_at: null };
    var conf = isNew ? true : !!v.confirmed_at, pid = 'w261f' + (isNew ? 'n' : v.id);
    return '<form class="w261f" data-w261form="' + (isNew ? '' : E(v.id)) + '" novalidate><h4>' + (isNew ? 'Add gifts' : 'Edit this batch') + '</h4>' +
      '<div class="w261two"><label for="' + pid + 'q">How many<input id="' + pid + 'q" name="quantity" type="number" min="1" step="1" inputmode="numeric" required value="' + E(v.quantity) + '"></label>' +
      '<label for="' + pid + 'w">What<input id="' + pid + 'w" name="what" maxlength="300" placeholder="Books, toys, hygiene kits" value="' + E(v.what || '') + '"></label></div>' +
      '<div class="w261two"><label for="' + pid + 'g">From <em>optional</em><input id="' + pid + 'g" name="given_by" maxlength="200" placeholder="A company, a school, a person" value="' + E(v.given_by || '') + '"></label>' +
      '<label for="' + pid + 'd">Date<input id="' + pid + 'd" name="received_on" type="date" value="' + E(v.received_on || '') + '"></label></div>' +
      '<div class="w261two"><label for="' + pid + 's">Status<select id="' + pid + 's" name="status"><option value="received"' + (v.status === 'received' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Received (in our hands)</option><option value="pledged"' + (v.status === 'pledged' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Pledged (promised)</option></select></label>' +
      '<label class="w261ck"' + (v.status === 'pledged' ? '' : ' hidden') + ' data-w261conf><input type="checkbox" name="confirmed"' + (conf ? ' checked' : '') + '> Confirmed with the giver (counts on the website)</label></div>' +
      '<label for="' + pid + 'n">Note <em>optional</em><textarea id="' + pid + 'n" name="note" maxlength="2000" rows="2">' + E(v.note || '') + '</textarea></label>' +
      (S.err ? '<p class="w261err" role="alert">' + E(S.err) + '</p>' : '') +
      '<div class="w261ra"><button type="submit" class="w261b"' + (S.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (S.busy ? 'Saving\u2026' : isNew ? 'Add them' : 'Save') + '</button><button type="button" class="w261b ghost" data-w261do="cancel">Cancel</button></div></form>';
  }
  function paint() {
    var b = box(); if (!b) return;
    var slug = b.getAttribute('data-w261slug'), d = S.data[slug]; if (!d) return;
    var goal = Number(d.gift_goal) || 0, n = Number(d.counted) || 0, pct = goal ? Math.max(0, Math.min(100, n / goal * 100)) : 0;
    var rows = d.rows || [];
    b.hidden = false;
    b.innerHTML = '<div class="w261hd"><h3>Gifts collected</h3><span class="w261n"><b>' + num(n) + '</b>' + (goal ? ' of ' + num(goal) : '') + '</span></div>' +
      (goal ? '<div class="w261bar" role="progressbar" aria-label="Gifts collected toward ' + num(goal) + '" aria-valuemin="0" aria-valuemax="' + goal + '" aria-valuenow="' + Math.min(n, goal) + '"><span style="width:' + (n > 0 ? Math.max(pct, 1.5) : 0).toFixed(1) + '%"></span></div>' : '') +
      '<p class="w261m">The drive\u2019s page on the website shows ' + num(n) + ': the batches received and the pledges someone confirmed. Money is counted apart, in the CRM\u2019s gifts.' +
      (Number(d.waiting_n) ? ' <b>' + (Number(d.waiting_n) === 1 ? 'One pledge waits' : num(d.waiting_n) + ' pledges wait') + ' for a Confirm (' + num(d.waiting) + ' items).</b>' : '') + '</p>' +
      (S.msg ? '<p class="w261ok" role="status">' + E(S.msg) + '</p>' : '') +
      (S.form && S.form.id === '' ? formHtml(null) : '<div class="w261ra"><button type="button" class="w261b" data-w261do="add">+ Add gifts</button></div>') +
      (S.err && !S.form ? '<p class="w261err" role="alert">' + E(S.err) + '</p>' : '') +
      (rows.length ? '<ul class="w261l">' + rows.map(rowHtml).join('') + '</ul>' : '<p class="w261m">No gifts recorded yet.</p>');
    syncConf();
  }
  function syncConf() {
    var f = document.querySelector('#w124sheet .w261f'); if (!f) return;
    var st = f.querySelector('select[name="status"]'), ck = f.querySelector('[data-w261conf]');
    if (st && ck) ck.hidden = st.value !== 'pledged';
  }
  function save(p, done) {
    var c = client(), b = box(); if (!c || !b) return;
    var slug = b.getAttribute('data-w261slug');
    S.busy = true; S.err = ''; if (S.form) paint();
    Promise.resolve(c.rpc('wa261_gift_save', { p: Object.assign({ slug: slug }, p) })).then(function (r) {
      S.busy = false;
      if (!r || r.error) { S.err = String((r && r.error && r.error.message) || 'Not saved. Try again.'); paint(); return; }
      S.form = null; S.confirmDel = null; S.msg = done || 'Saved.';
      return load(slug).then(function () { paint(); var a = document.querySelector('#w124sheet [data-w261do="add"]'); if (a) { try { a.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } });
    }).catch(function () { S.busy = false; S.err = 'Not saved: the Hub could not be reached.'; paint(); });
  }
  function rowById(id) { var b = box(), d = b && S.data[b.getAttribute('data-w261slug')]; return d ? (d.rows || []).filter(function (g) { return String(g.id) === String(id); })[0] || null : null; }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-w261do]') : null; if (!t || !t.closest('.w261g')) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    var a = t.getAttribute('data-w261do'), id = t.getAttribute('data-id');
    S.msg = '';
    if (a === 'add') { S.form = { id: '' }; S.err = ''; paint(); var q = document.querySelector('#w124sheet .w261f input[name="quantity"]'); if (q) q.focus(); return; }
    if (a === 'edit') { S.form = { id: String(id) }; S.err = ''; paint(); var q2 = document.querySelector('#w124sheet .w261f input[name="quantity"]'); if (q2) q2.focus(); return; }
    if (a === 'cancel') { S.form = null; S.err = ''; paint(); return; }
    if (a === 'remove') { S.confirmDel = String(id); paint(); return; }
    if (a === 'keep') { S.confirmDel = null; paint(); return; }
    if (a === 'removeok') { save({ action: 'remove', id: id }, 'Removed.'); return; }
    if (a === 'confirm') { var g = rowById(id); save({ action: 'confirm', id: id }, 'Confirmed: ' + num(g ? g.quantity : 0) + ' more on the website.'); return; }
    if (a === 'receive') { save({ action: 'receive', id: id, received_on: today() }, 'Marked received today.'); return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) { if (e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.w261f') && e.target.name === 'status') syncConf(); });
  document.addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
    var f = e.target; if (!f || !f.matches || !f.matches('.w261f')) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    var v = function (n) { var el = f.elements[n]; return el ? (el.type === 'checkbox' ? el.checked : el.value) : ''; };
    var q = String(v('quantity')).trim();
    if (!/^\d{1,7}$/.test(q) || Number(q) < 1) { S.err = 'How many gifts? A whole number above 0.'; paint(); var qi = document.querySelector('#w124sheet .w261f input[name="quantity"]'); if (qi) qi.focus(); return; }
    var id = f.getAttribute('data-w261form');
    save({ action: id ? 'edit' : 'add', id: id || null, quantity: q, what: v('what'), given_by: v('given_by'), received_on: v('received_on'), status: v('status'), confirmed: v('status') === 'pledged' ? !!v('confirmed') : false, note: v('note') },
      id ? 'Saved.' : 'Added: ' + num(q) + (v('status') === 'received' || v('confirmed') ? ' more on the website.' : ', waiting for a Confirm.'));
  });

  var css = document.createElement('style'); css.id = 'w261css';
  css.textContent = ''
    + '.w261g{margin:16px 0 4px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.14);border-top:4px solid ' + GOLD + ';border-radius:14px;padding:14px 16px;background:' + CREAM + ';}'
    + '.w261hd{display:flex;align-items:baseline;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;}.w261hd h3{margin:0;font-family:"Barlow Condensed",sans-serif;font-size:20px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.4px;color:' + GREEN + ';}'
    + '.w261n{font-size:14px;color:' + MUTED + ';}.w261n b{font-family:"Barlow Condensed",sans-serif;font-size:30px;color:' + GREEN + ';margin-right:4px;}'
    + '.w261bar{position:relative;height:10px;border-radius:6px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.1);overflow:hidden;margin:10px 0 6px;}.w261bar span{position:absolute;left:0;top:0;bottom:0;background:' + GOLD + ';border-radius:6px;}'
    + '.w261m{font-size:13px;color:' + MUTED + ';margin:6px 0 10px;line-height:1.45;}.w261m b{color:' + FLAG + ';font-weight:600;}.w261ok{font-size:13px;color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:600;margin:4px 0 8px;}.w261err{font-size:13px;color:' + FLAG + ';font-weight:600;margin:6px 0;}'
    + '.w261ra{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;margin:6px 0 2px;}'
    + '.w261b{font:inherit;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;border:1px solid ' + GREEN + ';background:' + GREEN + ';color:' + CREAM + ';border-radius:999px;padding:6px 13px;cursor:pointer;min-height:32px;}'
    + '.w261b.ghost{background:transparent;color:' + GREEN + ';border-color:rgba(16,63,57,.3);}.w261b:focus-visible{outline:2px solid ' + GOLD + ';outline-offset:2px;}.w261b[disabled]{opacity:.6;cursor:default;}'
    + '.w261l{list-style:none;margin:8px 0 0;padding:0;}.w261r{border-top:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.1);padding:10px 0;}.w261r.wait{background:linear-gradient(90deg,rgba(179,64,47,.06),transparent);}'
    + '.w261rt{display:flex;align-items:baseline;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:4px 8px;}.w261rt b{font-family:"Barlow Condensed",sans-serif;font-size:22px;color:' + GREEN + ';}.w261rt span{font-weight:600;color:' + INK + ';}'
    + '.w261chip{font-style:normal;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:600;border-radius:999px;padding:2px 9px;color:var(--c);background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--c) 12%,transparent);}'
    + '.w261rm{margin:3px 0 4px;font-size:13px;color:' + MUTED + ';overflow-wrap:anywhere;}'
    + '.w261cf{margin:6px 0 0;padding:8px 10px;border-radius:10px;background:' + SAND + ';font-size:13px;display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;align-items:center;}.w261cf span{display:flex;gap:6px;}'
    + '.w261f{margin:8px 0 4px;padding:10px 12px;border:1px dashed rgba(16,63,57,.25);border-radius:12px;}.w261f h4{margin:0 0 8px;font-size:14px;color:' + GREEN + ';}'

  var KEY_SKIP = 'wa272.skip', KEY_LATER = 'wa272.later';
  function myTasks() {
    try { var me = typeof WA_ME !== 'undefined' ? WA_ME : null; return me && typeof waTasksOf === 'function' ? waTasksOf(me.name) || [] : []; } catch (e) { return []; }
  }
  function overdue(t) { return !!t.due_date && t.due_date < isoToday(); }
  function dueToday(t) { return !!t.due_date && t.due_date <= isoToday(); }
  function skipped() {
    var o = null; try { o = JSON.parse(lsGet(KEY_SKIP) || 'null'); } catch (e) { o = null; }
    return o && o.day === isoToday() ? (o.ids || []) : [];
  }
  function ranked() {
    var skip = skipped().map(String);
    var rank = function (t) { return overdue(t) ? 0 : dueToday(t) ? 1 : t.due_date ? 2 : 3; };
    var a = myTasks().filter(function (t) { return t && t.id && !t.done; }).slice();
    a.sort(function (x, y) {
      var sx = skip.indexOf(String(x.id)) >= 0 ? 1 : 0, sy = skip.indexOf(String(y.id)) >= 0 ? 1 : 0;
      if (sx !== sy) return sx - sy;
      if (rank(x) !== rank(y)) return rank(x) - rank(y);
      return String(x.due_date || '9').localeCompare(String(y.due_date || '9'));
    });
    return a;
  }
  function todayCount() { return myTasks().filter(function (t) { return t && !t.done && dueToday(t); }).length; }

  /* ---------- the plain sentence ---------- */
  function paintSentence() {
    var head = document.querySelector('#mpBody > .mp-head'); if (!head) return;
    var el = document.getElementById('w272sum');
    if (!el || el.parentNode !== head) { el = document.createElement('p'); el.id = 'w272sum'; head.appendChild(el); }
    var all = myTasks().filter(function (t) { return t && !t.done; }), n = todayCount(), over = all.filter(overdue).length;
    var txt = n ? word(n) + (n === 1 ? ' thing' : ' things') + ' for today. ' + (over ? word(over) + (over === 1 ? ' is' : ' are') + ' overdue.' : 'Nothing is overdue.')
      : all.length ? 'Nothing is due today.' : 'You are clear for today.';
    if (el.textContent !== txt) el.textContent = txt;
  }

  /* ---------- Up next ---------- */
  var NX = { title: null, t: null };
  function nextHtml(list) {
    var t = list[0];
    if (!t) return '<h3>You are all caught up</h3><p>Nothing has your name on it right now.</p>';
    var dep = ''; try { dep = (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[t.department]) || ''; } catch (e) { dep = ''; }
    var when = overdue(t) ? 'Due ' + t.due_date : t.due_date ? (dueToday(t) ? 'Due today' : 'Due ' + t.due_date) : 'No due date';
    return '<small>Up next' + (overdue(t) ? ', overdue' : '') + '</small><h3>' + E(t.title) + '</h3><p>' + E(when) + (dep ? ', ' + E(dep) : '') + '</p>'
      + '<div class="acts"><button type="button" class="go" data-w272do="open" data-id="' + E(t.id) + '">Open task</button>'
      + '<button type="button" data-w272do="done" data-id="' + E(t.id) + '">Mark as done</button>'
      + (list.length > 1 ? '<button type="button" data-w272do="later" data-id="' + E(t.id) + '">Do this later</button>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function paintNext() {
    var body = document.getElementById('mpBody'); if (!body) return;
    var el = document.getElementById('w272next');
    if (!el || el.parentNode !== body) { el = document.createElement('section'); el.id = 'w272next'; el.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Up next'); el.setAttribute('aria-live', 'polite'); body.appendChild(el); }
    var list = ranked(), html = nextHtml(list), key = list[0] ? String(list[0].id) : '';
    if (el.__h === html) return;
    var swap = el.__h && el.__k !== key && !el.classList.contains('w272swap');
    el.__h = html; el.__k = key;
    var apply = function () { el.classList.toggle('clear', !list.length); el.innerHTML = html; el.classList.remove('w272swap'); };
    if (!swap) { apply(); return; }
    /* a change of task: one short blur crossfade, so the eye reads one card changing, not two swapping */
    el.classList.add('w272swap'); clearTimeout(NX.t); NX.t = setTimeout(apply, 140);
  }
  var toastT = null, undoFn = null;
  function toastUndo(msg, undo) {
    var el = document.getElementById('w272toast');
    if (!el) {
      el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w272toast'; el.setAttribute('role', 'status');
      el.innerHTML = '<span></span><button type="button">Undo</button>'; document.body.appendChild(el);
      el.querySelector('button').addEventListener('click', function () { var f = undoFn; undoFn = null; el.classList.remove('on'); if (f) f(); });
    }
    el.querySelector('span').textContent = msg; undoFn = undo;
    void el.offsetWidth; el.classList.add('on');
    clearTimeout(toastT); toastT = setTimeout(function () { el.classList.remove('on'); undoFn = null; }, 5000);
  }
  async function rerender() { try { if (typeof renderMyPage === 'function') await renderMyPage(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  document.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
    var b = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w272do]'); if (!b) return;
    var kind = b.getAttribute('data-w272do'), id = b.getAttribute('data-id'), num = Number(id);
    if (kind === 'open') { try { if (window.w116Board && window.w116Board.open) window.w116Board.open(isNaN(num) ? id : num); } catch (x) { say('Could not open that task'); } return; }
    if (kind === 'later') {
      var s = skipped().map(String); if (s.indexOf(String(id)) < 0) s.push(String(id));
      lsSet(KEY_SKIP, JSON.stringify({ day: isoToday(), ids: s })); paintNext(); return;
    }
    if (kind === 'done') {
      if (typeof toggleTask !== 'function') return;
      var title = (myTasks().filter(function (t) { return String(t.id) === String(id); })[0] || {}).title || 'Task';
      b.disabled = true;
      var ok = false; try { ok = await toggleTask(isNaN(num) ? id : num, true); } catch (x) { ok = false; }
      b.disabled = false;
      if (!ok) { say('Could not save that. Try again.'); return; }
      toastUndo('Done: ' + title, async function () { try { await toggleTask(isNaN(num) ? id : num, false); } catch (x) { say('Could not undo that'); } await rerender(); });
      await rerender();
    }
  });

  /* ---------- My tasks: today first, the rest behind Later ---------- */
  function foldTasks() {
    var page = document.getElementById('mypage'), sec = null;
    document.querySelectorAll('#mpBody .mp-sec').forEach(function (s) { var h = s.querySelector(':scope > h3'); if (!sec && h && /^My tasks/.test(h.textContent || '')) sec = s; });
    if (!sec || !page) return;
    var byId = {}; myTasks().forEach(function (t) { byId[String(t.id)] = t; });
    var rows = Array.prototype.slice.call(sec.querySelectorAll('[data-w109]')), later = 0;
    rows.forEach(function (r) {
      var t = byId[r.getAttribute('data-w109')], lt = !t || !dueToday(t), holder = (r.closest && r.closest('.w74extra')) || r;
      holder.classList.toggle('w272lt', lt); if (lt) later++;
    });
    later += Math.max(0, myTasks().filter(function (t) { return t && !t.done && !dueToday(t); }).length - later);
    sec.querySelectorAll('.w74grp').forEach(function (g) {
      var any = false, n = g.nextElementSibling;
      while (n && !n.classList.contains('w74grp')) { if (n.querySelector('[data-w109]') || n.hasAttribute('data-w109')) { if (!n.classList.contains('w272lt')) any = true; } n = n.nextElementSibling; }
      g.classList.toggle('w272lt', !any);
    });
    var open = lsGet(KEY_LATER) === '1'; page.classList.toggle('w272later', open);
    var note = sec.querySelector('#w272none');
    if (!rows.length || todayCount()) { if (note) note.remove(); }
    else if (!note) { note = document.createElement('p'); note.id = 'w272none'; note.className = 'mp-empty'; note.textContent = 'Nothing due today.'; sec.querySelector(':scope > h3').insertAdjacentElement('afterend', note); }
    var btn = document.getElementById('w272laterbtn');
    if (!later) { if (btn) btn.remove(); return; }
    if (!btn || btn.parentNode !== sec) { btn = document.createElement('button'); btn.type = 'button'; btn.id = 'w272laterbtn'; sec.appendChild(btn); }
    var lab = 'Later, ' + later + (later === 1 ? ' task' : ' tasks');
    if (btn.getAttribute('data-l') !== lab) { btn.setAttribute('data-l', lab); btn.innerHTML = '<span>' + lab + '</span><span class="c" aria-hidden="true">&#8250;</span>'; }
    btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(open));
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var b = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w272laterbtn, [data-w74all]'); if (!b) return;
    var open = b.id === 'w272laterbtn' ? lsGet(KEY_LATER) !== '1' : true;
    lsSet(KEY_LATER, open ? '1' : '0');
    var page = document.getElementById('mypage'); if (page) page.classList.toggle('w272later', open);
    var btn = document.getElementById('w272laterbtn'); if (btn) btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(open));
  });

  /* ---------- Your apps ---------- */
  function paintApps() {
    var body = document.getElementById('mpBody'), el = document.getElementById('w272apps'), A = window.WA_APP, Y = null;
    try { Y = A && A.yours ? A.yours() : null; } catch (e) { Y = null; }
    var ids = Y ? (Y.yours || []) : [], any = Y && (ids.length || (Y.more || []).length);
    if (!body || !A || !any) { if (el) el.remove(); return; }
    if (!el || el.parentNode !== body) { el = document.createElement('section'); el.id = 'w272apps'; el.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Your apps'); body.appendChild(el); }
    var tile = function (id) { var a = A.apps[id]; return a ? '<button type="button" data-w272app="' + E(id) + '"><span class="ti">' + (A.icon ? A.icon(a.ic) : '') + '</span>' + E(a.label) + '</button>' : ''; };
    var html = '<h3>Your apps</h3><div class="tiles">' + ids.slice(0, 7).map(tile).join('')
      + '<button type="button" class="all" data-w272app="*" aria-haspopup="true"><span class="ti">' + (A.icon ? A.icon('grid') : '') + '</span>All apps</button></div>';
    if (el.__h !== html) { el.innerHTML = html; el.__h = html; }
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var b = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w272app]'); if (!b) return;
    var id = b.getAttribute('data-w272app');
    if (id !== '*') { try { window.WA_APP.open(id); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } return; }
    e.stopPropagation();
    var g = document.querySelector('#w153dock .w153grid');
    if (g && g.offsetParent !== null) { g.click(); return; }
    var m = document.querySelector('[data-w163slot="menu"]'); if (m) m.click();
  }, true);

  /* ---------- Set up your Hub: the app, the notifications, the policies ----------
     Steel (02/11/26): "people should have these up until the action is complete", so the card has no Not now: each row
     stays until it is done, and the card leaves on its own once every row is. One tap does the work wherever the browser
     allows it; where it does not, the row says exactly what is in the way and what to do. On an iPhone the two steps
     have an order (Apple only allows Hub notifications from the Home Screen app), so the second waits for the first.
     Policies: the required ones a person has not signed, each with Open and Sign; a signed policy never comes back here
     (the Policies card of wave105 stays hidden on Home; the training page keeps its own). */

  var UA = navigator.userAgent || '';
  var IPAD = /iPad/.test(UA) || (/Macintosh/.test(UA) && (navigator.maxTouchPoints || 0) > 1);

  var FIREFOX = /Firefox\//.test(UA) && !IOS && !ANDROID;
  var BIP = false;
  window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', function (e) { BIP = true; try { e.preventDefault(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } paintSetup(); });   /* wave219 keeps the event; data-w219="install" fires it */
  window.addEventListener('appinstalled', function () { lsSet(KEY_APP, '1'); BIP = false; paintSetup(); closeTip(); });

  function push() { try { return window.__wa219 && window.__wa219.state ? window.__wa219.state() : null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function pushRelevant() { return staff(); }
  function pushOn() { var s = push(); return !!(s && s.mine); }
  /* done means confirmed: on for this device, and a test notification that arrived and was tapped (Steel, 02/11/26) */
  function pushDone() { return pushOn() && lsGet(KEY_OK()) === '1'; }
  var S = { shown: false, pushBusy: false, pushMsg: '', pushErr: '', pushKind: '', pols: null, polsAt: 0, polBusy: {}, polsPending: 0, test: '', testAt: 0, testTimer: null };
  function appRelevant() { return !FIREFOX; }
  function allDone() { return (!appRelevant() || appDone()) && (!pushRelevant() || pushDone()) && !S.polsPending; }

  /* ---------- the policies to sign (the same tables as wave105) ---------- */
  function meEmail() { try { return String((USER && USER.email) || ''); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function meName() { try { return (typeof WA_ME !== 'undefined' && WA_ME && WA_ME.name) || (USER && USER.name) || ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function loadPolicies(force) {
    if (!staff() || !meEmail() || typeof db === 'undefined') { S.pols = []; S.polsPending = 0; return Promise.resolve([]); }
    if (!force && S.pols && Date.now() - S.polsAt < 60000) return Promise.resolve(S.pols);
    return Promise.all([
      db.from('wa_policies').select('key,title,url,sort').eq('active', true).eq('required', true).order('sort'),
      db.from('wa_policy_signoffs').select('policy_key').eq('email', meEmail())
    ]).then(function (r) {
      var signed = {}; (((r[1] || {}).data) || []).forEach(function (s) { signed[s.policy_key] = true; });
      S.pols = (((r[0] || {}).data) || []).filter(function (p) { return !signed[p.key]; });
      S.polsAt = Date.now(); S.polsPending = S.pols.length; return S.pols;
    }).catch(function () { S.pols = S.pols || []; S.polsPending = S.pols.length; return S.pols; });
  }
  function opened(key) { try { return (JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem(KEY_OPENED) || '[]')).indexOf(key) >= 0; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function markOpened(key) { try { var a = JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem(KEY_OPENED) || '[]'); if (a.indexOf(key) < 0) a.push(key); sessionStorage.setItem(KEY_OPENED, JSON.stringify(a)); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  async function signPolicy(key, btn) {
    if (S.polBusy[key]) return; S.polBusy[key] = true; if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'true'); }
    try {
      var ins = await db.from('wa_policy_signoffs').insert({ email: meEmail(), name: meName(), policy_key: key });
      if (ins && ins.error) throw { db: String(ins.error.message || '') };
      say('Sign-off recorded, thank you');
      S.pols = (S.pols || []).filter(function (p) { return p.key !== key; }); S.polsPending = S.pols.length;
    } catch (e) { say((e && e.db) || 'Could not record the sign-off'); if (window.wa279Pol) window.wa279Pol.reload(); }   /* wave279: the database's refusal ("Read it for N more minutes first") shows as it is */
    S.polBusy[key] = false; paintSetup();
  }

  /* ---------- the rows ---------- */
  function rowApp() {
    if (!appRelevant()) return null;
    var done = appDone(), sub, act = '';
    if (standalone()) sub = 'You are using the Hub app.';
    else if (done) sub = 'Added. Open it from its icon.';
    else if (IOS) sub = 'Its own icon on your Home Screen. Under a minute.';
    else if (ANDROID) sub = 'Its own icon on your phone. Under a minute.';
    else sub = 'Its own window and icon on this computer, and on your phone too.';
    if (!done) act = (BIP ? '<button type="button" class="go" data-w219="install" data-w272s="again">Install</button><button type="button" class="lnk" data-w272s="how">On your phone</button>' : '<button type="button" class="go" data-w272s="how">Show me how</button>');
    return { k: 'app', ok: done, n: '1', t: 'Add the Hub as an app', s: sub, act: act };
  }
  function rowPush(n) {
    if (!pushRelevant()) return null;
    var s = push(), done = pushDone(), on = pushOn(), sub, act = '', err = false;
    var waitIos = IOS && !standalone();
    if (done) sub = 'On and confirmed for this device. Settings, Notifications chooses what reaches you.';
    else if (on && S.pushBusy) sub = S.pushMsg || 'Sending\u2026';
    else if (on && S.test === 'arrived') { sub = 'It arrived on this device. Tap the notification to finish.'; act = '<button type="button" class="lnk" data-w272s="saw">I saw it</button>'; }
    else if (on && S.test === 'sent') { sub = 'Sent. It should pop up in a moment: tap it, and this row ticks.'; act = '<button type="button" class="lnk" data-w272s="saw">I saw it</button>'; }
    else if (on && S.test === 'late') { sub = 'Nothing arrived in a minute. Check that this computer and the browser allow notifications for the Hub (Focus assist and Do not disturb hide them), then send again.'; err = true; act = '<button type="button" class="go" data-w272s="test">Send again</button><button type="button" class="lnk" data-w272s="saw">It showed up</button>'; }
    else if (on && S.test === 'failed') { sub = S.pushErr || 'The test did not go through. Try again.'; err = true; act = '<button type="button" class="go" data-w272s="test">Send again</button>'; }
    else if (on) { sub = 'On. Send yourself a test and tap it, so we know it reaches you.'; act = '<button type="button" class="go" data-w272s="test">Send a test</button>'; }
    else if (S.pushBusy) sub = S.pushMsg || 'Asking your browser\u2026';
    else if (waitIos) sub = 'Add the Hub first, then open it from its icon and turn these on there. Apple only allows them in the app.';
    else if (s && !s.supported) sub = 'This browser cannot show Hub notifications. Chrome, Edge, Firefox and Safari can.';
    else if (s && s.perm === 'denied') { sub = blockedText(); err = true; act = '<button type="button" class="go" data-w272s="recheck">Check again</button>'; }
    else if (S.pushErr && S.pushKind === 'reload') { sub = S.pushErr; err = true; act = '<button type="button" class="go" data-w272s="reload">Reload and finish</button>'; }
    else if (S.pushErr) { sub = S.pushErr; err = true; act = '<button type="button" class="go" data-w272s="push">Try again</button>'; }
    else { sub = 'When someone mentions you, gives you work or posts an announcement for you.'; act = '<button type="button" class="go" data-w272s="push">Turn on</button>'; }
    return { k: 'push', ok: done, n: String(n), t: 'Turn on notifications', s: sub, err: err, act: act };
  }
  function blockedText() {
    if (IOS) return 'Notifications are blocked for the Hub app. In the ' + (IPAD ? 'iPad' : 'iPhone') + '\u2019s Settings, open Notifications, then WA Hub, and allow them. Then come back here.';
    if (standalone()) return 'Notifications are blocked for the Hub app. Open the app\u2019s menu at the top (\u22ee), choose App info or Settings, allow Notifications, then come back here.';
    return 'Notifications are blocked for the Hub in this browser. Click the icon at the left of the address bar (a lock or two sliders), set Notifications to Allow, then come back here.';
  }
  function rowsPolicies(n0) {
    return (S.pols || []).map(function (p, i) {
      /* wave279: Sign waits for the reading time (wa279Pol, parts279/b_hooks.js): "Sign in N min", then Sign */
      var W = window.wa279Pol, seen = opened(p.key) || !!(W && W.opened(p.key)), busy = !!S.polBusy[p.key], v = W ? W.view(p.key, 'Sign', seen) : { dis: !seen, txt: 'Sign' };
      return { k: 'pol:' + p.key, ok: false, n: String(n0 + i), t: 'Read and sign: ' + p.title, s: seen ? 'Read it? Then sign here. HR keeps the date.' : 'Open it, read it, then sign here. HR keeps the date.',
        act: '<a class="lnk" href="' + E(p.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener" data-w272pol="open" data-w279polopen="' + E(p.key) + '" data-key="' + E(p.key) + '">Open</a><button type="button" class="go" data-w272pol="sign" data-key="' + E(p.key) + '"' + (busy ? ' aria-busy="true" disabled' : (W ? W.attrs(p.key, 'Sign', seen) : '') + (v.dis ? ' disabled' : '')) + '>' + (busy ? 'Saving\u2026' : E(v.txt)) + '</button>' };
    });
  }
  function rowHtml(r) {
    return '<div><div class="in"><span class="mk" aria-hidden="true"><i>' + r.n + '</i><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M5 12.5 10 17.5 19 7"/></svg></span><div class="tt"><b>' + E(r.t) + '</b><small' + (r.err ? ' class="err"' : '') + '>' + E(r.s) + '</small></div>' + (r.act && !r.ok ? '<div class="act">' + r.act + '</div>' : '') + '</div></div>';
  }
  function paintSetup() {
    var body = document.getElementById('mpBody'), el = document.getElementById('w272setup'), page = document.getElementById('mypage');
    var ready = staff() ? !!(push() && push().checked) : board();
    if (appDone()) { try { if (window.w112Api && window.w112Api.loaded && window.w112Api.loaded()) window.w112Api.set('app', true); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    if (staff() && S.pols === null && !S.polsLoading) { S.polsLoading = true; loadPolicies(false).then(function () { S.polsLoading = false; paintSetup(); }); }
    var done = allDone();
    /* after Reload and finish: the permission is already granted, so no tap is needed to carry on */
    if (ready && staff() && lsGet(KEY_AUTO) === '1') { lsSet(KEY_AUTO, null); var st0 = push() || {}; if (st0.perm === 'granted' && !pushOn() && !S.pushBusy) setTimeout(turnOnPush, 400); }
    if (!body || !page || !ready || (done && !el)) { if (el && !done) el.remove(); return; }
    if (done) {
      /* every row done: the card says so once, then leaves */
      if (el.classList.contains('w272bye')) return;
      el.classList.add('w272bye'); el.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
      setTimeout(function () { var x = document.getElementById('w272setup'); if (x && x.classList.contains('w272bye')) x.remove(); }, 320);
      if (S.shown) { S.shown = false; say('Your Hub is set up.'); }
      return;
    }
    if (!el || el.parentNode !== body) { el = document.createElement('section'); el.id = 'w272setup'; el.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Set up your Hub'); el.innerHTML = '<div><div class="hd"><h3>Set up your Hub</h3><span class="prog"></span></div><p class="why"></p><div class="rows"></div></div>'; body.appendChild(el); }
    el.classList.remove('w272bye'); el.removeAttribute('aria-hidden'); S.shown = true;
    if (!page.classList.contains('w272')) { var h = body.querySelector(':scope > .mp-online') || body.querySelector(':scope > .mp-head'); if (h && h.nextElementSibling !== el) h.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', el); }
    var rows = [], a = rowApp(); if (a) rows.push(a);
    var p = rowPush(rows.length + 1); if (p) rows.push(p);
    rows = rows.concat(rowsPolicies(rows.length + 1));
    var doneN = rows.filter(function (r) { return r.ok; }).length;
    el.querySelector('.prog').textContent = doneN + ' of ' + rows.length + ' done';
    el.querySelector('.why').textContent = board() ? 'Quick, and it keeps the Hub one tap away.' : 'Quick. It keeps the Hub one tap away, and finishes your onboarding.';
    /* rows are kept in place, so a tick draws and a finished row closes instead of the card redrawing */
    var host = el.querySelector('.rows'), keep = {};
    rows.forEach(function (r, i) {
      keep[r.k] = 1;
      var rw = host.querySelector('.rw[data-k="' + r.k + '"]');
      if (!rw) { rw = document.createElement('div'); rw.className = 'rw'; rw.setAttribute('data-k', r.k); rw.innerHTML = rowHtml(r); host.insertBefore(rw, host.children[i] || null); rw.__h = rw.innerHTML; }
      else {
        if (host.children[i] !== rw) host.insertBefore(rw, host.children[i] || null);
        var html = rowHtml(r);
        if (rw.__h !== html) {
          var inner = rw.querySelector('.in'), tt = inner.querySelector('.tt'), act = inner.querySelector('.act');
          inner.querySelector('.mk i').textContent = r.n; tt.querySelector('b').textContent = r.t;
          var sm = tt.querySelector('small'); sm.textContent = r.s; sm.className = r.err ? 'err' : '';
          var want = r.act && !r.ok ? '<div class="act">' + r.act + '</div>' : '';
          if ((act ? act.outerHTML : '') !== want) { if (act) act.remove(); if (want) inner.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', want); }
          rw.__h = html;
        }
      }
      rw.classList.toggle('ok', !!r.ok); rw.classList.remove('gone');
    });
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(host.querySelectorAll('.rw'), function (rw) {
      if (keep[rw.getAttribute('data-k')]) return;
      if (rw.classList.contains('gone')) return;
      rw.classList.add('ok'); setTimeout(function () { rw.classList.add('gone'); }, 420); setTimeout(function () { rw.remove(); }, 720);
    });
  }

  /* ---------- notifications: one tap, and the result said in the row ----------
     Steel (02/11/26) got 'This browser did not start the Hub's notification helper': wave219 waits ten seconds for the
     service worker and says only that. Here the helper is checked first: the browser's own refusal, or the worker's state,
     goes in the row, with what to do about it. When the permission still has to be asked, wave219 goes first, straight
     from the tap (Safari only allows the question there), and the helper is looked at only if that fails. */
  function waitActive(reg, ms) {
    return new Promise(function (res) {
      if (reg.active) return res(reg.active);
      var done = false, t = setTimeout(function () { if (!done) { done = true; res(null); } }, ms);
      function check() { if (reg.active && !done) { done = true; clearTimeout(t); res(reg.active); } }
      var sw = reg.installing || reg.waiting; if (sw) sw.addEventListener('statechange', check);
      reg.addEventListener('updatefound', function () { var n = reg.installing; if (n) n.addEventListener('statechange', check); });
    });
  }
  async function helper() {
    if (!('serviceWorker' in navigator)) return { ok: false, why: 'This browser has no notification helper (no service workers), so Hub notifications cannot work here.' };
    var reg = null, err = '';
    try { reg = await navigator.serviceWorker.getRegistration('/'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    if (!reg) { try { reg = await navigator.serviceWorker.register('/sw.js', { scope: '/' }); } catch (e) { err = String((e && e.message) || e || 'no reason given'); } }
    if (!reg) {
      if (/invalid state/i.test(err)) return { ok: false, kind: 'reload', why: 'This Hub window has lost touch with the browser\u2019s background helper (the browser calls it an invalid document state). Reload, and the Hub finishes turning them on by itself.' };
      var store = /storage|cookie|insecure|security|denied/i.test(err);
      return { ok: false, why: 'Your browser refused the Hub\u2019s notification helper (' + err.replace(/^Failed to register a ServiceWorker[^:]*:s*/i, '').slice(0, 160) + ').' + (store ? ' Allow cookies and site data for hub.welfareavenue.com in the browser\u2019s site settings, then reload the Hub.' : ' Reload the Hub and try again.') };
    }
    var act = await waitActive(reg, 10000);
    if (!act) { var st = reg.installing ? 'still installing' : reg.waiting ? 'waiting to start' : 'not running'; return { ok: false, why: 'The Hub\u2019s notification helper is ' + st + ' after ten seconds. Close every Hub window, open the Hub again and try once more.' }; }
    return { ok: true };
  }
  async function turnOnPush() {
    var A = window.__wa219;
    if (!A || !A.turnOn) { S.pushErr = 'The Hub is still loading. Try again in a moment.'; paintSetup(); return; }
    if (S.pushBusy) return;
    S.pushBusy = true; S.pushErr = ''; S.pushMsg = 'Asking your browser\u2026'; paintSetup();
    var st0 = push() || {};
    if (st0.perm === 'granted') {
      S.pushMsg = 'Starting the notification helper\u2026'; paintSetup();
      var h0 = await helper();
      if (!h0.ok) { S.pushBusy = false; S.pushMsg = ''; S.pushErr = h0.why; S.pushKind = h0.kind || ''; paintSetup(); return; }
      S.pushMsg = 'Saving this device\u2026'; paintSetup();
    }
    var slow = setTimeout(function () { S.pushMsg = 'Still working. If nothing happens, reload the Hub and try again.'; paintSetup(); }, 12000);
    /* wave219 says what went wrong in a toast; here it goes in the row instead */
    var said = '', t0 = window.toast, had = typeof t0 === 'function';
    if (had) window.toast = function (m) { said = String(m || ''); };
    var ok = false;
    try { ok = await A.turnOn(); } catch (e) { ok = false; said = said || String((e && e.message) || e || ''); }
    if (had) window.toast = t0;
    clearTimeout(slow); S.pushBusy = false; S.pushMsg = '';
    if (ok) { S.pushErr = ''; S.pushKind = ''; say('Hub notifications are on for this device. Now send yourself a test.'); }
    else {
      var st = push() || {};
      S.pushErr = st.perm === 'denied' ? '' : (said && !/^Hub notifications are on/.test(said) ? said : 'That did not work. Try again, or reload the Hub and try once more.');
      /* wave219's one-line answer about the helper: look at the helper and say what is really the matter */
      S.pushKind = '';
      if (/notification helper/.test(S.pushErr)) { try { var h1 = await helper(); if (!h1.ok) { S.pushErr = h1.why; S.pushKind = h1.kind || ''; } } catch (e) { /* keep */ } }
    }
    paintSetup();
  }
  async function sendTest() {
    var A = window.__wa219; if (!A || !A.test || S.pushBusy) return;
    S.pushBusy = true; S.pushMsg = 'Sending a test\u2026'; S.test = ''; paintSetup();
    var said = '', t0 = window.toast, had = typeof t0 === 'function';
    if (had) window.toast = function (m) { said = String(m || ''); };
    try { await A.test(); } catch (e) { said = said || String((e && e.message) || e || ''); }
    if (had) window.toast = t0;
    S.pushBusy = false; S.pushMsg = '';
    if (/^Sent/.test(said)) { S.test = 'sent'; S.testAt = Date.now(); clearTimeout(S.testTimer); S.testTimer = setTimeout(function () { if (S.test === 'sent') { S.test = 'late'; paintSetup(); } }, 60000); }
    else { S.test = 'failed'; S.pushErr = said || 'The test did not go through. Try again.'; }
    paintSetup();
  }
  function confirmPush(how) {
    if (!meEmail()) return;
    lsSet(KEY_OK(), '1'); S.test = ''; clearTimeout(S.testTimer);
    say(how === 'tap' ? 'Notifications confirmed on this device.' : 'Thanks. Notifications are set up on this device.');
    paintSetup();
  }
  /* the test arriving while the Hub is open, and the tap on it, come through wave219's worker messages */
  window.addEventListener('wa-push', function () { if (S.test === 'sent') { S.test = 'arrived'; paintSetup(); } });
  try {
    if ('serviceWorker' in navigator && navigator.serviceWorker.addEventListener) navigator.serviceWorker.addEventListener('message', function (e) {
      var d = e && e.data; if (!d || d.type !== 'wa-open') return;
      if ((S.test === 'sent' || S.test === 'arrived') && Date.now() - S.testAt < 15 * 60000) confirmPush('tap');
    });
  } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  async function recheck() {
    var A = window.__wa219; if (!A || !A.check) return;
    S.pushErr = ''; S.pushMsg = 'Checking\u2026'; S.pushBusy = true; paintSetup();
    try { await A.check(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    S.pushBusy = false; S.pushMsg = ''; paintSetup();
  }
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function () {
    /* back from the browser's settings or the phone's: look again */
    if (document.visibilityState === 'visible' && document.getElementById('w272setup') && !S.pushBusy) { var A = window.__wa219; if (A && A.check) { Promise.resolve(A.check()).then(paintSetup, paintSetup); } }
  });
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var b = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w272s],[data-w272pol]'); if (!b) return;
    var k = b.getAttribute('data-w272s'), pk = b.getAttribute('data-w272pol'), key = b.getAttribute('data-key');
    if (pk === 'open') { markOpened(key); setTimeout(paintSetup, 300); return; }   /* the link opens in a new tab on its own */

    if (k === 'push') { turnOnPush(); return; }

    if (k === 'reload') { lsSet(KEY_AUTO, '1'); try { location.reload(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return; }
    if (k === 'recheck') { recheck(); return; }
    if (k === 'again') { [800, 3000, 8000].forEach(function (ms) { setTimeout(paintSetup, ms); }); closeTip(); return; }
  });

  }

  }
